The relevant moment may last only seconds within a video spanning several minutes, creating an extremely low signal-to-noise ratio that makes PRVR more challenging than standard full-video retrieval. This task presents two intertwined challenges: (1) signal dilution, where coarse global representations blur the brief relevant signal into the dominant irrelevant surroundings;(2) curvature rigidity, where embedding all videos in the same fixed-geometry space distorts representations for videos that range from flat atomic events to deep compositional hierarchies. Existing PRVR methods have improved moment selection and cross-modal matching, but they still typically encode all videos in a single fixed-curvature retrieval space, limiting their ability to model diverse video structures. To address both challenges, we propose CurvSpec, a framework that learns content-adaptive curvature for video retrieval representations rather than imposing a fixed geometric prior. CurvSpec processes features through parallel Euclidean and hyperbolic attention layers, with independently learned curvatures assigned to the hyperbolic layers, and a content-aware fusion mechanism routes each input to its most suitable geometric regime. To further suppress signal dilution, CurvSpec represents each video with semantic centroids whose number is determined by the video's content complexity, projects them onto the learned manifold, and matches each query against its nearest centroid by geodesic distance. Experiments on ActivityNet Captions, TVR, and Charades-STA demonstrate state-of-the-art retrieval performance.

</details>


### [68] [RED: Reconstruction Evolution Dynamics for Generalizable AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36822)
*Wenpeng Mu,Junshan Jin,Tanfeng Sun,Xinghao Jiang,Qiang Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RED框架，利用多尺度VQ-VAE重建轨迹中逐尺度token可预测性的演变规律作为取证线索，在六个基准上取得92.5%平均准确率与97.5%平均精度，并对常见图像退化具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像生成器快速演进，现有检测器多依赖静态图像表示或端点重建差异，忽略中间重建阶段的演变信息；作者观察到真实图像与生成图像的相对token可预测性会随重建尺度发生反转，提示中间阶段蕴含被端点比较忽略的取证证据。

Method: 提出RED（Reconstruction Evolution Dynamics）框架：以冻结的多尺度VQ-VAE生成的重建轨迹为对象，在冻结CLIP编码器的共享特征空间中表示该轨迹；利用冻结VAR模型提供的逐尺度token负对数似然，学习图像自适应的阶段权重，将可预测性变化与视觉证据关联；再通过跨阶段证据聚合模块联合建模原图表示与加权重建特征，沿重建轨迹捕获互补取证线索。

Result: 在六个多样化基准上，RED在所评估方法中取得最高平均准确率92.5%与平均精度97.5%；进一步评估显示其对常见图像退化具有较强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 首次以逐尺度token可预测性引导跨中间重建状态的取证证据聚合，验证了重建演变信息对可泛化AI生成图像检测的价值，代码将在论文录用后公开。

Abstract: The rapid evolution of image generators calls for forensic cues that generalize beyond known generation mechanisms. Existing detectors often rely on static image representations or endpoint reconstruction discrepancies, leaving the evolution of intermediate reconstruction stages underexplored. We observe that the relative token predictability of real and generated images can reverse across reconstruction scales, suggesting that intermediate stages may expose forensic evidence overlooked by endpoint comparisons. Motivated by this observation, we propose RED (Reconstruction Evolution Dynamics), a framework that captures transferable forensic cues from coarse-to-fine reconstruction evolution. To our knowledge, RED is the first framework to use scale-wise token predictability to guide forensic evidence aggregation across intermediate reconstruction states. It represents the reconstruction trajectory produced by a frozen multiscale VQ-VAE in the shared feature space of a frozen CLIP encoder. To connect the observed predictability variations with visual evidence, RED learns image-adaptive stage weights from scale-wise token negative log-likelihoods provided by a frozen VAR model. A cross-stage evidence aggregation module then jointly models the original-image representation and the weighted reconstruction features, capturing complementary forensic cues through interactions along the reconstruction trajectory. Experiments on six diverse benchmarks demonstrate that RED achieves the highest average accuracy of 92.5\% and average precision of 97.5\% among the evaluated methods. Further evaluations show strong robustness to common image degradations, supporting the value of reconstruction evolution for generalizable AI-generated image detection. The code will be made publicly available upon acceptance of this paper.

</details>


### [69] [Learning via Self-Consistency for Diffusion-based Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36826)
*Zhenghao Ni,Weimin Qiu,Meng Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将LLM中的自洽性（self-consistency）思想迁移到扩散式视频生成模型中，先在测试时通过多rollout采样聚合共识预测，再以拒绝微调（RFT）将共识蒸馏进模型，使单次生成即可获得大幅提升。在迷宫求解、视觉搜索和指代分割三任务上均显著改善视频感知与推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散式视频生成在视觉推理、感知等任务上展现出零样本能力，但生成过程本质上是随机的，而多数下游视觉任务要求确定性输出。受大语言模型链式思维推理中自洽性有效性的启发，作者探究自洽性能否同样提升基于扩散的视频推理。

Method: 1）无需训练测试时缩放：多次采样视频生成，从多个rollout中提取路径、位置或掩码并聚合为共识预测；为降低多rollout开销，在去噪轨迹早期读出预测，在保持共识质量的同时将去噪步数减少一半以上。2）拒绝微调（RFT）：将共识预测蒸馏回视频生成模型，使模型内化多采样共识的优势，推理时仅需单次生成。

Result: 在迷宫求解、视觉搜索和指代分割三个任务上，自洽推理与共识蒸馏均显著提升视频感知与推理，且无需真值视频或任务特定验证器。视觉搜索中，自洽性将准确率从单次生成的48.4%提升至99.0%；4×4迷宫求解中，基于共识的训练将单次生成严格成功率从72.0%提升至84.0%，推理延迟不变。

Conclusion: 自洽性可有效迁移至扩散式视频生成，多rollout共识推理与共识蒸馏能在无真值视频、无任务特定验证的条件下大幅提升视频推理性能；蒸馏模型以单次rollout即可保留大部分共识收益，兼顾性能与推理效率。

Abstract: Video generation models have demonstrated emerging zero-shot capabilities for visual reasoning, perception, and other vision tasks. However, diffusion-based video generation is inherently stochastic, while many downstream vision tasks are deterministic. Motivated by the effectiveness of self-consistency in chain-of-thought reasoning for large language models, we investigate whether self-consistency can similarly improve diffusion-based video reasoning. We first introduce a training-free test-time scaling method that samples multiple video generations and aggregates their predictions through self-consistency. Specifically, we aggregate extracted paths, locations, or masks from multiple rollouts into a consensus prediction. To reduce the inference overhead of multi-rollout generation, we read out predictions early in the denoising trajectory, which preserves consensus quality while reducing denoising steps by more than half. We further propose Rejection Fine-Tuning (RFT) to distill consensus predictions into the video generation model. The resulting model internalizes the benefit of multi-sample consensus and requires only a single generation at inference time, while substantially outperforming the original model. Experiments on three tasks, including maze solving, visual search, and referring segmentation, show that both our self-consistency inference and consensus distillation dramatically improve video-based perception and reasoning, without requiring ground-truth videos or task-specific verification. For visual search, self-consistency raises task accuracy from 48.4% for a single generation to 99.0%. The distilled model retains much of the consensus benefit with a single rollout. For 4-by-4 maze solving, consensus-based training improves the single-generation strict success rate from 72.0% to 84.0% with the same inference latency.

</details>


### [70] [Motion Concept Unlearning in Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36832)
*Ping Liu,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究视频扩散Transformer中的运动概念擦除，提出免训练方法MUTE，在20个运动概念上超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到视频扩散模型能生成踢、刺、射击等动作的真实画面，引发安全担忧，需要针对性概念擦除。现有概念擦除研究多集中于文生图和T2V中的静态概念，运动概念擦除基本未被探索。

Method: 通过因果干预发现：文本条件注意力携带概念特定的运动信息并支持选择性干预，而扰动时间位置编码会同时抑制目标与非目标动态；直接迁移图像级权重擦除方法ESD到视频DiT效果有限且不均匀，因为降低擦除训练损失并不能消除条件与无条件预测差中的概念信号，CFG会在每个去噪步放大该信号。据此提出运动概念擦除的三项要求：概念特异性、空间选择性、时间自然性。MUTE在每步去噪中通过token中性化提取概念方向，由该方向的内在结构导出空间门控，并在应用CFG前从速度输出中减去相应修正。MUTE免训练，无需修改权重。

Result: 在20个运动概念上，MUTE在Wan2.1-T2V上优于代表性的提示级、权重级和推理时基线，且相同公式可迁移至CogVideoX，支持其跨不同T2V注意力架构的适用性。

Conclusion: MUTE为视频DiT中的运动概念擦除提供了免训练、无需改权重的有效方案，具备跨架构泛化能力，拓展了概念擦除在动态概念上的研究。

Abstract: Text-to-video (T2V) diffusion models can generate realistic depictions of actions such as kicking, stabbing, and shooting, raising safety concerns that motivate targeted concept erasure. Although concept erasure has been extensively studied for static concepts in text-to-image and T2V models, erasing motion concepts remains largely unexplored. We present a systematic study of motion concept erasure in video Diffusion Transformers (DiTs). Through causal interventions, we show that text-conditioning attention carries concept-specific motion information and supports selective intervention, whereas perturbing temporal positional encoding suppresses both target and non-target dynamics. We further find that directly adapting ESD, a representative weight-level image erasure method, to a video DiT yields modest and uneven motion suppression: reducing its erasure training loss does not by itself remove the concept signal from the difference between the conditional and unconditional predictions, which classifier-free guidance (CFG) then scales at every denoising step. From these findings, we derive three requirements for motion concept erasure: concept specificity, spatial selectivity, and temporal naturalness. Each determines one component of MUTE (Motion concept Unlearning in Text-to-video gEneration): at each denoising step, MUTE extracts a concept direction through token neutralization, derives a spatial gate from the direction's intrinsic structure, and subtracts the resulting correction from the velocity output before CFG is applied. MUTE is training-free and requires no weight modification. Experiments on 20 motion concepts show that MUTE outperforms representative prompt-level, weight-level, and inference-time baselines on Wan2.1-T2V, and the same formulation transfers to CogVideoX, supporting its applicability across distinct T2V attention architectures.

</details>


### [71] [You Cannot Recover What Was Never Measured: Quantifying the Information Ceiling of Ultra-Low-Field MRI Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36837)
*Prathamesh Pradeep Khole,Shreya Handa,Utkarsh Gupta,Razvan Marinescu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可验证的测量方案，量化真实64 mT低场MRI中实际保留的个体特异性信息，发现其空间分辨上限约为平面内3–4 mm半周期，而主流合成退化数据比真实扫描多保留约1 mm信息；训练于合成配对的生成超分模型在真实配对数据上，其细粒度细节与被试本人3T扫描的相关性不高于与陌生人3T扫描的相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式超分模型可将便携式64 mT MRI"还原"为类3T图像，但现有评测普遍基于人工合成退化配对，并使用PSNR、SSIM与逐像素不确定性作为指标。已有工作虽承认模型存在幻觉、问题本身病态，但尚无研究定量测量真实低场扫描究竟包含多少个体特异性信息，因此无法判断模型输出是恢复还是捏造。

Method: 使用三个公开数据集中同一被试的64 mT与3T配对扫描；设计一套测量协议，并在答案已知的检验上先行验证其有效性；以该协议测量真实64 mT扫描中个体特异结构的可恢复空间尺度，并与标准合成退化所保留的信息做对比；随后在真实配对采集上测试已训练的扩散模型及一个公开外部模型，覆盖GAN、扩散、Transformer三类家族共24次训练运行，检验输出细节与被试自身3T扫描及陌生人3T扫描的相关性，以及样本方差不确定性是否可区分伪造结构与重建困难。

Result: 在整体脑范围内，真实64 mT扫描仅在平面内约3–4 mm半周期处保留个体特异结构，穿平面方向更粗；标准合成退化比这一上限多保留约1 mm的被试信息，说明基于合成配对的训练与评测所依赖的信息真实扫描器从未记录。在所有可测忠实度的被试上，模型输出的细粒度细节与被试本人3T扫描的相关性不高于与陌生人3T扫描的相关性；样本方差不确定性无法区分被捏造的结构与重建困难。

Conclusion: 由于PSNR与SSIM衡量的是与参考图像的相似度，而非细节是否属于该被试，以此类指标打分的基准无法区分"恢复"与"伪造"。测量协议代码将公开，以便对新模型的可恢复性主张进行检验。

Abstract: Generative super-resolution models can turn portable 64 mT MRI into images that look like 3T scans, and the field evaluates them with PSNR, SSIM, and pixelwise uncertainty, most often on pairs built by synthetically degrading high-field images. Prior work acknowledges that these models hallucinate and that the problem is ill posed, but to our knowledge no study measures how much information about the individual subject the real low-field scan actually contains. We measure it. Using paired 64 mT and 3T scans of the same subjects from three public datasets, and a measurement protocol validated on tests whose correct answer is known in advance, we find that, judged over the whole brain, real 64 mT scans carry structure specific to the individual only down to approximately 3 to 4 mm half-pitch in plane, and coarser still through plane. Standard synthetic degradations preserve subject information roughly 1 mm beyond this ceiling, so models trained and benchmarked on synthetic pairs are evaluated on information that real scanners never record. We then test trained diffusion models and a publicly released external model on real paired acquisitions; 24 trained runs of five architectures (GAN, diffusion, and transformer families) give the coverage of the audit. On every subject where faithfulness can be measured, fine output detail is no more correlated with the subject's own 3T scan than with a stranger's, while sample-variance uncertainty does not distinguish fabricated structure from reconstruction difficulty. Because PSNR and SSIM score resemblance to a reference rather than whether detail belongs to the subject, a benchmark scored by them cannot tell recovery from fabrication. Code for the measurement protocol will be released so that recoverability claims can be tested for newer models.

</details>


### [72] [On-Policy Visual Evidence Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36838)
*Shaohang Wei,Feifan Song,Guangyue Peng,Wenhao Yu,Wei Li,Wen Luo,Yang Xu,Yufan Shen,Luke Mao,Yang Du,Asher Qin,Houfeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReVuE：一种面向视觉智能体的on-policy蒸馏方法，通过对比多条学生轨迹、诊断Acquire/Read/Ground阶段的首个失败并生成反思，将轨迹级证据诊断转化为定向的token级监督。在11个基准上超越所有OPD基线，同时减少推理与工具调用冗余。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉智能体通过推理与图像操作交错来解题，图像操作会改变后续推理可用的证据，因此证据获取(Acquire)、读取(Read)、答案对齐(Ground)阶段的局部错误会沿轨迹传播导致错误答案。现有多模态OPD方法主要围绕原图构造或对比辅助视图以增强监督，未显式建模学生动作、观察结果与后续推理之间的联系，难以针对不同失败阶段给出定制化纠正。

Method: 提出Reflection on Visual Evidence (ReVuE)：对同一查询比较多条学生生成的轨迹，汇总观察到的视觉证据，诊断Acquire、Read、Ground三个阶段中最早出现的失败；将由此产生的反思作为训练时上下文提供给教师；依据反思对教师预测的影响程度，对token级蒸馏损失进行分组与重加权，从而把轨迹级证据诊断转化为针对性的token级监督。

Result: 在覆盖Qwen2.5-VL与InternVL3.5两个模型家族的11个基准上，ReVuE在感知、数学推理和通用任务的加权平均得分均优于所有评估的OPD基线；同时减少了推理与工具调用的冗余，并提升了工具调用准确率和任务准确率。

Conclusion: 显式建模动作—观察—推理之间的证据链条并诊断首个失败阶段，可将轨迹级诊断有效转化为token级监督信号，指导视觉智能体改进视觉证据获取与推理能力。

Abstract: Visual agents solve problems by interleaving reasoning with image operations, and on-policy distillation (OPD) provides guidance from a strong teacher on student-generated interaction trajectories. However, image operations change the evidence available for subsequent reasoning, so local errors in evidence acquisition (Acquire), reading (Read), or answer grounding (Ground) can propagate through the trajectory and lead to incorrect answers. Existing multimodal OPD methods primarily construct or contrast auxiliary views of the original image to strengthen supervision, without explicitly modeling the connections between student actions, resulting observations, and subsequent reasoning. This limits their ability to provide corrections tailored to different failure stages. We introduce Reflection on Visual Evidence (ReVuE), an on-policy distillation method for visual agents. ReVuE compares multiple student-generated trajectories for the same query, summarizes the observed visual evidence, and diagnoses the first failure across the Acquire, Read, and Ground stages. The resulting reflections provide training-time context for the teacher. We group and reweight token-level distillation losses according to how strongly these reflections affect the teacher's predictions. This design translates trajectory-level evidence diagnosis into targeted token-level supervision, guiding students to improve their visual evidence acquisition and reasoning. Across 11 benchmarks spanning the Qwen2.5-VL and InternVL3.5 model families, ReVuE outperforms all evaluated OPD baselines in weighted-average scores for perception, mathematical reasoning, and general tasks. ReVuE also reduces redundancy in reasoning and tool calls while improving tool-call accuracy and task accuracy. Code is available at https://github.com/sylvain-wei/ReVuE

</details>


### [73] [Does the VGGT Family Need All Its Layers?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36842)
*Fengyi Zhang,Holger Caesar,Xiangyu Sun,Zheng Zhang,Zi Huang,Yadan Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究VGGT、π³、VGGT-Ω前馈几何模型的层冗余，通过3018种剪枝配置在七个位姿与稠密几何指标上评估，发现冗余集中于早期与晚期两个区域，并据此提出高效剪枝与线性校准恢复方法，最多减少44%聚合器参数且精度接近原模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究前馈几何模型中哪些层对保持相机位姿与稠密3D结构是必要的，揭示层冗余规律以实现高效剪枝。

Method: 对VGGT、π³、VGGT-Ω进行3018种剪枝配置，在四个室内外数据集的七个相机位姿与稠密几何指标上评分；分析层删除区间及其可加性，利用CKA作为区间退化的廉价代理，并采用闭式线性校准进行剪枝后恢复。

Result: 发现四个结论：(i) 可移除层聚集于早期主导区与较窄的晚期区，跨中间层的删除破坏性更强，与文献报道的中后部冗余模式相反；(ii) 两区间联合退化近似等于各自退化之和，将剪枝搜索的模型评估从O(L⁴)降至O(L²)；(iii) CKA可作为区间退化的更廉价代理，权衡剪枝质量与校准成本；(iv) 闭式线性校准无需端到端重训练即可恢复精度，最小二乘分析表明共享映射会带来额外重建损失，需token感知恢复，仅用100个校准场景拟合的恢复映射可泛化到留出场景与未见数据集。模型聚合器参数最多减少44%，精度与完整模型相当。

Conclusion: 前馈几何模型的层冗余呈早期主导与晚期次要的双区域模式，区间删除退化近似可加，CKA与闭式线性校准可实现高效剪枝与恢复，最多减少44%参数并保持竞争力精度。

Abstract: Which layers of a feed-forward geometry model are needed to preserve both camera poses and dense 3D structure? We study layer redundancy in VGGT, $π^3$, and VGGT-$Ω$: 3,018 pruned configurations, scored on seven camera-pose and dense-geometry metrics across four indoor and outdoor datasets. Four findings follow: (i) Removable layers cluster in two redundancy regions: a dominant early region and a narrower late one, while deletions spanning the intervening layers are consistently more disruptive. This recurring pattern holds across models, datasets, and metrics, and contrasts with the middle-to-late redundancy commonly reported in the literature. (ii) Within these regions, we observe that the joint degradation from deleting two intervals is approximately the sum of their individual degradations, reducing the number of model evaluations for pruning search from $O(L^4)$ to $O(L^2)$, where $L$ is the aggregator depth. (iii) We find that CKA provides a cheaper representation-based proxy for interval degradation, offering a practical trade-off between pruning quality and calibration cost. (iv) Closed-form linear calibration recovers accuracy after pruning without end-to-end retraining. A least-squares analysis shows that using a shared map for special and patch tokens generally incurs excess reconstruction loss, motivating token-aware recovery. Recovery maps fitted on just 100 calibration scenes generalize to held-out scenes and unseen datasets. The resulting models reduce aggregator parameters by up to 44% while maintaining accuracy comparable to their intact counterparts. Code and experimental results will be available at our project page: https://xian-bei.github.io/vggt-family-layer-redundancy/

</details>


### [74] [GlassFormer: Learning Real-time Glass Segmentation using Radar-Depth Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36844)
*Suhani Grover,Astik Srivastava,Viswas Dinesh,Avinash Sharma,K. Madhava Krishna*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该工作提出融合毫米波雷达与RGB-D的多模态框架GlassFormer，利用雷达在玻璃表面的强反射生成空间先验，通过跨模态注意力实现实时透明表面分割，在低光等恶劣条件下显著优于纯视觉方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 透明表面（玻璃墙、门、镜面）在机器人感知中是持续存在的失效场景：RGB相机看到的是玻璃背后的背景，LiDAR、ToF、RGB-D等深度传感器在透明区域常返回无效或背景测量，导致系统误将玻璃判为可通行空间，威胁导航安全。现有基于RGB的玻璃分割方法依赖反射、边界和语义上下文等视觉线索，在低光、眩光、无纹理或部分遮挡时性能退化。作者希望引入一种在视觉与深度失效处依然可靠的物理线索。

Method: 提出多模态融合框架，将毫米波雷达与RGB-D传感结合用于实时透明表面分割。核心思路是利用雷达在玻璃表面的强反射提供与视觉/深度不一致的几何线索，基于这种跨模态不一致性生成“雷达引导的空间先验”，并通过跨模态注意力机制将其集成到轻量级Transformer分割网络GlassFormer中。

Result: 在覆盖全部场景类型的混合条件测试集上，GlassFormer取得0.88 mIoU；在专门用于考验纯视觉方法的低光测试集上取得0.59 mIoU。相较纯视觉基线有显著鲁棒性提升，同时在资源受限平台上保持实时性能。

Conclusion: 雷达作为与视觉互补的模态，能够在视觉和深度传感器失效的透明区域提供可靠几何线索；通过雷达引导先验与跨模态注意力融合，可在不牺牲实时性的前提下大幅提升透明表面分割在低光等困难条件下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Transparent surfaces are ubiquitous in built environments, yet they remain a persistent failure case for robotic perception. RGB cameras perceive the background behind glass rather than the surface itself, while depth sensors such as LiDAR, time-of-flight, and RGB-D often return invalid or background measurements in transparent regions. As a result, systems that rely solely on optical sensing may misinterpret glass walls, doors, or mirrors as free space, compromising safe and reliable navigation. Existing glass segmentation approaches address this by learning visual cues such as reflections, boundaries, and semantic context from RGB images. While effective under favourable lighting and viewing conditions, these cues degrade in low-light environments, under glare, or when glass surfaces are featureless or partially occluded. In this work, we propose a multimodal framework that fuses millimetre-wave radar with RGB-D sensing for real-time transparent surface segmentation. Radar reflects strongly off glass surfaces, providing a geometric cue that remains reliable precisely where vision and depth fail. We exploit this cross-modal inconsistency to generate a radar-guided spatial prior, which is integrated into a lightweight transformer-based segmentation network, GlassFormer, via cross-modal attention. We report results on a mixed-condition test split covering all scene types and a dedicated low-light split designed to stress vision-only methods. GlassFormer achieves 0.88 mIoU on the mixed split, and 0.59 mIoU on the low light split, demonstrating substantial robustness gains over vision-only baselines while maintaining real-time performance on resource-constrained platforms.

</details>


### [75] [RoXDrive: Closed-Loop Reinforcement Learning for End-to-End Autonomous Driving via Action-Faithful Rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36851)
*Hongbin Lin,Chaoda Zheng,Yiming Yang,Xiangyu Li,Shijia Chen,Jinhao Deng,Kangjie Chen,Dongbin Zhang,Jie Feng,Yu Zhang,Xianming Liu,Shuguang Cui,Boyang Wang,Zhen Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 RoXDrive——一种即插即用的闭环强化学习框架，通过识别"动作忠实"的世界模型 rollout 来实现可靠的自动驾驶策略优化，在 nuScenes 与内部数据集上分别将安全违规降低 27.6% 和 33.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶策略通常在日志演示上做模仿学习，却无法观测自身动作的后果，导致闭环部署中的因果混淆。现有 RL 后训练方案存在不足：基于重建的模拟器反事实交互能力有限；合成模拟器虽支持长时程闭环交互但 sim-to-real 差距大；视频世界模型虽能生成真实的多步未来预测，却未必忠实反映动作条件，造成动作—视觉不匹配。

Method: RoXDrive 为即插即用闭环 RL 框架，分两阶段：1）模型预训练：除基于模仿的策略预训练外，设计动作—视觉忠实性评估器（Action-Vision Faithfulness Evaluator）用于逆动力学估计，并配合几何感知的辅助轨迹监督，从而长时程评估视觉 dynamics 是否忠实反映所条件的 ego 动作。2）动作忠实 RL 后训练：智能体迭代地与世界模型交互形成长时程场景 rollout，仅保留动作忠实的样本用于密集的安全感知评分与场景级闭环 RL 后训练。

Result: 在 nuScenes 及超过 13 万训练场景的内部数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在不同规划器上均取得一致增益：nuScenes 上使用 DiffusionDrive 将安全违规降低 27.6%，内部数据上使用 Qwen3-VL 降低 33.7%。

Conclusion: 通过识别动作忠实的世界模型 rollout 并据此进行闭环 RL 后训练，RoXDrive 能有效缓解动作—视觉不匹配问题，实现可靠且具泛化性的策略优化，显著提升自动驾驶安全性。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving policies are commonly trained via imitation learning on logged demonstrations without observing the consequences of their own actions, leading to causal confusion in closed-loop real-world deployment. To address this issue, reinforcement learning (RL) post-training offers a promising alternative by leveraging world models as interactive training environments to enable future scene generation for policy improvement. Nevertheless, existing approaches either rely on reconstruction-based simulators, offering limited counterfactual interaction, or adopt synthetic simulators to enable long-horizon closed-loop interaction at the cost of a substantial sim-to-real gap. Recently, video world models have exhibited the ability to generate realistic multi-step future rollouts but may not faithfully reflect action conditions, resulting in action-vision mismatch. In this paper, we introduce RoXDrive, a plug-and-play closed-loop RL framework that enables reliable policy optimization by identifying action-faithful world-model rollouts, consisting of two stages: 1) Model pre-training: In addition to imitation-based policy pre-training, we devise an Action-Vision Faithfulness Evaluator for inverse dynamics estimation with our geometry-aware auxiliary trajectory supervision, enabling long-horizon assessment of whether visual dynamics faithfully reflect the conditioning ego actions. 2) Action-faithful RL post-training: Agents iteratively interact with world models to form long-horizon scene rollouts, retaining only action-faithful ones for dense safety-aware scoring and scene-level closed-loop RL post-training. Extensive experiments on nuScenes and an in-house dataset with over 130K training scenarios demonstrate consistent gains across planners, reducing safety violations by 27.6% with DiffusionDrive on nuScenes and 33.7% with Qwen3-VL on the internal data.

</details>


### [76] [Socialality Anchors: Towards Group-bounded Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36852)
*Ziqian Zou,Conghao Wong,Qinmu Peng,Xinge You*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Socialality轨迹预测框架，用可解释的Socialality锚点与扩展分组窗口实现稳定、上下文自适应的分组推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群体成员关系体现共享意图、协同运动与稳定相互适应，是持续且有语义的社交先验。但现有群体建模多依赖固定阈值、仅由观察窗内相对位置推断群体，忽略了分组规则应因人而异、时间连贯且随性格、文化背景与交互情境自适应。

Method: 受人类社交感知启发（边界敏感情境用人际距离，动态交互用相对速度一致性），提出Socialality框架：引入双标量控制的分组核Socialality，联合历史观测与短期未来轨迹预览学习个体特异的分组规则；使用扩展分组窗口与组内/组外感知机制建模交互。

Result: 在标准基准上大量实验显示性能提升；通过定性分析与锚点分布统计研究验证了Socialality锚点的可解释性与稳定性。

Conclusion: Socialality通过可解释锚点与上下文自适应分组，克服固定阈值与仅基于位置的群体推断局限，提升轨迹预测性能并保持分组推断的稳定与可解释。

Abstract: Trajectory prediction is a key component for understanding human behavior patterns in dynamic scenes. Researchers have devoted substantial efforts to modeling social interactions, especially group-wise interactions, since group membership often reflects shared intention, coordinated motion, and stable mutual adaptation, thus providing a persistent and semantically meaningful social prior for forecasting. However, existing group modeling methods may rely on a fixed threshold and infer groups mainly from agents' relative positions within the observation window, overlooking the fact that grouping rules should be agent-specific, temporally coherent, and context-adaptive across diverse personalities, culturalities, and evolving interaction contexts. Inspired by human social perception that alternates between interpersonal distance in boundary-sensitive situations and relative speed consistency in dynamic interactions, we propose Socialality, a human-inspired trajectory prediction framework with interpretable Socialality anchors and an extended grouping window for stable, context-aware grouping inference. Concretely, Socialality introduces a duo-scalar-controlled grouping kernel Socialality that jointly leverages historical observations and short-term future trajectory previews to learn agent-specific grouping rules, and employs a group-wise perception mechanism to model in-group and out-of-group interactions in an intuitive and explainable manner. Furthermore, we conduct extensive experiments on standard benchmarks to demonstrate the performance gains of Socialality, and provide qualitative analyses and statistical studies of anchor distributions to verify the interpretability and stability of the proposed Socialality anchors.

</details>


### [77] [S2T-Unet: A Structure-to-Style Framework for Inter-Modality MRI Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36866)
*Yichao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出S2T-Unet，将MRI跨模态翻译显式分解为结构编码（矢量量化码本捕捉模态不变结构）与风格变换（解码器特征条件化调制编码器表示以恢复目标模态强度/对比度），在IXI数据集四个翻译任务上达到或优于SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MRI跨模态翻译方法通常只学习端到端的强度映射，未显式分离模态无关的结构信息与模态特有的表观信息，易导致结构信息丢失或生成不真实的图像细节。

Method: S2T-Unet采用“结构到风格”框架：在低层bottleneck引入矢量量化，用可学习的离散码本编码模态不变的结构信息；在高层设计模态变换模块，以解码器特征为条件对编码器表示进行调制与变换，从而恢复目标模态特有的强度和对比度信息。

Result: 在IXI多对比度MRI数据集上的四个翻译任务中，S2T-Unet的表现与当前最先进方法相当或更优。

Conclusion: 显式解耦结构信息与模态风格有助于在保持解剖结构的同时生成真实的目标模态图像，验证了该结构-风格分离建模策略在MRI跨模态翻译中的有效性。

Abstract: Inter-modality MRI translation aims to synthesize missing MRI modalities from available acquisitions, reducing the need for additional scanning while preserving clinically relevant anatomical information. However, existing image translation methods often learn intensity mappings without explicitly separating modality-invariant structural information from modality-specific appearance, which may lead to structural information loss or unrealistic image details. In this work, we propose S2T-Unet, a structure-to-style framework that explicitly models these two aspects. Specifically, vector quantization is introduced at the lower-level bottleneck to encode modality-invariant structural information using a learned discrete codebook. At higher levels, a modality transformation module uses decoder features to condition and transform encoder representations toward the target modality, thereby recovering modality-specific intensity and contrast information. Experiments on the IXI multi-contrast MRI dataset across four translation tasks demonstrate that S2T-Unet is comparable or outperform with state-of-art method.

</details>


### [78] [S4VY: Segment Anything in Feed-Forward 4D Visual Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36875)
*Jingdong Zhang,Xin Li,Jan Kautz,Wenping Wang,Chris Choy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出S4VY，一个基于前馈4D视觉几何的Segment Anything模型，通过时空查询解码器从RGB观测生成类无关的4D实例掩码，并结合主动树搜索与双流定位器实现自然语言引导的场景定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态场景中的精确实例分割对机器人和自动驾驶等下游任务至关重要。现有Segment Anything模型主要基于2D图像或视频掩码，通过序列记忆保持身份，而基于前馈视觉几何的可提示4D实例分割仍未被充分探索。

Method: S4VY将共享的视觉-几何特征通过时空查询解码器转换为穷尽的类无关4D实例掩码，每个持久对象查询在所有观测中绑定同一实体。该表示支持无提示分割以及点和框条件选择，无需种子掩码或时间顺序。此外，开发了用于大型4D场景观测空间自然语言定位的智能体框架：主动树搜索识别相关帧而非扫描所有固定窗口；双流定位器通过互补的边界框预测和对象查询匹配，结合细粒度VLM视觉先验与几何一致的实例特征；独立评判器从预测中选择最终4D实例掩码。

Result: 大量实验表明，在静态和动态场景的统一评估下，该方法取得了最先进的4D实例分割性能和强大的语言引导定位性能。

Conclusion: S4VY通过前馈4D视觉几何统一了可提示4D实例分割与语言引导定位，为动态场景理解提供了新范式。

Abstract: Accurate instance segmentation in dynamic scenes is important for downstream applications such as robotics and autonomous driving. Existing Segment Anything models operate primarily on 2D image or video masks and preserve identity through sequential memory, while promptable 4D instance segmentation built upon feed-forward visual geometry remains underexplored. We introduce S4VY, a Segment Anything model built on feed-forward 4D visual geometry. From a set of RGB observations, S4VY transforms shared visual-geometric features into an exhaustive set of class-agnostic 4D instance masks through a space-time query decoder, with each persistent object query binding one entity across all observations. This representation supports prompt-independent segmentation as well as point- and box- conditioned selection, without requiring a seed mask or temporal ordering. We further develop an agentic harness for natural-language grounding in the large observation space of a 4D scene. Active tree search identifies relevant frames without scanning every fixed window; a dual-stream grounder combines fine-grained VLM visual priors with geometry-consistent instance features through complementary bounding-box prediction and object-query matching; and an independent critic selects the final 4D instance mask from their predictions. Extensive experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art 4D instance segmentation and strong language-guided grounding performance under a unified evaluation spanning static and dynamic scenes.

</details>


### [79] [Less Supervision, Better Generalization: Weakly Supervised Fake Region Localization in Diffusion-Edited Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36882)
*Junhee Lee,Donghyeon Jeon,Taeoh Kim,Beomyoung Kim,MyeongAh Cho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReGFLoW：首个弱监督扩散编辑伪造区域定位框架，用扩散重建误差作为空间引导，实现优于全监督基线的跨域泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编辑区域定位对可解释取证很重要，但伪影细微、空间分散且与语义/物体边界不对齐。现有方法依赖受控编辑流程的像素级监督，难以扩展，且标注区域内外的伪影分布不一致会引入误导信号：伪影常超出标注区域，而未标注像素被当作真实，限制了模型捕获可迁移证据与跨生成器泛化。

Method: 提出ReGFLoW（Reconstruction-Guided Fake Localization under Weak supervision），仅需图像级真/假标签；利用扩散重建误差作为密集空间引导，注入特征空间与分数空间；通过以伪影为中心的多实例学习（multiple instance learning），在不依赖语义亲和或边界伪掩码先验的情况下利用局部扩散证据。

Result: 大量实验表明，ReGFLoW的域外泛化能力强于全监督基线。

Conclusion: 弱监督+扩散重建引导可有效替代像素级监督，缓解标注误导问题，提升跨生成器与跨数据集的迁移定位性能。

Abstract: Localizing AI-edited regions is essential for interpretable forensic analysis, but remains challenging due to subtle and spatially distributed artifacts that are misaligned with semantic or object boundaries. Existing approaches rely on pixel-level supervision from controlled editing pipelines, which is difficult to scale and can introduce misleading signals: artifacts frequently extend beyond annotated regions, while out-of-mask pixels are treated as authentic. This limits models' ability to capture transferable evidence and generalize across generators and datasets. To address these issues, we propose ReGFLoW, a Reconstruction-Guided Fake Localization framework under Weak supervision, which is the first weakly supervised approach for diffusion-edited fake region localization. ReGFLoW requires only real/fake labels at the image-level and uses diffusion reconstruction errors as dense spatial guidance to inject them into both feature and score spaces. Furthermore, by artifact-centric multiple instance learning, ReGFLoW utilizes localized diffusion evidence without relying on semantic-affinity or boundary-based pseudo-mask priors. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ReGFLoW achieves stronger out-of-domain generalization than fully supervised learning baselines.

</details>


### [80] [ProGuT: Label-Efficient Panoptic Segmentation for Forest Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36891)
*Pankaj Deoli,Karsten Berns*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ProGuT，用 CLIP 原型聚类与多尺度几何先验生成全景伪标签，无需逐图掩码或额外传感器，在森林数据集上显著优于无监督基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 森林场景的全景分割瓶颈在于实例分离而非语义质量：现有无监督方法生成的 stuff 图可用，但 thing 质量近乎为零；依赖深度或光流的实例发现又需要不常具备的传感器。

Method: ProGuT（Prototype Guided Training）：仅需无标注图像与一次性的 cluster-to-class 映射，无需逐图像训练掩码。先对 CLIP patch 特征聚类，再通过多尺度几何先验，利用结构张量证伪非树干结构，从而恢复树干实例；相比深度、光流或类别监督方法，伪标签生成成本更低。

Result: 在 Our-forest 数据集上达到 PQ 65.2（相对初始伪标签质量提升 2.6 倍）；在 Freiburg Forest 上 mIoU 65.9，优于 PiCIE（45.3 IoU）与 STEGO（57.6 IoU）；在类无关树干实例基准上也超过现有无监督方法。

Conclusion: ProGuT 以低成本无监督方式有效解决了森林全景分割中的实例分离瓶颈，伪标签可直接用于下游任务，并在全景质量、语义分割及类无关树干实例任务上全面超越无监督基线。

Abstract: Panoptic segmentation in forest environments is bottlenecked not by semantic quality but by instance separation; existing unsupervised panoptic approaches produce usable stuff maps but near-zero thing quality. Depth or flow-based instance discovery methods needs sensors that are not always available. We present ProGuT (Prototype Guided Training), which produces panoptic pseudo-labels without per-image training masks, needing only unlabeled images and one-time cluster-to-class mapping. ProGuT clusters CLIP patch features, then recovers trunk instances through multiscale geometric prior that falsifies non-trunk structures via structure-tensor. This is cheap compared to depth, flow or class-supervision methods to create pseudo labels. These are then used for downstream tasks which we evaluate against other unsupervised baselines. ProGuT achieves a Panoptic Quality (PQ) of 65.2 on Our-forest dataset (2.6x improvement over the initial pseudo-label quality) and reaches 65.9 mIoU on Freiburg Forest, outperforming unsupervised baselines like PiCIE (45.3 IoU) and STEGO(57.6IoU). Additionally, ProGuT outperforms existing unsupervised methods for class-agnostic trunk instance benchmark.

</details>


### [81] [DiffReID: Discriminative Diffusion Model for Object Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36894)
*Yingquan Wang,Pingping Zhang,Dong Wang,Huchuan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DiffReID：利用判别式扩散模型逐步学习身份感知分布并生成身份不变特征，提升目标ReID的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有目标ReID方法因数据集规模与多样性有限而泛化性不足；且模型倾向提取语义模式，而非学习身份感知的特征分布。

Method: 基于CLIP模型通过提示微调获得身份感知文本特征；提出视觉引导噪声生成器（VNG）初始化概率噪声并逐步破坏文本特征；以视觉特征为条件，设计轻量去噪器（LWD）逐步去噪以学习身份感知分布；从随机采样的视觉引导噪声生成身份不变引导特征；最后提出互增强约束（MEC）促进视觉特征与引导特征相互学习。

Result: 在五个目标ReID基准上的大量实验表明，该方法优于大多数现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: DiffReID通过扩散模型学习身份感知分布并生成身份不变特征，结合互增强约束提升表示的鲁棒性与判别性，有效改善目标ReID的泛化性能。

Abstract: As a fundamental image processing task, object Re-Identification (ReID) aims to retrieve objects across non-overlapping cameras. Recently, with the development of deep learning, significant advancements have been made in object ReID. However, most existing methods suffer from generalization due to the limited size and diversity of ReID datasets. Meanwhile, current models tend to focus on extracting semantic patterns rather than learning identity-aware feature distributions. To address these issues, we propose a novel feature learning framework named \textbf{DiffReID} for object ReID. It leverages a discriminative diffusion model to gradually learn identity-aware distributions and generate identity-invariant features. More specifically, with the Contrastive Language-Image Pre-training (CLIP) model, we first obtain identity-aware text features by prompt tuning. Then, we propose a Vision-guided Noise Generator (VNG) to initialize probabilistic noises and gradually corrupt identity-aware text features. Afterwards, we take visual features as conditions and propose a Light Weight Denoiser (LWD) to denoise the corrupted text features step-by-step for identity-aware distribution learning. To obtain discriminative features, we further generate identity-invariant guided features from randomly sampling visual-guided noises. Finally, we propose a Mutual Enhancement Constraint (MEC) to facilitate mutual learning between visual features and guided features to enhance the representation robustness and discrimination. Extensive experiments on five object ReID benchmarks demonstrate that our method shows better results than most state-of-the-art methods. The source code is available at https://github.com/AWangYQ/DiffReID.

</details>


### [82] [SafeVantage: Vantage-Aware Memory for Reliable Embodied Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36906)
*Sean Hardesty Lewis,Zuyi Guo,Benwang Chen,Zirui Liu,Hongyi Lin,Heye Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SafeVantage：一种视角感知的语义记忆与主动采集框架，通过保留声明级支撑视角、相机位姿与目标位置估计，结合可观测性预测与标定决策头，在部分可观测下实现更可靠、更省行程的 Yes/No/Abstain 决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测条件下的具身决策需要信息量充足的观测与充分证据，但纯语义分数无法揭示哪些视角支撑某个论断、也不指出应在何处补充证据；同时需要区分"正向支撑"与"搜索覆盖"。

Method: 1) 视角感知语义记忆：为每条声明保留支撑视角、相机位姿与估计目标位置，并将正向支撑与搜索覆盖分开存储；2) 学习式候选可观测性模型：基于声明锚定几何预测可达视角下的目标可见性；3) 视角选择依据终端决策损失的期望下降，并计入移动成本与几何上互异的佐证；4) 标定决策头融合支撑、空间一致性与覆盖度，输出 Yes/No/Abstain。

Result: 在覆盖232个未见 ProcTHOR 房屋、每方法与动作预算7,424对回合的类别存在性基准上：8与12步动作时宏F1分别提升24.7%与12.0%，两种预算下风险更低、回答率更高，8步时行程减少31.7%；等输入 HM3D 实验在固定观测下选择性风险更低；ScanNet 干预实验显示恢复支撑视角可改善下游 VLM 回答；消融验证候选可观测性对决策质量与采集效率的贡献。

Conclusion: 结果表明，声明级视角证据对连接语义记忆、主动采集与可靠决策具有实际价值，可在有限动作预算下提升决策可靠性并降低移动代价。

Abstract: Reliable embodied decisions under partial observability require informative observations and sufficient supporting evidence. However, semantic scores alone do not reveal which viewpoints justify a claim or where additional evidence should be acquired. We introduce SafeVantage, a vantage-aware semantic memory and active acquisition framework that retains each claim's supporting views, camera poses, and estimated target location, keeping positive support distinct from search coverage. A learned candidate-observability model uses claim-grounded geometry to predict target visibility at reachable viewpoints. These predictions guide view selection through expected reduction in terminal decision loss, accounting for travel cost and geometrically distinct corroboration. A calibrated head then combines support, spatial consistency, and coverage to produce Yes, No, or Abstain decisions. We evaluate SafeVantage on a category-presence benchmark spanning 232 unseen ProcTHOR houses and 7,424 paired episodes per method and action budget. Compared with validation-selected equal-budget baselines, SafeVantage achieves macro-F1 gains of 24.7% and 12.0% at eight and twelve actions, respectively, with lower risk and higher answer rates at both budgets and 31.7% less travel at eight actions. Equal-input HM3D experiments show lower selective risk under fixed observations, while controlled ScanNet interventions show that restoring supporting views improves downstream VLM answers. Ablations further support the contribution of candidate observability to decision quality and acquisition efficiency. Results demonstrate the value of claim-level viewpoint evidence for connecting semantic memory, active acquisition, and reliable decision-making. Code is available at https://safevantage.github.io

</details>


### [83] [Representation Dynamics Reveal Semantic Saliency and Similarity for Visual Token Pruning in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36916)
*Weixuan Li,Zikun Zhou,Xinyi Zhuang,Xinyan Guo,Rui Tian,Chuyao Zhang,Lin Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文分析视觉token表征动态，发现token更新幅度的前景显著性与层深相关，更新方向相似性更能区分同类与异类token，据此提出免训练的MSDG-Prune剪枝方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型因长视觉token序列导致高推理延迟，现有剪枝方法多用注意力图或输出特征估计重要性，而利用表征变化的方法对"何时、如何"反映前景显著性与语义一致性理解不足。

Method: 分析跨编码器深度的视觉token表征动态，得出两点发现；据此提出MSDG-Prune：按更新方向相似性对token分组，并用选定深度窗口内由更新幅度导出的查询加权显著性进行组级token剪枝，无需训练。

Result: 在四个MLLM上验证有效性与泛化性；在LLaVA-NeXT上仅用5.6%视觉token即保留91.9%原始性能，并实现7.8倍预填充加速。

Conclusion: token更新幅度与方向蕴含前景显著性和语义一致性信息，利用二者进行免训练组级剪枝可高效压缩视觉token而不显著损失性能。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) incur high inference latency from long visual token sequences. Existing pruning methods commonly use attention maps or output features to estimate token importance or redundancy. Several recent approaches also exploit representation changes, but when and how these changes reflect foreground saliency and semantic consistency remain insufficiently understood. We analyze visual token representation dynamics across encoder depth and uncover two findings. First, the relationship between token update magnitudes and foreground saliency is layer-dependent: large token updates concentrate on foreground regions in two depth intervals, separated by several sink-dominated layers at intermediate depths. Second, similarities between token update directions better distinguish same-class from different-class tokens than those between encoder output features. Building on these findings, we propose MSDG-Prune, a training-free method that uses update magnitudes and directions to preserve salient and diverse visual information. Specifically, we group tokens by update-direction similarity and use query-weighted saliency derived from update magnitudes across a chosen depth window for group-wise token pruning. Extensive experiments across four MLLMs demonstrate the effectiveness and generalizability of MSDG-Prune. On LLaVA-NeXT, it retains 91.9% of uncompressed performance on average with only 5.6% of visual tokens, while achieving a 7.8x prefilling speedup. Code is available at https://github.com/liweixuan-hitsz/MSDG-Prune.

</details>


### [84] [Seg3DParts: Segmentation-Grounded Controllable Part-Level 3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36918)
*Jiantao Lin,Meixi Chen,Yingjie Xu,Chenbo Fu,Leyi Wu,Hao Chen,Yinchuan Li,Ying-Cong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Seg3DParts，一种以分割为显式引导信号的框架，可从单张图像可控地生成部件级 3D 资产，并构建了大规模数据集 PartObjectNet。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部件级 3D 资产对编辑、重装配与交互至关重要，但从单图恢复此类结构面临遮挡、边界模糊及多部件一致性推理等挑战。现有方法通常隐式推断部件身份与空间分配，难以同时实现可控的部件级生成与一致的多部件结构。

Method: 提出 Seg3DParts：将分割作为显式 grounding 信号，在生成过程中定义部件身份，使每个部件锚定到对应图像区域；引入结构化跨部件交互（structured cross-part interaction），让各部件在生成全程交换全局上下文；最终在共享规范空间中直接生成对齐良好的部件网格，无需事后对齐。此外构建 PartObjectNet 数据集。

Result: PartObjectNet 包含超过 200K 个物体、1M 个标注部件。实验表明 Seg3DParts 在几何质量、跨部件一致性及部件级可控性上优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过显式分割引导与跨部件上下文交互，Seg3DParts 实现了可控且结构一致的部件级 3D 生成，并借助大规模数据集验证了其优越性。

Abstract: Part-level 3D assets are essential for editing, reassembly, and interaction, yet recovering such structure from a single image remains challenging due to occlusion, ambiguous boundaries, and the need for coherent multi-part reasoning. Existing approaches struggle to achieve both controllable part-level generation and coherent multi-part structure, as part identity and spatial allocation are typically inferred implicitly. We present Seg3DParts, a segmentation-grounded framework for controllable part-level 3D generation from a single image. By treating segmentation as an explicit grounding signal, our method defines part identity during generation, enabling each component to be anchored to a corresponding image region. To ensure coherent assemblies, we introduce structured cross-part interaction that allows components to exchange global context throughout the generative process. As a result, Seg3DParts directly generates well-aligned part meshes in a shared canonical space without post-hoc alignment, supporting flexible and controllable decomposition. We further introduce PartObjectNet, a large-scale dataset with over 200K objects and 1M annotated parts. Experiments demonstrate that Seg3DParts achieves superior geometry quality, cross-part coherence, and part-level controllability over existing methods.

</details>


### [85] [SFE-VGGT: Source-Free VGGT Distillation for Event-Based Monocular Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36929)
*Thai Duy Nguyen,Addison Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无源框架SFE-VGGT，利用事件流重建代理帧作为几何教师，结合可靠性感知蒸馏策略，将VGGT几何先验迁移至事件域，无需配对RGB或深度标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件相机深度估计方法依赖同步RGB-事件配对或深度标注来从视觉基础模型进行跨模态蒸馏，严重限制了实际部署。因此需要一种无需真实源RGB数据的无源蒸馏方案。

Method: 从目标事件流直接重建代理帧作为冻结的几何教师，消除对真实源RGB的依赖；针对代理帧监督不完美且空间变化的问题，提出可靠性感知蒸馏策略：密度感知特征蒸馏（突出信息丰富的事件区域）、置信度加权深度蒸馏（依据师生相对预测置信度动态调节监督）；并提出跨帧关系一致性损失，利用可靠帧间对应关系强制时序几何稳定性，无需时序一致的教师深度。

Result: 在标准条件下精度接近依赖RGB的基线，在夜间等困难场景显著超越。在MVSEC夜间序列上，平均10米深度误差较EventVGGT降低15.3%；在真实数据集上具有稳健的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 证明在严格无源监督下，可将高效几何先验迁移至事件相机，为事件域深度估计提供无需配对RGB与深度标注的可行路径。

Abstract: Recent event-based depth estimation methods successfully transfer geometric priors from vision foundation models via cross-modal distillation. However, their reliance on synchronized RGB-event pairs or depth annotations during training severely restricts practical deployment. To overcome this bottleneck, we propose SFE-VGGT, a novel source-free framework that distills the geometric priors of VGGT to the event domain without any paired RGB observations. Our core idea is to reconstruct surrogate frames directly from the target event stream to act as a frozen geometric teacher, entirely eliminating the need for genuine source RGB data. Crucially, as these surrogate frames inherently yield imperfect and spatially varying supervision, directly distilling from them propagates artifacts. To resolve this, we introduce a novel reliability-aware distillation strategy. This includes Density-Aware Feature Distillation to emphasize informative event regions, and Confidence-Weighted Depth Distillation to dynamically regulate supervision based on relative teacher-student prediction confidence. Meanwhile, we propose a Cross-Frame Relational Consistency loss that enforces temporal geometric stability using reliable inter-frame correspondences, bypassing the need for temporally consistent teacher's depth. Extensive experiments demonstrate that, despite source-free, our SFE-VGGT closely matches the accuracy of RGB-dependent baselines under standard conditions and significantly surpasses them in challenging nighttime scenarios. Across MVSEC nighttime sequences, SFE-VGGT reduces the average 10 m depth error by 15.3% compared with EventVGGT. Moreover, our method exhibits robust zero-shot generalization across real-world datasets, proving that highly effective geometric priors can be transferred to event cameras using strictly source-free supervision.

</details>


### [86] [WeLike2Party! In-Context Motion Transfer for Multi-Human Image Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36937)
*Sangeyl Lee,Seunghyun Shin,Seungho Park,Wooseok Jeon,Hae-Gon Jeon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 WeLike2Party 多人物动画框架，摒弃推理时的显式姿态/网格提取，采用直接上下文视频条件，并引入 Reference Asymmetric RoPE Conditioning 与 Identity Binding Supervision，同时构建 MotionTwin 数据集与 MotionTwin-Bench 基准，在多人物交互与严重遮挡场景下实现更优的主体级视觉保真度、身份-运动绑定与感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类图像动画旨在将驱动视频中的运动迁移至参考图像主体。尽管视频生成进展显著，多主体交互下的高保真动画仍是难题。现有方法多依赖 2D 骨架或参数化人体网格等显式运动表示，在人际遮挡时难以保持身份-运动绑定，因此需要新的解决方案。

Method: 提出 WeLike2Party，基于直接上下文视频条件（in-context video conditioning）的多人物动画框架，推理时无需显式姿态或网格提取。引入 Reference Asymmetric RoPE Conditioning 以保留细粒度外观细节，并采用 Identity Binding Supervision 将每个参考身份与其预期运动轨迹关联。为支持跨身份训练，构建 MotionTwin 大规模合成数据集，含 14.4K 跨身份视频对（共享主体与相机运动），总计 84.3 小时逼真视频；并提出 MotionTwin-Bench 跨身份基准，用于评估主体级视觉保真度与身份-运动绑定。

Result: 在 MotionTwin-Bench 与真实世界视频上的大量实验表明，WeLike2Party 在主体级视觉保真度、身份-运动绑定与整体感知质量上优于近期最先进方法，尤其是在存在明显遮挡的多人物交互场景中。

Conclusion: 该工作通过无显式运动表示的直接上下文条件框架，结合专用条件机制、监督策略及配套数据集与基准，有效提升了多人物交互动画在遮挡条件下的保真度与身份-运动绑定能力，为多主体人类图像动画提供了新范式与评测基础。

Abstract: Human image animation aims to transfer motion from a driving video to subjects in a reference image. Despite remarkable progress in video generation, achieving high-fidelity animation of multiple interacting subjects remains a challenge. Many existing approaches rely on explicit motion representations such as 2D skeletons or parametric body meshes and struggle to preserve identity-motion binding under inter-person occlusion. To address this limitation, we propose WeLike2Party, a multi-human animation framework built on direct in-context video conditioning without explicit pose or mesh extraction at inference. We further introduce Reference Asymmetric RoPE Conditioning to preserve fine-grained appearance details, and Identity Binding Supervision to associate each reference identity with its intended motion trajectory. To support cross-identity training, we construct MotionTwin, a large-scale synthetic dataset comprising 14.4K cross-identity video pairs with shared subject and camera motions, totaling 84.3 hours of photorealistic video. We additionally present MotionTwin-Bench, a cross-identity benchmark specifically designed to evaluate subject-level visual fidelity and identity-motion binding. Extensive experiments on MotionTwin-Bench and real-world videos demonstrate that WeLike2Party outperforms recent state-of-the-art methods in subject-level visual fidelity, identity-motion binding, and overall perceptual quality, particularly in multi-person interactions with substantial occlusion.

</details>


### [87] [DispFlow-GS: Displacement Flow Supervision with Motion Disentangling for Monocular Deformable 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36940)
*Thai Duy Nguyen,Haitian Zhang,Addison Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文指出可变形3DGS中基于高斯流的运动监督因与光流存在域差异而效果有限，提出以位移流(Displacement Flow)为核心的监督框架，并通过中间视角渲染解耦场景运动与相机运动；同时揭示运动保真度与图像指标的失配现象，提出运动感知指标DRC，实验显示运动定位与运动-渲染一致性分别最多提升39%和6%，而图像指标仅变动约0.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态场景重建对机器人感知至关重要，需要时间一致的表征。现有可变形3DGS方法通过将渲染的高斯流与光流对齐来施加运动监督，但作者发现这类监督对运动建模的提升有限，其根本原因在于渲染高斯流与光流之间存在域差异(domain gap)，导致优化信号间接且不稳定。

Method: 1) 提出基于位移流(Displacement Flow)的运动监督框架，将每个高斯点的3D位移直接splat到图像平面，提供直接且稳定的优化信号；2) 通过中间视角渲染(intermediate-view rendering)解耦场景运动与相机运动，获得更可靠的运动会验并施加针对形变与几何的约束；3) 针对运动保真度与图像评估的失配，提出形变-渲染一致性(DRC)指标，度量预测形变与渲染提升之间的对齐程度。

Result: 在动态场景基准上，运动定位(motion localization)与运动-渲染一致性最多分别提升39%和6%，而基于图像的指标仅变化约0.1%，验证了运动保真度与图像评估之间存在失配，也说明DRC对运动感知评估的重要性。

Conclusion: 渲染高斯流与光流的域差异是现有运动监督范式的根本局限；位移流监督配合中间视角解耦能显著改善运动定位与一致性；传统图像指标无法反映运动质量，需引入DRC等运动感知指标进行评价。

Abstract: Accurate dynamic scene reconstruction is important for robotic perception, where temporally consistent representations of dynamic environments are essential. Deformable 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) models dynamic scenes through deformation fields, and recent methods incorporate motion supervision by aligning rendered Gaussian flow with optical flow. However, we find that such Gaussian-flow-based supervision provides only limited improvements in motion modeling. We identify a fundamental limitation of this supervision paradigm, namely a domain gap between rendered Gaussian flow and optical flow. To address this limitation, we propose a motion supervision framework built on Displacement Flow, which splats per-Gaussian 3D displacements onto the image plane to provide direct and stable optimization signals. We further disentangle scene motion from camera motion via intermediate-view rendering, enabling more reliable motion priors and targeted constraints on deformation and geometry. We also observe a discrepancy between motion fidelity and image-based evaluation, where improved motion awareness does not necessarily translate into better rendered image quality or higher image-based metric scores. Motivated by this mismatch, we introduce Deformation-Rendering Consistency (DRC), a motion-aware metric that measures the alignment between predicted deformation and rendering improvement. Experiments on dynamic scene benchmarks show substantial improvements in motion localization and motion--rendering consistency, reaching up to 39% and 6%, respectively, while image-based metrics change by only about 0.1%. These results confirm the observed mismatch between motion fidelity and image-based evaluation, demonstrating the significance of DRC for motion-aware evaluation.

</details>


### [88] [Beyond Readability: Evaluating Task Information Recoverability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36957)
*Yiwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 视觉可读性与任务信息可恢复性是两个不同量。论文提出一种显式刻画观测、查询、目标与可用知识的评估视角，度量不同恢复路径成功集合的重叠，并以书籍封面实验验证实体关联恢复可超越直接光学恢复边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接视觉可读性与任务信息可恢复性并不等同：即使无法从固定观测中解码目标，也可能通过其他恢复路径获取该目标。因此需要一种能区分不同信息获取路径的评估框架。

Method: 提出显式化的评估视角，明确观测、查询、目标与可用知识，并测量各恢复路径成功集合的重叠。区分两类路径：直接光学恢复（从图像读取目标，可选经恢复处理）与实体关联恢复（利用残存视觉证据识别所描绘实体，再经由指定知识资源中的实体—属性关系访问目标，可依赖存储知识或外部来源）。用受控书籍封面研究实例化外部访问：固定“标题—作者”目录，在分辨率退化条件下比较光学作者恢复与视觉标题解析加确定性查找。

Result: 在不同视觉—语言模型上，实体关联恢复的成功在测试范围内持续存在，尽管绝对性能差异显著，仍显示出超出直接视觉前沿的信息获取能力；同时仍存在一个可观的光学独占区域。

Conclusion: 视觉退化评估不应仅看直接可读性，而应结合沿指定路径与知识资源可获取的任务信息；两类结果互补，表明需要以任务信息可及性为核心的评估方式。

Abstract: Direct visual readability and task-information recoverability are different quantities. Failure to decode a target from a fixed observation need not eliminate access to that target through another recovery route. We develop an evaluation perspective that makes the observation, query, target, and available knowledge explicit and measures the overlap between routes' success sets. For information available on the original visible surface under suitable imaging conditions, direct optical recovery reads the target from the image, optionally after restoration; entity-linked recovery uses residual visual evidence to identify the depicted entity and accesses its target through an entity--attribute relation in a specified knowledge resource. Such access can draw on stored knowledge or an external source. A controlled book-cover study instantiates external access with a fixed title--author catalog, comparing optical author recovery with visual title resolution and deterministic lookup under resolution degradation. Entity-linked successes persist across the tested vision--language models, revealing information access beyond the tested direct visual frontier despite substantial differences in absolute performance. A substantial optical-only region remains. These complementary outcomes show why visual degradation should be evaluated through the task information accessible along specified routes and knowledge resources, alongside direct readability.

</details>


### [89] [Prior-Driven Enhancements in 3D Gaussian Splatting: Normals and Depths Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36969)
*Gyeonggwan Lee,Seunghwan Hong,Junghun Suh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对3DGS因依赖SfM稀疏点集与视角相关属性而导致的几何误差与视觉伪影，本文通过融合表面法线与稠密深度等几何先验对优化过程进行正则化，提升了复杂场景下的重建质量与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D Gaussian Splatting（3DGS）虽在效率与视觉质量上表现优异，但其依赖SfM初始稀疏点集和视角相关属性，在复杂场景中易出现几何不准确和视觉伪影。为克服这些缺陷，需要引入几何先验来约束优化过程，从而提升几何一致性与渲染质量。

Method: 提出一种改进的3DGS方法，在优化中融入几何先验进行正则化：一方面利用表面法线正则化，将高斯协方差与局部表面结构对齐以增强几何一致性；另一方面结合稠密深度先验与SfM初始点，改善逐像素深度估计，提高精度并减少歧义。

Result: 在街道场景、高反射环境等具有挑战性的数据集上进行评估，并跨多个SfM管线测试；结果表明方法在不同环境下均具兼容性与鲁棒性，几何精度和视觉质量均有提升。

Conclusion: 该方法通过几何先验正则化有效缓解了3DGS在复杂环境中的几何失准与视觉失真，为复杂场景下的实时3D渲染提供了可靠解决方案。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) is a state-of-the-art technique for 3D scene rendering, offering high efficiency and excellent visual quality. However, because 3DGS relies on an initial sparse point set from Structure-from-Motion (SfM) and view-dependent properties, it can suffer from geometric inaccuracies and visual artifacts, particularly in complex scenes. To address these challenges, we propose an improved 3DGS approach that regularizes the optimization process by integrating geometric priors, including surface normals and dense depth information. Surface normal regularization improves geometric consistency by aligning Gaussian covariance with local surface structures, while dense depth priors combined with an initial points from SfM enhance per-pixel depth estimation, increasing accuracy and reducing ambiguities. These enhancements enable robust handling of diverse and complex real-world scenarios, minimizing visual distortions and improving reconstruction quality across various environments. To validate our method, we evaluate it on challenging datasets, including street-view scenes and highly reflective environments, while testing it across multiple SfM pipelines. Our results demonstrate compatibility across diverse environments and highlight the robustness of our approach. Experimental findings further show that our method enhances geometric accuracy and visual quality, establishing a reliable solution for real-time 3D scene rendering in complex environments.

</details>


### [90] [Structured Visual Target Learning For Cross-Subject eeg-to-image retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36971)
*Salini Yadav,Taveena Lotey,Mickaël Coustaty,Pravendra Singh,Partha Pratim Roy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文从视觉目标端出发改进跨被试EEG到图像检索，通过结构化视觉视图聚合与免训练表征精炼，在THINGS-EEG2上显著提升检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨被试EEG到图像检索要求源被试上训练的神经表征对未见被试仍与视觉嵌入空间对齐。现有方法主要聚焦于EEG端，本文则从视觉目标端切入以解决该问题。

Method: 保留Perception Encoder的空间信息，将其patch网格转换为紧凑的学习视觉视图集合，并用块结构、内容依赖的路由器为每张图像聚合这些视图；视觉目标与EEG编码器通过对比学习联合训练，并施加跨源被试的MMD正则化；部署阶段提出免训练的表征精炼，在不更新任一编码器的情况下对齐冻结嵌入。

Result: 在THINGS-EEG2的留一被试评估下，结构化目标达到35.3%/65.6%的Top-1/Top-5准确率，为对比方法中最优；经精炼后提升至48.1%/77.1%，相对最强对比方法Top-1提升18.5%，且在全部十个留出被试上均有改善。

Conclusion: 从视觉目标端建模并结合免训练精炼，可有效提升跨被试EEG到图像检索的泛化与对齐性能。

Abstract: Cross-subject EEG-to-image retrieval requires a neural represen- tation trained on source subjects to remain aligned with a visual embedding space for an unseen subject. Whereas existing methods primarily focus on the EEG side, we address this problem from the perspective of the visual target. Our approach preserves the spatial information of the Perception Encoder, converts its patch grid into a compact set of learned visual views, and aggregates them for each image with a block-structured, content-dependent router. The target is learned jointly with the EEG encoder through contrastive learning with MMD regularization across source subjects. For deployment, we propose a training-free representation refinement that aligns frozen embeddings without updating either encoder. Under leave- one-subject-out evaluation on THINGS-EEG2, the structured target achieves 35.3%/65.6% Top-1/Top-5 accuracy, the best among com- pared methods. Refinement raises this to 48.1%/77.1%, an 18.5% Top-1 gain over the strongest compared method, improving all ten held-out subjects.

</details>


### [91] [A Dual-Track Curation-and-Classification Framework for Resolving Ground-Truth Label Noise in Operational Sentinel-2 Wheat Area Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36975)
*Kasimali Agharia,Ujjwal Kumar Gupta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究针对行政记录与遥感分类产品之间的面积不一致问题，利用13期Sentinel-2 NDVI时间序列和849个审核样本，构建了"双重路径（数据整理+分类）"框架，通过特征敏感性分析与多分类器对比，实现Punjab省Patiala区2022年Rabi季小麦种植面积的业务化估算，其预测面积与官方表格目标仅偏差+2.99%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 业务化估算小麦种植面积长期受制于行政记录与遥感分类产品之间的不一致（administrative reference discordance）。官方空间参考掩膜与官方表格面积目标之间存在显著偏差，导致验证标准本身存在噪声，因此需要一种能在标签噪声（label-noisy）行政环境下实现面积调和的方法框架。

Method: 以印度Punjab省Patiala区2022年Rabi季为研究区，采用13个时相的Sentinel-2 NDVI时间序列。通过迭代式规则引导自助采样（rule-based bootstrapping）构建849个样本的审核参考数据集。在此基础上开展两项工作：(1) 特征敏感性分析，分别用Cohen's d与梯度提升信息增益独立评估各时相判别力；(2) 分类器基准测试，在相同的679/170训练-测试划分下比较1D-CNN、LSTM、CNN-LSTM混合模型与XGBoost四种分类器，并开展全区36.25百万有效像元的满总体部署。

Result: 特征敏感性分析中两种独立方法一致表明2月至3月的灌浆期（grain-fill window）最具判别力。基准测试中XGBoost总体精度最高（78.82%），优于深度学习基线（64–66%），这与树集成模型在低样本条件下参数-样本比更优的特性一致。满总体部署时业务分类器达到86.31%精确率和71.05%召回率；预测小麦面积相对官方表格目标仅偏差+2.99%，而政府空间参考掩膜相对同一目标存在+25.11%的正向面积偏差。

Conclusion: 在审核者整理（auditor-curated）的参考集上训练的分类器，其面积估计比常规用于验证的空间产品更贴近官方表格面积，揭示了验证基准本身的偏差。作者将"整理-分类"双重路径框架作为标签噪声行政场景下作物面积调和的方法学参考。

Abstract: Operational estimation of wheat-cultivated area is persistently constrained by discordance between administrative record-keeping and remotely sensed classification products. We address this administrative reference discordance for the 2022 Rabi season in Patiala district, Punjab, India, using a thirteen-timestep Sentinel-2 NDVI time series. A curated 849-sample reference dataset, developed through an iterative rule-based bootstrapping procedure, underpins both a feature sensitivity analysis and an operational classifier. Feature sensitivity independently assessed via Cohen's d and gradient-boosted information gain converges on the February-to-March grain-fill window as most discriminative. Four classifiers (1D-CNN, LSTM, hybrid CNN-LSTM, and XGBoost) were benchmarked on an identical 679/170 sample split. XGBoost achieved the highest overall accuracy (78.82%) against deep-learning baselines (64-66%), consistent with tree-based ensembles' favourable parameter-to-sample ratio in low-sample regimes. At full-population deployment across 36.25 million valid district pixels, the operational classifier attained 86.31% precision and 71.05% recall. The predicted wheat extent deviated by only +2.99% from the official tabular target, whereas the government's spatial reference mask exhibited a +25.11% positive area bias against the identical target. This asymmetry indicates that a classifier trained on an auditor-curated reference set reconciles more closely with the official tabular area than the spatial product conventionally used to validate it. We present this dual-track curation-and-classification framework as a methodological reference for crop-area reconciliation in label-noisy administrative settings.

</details>


### [92] [UltraMatch: Transport Path Routing for Ultra-Fast and Memory-Efficient Image Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36980)
*Jiajun Le,Yifan Lu,Zizhuo Li,Lei Cao,Junjun Jiang,Jiayi Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UltraMatch 通过轻量级 Transport Path Router 仅路由少量候选匹配路径，规避稠密 token 级匹配的二次计算与内存开销，实现超高效、可扩展的半稠密匹配，速度更快、显存占用极低且支持高分辨率推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有半稠密匹配器虽在精度和效率上有进步，但粗略匹配阶段仍需进行稠密 token 级匹配，带来高昂的计算与内存成本（二次复杂度），成为性能瓶颈。

Method: 核心为轻量级 Transport Path Router，作用于粗略块表示，对每个源块的目标块候选进行排序并仅保留少量，将后续 token 级匹配限制在选定路径上，避免构建完整的 token-to-token 匹配矩阵；设计稀疏全局 Dual-Softmax，仅在路由得到的块候选上进行匹配同时保留全局竞争。此外采用面向部署的结构重参数化特征提取与参数共享的微型精匹配头以降低推理成本与内存。

Result: 在半稠密匹配器中达到有竞争力的精度，同时比 SuperPoint+LightGlue 快 1.67 倍，峰值推理内存仅 0.44 GiB；可在单张 RTX 3090 上支持高达 6K 分辨率推理，而现有半稠密匹配器在不到 2K 时即内存不足。该路由策略可迁移，在 EDM 和 ELoFTR 上实现约 2 倍端到端加速且无精度损失。

Conclusion: 通过稀疏路由替代稠密 token 级匹配，UltraMatch 在保持精度的同时显著提升效率、降低内存并增强可扩展性，且路由策略具备良好迁移性。

Abstract: Despite recent advances in accuracy and efficiency, coarse matching remains an indispensable yet costly stage in existing semi-dense matchers due to dense token-level matching. We present UltraMatch, an ultra-efficient and scalable semi-dense matching framework that bypasses the quadratic computation and memory cost of dense token-level matching by routing only a small fraction of candidate matching paths. At its core, a lightweight Transport Path Router operates on coarse block representations to rank candidate target blocks for each source block and retain only a small set, restricting subsequent token-level matching to the selected paths and avoiding the construction of the full token-to-token matching matrix. We further design a sparse global Dual-Softmax that performs matching only over the routed block candidates while retaining global competition across the sparse matching space. Beyond matching acceleration, UltraMatch employs deployment-oriented structural reparameterization for feature extraction and a tiny fine matching head with shared parameters, further reducing inference cost and memory consumption. UltraMatch achieves competitive accuracy among semi-dense matchers, while running 1.67$\times$ faster than SuperPoint+LightGlue with only 0.44 GiB peak inference memory. Its scalability enables inference at up to 6K resolution on a single RTX 3090, whereas existing semi-dense matchers run out of memory before reaching 2K. Our routing strategy is also transferable, delivering about 2$\times$ end-to-end speedup in EDM and ELoFTR without accuracy loss. The project repository is available at https://github.com/JiajunLe/UltraMatch.

</details>


### [93] [Salt++: Context-Aligned Post-Training for Few-Step Streaming Multimodal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36995)
*Xingtong Ge,Yutong Wang,Lunjie Zhu,Haitao Lin,Fangyu Lin,Yushi Huang,Xin Zhang,Yi Zhang,Yu Liu,Jun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Salt++ 是一个两阶段后训练框架，通过 Causal Self-Flow 与上下文对齐的自回归 DMD，解决少步流式音视频生成中上下文错配与监督不足问题，在 480p 4 步设定下显著提升视觉与运动质量，并扩展至高分辨率生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少步流式音视频生成同时需要因果建模与步数蒸馏，但常规训练存在两个上下文相关问题：教师强制将干净历史与含噪目标配对，仅通过速度预测间接监督预测性上下文表征；而在因果分布匹配蒸馏（DMD）中直接复用双向评分模型，会造成生成上下文与评分上下文的不匹配。

Method: 提出两阶段后训练框架 Salt++：1）Causal Self-Flow（CSF）利用上下文信息不对称性，固定含噪目标、变化历史，使噪声混合历史的学生模型中间表征对齐干净历史的指数移动平均教师模型，作为自监督信号促进语义提取与跨模态对齐；2）上下文对齐的自回归 DMD，在生成器采样、假评分训练与真评分评估间共享因果掩码和前缀，在块条件 KL 目标下匹配生成分布与参考分布，配合校准的教师引导先验进行干净前缀少步蒸馏，并自适应生成历史，无需切换目标或额外一致性蒸馏；另有独立的尺度级后训练阶段。

Result: 在 480p、相同 4 步因果设定下，于 JavisBench 上较 OmniForcing 分别提升视觉质量 57%、运动质量 45%；尺度级后训练将其扩展至 4 步 1664×960 生成，在七项报告指标中的六项上优于双向 LTX-2。

Conclusion: Salt++ 通过 CSF 的自监督表征对齐与上下文对齐的 AR DMD，有效缓解了少步流式音视频生成中的上下文错配与间接监督问题，在保持因果少步设定的同时实现质量提升，并具备向高分辨率生成扩展的能力。

Abstract: Few-step streaming audio--video generation requires both causal modeling and step distillation, yet standard training recipes face two context-related challenges. Teacher forcing pairs clean history with a noisy target, but supervises predictive contextual representations only indirectly through velocity prediction. Meanwhile, directly reusing bidirectional score models in causal Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD) creates a mismatch between generation and scoring contexts. We address these challenges with Salt++, a two-stage post-training framework comprising Causal Self-Flow (CSF) and context-aligned autoregressive DMD. CSF exploits contextual information asymmetry by varying the history while keeping the noisy target fixed: a noise-mixed-history student aligns its intermediate representations with those of a clean-history exponential-moving-average teacher. This self-supervised signal encourages the student to extract semantic information and improves cross-modal alignment. Context-aligned AR DMD shares the causal mask and prefix across generator sampling, fake-score training, and real-score evaluation to match generated and reference distributions under a block-conditional KL objective. With calibrated teacher guidance, it performs clean-prefix few-step distillation and then adapts to generated histories without switching objectives or requiring separate consistency distillation. At 480p, Salt++ improves visual and motion quality by 57% and 45% over OmniForcing on JavisBench under the same 4-step causal setting. A separate scale-wise post-training stage extends Salt++ to 4-step $1664\times960$ generation, outperforming bidirectional LTX-2 on six of seven reported metrics. Project page: https://xingtongge.github.io/Saltpp

</details>


### [94] [Parameterized Stripe Attention for Efficient Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37001)
*Xingyu Jia,Baole Ai,Ang Wang,Kang Zhao,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出PSA（参数化条纹注意力），通过统一刻画视频DiT注意力中的周期性对角条纹结构，实现单一高效CUDA内核处理多种稀疏模式，并结合免训练离线搜索自动确定各注意力头的最优稀疏配置，在HunyuanVideo和Wan 2.1上分别取得1.57倍和1.37倍的端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Diffusion Transformers（DiTs）能生成高质量视频，但推理延迟高，主要瓶颈在于计算开销巨大的全时空注意力。现有稀疏注意力方法面临"灵活性—效率"两难困境：预定义掩码缺乏灵活性，难以捕捉多样化的注意力模式；运行时动态确定的掩码则引入额外开销并牺牲硬件效率。

Method: 作者指出已有方法的关键局限在于缺乏对DiT注意力的统一结构性刻画，并确立视频DiT注意力在时间与空间维度上呈现"周期性对角条纹结构"。据此提出PSA（参数化条纹注意力），将观察到的条纹规律形式化，统一多样化的注意力模式以高效生成掩码，使单一硬件高效的CUDA内核能够处理所有稀疏模式，并达到FlashAttention-3级别的模型FLOPs利用率（MFU）。此外提出免训练的离线搜索算法，在给定误差容忍度下自动为每个注意力头最大化稀疏度。

Result: 在HunyuanVideo和Wan 2.1上的实验表明，PSA相比FlashAttention-3基线分别取得1.57倍和1.37倍的端到端加速，且视觉质量退化可接受。

Conclusion: 通过统一的结构化条纹注意力表示与单内核实现，PSA在保持视觉质量的前提下显著降低了视频DiT的推理延迟，验证了结构先验与硬件高效内核相结合的有效性。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) enable high-quality video generation but suffer from substantial inference latency, primarily attributable to the computationally expensive full spatio-temporal attention. While sparse attention methods offer potential solutions, existing approaches face an inherent flexibility--efficiency dilemma: predefined masks lack the flexibility to capture diverse attention patterns, while runtime-determined masks introduce overheads and sacrifice hardware efficiency. We identify the lack of a unified structural characterization of DiT attention as a key limitation of existing methods, and establish that video DiT attention exhibits \textbf{periodic diagonal stripe structures} along both temporal and spatial dimensions. To formally encode these structured patterns within a single efficient kernel, we present {\bf PSA}, a parameterized stripe attention that formalizes the observed stripe regularity, unifying diverse attention patterns for efficient mask generation. This unified representation enables a single hardware-efficient CUDA kernel to process all sparse patterns, achieving FlashAttention-3-level Model FLOPs Utilization. To determine optimal sparsity configurations, we propose a training-free offline search algorithm that automatically maximizes sparsity under a specified error tolerance for each attention head. Experiments on HunyuanVideo and Wan~2.1 demonstrate that PSA achieves 1.57$\times$ and 1.37$\times$ end-to-end speedups over FlashAttention-3 baselines, with acceptable visual quality degradation.

</details>


### [95] [Visual Parallel Search: Learning to Search High-Resolution Images with Parallel Tile Inspection and Adaptive Zoom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37002)
*Xijia Tao,Yihua Teng,Xinyu Fu,Cheng Gong,Ziru Liu,Xudong Xie,Rui Liu,Lingpeng Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VPS视觉并行搜索框架，主智能体用grid_search并行检查图像块，再自适应调用zoom_in，在14/15个同模型比较中提升准确率，最高8.0分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率视觉问答失败常因多模态模型无法获取回答问题所需的小范围、空间局部证据；顺序缩放虽可恢复细节，但要求主模型在获得可靠全局概览前就选定区域。

Method: 主智能体先调用grid_search，借助问题条件化的子智能体并行检查图像块；随后自适应调用zoom_in。另构建带无提示验证的监督流水线与成对角色专属GRPO代理，用于学习控制器与图像块读取器两种角色。

Result: 相比专用仅缩放搜索，VPS在15个同模型比较中的14个提升平均准确率，最高提升8.0分，对较小主模型提升尤为明显；ZoomBench在各测试规模上保持约3.2分增益。SFT在全部五个基准划分上提升观测准确率，HR-Bench 4K提升4.17分。角色专属RL重塑搜索行为：仅主模型RL将平均工具使用从2.65降至2.11，内部四响应评估pass@1相近，外部准确率变化不一。联合训练揭示局部证据读取与全局搜索控制间的不对称性。

Conclusion: VPS既可作有效的推理期脚手架，也是一种可训练分解方式，用于视觉证据获取。

Abstract: High-resolution visual question answering often fails because a multimodal model does not acquire the small, spatially localized evidence needed to answer a question. Sequential zooming can recover detail, but it asks the main model to choose a region before obtaining a reliable overview. We introduce VPS, a visual parallel-search framework in which a main agent first invokes grid_search to inspect image tiles in parallel with question-conditioned sub-agents, and then adaptively invokes zoom_in PSisual Parallel Search improves mean accuracy over dedicated zoom-only search in 14 of 15 same-model comparisons, with gains up to 8.0 points and especially strong improvements for smaller main models. ZoomBench retains an approximately 3.2-point gain at every tested size. We further develop a supervision pipeline with hint-free verification and a paired role-specific GRPO surrogate for learning the controller and tile-reader roles. SFT improves observed accuracy on all five benchmark splits, including a 4.17-point gain on HR-Bench 4K. Role-specific RL further reshapes search behavior: main-only RL reduces mean tool use from 2.65 to 2.11 with similar pass@1 in an internal four-response evaluation, while external accuracy changes are mixed. Joint training reveals an asymmetry between local evidence reading and global search control. Together, these results support VPS as an effective inference-time scaffold and a trainable decomposition for visual evidence acquisition.

</details>


### [96] [VesselBench-800K: A Large-scale Perception Benchmark for Multimodal Vessel Detection, Counting, and Density Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37003)
*Danfeng Hong,Chenyu Li,Jocelyn Chanussot*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VesselBench-800K，一个全球尺度、多模态遥感船舶感知基准数据集，包含80万张512x512图像（光学与SAR配对），面向检测、计数与密度估计任务，并对多种先进模型进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有船舶感知数据集多局限于光学遥感图像中的通用目标检测任务；单一光学模态在复杂海上场景（云雨天气多变、需昼夜覆盖、单模态固有局限）下难以有效感知船舶目标，因此亟需多模态大规模基准数据集。

Method: 构建VesselBench-800K数据集，规模为80万张512x512像素图像，图像对涵盖光学与SAR两种模态，来自多平台、多传感器、多场景、多拍摄高度及合成来源，空间分辨率覆盖4.5m至0.1m；在此基础上对多种先进的检测、计数与密度估计模型进行定性与定量评估。

Result: 该数据集为该领域规模最大的全球性多模态船舶感知基准；通过对众多SOTA模型的定性与定量对比评估，揭示了此前未被识别的线索，验证了数据集的挑战性与价值。

Conclusion: VesselBench-800K填补了多模态遥感船舶感知基准的空白，有望显著推进对海上交通的理解；数据集将公开于 https://github.com/danfenghong/IEEE_TGRS_VesselBench 以支持社区发展。

Abstract: Vessel perception from space is crucial for a wide range of maritime applications, from traffic monitoring to environmental protection. However, most existing datasets predominantly focus on general object detection tasks in optical remote sensing (RS) images. Relying solely on single-modality optical RS images proves inadequate for effectively perceiving vessel objects in complex maritime scenarios, where ever-changing weather conditions (e.g., clouds and rain), the need for day-and-night coverage, and the inherent limitations of a single imaging modality pose significant challenges. To fill this gap, we introduce VesselBench-800K, the largest-to-date benchmark dataset on a global scale for vessel perception in multimodal RS images. As its name suggests, VesselBench-800K comprises 800,000 images, each at a resolution of 512x512 pixels, specifically curated for vessel perception tasks such as detection, counting, and density estimation. These multimodal image pairs (i.e., optical, SAR) are collected from diverse platforms, sensors, scenes, shooting heights, and synthetic sources, spanning spatial resolutions from 4.5m to 0.1m. Furthermore, we evaluate numerous state-of-the-art detection, counting, and density estimation models on VesselBench-800K through both qualitative and quantitative comparisons. By revealing previously unrecognized cues, this dataset holds immense potential to significantly advance our understanding of marine traffic. Our VesselBench dataset will be publicly available at https://github.com/danfenghong/IEEE_TGRS_VesselBench to support and contribute to community development.

</details>


### [97] [World2Motion: Turning Video World Models into 3D Human Motion Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37004)
*Tu Fangyuan,Xiangyue Zhang,Yiyi Cai,Yichen Peng,Kunhang Li,Bo Zheng,Zhixiang Wang,Kaipeng Zhang,Erwin Wu,Haoran Xie,Haiyang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: World2Motion将视频世界模型Cosmos 3改造为单阶段3D人体运动生成器，仅用单张图像和文本提示即可生成场景感知的3D运动及对应视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D运动生成器受限于运动数据集的环境覆盖不足，泛化能力受限；而视频世界模型（如Cosmos 3）虽具备更广的环境先验，却无法直接生成全身运动，从其生成视频中恢复运动需要昂贵的两阶段推理。

Method: 1) 构建结合合成视频—运动配对与真实视频配估计3D运动的训练数据集；2) 提出shift-decoupled噪声调度，通过共享去噪进程为视频和运动分配不同噪声水平，以适配两种模态的不同去噪需求并减少运动抖动。

Result: 在多源交互基准上，World2Motion在运动—文本对齐和场景交互方面优于所评估的3D运动生成器；交互成功率与两阶段基线相当，同时推理速度约快3.3倍。

Conclusion: 通过将视频世界模型改造为单阶段3D运动生成器，并解决配对数据稀缺与时间不稳定问题，World2Motion在保持交互质量的同时显著提升了推理效率。

Abstract: We present World2Motion, a framework that generates scene-aware 3D human motion and corresponding video from a single image and a text prompt. While existing 3D motion generators learn from motion datasets, their generalization is constrained by limited coverage of environments. In contrast, video world models such as Cosmos 3 offer broader environmental priors but are not designed for full-body motion generation; recovering motion from their generated videos requires costly two-stage inference. To address these, we turn Cosmos 3 into a single-stage 3D motion generator. This adaptation has two challenges: the scarcity of paired video--motion data and temporal instability in the generated motion. First, we construct a training dataset combining synthetic video--motion pairs with real videos paired with estimated 3D motion. Second, we propose a shift-decoupled noise schedule that assigns different noise levels to video and motion through shared denoising progress. This design accommodates the different denoising requirements of the two modalities, reducing motion jitter. Experiments on a multi-source interaction benchmark show that World2Motion has better motion--text alignment and scene interaction compared with the evaluated 3D motion generators. It also matches the interaction success rate of the two-stage baseline while achieving approximately 3.3$\times$ faster inference.

</details>


### [98] [Embedded Bi-Temporal Building Damage Assessment for On-Board Data Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37013)
*Thomas Goudemant,Benjamin Francesconi,Marjorie Bellizzi,Adrien Dorise*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于YOLOX孪生检测器的双时相建筑损毁评估流水线，在地面将灾前参考影像压缩至紧凑潜空间（最高64倍）上传卫星，在轨仅下传目标级产品（边界框与损毁类别），并引入潜空间位移估计与校正模块应对配准误差，最终部署于Versal VCK190与Jetson AGX Orin两个嵌入式平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然灾害后的建筑损毁快速评估对应急响应至关重要。遥感卫星虽能快速获取灾后影像，但其利用受限于上下行链路容量和地面处理延迟，因此需要在星地链路两端压缩信息传输量。

Method: 1）构建基于YOLOX的孪生检测器双时相损毁评估流水线；2）地面端将灾前参考影像编码为紧凑潜空间（压缩比最高64倍）并上传；3）星上端将参考与灾后新采集影像对比，仅下传边界框和损毁类别等目标级产品；4）引入潜空间位移估计与校正模块，从粗特征层级回归全局偏移，在融合前重新对齐灾后特征。

Result: 在xBD数据集上，强压缩的参考影像仍保留大部分检测性能；位移校正模块显著提升了对去配准的鲁棒性，尤其在大位移情况下（仅融合的变体此时会失效），同时提升名义精度并与最强压缩兼容。硬件部署方面，核心检测器及其压缩版本可干净地移植到Xilinx Versal VCK190和NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin，但实现长程鲁棒性所需的算子仅在Jetson GPU上存活，Versal DPU不支持。

Conclusion: 该流水线通过双向信息压缩有效降低星地链路数据交换量，所提潜空间位移校正模块增强了配准误差下的鲁棒性；但长程鲁棒性算子在嵌入式平台上的可移植性存在硬件差异，Jetson GPU优于Versal DPU。

Abstract: Rapid assessment of building damage after natural disasters is essential to support emergency response. Earth Observation satellites can acquire relevant imagery shortly after an event, but exploitation is limited by uplink and downlink capacity and by ground-processing latency. We address this with a bi-temporal building damage assessment pipeline built on a siamese detector derived from YOLOX, designed to compress information at both ends of the ground/space link. On the ground, pre-disaster reference images are encoded into a compact latent space -- compressed by up to a factor of 64 -- and uplinked to the satellite. On board, this reference is compared with a fresh post-disaster acquisition so that the downlink carries only actionable object-level products, bounding boxes and damage classes, instead of full scenes. This cuts the data exchanged in both directions, while on xBD the strongly compressed reference still preserves most of the detection performance.
  Because on-board acquisitions suffer from residual pre/post co-registration errors, we introduce a latent-space shift estimation and correction module that regresses the global offset from the coarse feature level and realigns the post-disaster features before fusion. It substantially improves robustness to de-registration -- especially under large shifts, where fusion-only variants collapse -- while also raising nominal accuracy and remaining compatible with the strongest compression. We finally port the pipeline to two embedded targets, a Xilinx Versal VCK190 and an NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin, and report hardware performance (latency, throughput, power efficiency). The core detector and its compression port cleanly to both, but the operators needed for long-range robustness survive only on the Jetson GPU, whereas the Versal DPU does not.

</details>


### [99] [RBF-GNN: Rational Basis Functions for Pseudo-Coordinate based Graph Convolutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37015)
*Paweł Batorski,Abtin Pourhadi,Paul Swoboda*

Main category: cs.CV
