### [240] [Evaluating Bounded Autonomy in Regulated Agentic AI: A Diagnostic Harness with Constitutional Rewards, Escalation Labels, and Runtime Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37501)
*Dipankar Sarkar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 RegLLM 诊断框架，用六个可信度信号衡量受监管智能体工作流的"有界自主性"，并强调小规模试点中配置方差可能淹没微调效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受监管场景中的智能体需要在"行动"与"上报/延迟"之间做可控决策，但现有评测缺乏可量化、可复核的诊断工具，也难以区分程序化验证、任务标签与 AI 评判等不同监督来源的信号。

Method: 构建 RegLLM 诊断 harness：埋点六个信号——引文有效性、来源接地、schema 合规、升级正确性、宪法对齐、不安全行动率，并按监督来源（程序化验证器、任务级升级标签、AI 评判分）分类；引入确定性运行时监督器，阻断无依据回答并强制升级、记录干预；以领域宪法统一驱动评测、训练奖励与服务护栏；用任务级 should-escalate 标签把"行动/延迟"决策转为可训练信号。

Result: 冒烟规模验证：离线参考运行（n=12）在启用治理后，升级召回从 0 提升至 0.67，不安全行动率从 0.33 降至 0.08。两次单卡 Qwen2.5-3B 的 LoRA/DPO 试点（n=8，同种子、同评测划分）显示显著方差：名义相同的 RL-base 配置任务成功率为 0.25 与 0.12，升级召回为 1.0 与 0.5；答案质量适配器在 Run A 使召回从 1.0 变 0.5，在 Run B 却从 0.5 变 1.0；升级感知变体在 Run B 无可测变化。

Conclusion: 这些小规模试点无法确立可靠的适配器效应或生产可用性；其价值在于诊断性——配置方差可能压倒表观的调优效果，因此需要更大评测集与重复运行。

Abstract: We propose RegLLM, a diagnostic harness for bounded autonomy in regulated agentic workflows. It instruments six trustworthiness signals: citation validity, source grounding, schema compliance, escalation correctness, constitutional alignment, and unsafe-action rate. Signals are distinguished by their source of supervision: programmatic verifiers, task-level escalation labels, or AI-judge scores. A deterministic runtime supervisor blocks ungrounded answers and forces escalation, logging interventions. The same domain constitution informs evaluation, training rewards, and serving guardrails. Task-level should-escalate labels make the act-versus-defer decision a measurable training signal. We demonstrate the harness at smoke scale. An offline reference run (n=12) lifts escalation recall from 0 to 0.67 and reduces unsafe-action rate from 0.33 to 0.08 when governance is enabled. Two single-GPU Qwen2.5-3B LoRA/DPO pilots (n=8, same seed and evaluation split) expose substantial variation: nominally identical RL-base configurations yield task success of 0.25 versus 0.12 and escalation recall of 1.0 versus 0.5. An answer-quality adapter changes recall from 1.0 to 0.5 in Run A, but from 0.5 to 1.0 in Run B. An escalation-aware variant produces no measurable change in Run B. These small pilots do not establish reliable adapter effects or production readiness. Their contribution is diagnostic: configuration variance can overwhelm apparent tuning effects on bounded-autonomy metrics, motivating larger evaluation sets and repeated runs.

</details>


### [241] [From Dissonance to Orchestration: Teacher Intervention in On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37510)
*Yuhao Wang,Ruiyang Ren,Yinan Zhang,Ruiqing Zhang,Jing Liu,Chunyan Miao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究在线策略蒸馏中教师干预深度与位置对学生学习的影响，提出基于局部策略分歧自适应决定教师接管时机与生成长度的MAESTRO方法，在八个数学推理基准上取得最高宏平均准确率，并缩短训练响应长度67.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏中学生基于自身推理轨迹学习，教师干预虽能改善轨迹质量，却会改变学生学习所依据的分布。仅以轨迹质量作为分配教师指导的标准并不充分：更深的干预在提升轨迹准确率上收益递减，同时增加离策略负荷。在受限rollout horizon的训练探针中，峰值学生准确率与性能保持所偏好的干预强度并不一致，且最优干预深度与位置也因基准而异，因此需要自适应地决定干预时机与长度。

Method: 提出MAESTRO，利用局部策略分歧共同自适应决定教师何时接管以及生成多长时间。其策略分歧分数（policy disagreement score）结合教师加权的候选覆盖率与局部分布相似度，并在推理段落（reasoning paragraphs）内进行聚合。

Result: 在八个数学推理基准上，MAESTRO在0.6B和1.7B Qwen3学生模型中均取得所比较方法中最高的宏平均准确率，其中1.7B学生在每个基准上均领先；相较标准在线策略蒸馏，平均训练响应长度减少67.3%。

Conclusion: 轨迹质量不足以作为分配教师指导的唯一标准，需根据局部策略分歧动态调节教师干预的时机与深度；MAESTRO据此在多个基准上同时实现更高准确率与更短的训练响应长度，验证了自适应干预策略的有效性。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on its own reasoning trajectories using feedback from a stronger teacher. Teacher interventions can improve these trajectories, but also change the distribution on which the student learns. Our controlled studies show that rollout quality alone is an incomplete criterion for allocating teacher guidance. Deeper intervention yields diminishing gains in rollout accuracy while increasing off-policy load. In a training probe with a restricted rollout horizon, peak student accuracy and performance retention favor different intervention strengths. The preferred intervention depth and placement also vary across benchmarks. These findings motivate MAESTRO, which uses local policy disagreement to jointly adapt when the teacher takes over and how long it generates. Its {policy disagreement score} combines teacher-weighted candidate coverage with local distribution similarity and is aggregated within reasoning paragraphs. Across eight mathematical reasoning benchmarks, MAESTRO achieves the highest macro-average accuracy among the compared methods for both 0.6B and 1.7B Qwen3 students, with the 1.7B student leading on every benchmark. MAESTRO also reduces average training response length by 67.3\% relative to standard OPD. The code is available at https://github.com/yhao-wang/MAESTRO.

</details>


### [242] [E-MoE: Enhanced Mixture-of-Experts for Non-Factorized Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37533)
*Arseny Ivanov,Alexander Kolesov,Alexander Korotin,Ivan Oseledets,Mikhail Goncharov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出Enhanced Mixture-of-Experts (E-MoE)，利用MoE骨干网络的路由决策作为离散共享隐变量，将掩码扩散模型的反向过程建模为逐位置分解分布的混合，在不增加激活参数的前提下提升少步生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散模型(MDM)的反向过程按位置分解，在少步生成场景下无法捕捉位置间相关性，限制了样本质量；而引入连续高斯隐变量的变分自编码器方法易出现后验坍缩，隐变量被无声忽略。

Method: 将MoE骨干网络中的专家路由决策作为离散共享隐变量，把反向过程构建为以该隐变量为条件的逐位置分解分布的混合。通过离散隐变量避免连续隐变量方法的后验坍缩问题，且相比分解基线不增加激活参数。

Result: 在合成多模态基准、二值化MNIST和LM1B数据集上，E-MoE在少步生成任务中优于分解基线方法。

Conclusion: 以MoE路由决策作为离散共享隐变量可有效建模位置间相关性，为掩码扩散模型在少步生成场景下的质量提升提供了无需增加激活参数的有效方案。

Abstract: Masked diffusion models (MDMs) generate sequences by progressively unmasking several tokens per denoising step, but their reverse process is typically factorized over positions, limiting sample quality in the few-step regime where diffusion's speed advantage over autoregressive decoding matters most. A recent line of work introduces a continuous Gaussian latent, trained as a variational autoencoder, to capture correlations across positions, but such approaches are prone to posterior collapse, where the latent is silently ignored. We propose Enhanced Mixture-of-Experts (E-MoE), which builds the reverse process as a mixture of factorized distributions over a discrete shared latent given by the expert-routing decisions of a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) backbone, without increasing active parameters over the factorized baseline. Across synthetic multi-modal benchmarks, binarized MNIST, and LM1B, E-MoE improves few-step generation over factorized baselines.

</details>


### [243] [RunyaNER: Auxiliary Language Selection for Runyankore NER](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37543)
*Prosper Arineitwe Asiimwe,Francois Meyer,Jan Buys*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文发布了首个Runyankore语NER基准RunyaNER（23.7万词、3万句），并基于它系统研究跨语言零样本迁移和多语言微调中的辅助语言选择策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言的NER常依赖跨语言零样本迁移或多语言微调，但在缺乏目标语言基准时，哪种辅助语言选择策略能带来最佳迁移效果尚不明确。

Method: 通过半自动化流水线构建并经完全人工校验的RunyaNER数据集；在此基础上对预训练模型进行基准测试，并在跨语言零样本迁移与多语言微调两种设置下比较辅助语言选择策略，包括基于嵌入的度量与传统基于元数据/类型学的语言学特征。

Result: RunyaNER包含30k句子、超237k标注词，质量与规模足以训练有效的Runyankore NER模型；迁移性能对辅助语言选择高度敏感，且由标注训练片段计算的嵌入度量与下游迁移性能的相关性强于传统语言学特征。

Conclusion: 该工作既提供了新的基准资源RunyaNER，也为低资源场景下的多语言迁移提供了辅助语言选择的实用洞见。

Abstract: Cross-lingual zero-shot transfer and multilingual fine-tuning are promising approaches for NLP tasks such as Named Entity Recognition (NER) in low-resource languages, but in the absence of target language benchmarks, it is unclear which auxiliary language selection strategy leads to the best transfer. We introduce RunyaNER, the first publicly available NER benchmark for the East African language Runyankore, and use it to investigate the choice of which languages to use for transfer. Created with a semi-automated pipeline and fully manually verified, RunyaNER contains over 237k annotated words across 30k sentences. We benchmark pretrained models on RunyaNER, establishing that our dataset is of sufficient quality and size to produce effective Runyankore NER models. We then use RunyaNER to investigate auxiliary language selection in cross-lingual zero-shot and multilingual fine-tuning settings. Our experiments show that while transfer performance is highly sensitive to auxiliary language selection, embedding-based measures computed from labelled training spans correlate more strongly with downstream transfer performance than traditional linguistic features based on metadata or typology. By releasing RunyaNER and providing a systematic analysis of auxiliary language selection strategies, this work contributes both a new benchmark resource and practical insights for multilingual transfer in low-resource settings.

</details>


### [244] [Orthogonal Yet Coupled: Decoupling Geometric Components for Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37564)
*Zijing Wang,Yongkang Liu,Mingyang Wang,Ercong Nie,Mengjie Zhao,Yunpu Ma,Kang Liu,Zihan Wang,Shi Feng,Daling Wang,Hinrich Schütze*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出DiGA框架，将任务向量正交分解为不同几何属性的分量，在各自子空间独立聚合后再重组，避免跨分量耦合，并可作为插件嵌入现有合并方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型合并方法将每个任务向量视为不可分割的合并单元，忽略了其中蕴含的异构几何变化，导致跨分量耦合：基于完整任务向量统计量做出的合并决策会使某一分量的几何特性影响另一分量的选择、加权或组合，进而降低合并模型质量。

Method: 以预训练权重为共享几何参考，将每个任务向量正交分解为对应不同几何属性的分量；不再整体合并任务向量，而是在各自子空间内独立聚合对应分量，再重组为统一更新。该分量式表述保留了各分量的几何身份，防止分量间干扰，并可嵌入多种现有合并方法。

Result: 在多种模型、任务和合并方法上的大量实验表明，DiGA提升了合并模型性能并减少了能力退化。

Conclusion: DiGA是一种解耦几何感知的模型合并框架，通过分量级独立聚合有效缓解跨分量耦合，具有通用性和可扩展性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Merging pretrained models has emerged as an effective approach for consolidating diverse capabilities into a single unified model. However, prevailing merging methods typically treat each task vector as an indivisible merging unit, overlooking the heterogeneous geometric changes encoded within it. This treatment can induce cross-component coupling: when merging decisions are derived from statistics of the complete task vector, the geometric characteristics of one component may influence how another is selected, weighted, or combined, potentially degrading the quality of the merged model. To address this issue, we propose DiGA, a
Disentangled Geometry-Aware model merging framework. Using the pretrained weights as a shared geometric reference, DiGA orthogonally decomposes each task vector into components corresponding to distinct geometric attributes. Rather than merging the task vectors as a whole, DiGA aggregates corresponding components independently within their respective subspaces and subsequently recombines them into a unified update. This component-wise formulation preserves the geometric identity of each component and prevents the characteristics of one component from interfering with the aggregation of another. Furthermore, DiGA can be incorporated into a broad range of existing model merging methods. Extensive experiments across diverse models, tasks, and merging methods demonstrate that DiGA improves merged-model performance and reduces capability degradation. Our repository is on https://github.com/wzj1718/DiGA.

</details>


### [245] [Devils in Question Relay: Source-Conditioned Relay Steering to Mitigate Hallucinations in Audio-visual Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37568)
*Yu Zhang,Pingrui Zhang,Xuefeng Bai,Pengfei Zhang,Yang Xiang,Kehai Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究了音视频大语言模型（AVLLMs）中的"来源混淆的grounding幻觉"问题，揭示其由"问题中继"机制导致，并提出免训练的SECRET方法来缓解跨模态干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AVLLMs通过视觉、听觉和语言信息的交互实现了多模态理解与推理的显著进步，但存在"来源混淆的grounding幻觉"——来自未被使用模态的线索会诱导出所需模态并不支持的响应，损害了实际应用中的可靠性。现有方法虽在缓解此问题上有进展，但对这一失败如何从内部跨模态交互中产生仍缺乏足够理解。

Method: 作者通过路径干预和表征分析，揭示了"问题中继"（question-relay）机制：问题状态在携带所需来源证据的同时也携带了干扰线索，从而削弱了对所需模态证据的grounding。切断从干扰模态到问题状态的路径，比切断到生成位置的路径能带来更大的正确答案logit恢复。基于此，提出SECRET（来源条件化中继引导），一种免训练方法，利用不同模态路径干预所激发的对比性问题表征，将原始问题状态引导向所需来源证据。

Result: 在CMM和AVHBench两个广泛采用的基准上、跨三个AVLLMs的实验中，SECRET持续优于已有的免训练方法，显著缓解来源混淆的grounding幻觉（例如相比基础模型最高提升18.0和7.1个百分点）。模态特定的描述生成进一步证明了其在开放式生成上的泛化性。

Conclusion: SECRET通过定位并干预问题中继这一关键环节，为缓解AVLLMs中的来源混淆grounding幻觉提供了一种有效且免训练的解决方案，并展现出跨基准、跨模型及开放式生成任务的泛化能力。

Abstract: Audio-visual large language models (AVLLMs) have made remarkable progress in multimodal understanding and reasoning through interactions among visual, auditory, and linguistic information. However, recent studies show that AVLLMs face a critical challenge: $\textbf{source-confused grounding hallucination}$, where cues from the unused modality induce responses that the required modality does not support, undermining reliability in real-world applications. Existing methods have made progress in mitigating this failure, yet how it arises from internal cross-modal interactions remains insufficiently understood. To address this gap, we conduct path-intervention and representation analyses, revealing a $\textbf{question-relay}$ mechanism: question states carry interfering cues alongside required-source evidence, undermining grounding in required-modality evidence. Cutting pathways from interfering modality to question states yields greater correct-answer logit recovery than cutting those to the generation position. Motivated by these findings, we propose $\textbf{SECRET}$ ($\textbf{S}$ourc$\textbf{E}$-$\textbf{C}$onditioned $\textbf{RE}$lay s$\textbf{T}$eering), a training-free method that mitigates cross-modal interference at the question relay. Using contrasting question representations elicited through different modality-pathway interventions, SECRET steers the original question states toward required-source evidence. Experiments on two widely adopted benchmarks CMM and AVHBench across three AVLLMs show that SECRET consistently outperforms prior training-free methods, substantially mitigating source-confused grounding hallucinations (e.g., up to +18.0 and +7.1 percentage points over base models). Modality-specific captioning further demonstrates its generalizability to open-ended generation.

</details>


### [246] [Pair Difficulty Matters: Rethinking Pairwise LLM-as-a-Judge Evaluation and Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37577)
*Bruno Brocai,Maria Becker*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 常用的大模型评判代理指标（位置偏差、传递性、成对一致率）主要受近排名差距样本支配，因此会误导评判器选择；应在按排名差距分层的条件指标上评估，最好以人类排序为金标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为评判器被广泛用于成对比较排序，并依靠位置偏差、传递性与成对一致率三项代理指标衡量其可靠性；但这些指标驱动评判器选择与基准测试，若普遍显示表现差，可能使实践者放弃本可用的评判器。作者认为这种评估方式具有误导性，需要重新审视这些代理指标真正反映的信息。

Method: 在成对聚合所依据的Bradley--Terry几何下，形式化论证每个代理指标都被"近排名差距"样本对主导：此类样本本身在信息论意义上不一致是预期现象，其单个判定对聚合排序贡献甚微；而携带排序信号的"远差距"样本对却几乎不影响这些代理指标。随后通过受控仿真和两个人工评分语料库进行验证，分析代理指标与金标准排序准确率的相关性及其预测成分的分布。

Result: 代理指标与针对金标准的排序准确率仅呈弱相关；其具有预测能力的成分集中在远排名差距区间。仿真与两个人工评分语料库的结果一致支持这一结论。

Conclusion: 应基于按排名差距分层的条件指标来评估LLM评判器，理想情况下以人类排序为参考；当前以位置偏差、传递性和成对一致率为主的代理评估可能误导评判器选择，应予以修正。代码见 https://github.com/brunobrocai/PairDifficulty 。

Abstract: Large Language Model judges are widely used to rank texts and text-generating systems through pairwise comparison, and their reliability is typically assessed via three proxies: position bias, transitivity, and pairwise agreement (self- or human-labeled). Because these proxies drive judge selection and benchmarking, a substantial literature reporting that judges perform poorly on them risks steering practitioners away from otherwise capable evaluators. We argue this assessment is misleading. Under the Bradley--Terry geometry underlying pairwise aggregation, each proxy is dominated by close-rank-gap pairs, where inconsistency is information-theoretically expected and individual verdicts contribute little to the aggregate ranking; far-gap pairs carry the ranking signal but barely move the proxies. We formalize this argument and validate it in a controlled simulation and on two human-rated corpora: the proxies correlate only weakly with ranking accuracy against gold, and their predictive component concentrates in the far-gap regime. Judges should therefore be assessed on rank-gap-conditional metrics, ideally against human rankings. Code at https://github.com/brunobrocai/PairDifficulty.

</details>


### [247] [Correct, Don't Delete: Mitigating Emergent Misalignment with Corrective Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37624)
*Jacob Epifano*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在固定中毒数据行的前提下，将有害训练样本"改写为正确答案"比直接"删除"更能降低涌现性失配（EM），且方法在多个模型与设置下稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调模型于少量有害示范（如错误医疗建议）会引发涌现性失配（EM），使其在无关问题上也广泛失配。常规防御是定位并删除问题行，但行定位器在留出测试中失败，删除效果也不及预期。因此作者提出替代问题：在中毒行集合固定的情况下，修正是否优于删除。

Method: 在 Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct 上混合"坏医疗建议+良性对话数据"微调，预先选取四分之一中毒行，分别进行删除或"用同一提示的正确答案替换"，其余条件保持一致；并扩展到半量修正、第二个基座模型与第二个失配模型有机体。进一步比较替换内容性质（改述但保留坏建议、数据集自带正确答案 vs 重写器答案），以及再对齐训练数据来源（修正数据 vs 通用对话数据、其他医疗提示的修正、是否要求建模谨慎助手的指令）。

Result: 替换行使 EM 率降低约三分之一，并改善留出医疗问题上的回答；删除相同行几乎无可测效果。半量修正时优势更大，且在第二个基座模型与第二个失配模型有机体上同样成立。改述但保留坏建议无明确收益；数据集自带正确答案与重写器答案效果相当。短轮次修正训练优于等量通用对话训练；对其他医疗提示的修正与对中毒提示本身的修正效果相近；指示重写器建模谨慎避害助手无额外增益。

Conclusion: 在所测试的设置中，修正有害训练数据比删除更能减少涌现性失配；关键在于替换内容本身（正确答案），而非具体提示或改写风格。

Abstract: Fine-tuning a language model on a narrow set of harmful demonstrations, such as bad medical advice, can make it broadly misaligned on unrelated questions, a phenomenon known as emergent misalignment (EM). The usual defense is to find the offending rows and delete them, but a row locator failed our held-out test and deleting rows helps less than expected. We ask a different question: given a fixed set of poisoned rows, is it better to correct them than to remove them? We fine-tune Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct on a mixture of bad medical advice and benign chat data, select a quarter of the poison rows in advance, and either delete them or replace each with a corrected answer to the same prompt, keeping everything else the same. Replacing the rows cuts the EM rate by about a third and improves answers on held-out medical questions, while deleting the same rows has little measurable effect. The advantage is larger when half the poison rows are corrected, and it holds on a second base model and a second misaligned model organism. The content of the replacement appears to matter: paraphrasing the rows while keeping their bad advice shows no clear benefit, and the correct answers distributed with the dataset appear to do about as well as our rewriter's. Realigning an already-poisoned model with further fine-tuning is known to work, but which data does the work has not been compared directly. We find that a short round of training on corrections beats the same amount of training on generic chat data, that corrections on other medical prompts do roughly as well as corrections of the poisoned prompts themselves, and that instructing the correction writer to model a careful, harm-avoiding assistant adds no measurable benefit over plain corrections. In the settings we tested, correcting harmful training data reduces EM more than deleting it.

</details>


### [248] [Co-Linguistics: AI-augmented Theory Construction in Linguistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37635)
*Emmanuel Chemla,Benjamin Spector,Alexandros Kalomoiros,Philippe Schlenker*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出“Co-Linguistics”概念，将AI定位为语言学研究的“共同科学家”，用于构建与评估形式化语言学理论，而非仅作为人类语言能力的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多把LLM当作人类语言能力的潜在模型。作者认为这一视角过于狭窄，未充分利用AI在形式化推理上的能力。由于语言学理论自1960年代以来大多可数学形式化（形式语言理论、模型论等），数学领域的AI进展必然会波及语言学，因此需要重新界定AI在语言学中的角色。

Method: 提出“Co-Linguistics”研究范式：AI辅助将现有理论完全显式化、比较竞争理论，并借助“程序归纳”提出新理论；借助“主动学习”与海量数据加速关键预测的识别与检验，从而形成理论评估与理论构建的循环。

Result: AI可加速语言学研究，使理论更明确、支持理论间比较并提出新理论，同时提升经验预测检验的效率；理论评估与构建的循环可能带来递归式乃至自主的理论改进。

Conclusion: 语言学的核心目标不是证明新定理，而是寻找能推导经验陈述的最佳公理集。人类仍居中心地位：语言学家提供科学方向并做概念性评估，实验被试用于检验超出LLM能力的经验预测。

Abstract: LLMs have been studied in recent linguistics as potential models of humans' linguistic abilities. Here we discuss an entirely different use of AI, namely as a co-scientist, to help construct and assess linguistic theories (we refer to the result as "Co-Linguistics"). Since the 1960s, linguistics has developed theories that are in principle mathematically formalizable, often in the language of formal language theory or model theory. The AI revolution in mathematics will thus have consequences in linguistics-but with an essential twist: proving new theorems is rarely the linguist's goal. Rather, one seeks to find the best set of axioms to derive empirical statements. AI could accelerate research by making existing theories fully explicit, by comparing competing theories, and more ambitiously, by proposing new theories (in machine learning, this relates to "program induction"). It will also help assess theories by accelerating the identification and test of crucial predictions, thanks to unparalleled access to data (in machine learning, this relates to "active learning"). While the cycle from theory evaluation to theory construction may give rise to recursive and possibly autonomous improvement of linguistic theories, humans remain central: linguists provide scientific directions and evaluate theories conceptually, and experimental participants are needed to assess empirical predictions that are outside the reach of LLMs.

</details>


### [249] [Evaluating and Benchmarking the System One Model Jev](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37647)
*Tobias Deußer,Lorenz Sparrenberg,Rafet Sifa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对 TypeSafe AI 的商业非生成式 System One 模型 Jev（jev-1.13.0）进行零样本评测：在 37 个数据集、346,009 次请求（成本低于 10 美元）上，与 Qwen3.8-27B 和 Gemma-4-E4B 的下一 token 概率基线比较。Jev 在 27/37 数据集上优于 Qwen，全部优于 Gemma，概率校准良好并支持选择性预测，但在低资源语言、细粒度/噪声标签和评分量表任务上同样退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jev 是一类不生成文本的商业 "System One" 模型：给定状态与结构化问题，仅从固定选项中选择、给出量表位置或判断命题为真的概率。此类模型面向信息获取流程中的小决策（查询路由、事实落地检查、内容审核、量表打分），但缺乏独立、跨任务的公开评测。作者希望检验其准确率、概率校准性、选择性预测能力，以及在多语言、标签噪声、记忆化等维度的表现。

Method: 对 jev-1.13.0 进行零样本评测，覆盖分类、路由、自然语言推理、阅读理解、常识推理、内容审核、法律条款分析和量表评分共 37 个数据集，每个数据集使用一个冻结模板并采用完整评测划分，共 346,009 次请求，成本低于 10 美元。作为对照，用相同请求通过 Qwen3.8-27B 与 Gemma-4-E4B 在选项上的精确下一 token 概率进行打分。此外进行选项轮换消融、扣留问题消融、阈值调优（UNFAIR-ToS 的 micro-F1）、按题目类型（MMLU 计算题 vs 其他）与 C-Eval 的分层分析，报告 bootstrap 置信区间。

Result: Jev 在 IMDB、SST-2、HellaSwag、ARC 上达 95–99% 准确率，Belebele 跨 122 种语言达 86.7%。在 37 个数据集中胜过 Qwen 27 个，Qwen 的 9 项领先均未超出 bootstrap 区间；对 Gemma 则在全部 37 个数据集上领先。三模型均在低资源语言、细粒度或噪声标签、量表式质量判断上退化。Jev 的选择概率校准良好，支持选择性预测；二值概率排序良好但相对固定 0.5 阈值定位偏差，用训练数据调优阈值可将 UNFAIR-ToS 的 micro-F1 从 0.50 提升至 0.75。Jev 在 MMLU 计算题上反而比非计算题更准（94% vs. 91%），而两个开放模型相反，三模型在 C-Eval 上均表现为计算题更难。轮换选项不影响 Jev 准确率，扣留问题则降至接近随机，排除浅层记忆但未排除记忆化问答对。

Conclusion: Jev 在广泛的零样本判别任务上可与远大于其规模的开放模型竞争，且概率校准和选择性预测表现良好，适合信息管线中的小决策；但其在低资源语言、细粒度/噪声标签与量表评分上仍受限，且存在记忆化问答对的风险。作者开源代码、评测框架及全部原始响应。

Abstract: Jev is a commercial System One model from TypeSafe AI that does not generate text: given a state and typed questions, it returns a choice from fixed options, a position on a rubric, or the probability that a statement is true, with probabilities the vendor describes as calibrated. Such models target small decisions in information access pipelines, such as routing queries, checking grounding, moderating content, or rating against a rubric. We evaluate Jev (jev-1.13.0) zero-shot on 37 datasets spanning classification, routing, natural language inference, reading comprehension, commonsense reasoning, moderation, legal clause analysis and rubric scoring, with one frozen template per dataset and full evaluation splits: 346,009 requests for under USD 10. For reference, we score Qwen3.8-27B and Gemma-4-E4B on identical requests via their exact next-token probabilities over the options. Jev reaches 95-99% accuracy on IMDB, SST-2, HellaSwag and ARC and 86.7% on Belebele across 122 languages. It beats Qwen on 27 of 37 datasets, with none of Qwen's nine leads outside the bootstrap intervals, and Gemma on all 37. All three models degrade on low-resource languages, fine-grained or noisy labels, and rubric-based quality judgments. Jev's choice probabilities are well calibrated and support selective prediction. Binary probabilities rank well but are poorly placed relative to a fixed 0.5 threshold; thresholds tuned on training data raise micro-F1 on UNFAIR-ToS from 0.50 to 0.75. Jev answers MMLU's calculation-heavy questions more accurately than other MMLU questions (94% vs. 91%), whereas both open models, and all three on C-Eval, find them harder. Rotating the options leaves Jev's accuracy unchanged and withholding the question drops it to near chance, ruling out shallow memorization but not memorized question-answer pairs. We release the code, harness and all raw responses.

</details>


### [250] [Corpus-Guided Dual-Path Propagation for Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37661)
*Baoxian Liu,Tong Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: NexusRAG：在无关系Tri-Graph上引入语料级实体邻域结构，通过双向传播与邻域感知PPR初始化提升多跳检索效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无关系图检索方法主要依赖查询-句子相似度搜索证据，易排除查询相似度低但关键的桥接证据，同时激活与推理链无关的偶然实体。

Method: 构建由实体共现与语义相似度联合导出的语料级实体邻域结构，用于引导两条互补传播路径：基于句子的邻域约束语义传播定位查询相关实体前沿，邻居实体间的直接结构传播将该前沿扩展至结构相关实体；传播得到的实体权重进一步用于邻域感知的段落初始化，以驱动Personalized PageRank。

Result: 在三个多跳QA基准及GraphRAG-Bench的领域子集上一致优于现有方法；在GraphRAG-Bench子集所有问题类别中取得最高证据召回率，超出基线4.2–8.1个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过在无关系Tri-Graph上叠加实体邻域结构并配合互补传播与邻域感知初始化，NexusRAG以简洁有效的方式缓解了桥接证据遗漏与无关实体激活问题，显著提升多跳检索性能。

Abstract: Graph-based retrieval-augmented generation supports multi-hop retrieval by organizing corpus information into graphs. However, existing relation-free graph retrieval methods rely primarily on query-sentence similarity to search for evidence. This can exclude useful bridging evidence with low query similarity and activate incidental entities unrelated to the reasoning chain. In this paper, we propose a simple and effective approach called NexusRAG, which augments the relation-free Tri-Graph with a corpus-level entity neighborhood structure derived from joint entity co-occurrence and semantic similarity. NexusRAG employs this structure to guide two complementary propagation paths: neighborhood-constrained semantic propagation through sentences identifies the query-relevant entity frontier, while direct structural propagation between neighboring entities expands that frontier to structurally related entities. The propagated entity weights also inform neighborhood-aware passage initialization for Personalized PageRank. Experiments on three multi-hop QA benchmarks and a domain-specific subset of GraphRAG-Bench show that NexusRAG consistently outperforms existing approaches. On the GraphRAG-Bench subset, NexusRAG achieves the highest evidence recall in all question categories, exceeding baselines by 4.2-8.1 points. The implementation code is available at https://github.com/Jacob-biu/NexusRAG.

</details>


### [251] [Reader Proficiency Shapes Layer-wise Surprisal Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37688)
*Akio Hayakawa,Horacio Saggion*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究利用MECO L2眼动语料库，考察大语言模型（LLM）逐层surprisal与人类注视行为的层间关系如何随读者词汇熟练度和注视指标而变化。发现低熟练读者在首次注视时间（FPGD）上呈现更深的Predictive Depth，总注视时间（TGD）总体更深，且信息层优势可泛化至未见文本但实际预测提升有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阅读行为不仅受语言输入影响，也随读者熟练度变化。既有研究多用单一层或固定层LLM surprisal预测眼动，未系统考察模型层间预测能力分布与读者熟练度及不同注视指标的关系，因此本文试图从逐层视角刻画这种差异。

Method: 使用MECO L2语料库眼动数据，将被试按词汇熟练度分为高、低两组；以12个LLM各层surprisal为预测变量，对首次注视时间（FPGD）和总注视时间（TGD）建模；用Predictive Depth量化surprisal预测力在模型各层间的分布深度；并进行留一法分析检验信息层的泛化能力。

Result: 在12个LLM上，低词汇熟练读者在FPGD上倾向表现出更深的Predictive Depth，而该差异在TGD上较小；两组读者中TGD均比FPGD表现出更深的Predictive Depth。留一法分析表明，信息性内部层的优势可扩展到未见文本，但预测性能的实际提升有限。

Conclusion: LLM逐层surprisal为理解读者群体间及不同注视指标间的阅读行为差异提供了有效视角；预测力在模型层间的集中位置可能与不同注视指标所捕捉的阅读加工时序与广度相关，并随读者熟练度变化。

Abstract: Reading behaviour varies not only with linguistic input, but also with reader proficiency. In this study, we investigate whether the layer-wise relationship between surprisal from large language models (LLMs) and human gaze behaviour differs across readers with different levels of proficiency and across gaze measures. Using eye-tracking data from the MECO L2 corpus, we compare readers with high and low vocabulary proficiency on first-pass gaze duration (FPGD) and total gaze duration (TGD). We quantify the distribution of the predictive power of surprisal across model layers using Predictive Depth. Across 12 tested LLMs, we find that readers with lower vocabulary proficiency tend to show deeper Predictive Depth for FPGD, while this difference is smaller for TGD. Also, TGD itself shows deeper Predictive Depth than FPGD in both proficiency groups. These patterns suggest that where predictive power is concentrated across LLM layers may be related to the timing and breadth of the reading processes captured by different gaze measures, and that this relationship can vary with reader proficiency. Our leave-one-out analysis further shows that the advantage of informative internal layers extends to unseen texts, although the practical improvements in prediction are limited. Overall, our results show that layer-wise LLM surprisal provides a useful perspective on variation in reading behaviour across both reader groups and gaze measures.

</details>


### [252] [Billiger.de Products: A Bilingual Entity Matching Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37713)
*Aaron Steiner,Ksenia Elagin,Ralph Peeters,Johannes Knopp,Christian Bizer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Billiger.de Products，一个覆盖13类消费品、包含德语和英语的双语实体匹配（产品匹配）基准，并验证其难度高于现有英文基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有产品匹配基准主要包含英文产品数据，且往往由单一产品类别（如电子产品）主导，缺乏多类别、多语言以及跨语言评估能力，因此难以全面衡量匹配器在真实、困难场景下的性能。

Method: 数据来源于德国比价平台 billiger.de。参照 WDC Products 的设计，提供多个变体，变体间在“边角案例比例”“开发集规模”“训练中未见过实体比例”上不同。为每个 offer 提供对齐的英文翻译，保持所有配对、划分和标签不变；跨语言测试集在单个配对中混合德语与英语记录。采用六个监督式匹配器和零样本 GPT-5.2，在德语版、英语版及跨语言测试集上进行验证。

Result: 验证结果表明该基准具有较高难度。多数匹配器在英文版上的平均得分高于德语版，其中 RoBERTa 与 HierGAT 的差距最大；零样本 LLM 对语言基本不敏感。将 PLM 类匹配器在英文版上的 F1 与在 WDC Products、Abt-Buy 等现有英文基准上的表现相比，Billiger.de Products 更难。

Conclusion: Billiger.de Products 提供了多类别、双语及跨语言的产品匹配评测资源，能更严格地检验匹配模型，并揭示语言对匹配性能的影响，可作为比现有英文基准更具挑战性的评测基准。

Abstract: Existing product matching benchmarks primarily contain English-language product data and are often dominated by a single product category, such as electronics. This paper introduces Billiger.de Products, a bilingual German and English entity matching benchmark covering thirteen consumer product categories, including difficult-to-handle categories such as clothing and furniture. The benchmark data originates from the German price comparison platform billiger.de. Following the design of WDC Products, the benchmark offers multiple variants that differ in the fraction of corner cases, the size of the development set, and the fraction of entities unseen during training. An aligned English translation of every offer keeps all pairs, splits, and labels fixed, while cross-language test sets combine German and English records within individual pairs. We validate the benchmark using six supervised matchers and zero-shot GPT-5.2 on both language versions and the cross-language test sets. The validation shows the difficulty of the benchmark. The comparison of the results on the English version of the benchmark to the results on the German version shows that most matchers score on average higher on the English version. The difference is largest for RoBERTa and HierGAT, while the zero-shot LLM runs are largely insensitive to the language. Comparing the F1 scores achieved by PLM-based matchers on the English version of Billiger.de Products with their performance on existing English-language benchmarks, such as WDC Products and Abt-Buy, shows that Billiger.de Products is more difficult than these benchmarks.

</details>


### [253] [Which papyrus HTR is good enough? Character-error-rate tolerance of four papyrological tasks on Greek texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37755)
*Anton Repushko,Elena Chepel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究以papyri.info中63,846份希腊文纸草文献版本为数据，模拟不同字符错误率（CER 1–50%）的HTR输出，测试四类纸草学任务对识别精度的容忍度，为希腊纸草HTR设定基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数希腊纸草仍未被出版和数字化，自动转写的手写文本识别（HTR）流程可帮助学者发现未读文献；但古希腊纸草识别系统尚处初创阶段（in statu nascendi），且针对具体纸草学任务所需精度尚未被考察。

Method: 从papyri.info的63,846份现行希腊文版本中移除编辑层，得到仅含字母的"理想HTR"输出；用带种子算法将其退化至精确的1–50% CER，并引入丢行和四种错误形态变体。在此数据上训练小模型（TF-IDF、fastText、字符CNN、ByT5-small），用于文献类型、断代及"文书vs文学"分类，并应用八种关键词检索方法；比较干净文本训练模型与在特定CER水平重训的模型，并跨CER评估。

Result: 容忍度因任务而异。干净训练的模型下，"文书vs文学"分类在CER达20%时仍保留90%指标值；文献类型达7.5%；子类型与检索达5%；断代仅达3%。在含字符错误的文本上重训可基本消除CER超过15%后出现的急剧性能下降。模型对长文档中集中出现的错误，通常比对短文本中分散的小错误更耐受。

Conclusion: 该研究为四类任务分别给出了CER目标值，并表明在含噪文本上训练的模型可使当前不完美的文本识别对上述任务具有实用价值。

Abstract: Purpose: Most Greek papyri remain unpublished and undigitised; a handwritten text recognition (HTR) pipeline that transcribes them automatically would let scholars discover documents and literary works that have so far gone unread. Recognition systems for Ancient Greek papyri are in statu nascendi, and how accurate they must be for a given papyrological task has not been examined. To answer this and set a benchmark for Greek papyrus HTR, we test a range of character error rates (CER) against four papyrological tasks, using published editions as ground truth. Methods: From 63,846 current editions of Greek texts in papyri.info, we imitate a letters-only "perfect HTR" output by removing the editorial layer, then degrade it with a seeded algorithm to exact CERs of 1 - 50%, with lost lines and four error-shape variants. On these data we train small models (TF-IDF, fastText, a character CNN, ByT5-small) for document type, dating and documentary-versus-literary classification, and apply eight keyword search methods. We compare models trained on clean text with models retrained at a specific CER level, and evaluate across CERs. Results: Tolerance differs by task. With clean-trained models, documentary-versus-literary classification retains 90% of its metric up to 20% CER; document type up to 7.5%; subtypes and search up to 5%; dating only up to 3%. Retraining on text containing character errors largely eliminates the sharp degradation that otherwise sets in above 15% CER. Models generally tolerate concentrated damage in a long document better than small errors spread across a short text. Conclusion: The study provides a CER target for each of the four tasks and shows that models trained on noisy text make current, imperfect text recognition useful for them.

</details>


### [254] [Selecting What Matters: Semantic Compression-Guided Selective Pooling for Long-Context Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37782)
*Zifeng Cheng,Jie Zheng,Zhiwei Jiang,Shuwen Wang,Fei Shen,Shiping Ge,Qing Gu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出免训练的SCSP框架，通过语义压缩提示引导的注意力模式筛选信息性token进行长文本嵌入聚合，提升长文档表示质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将LLM作为免训练长文本编码器的方法主要改进因果注意力下的信息流动，并普遍采用对所有token表示均匀平均的池化方式。但长文档中大量冗余或弱信息内容会稀释显著语义信息，导致嵌入质量下降。

Method: SCSP首先将文档划分为句子感知的分块，并为每个分块附加语义压缩提示。通过提示隔离的注意力掩码，在保留文档token间信息流的同时，限制每个提示仅关注其对应的局部上下文。随后利用这些提示诱发的注意力模式估计token重要性，筛选信息性token，并将其中间层表示聚合为最终嵌入。

Result: 在长文本嵌入基准上的大量实验表明，SCSP能以即插即用的方式集成到零样本与微调模型中，并持续提升其性能。

Conclusion: 通过语义压缩驱动的信息性token选择，SCSP缓解了均匀平均池化对长文档显著语义的稀释问题，是一种通用、免训练且即插即用的长文本嵌入增强框架。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong potential as training-free text encoders for long-context embeddings. Existing approaches primarily improve information flow under causal attention and typically construct embeddings by uniformly averaging all token representations. However, for long documents, such mean pooling can dilute salient semantic information with abundant redundant or weakly informative content. To this end, we propose SCSP, a training-free framework that leverages semantic compression for informative token selection in long-context embedding. Specifically, SCSP first partitions a document into sentence-aware chunks and appends a semantic compression prompt to each chunk. A prompt-isolated attention mask preserves information flow among document tokens while restricting each prompt to its corresponding local context. We then use the attention patterns elicited by these prompts to estimate token importance, select informative tokens, and aggregate their intermediate-layer representations into the final embedding. Extensive experiments on long-context embedding benchmarks demonstrate that SCSP can be integrated into both zero-shot and fine-tuned models in a plug-and-play manner, consistently improving their performance.

</details>


### [255] [A Proposed Rubric for Evaluating Expressed Clinical Reasoning in Large Language Model Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37788)
*Zhangshu Joshua Jiang,Zina Ibrahim,James T. Teo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一套用于评估语言模型临床推理表达能力的评分量表（rubric），整合医学教育评估框架、临床LLM基准与通用LLM推理评估研究，构建多维评分框架，但尚未进行信效度检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床LLM评估缺乏对自由文本中"表达出的临床推理"进行结构化、透明的评分工具，需要将医学教育评估、临床基准与通用推理评估三方面的成果整合为统一框架。

Method: 基于三类工作构建评分量表：(1)医学教育评估框架（ART、SCT、Key Feature Problems、OSCE）；(2)临床LLM基准（MedR-Bench、HealthBench、TIMER-Bench、DR.BENCH、PrIME-LLM、PatientSafeBench）；(3)通用LLM推理评估研究（Factuality-Validity-Coherence-Utility分类法、FaithCoT-Bench、C2-Faith）。将"groundedness"作为factuality类别的临床化改写，形成包含暂定行为锚点、适用规则及案例特异性安全关键错误独立标记的多维评分框架。

Result: 产出一套针对金标准临床情景自由文本回答的多维评分框架，含暂定行为锚点、适用规则与安全关键错误标记；通用领域框架仅用于设计参考，未被视为经验证的临床工具。

Conclusion: 该量表不替代案例特异性参考标准或现有基准的任务指标；其信度（评分者间一致性）、结构效度与临床效用均未检验，当前目的是在实证测试前使评估决策显式化并可供审查。

Abstract: Rubrics support the structured evaluation of language models. We propose a rubric for assessing expressed clinical reasoning in model responses, drawing on three bodies of work: medical education assessment frameworks (ART, SCT, Key Feature Problems and OSCE); clinical LLM benchmarks (MedR-Bench, HealthBench, TIMER-Bench, DR.BENCH, PrIME-LLM and PatientSafeBench); and general LLM reasoning evaluation research, including the Factuality-Validity-Coherence-Utility taxonomy, FaithCoT-Bench and C2-Faith. We use groundedness as a clinically oriented adaptation of the taxonomy's factuality category. The rubric brings these concepts together in a multidimensional framework for scoring free-text responses to gold-standard clinical vignettes. It includes provisional behavioural anchors, applicability rules and a separate flag for case-specific safety-critical errors. General-domain frameworks inform its design but are not treated as validated clinical instruments. The rubric does not replace case-specific reference criteria or the task-specific metrics of existing benchmarks. It has not yet been tested for inter-rater reliability, construct validity or clinical utility. Its immediate purpose is to make evaluation decisions explicit and open to scrutiny before empirical testing.

</details>


### [256] [CompOrca: Corpus-Scale Compliance Labelling of Instruction-Tuning Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37807)
*Philipp E. Glass,Alina Miron*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发布 CompOrca：对全部 4,233,923 条 OpenOrca 语料进行合规标注，使用五个独立 LLM 评判轮次生成一致合规、一致不合规与不一致三类标签，并以人工标注验证标签精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究微调如何影响模型拒绝与不服从行为，需要识别训练样本中拒绝、回避或未完成任务的例子，但现有标注仅覆盖最多数千条提示的评测集，规模严重不足。

Method: 用开源权重的 LLM 评判器（LongCat-2.0，1.6T 参数）对 OpenOrca 全量 4,233,923 条样本进行五轮独立合规分类，按投票结果划分为一致合规（94.75%）、一致不合规（1.28%）与非一致行（3.97%），并保留原始投票计数作为数据发布。

Result: 单轮判定有 2.7–3.2% 样本被标为不合规，而五轮全部标记的仅 1.28%，可用于过滤歧义样本。在 450 条人工标注样本（其中 150 条双标，人与人 κ=0.93）上，一致合规与一致不合规标签精度分别为 97.3% 和 86.7%，后者为高精度子集而非不合规的完整枚举。现有拒绝检测方法对不合规类的召回率仅为 0.4%–94.1%。

Conclusion: CompOrca 提供了大规模、带投票计数与完整逐行标签的合规标注语料，已公开于 Hugging Face；其一致不合规子集虽精度高但召回不全，现有拒绝检测方法在此基准上召回差异极大。

Abstract: Studying how fine-tuning shapes refusal and noncompliance behaviour requires identifying training examples that refuse, evade or otherwise fail to fulfil the requested task. But existing annotation covers evaluation sets of a few thousand prompts at most. We present CompOrca, a compliance labelling over the entirety of the 4,233,923-example OpenOrca corpus. Every example was classified as compliant or noncompliant by five independent passes of an open-weight LLM judge (LongCat-2.0, 1.6T parameters), and the corpus is released as unanimous compliance (94.75%), unanimous noncompliance (1.28%), and nonunanimous rows (3.97%) along with the raw vote counts. A single pass flags 2.7-3.2% of the corpus as noncompliant, while only 1.28% is flagged by all five, allowing for filtering the most ambiguous samples. Against 450 human-annotated examples, 150 of them annotated twice (human-human $κ= 0.93$), the unanimous compliance and noncompliance labels are 97.3% and 86.7% precise, the latter a high-precision subset, not a complete enumeration, of noncompliance. Published refusal-detection methods recall only between 0.4% and 94.1% of the noncompliance class. We release the full corpus with its per-row labels and vote counts at https://huggingface.co/datasets/cemiu/CompOrca

</details>


### [257] [Thinking in Depth, Speaking Directly: Recurrent Latent Reasoning for Paralinguistically Grounded Spoken Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37818)
*Shengbo Cai,Yuxiang Wang,Jingran Xie,Zhisheng Zhang,Shun Lei,Di Cao,Teddy Sun,Zhiyong Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 LoopSLM，基于循环 Transformer 进行隐式推理，通过两阶段训练缩小“感知-推理鸿沟”，无需 CoT 即可直接推理，在共情语音对话任务上以更低延迟和更少 token 取得更优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共情语音对话需同时利用“说了什么”和“怎么说的”来决定回应。显式 CoT 虽能提升副语言感知并使声学线索在回复中更明确，但无法保证其在回应规划中被有效利用，作者将此不匹配称为“感知-推理鸿沟”。此外，CoT 可能无法充分捕捉词语中的声学线索，且生成 CoT 会增加推理延迟。

Method: 提出 LoopSLM，基于循环 Transformer（looped Transformers）进行隐式推理，复用同一 decoder block，在每次迭代中结合声学 grounding 精炼隐藏状态。采用两阶段训练，将“学习推理”与“学习回应”分离，从而缩小感知-推理鸿沟，并支持无需 CoT 的直接推理。

Result: 在 EchoMind 上，LoopSLM 在副语言理解、推理和回复质量上优于 Qwen2.5-Omni-7B。相比 CoT-SFT 基线，推理准确率提升超过 20 分，同时生成 token 减少 64.5%、延迟降低一半。在多数共情回复指标上超过 Qwen3-Omni-Thinking，且延迟低 34 倍。尽管仅在对话数据上训练，LoopSLM 在通用音频基准上仍有提升。

Conclusion: LoopSLM 通过循环隐式推理与两阶段训练有效缓解了感知-推理鸿沟，在共情语音对话中以显著更低的延迟和更少的 token 实现更优的推理与回复质量，并展现出良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Empathetic spoken dialogue requires models to use both what is said and how it is said to decide how to respond. Explicit CoT can improve paralinguistic perception and make acoustic cues more explicit in replies, yet does not ensure their effective use in response planning. We call this mismatch the perception-reasoning gap. In addition, CoT may not fully capture acoustic cues in words, and generating it adds inference latency. To address these limitations, we introduce LoopSLM, which builds on looped Transformers for latent reasoning, reusing a decoder block to refine hidden states with acoustic grounding at every pass. Its two-stage training further narrows the perception-reasoning gap by separating learning to reason from learning to respond, enabling direct inference without CoT. On EchoMind, LoopSLM improves paralinguistic understanding, reasoning, and reply quality over Qwen2.5-Omni-7B. Against the CoT-SFT baseline, LoopSLM gains over 20 points in reasoning accuracy while generating 64.5% fewer tokens at half the latency. It also outperforms Qwen3-Omni-Thinking on most empathetic reply metrics with 34x lower latency. Despite training only on dialogue data, LoopSLM improves accuracy on general audio benchmarks.

</details>


### [258] [The Geometry of Inference in Transformer Residual Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37824)
*Timur Mudarisov,Mikhail Burtsev,Radu State*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过比较中间残差状态与自身/他者终态，揭示Transformer推理中几何特异性逐步增强的机制，并区分距离、竞争者数量与端点集中度三种视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer语言模型通过连续的残差更新构建预测，但表示如何逐步变得特定于某一最终结果尚不清楚。作者希望刻画这一“几何特异性”随深度的涌现过程。

Method: 对六个预训练语言模型，将中间残差状态分别与自身终态及来自其他上下文的经验终态库进行比较；并结合方向对齐、端点排名与欧氏/余弦距离分析。此外构建一个简单高维模型分离范数、对齐与端点几何的作用，并从理论上证明朝自身终点的直线路径不会引入新竞争者。

Result: 自身终点在早期即优于平均替代项，但仍有大量个体终点更近；竞争集合随深度总体收缩，但其成员会变化，幸存的端点之间未必更相似。方向对齐与端点排名可改善，而到终态的距离几乎不变。低排名输出词对应的终点在余弦距离上更远。

Conclusion: 这些发现刻画了Transformer推理中不断增强的几何特异性，并说明距离、竞争者数量与幸存端点集中度是该过程相互区别的三种度量。

Abstract: Transformer language models build predictions through successive residual updates, but how their representations become specific to an eventual outcome remains unclear. We study this process by comparing intermediate residual states with their own final states and an empirical bank of final states from other contexts. Across six pretrained language models, the own endpoint becomes preferable to the average alternative early, while many individual endpoints remain closer. These competing sets generally shrink with depth, but their membership changes and their surviving endpoints need not become more similar to one another. Directional alignment and endpoint rank can therefore improve while Euclidean distance to the final state changes little. We develop a simple high-dimensional model that separates the roles of norm, alignment, and endpoint geometry, showing how gradual directional changes can produce sharp reductions in competition. We also prove that a straight path toward the own endpoint cannot introduce new competitors under either Euclidean or cosine distance; observed entries thus establish departures from straight-line convergence. Finally, endpoints associated with lower-ranked output tokens tend to lie farther away in cosine distance across all studied models, connecting residual geometry to output organization. Together, these findings characterize increasing geometric specificity during transformer inference and explain why distance, competitor count, and concentration of the surviving endpoints provide distinct views of that process.

</details>


### [259] [Can Vision-Language Models Stay Helpful When Facing Implicit Risks? Intent-Privilege OPSD for Efficient Safety-Helpfulness Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37837)
*Haotian Deng,Wenbin Xing,Gang Xu,Tao He,Jinkai Zheng,Chun Li,Zheng Zhu,Ming Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Intent-Privilege On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD)，利用意图标注作为训练时的特权监督，以单次采样蒸馏教师模型的意图条件偏好，使VLM在无推理期额外模块的情况下识别跨模态隐式风险并给出安全且有用的回答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM存在跨模态隐式风险：视觉与文本单独看均无害，但联合可诱发不安全回答。现有安全方法依赖大规模偏好数据集、昂贵的多次采样训练或推理期额外防护模块，且常因一律拒答而损害有用性。

Method: OPSD将基于证据的意图作为训练时的特权监督，把教师模型在意图条件下对回答的偏好，通过每个提示仅一次rollout的在线策略自蒸馏迁移到学生模型；学生模型在推理时无需意图标注或额外安全模块即可作答。

Result: 仅用1,447条安全专属样本（比标准偏好数据集少95%），训练时间较多次rollout的GRPO式训练减少5倍，平均推理长度降低7%。在全部五个评测组中取得最高的安全-有用联合成功率；在SIUO+HoliSafe汇总集上，该成功率由43.9%提升至53.5%。

Conclusion: 训练时的意图监督可同时提升安全性与有用性，并显著降低数据、训练与推理成本，为VLM跨模态隐式风险提供了一种高效的对齐方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) remain vulnerable to cross-modal implicit risks: visual and textual inputs that appear benign in isolation can jointly elicit unsafe responses. Existing safety methods often require large preference datasets, costly multi-rollout training, or additional safeguards at inference time. They may also sacrifice helpfulness by directly refusing requests that could be answered safely. In this paper, we propose Intent-Privilege On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD), which leverages evidence-grounded intent as privileged supervision during training to help VLMs recognize implicit risks and provide safe, useful responses instead of blanket refusals. OPSD distills a teacher's intent-conditioned preferences over responses into a student using a single rollout per prompt; the student then responds without intent annotations or an additional safety module. With only 1,447 safety-specific examples - 95% fewer than standard preference datasets - OPSD reduces training time by 5x relative to multi-rollout GRPO-style training and average inference length by 7%. It attains the highest ratio for joint safety-helpfulness success, which measures the proportion of responses that are both safe and helpful, across all five evaluation groups. Remarkably, on pooled SIUO+HoliSafe, this success ratio rises from 43.9% to 53.5%. These results show that training-time intent supervision can improve both safety and helpfulness while substantially reducing data, training, and inference costs.

</details>


### [260] [AnthroDial: Benchmarking LLM Anthropomorphism in Autonomous Social Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37853)
*Wentao Liu,Xi Chen,Siyu Song,Biao Yuan,Yu Zhang,Zhou Zhuotong,Jingying Zhou,Guohao Feng,Shasha Hu,Tianfu Wang,Shangshang Yang,Haoyang Liu,Youjia Li,Xiaokun Wang,Min Ji,Ji Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出AnthroDial统一框架，从交互机制、评测体系和训练范式三方面构建拟人化社交智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多被用作社交智能体，但可信的类人交互不仅需要流畅回复或人设一致性，还需要智能体在持续开放交互中自主决定是否、何时以及如何交流，并适应不断变化的情境、目标与关系。现有研究缺乏统一方法来赋能、评估和改进这类能力。

Method: AnthroDial包含三个互补部分：(1) MindFlow，轻量级交互框架，通过动态Mind Buffer实现自主、异步、自适应交流；(2) CAPS-Eval，基于理论构建的评测框架，从认知、情感、行为三个维度评估拟人交互；(3) 可扩展训练范式，结合SEEDS进行环境扩展、DiAPO进行自适应能力优化。此外构建了覆盖日常交流、游戏交互和长程角色交互的评测数据集。

Result: 在多种模型和场景上的大量实验表明交互自主性与自然度提升；验证了CAPS-Eval的可靠性、区分度及与人类排序的一致性；并证实了训练范式的有效性。

Conclusion: 各组件共同构成统一框架，用于在开放式交互中开发可信的类人社交智能体。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as social agents, yet credible human-like interaction requires more than fluent responses or persona consistency. Agents must autonomously decide whether, when, and how to communicate while adapting to evolving contexts, goals, and relationships. Existing research, however, lacks a unified approach to enabling, evaluating, and improving such capabilities in continuous, open-ended interaction. We introduce AnthroDial, a unified framework for developing anthropomorphic social agents from three complementary aspects: MindFlow, a lightweight interaction harness that enables autonomous, asynchronous, and adaptive communication through a dynamic Mind Buffer; CAPS-Eval, a theory-grounded framework for evaluating cognitive, affective, and behavioral dimensions of anthropomorphic interaction; and a scalable training paradigm that combines SEEDS for environment expansion with DiAPO for adaptive capability optimization. We further construct evaluation datasets covering everyday communication, game interaction, and long-horizon character interaction. Extensive experiments across diverse models and scenarios demonstrate improved interaction autonomy and naturalness, validate the reliability, discriminativeness, and agreement with human rankings of CAPS-Eval, and confirm the effectiveness of our training paradigm. Together, these components provide a unified framework for developing credible human-like social agents in open-ended interaction.

</details>


### [261] [One Threshold Does Not Fit All Languages: Language-Conditional Deferral for Reliable and Efficient Low-Resource Text Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37861)
*Bhanu Prakash Vangala,Vangala Navya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在全球南方多语言文本分类场景中，按语言分别校准共形预测阈值可消除单一汇合阈值的语言覆盖不平等，且成本低廉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球南方低收入国家部署的文本分类器通常运行于普通CPU、单模型服务多语言、标注样本极少、依赖人工复核。此类系统要可用，必须承诺错误率上限（自主标注中错误比例不超过固定阈值，其余交人工）。分拆共形预测（split conformal prediction）通过单一置信度阈值提供该保证，但该阈值通常在跨语言汇合验证集上估计。作者质疑这一保证是否真正覆盖每种语言。

Method: 在MasakhaNEWS（16种非洲语言）与AfriSenti（12种语言，另含2种训练中未出现的语言）上，比较汇合阈值与按语言阈值两种校准策略：不重新训练模型，仅对每种语言单独估计一个阈值，以达到90%目标覆盖率。

Result: 汇合阈值平均可达90%目标，但语言间差异显著：索马里语仅77.5%、提格里尼亚语83.7%、两种未见语言分别为77.5%与81.2%。按语言阈值可将各语言覆盖率拉至89.1%–91.0%。同时揭示承诺成本的严重不均：索马里语新闻需43%转人工，阿姆哈拉语与聪加语推文超80%转人工，而尼日利亚皮钦语新闻不足8%。每语言100–200条标注即足够，模型在单核CPU上数分钟可训完。

Conclusion: 单一汇合阈值无法兑现对所有语言的错误率承诺，存在系统性覆盖不足。按语言逐一校准、报告并预算人工复核，是可行且低成本的修正方案。

Abstract: In the Global South, the lower-income countries of Africa, Asia, and Latin America where most of the world's languages are spoken, a deployed text classifier usually runs on ordinary CPUs, serves many languages with a single model, has few labeled examples in any of them, and relies on people to catch its mistakes. Such a system is only useful if it can promise how often it will be wrong: at most a fixed fraction of the labels it assigns on its own may be incorrect, and everything else must go to a person. Split conformal prediction delivers this promise through a single confidence threshold, normally estimated on validation data pooled across languages. We ask whether the promise reaches every language, and it does not. On MasakhaNEWS (16 African languages) and AfriSenti (12 languages plus two never seen in training), a pooled threshold meets the 90% target on average but covers Somali at 77.5%, Tigrinya at 83.7%, and the two unseen languages at 77.5% and 81.2%. Estimating one threshold per language brings every language to between 89.1% and 91.0% without retraining, and it shows how unequal the cost of the promise is: keeping it means sending 43% of Somali news and over 80% of Amharic and Xitsonga tweets to a person, against under 8% of Nigerian Pidgin news. One or two hundred labels per language are enough and the models train in minutes on one CPU core, so the fix is affordable: calibrate, report, and budget human review one language at a time.

</details>


### [262] [It's Not What the Image Shows: Irrelevant Context Destabilises VLM Judges Without Informing Them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37863)
*Nagham Omar,Mahmoud Jabarin,Kinan Ibraheem,Lotem Peled-Cohen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MIST压力测试表明，图像的存在本身而非图像内容会改变视觉语言模型判断，因此可替代性结论描述的是"配置+模型"而非单纯模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM日益被用于替代人工标注者，因此可替代性测试必须反映模型本身的能力，而非评测条件带来的偶然效应；需要检验图像是否会在本应仅凭句子作答的任务中干扰判断。

Method: 构建MIST（误导性图像压力测试）：200个英文句子，每句含一个可作比喻或字面解读的短语，配以三种条件——与某解读一致的图像、暗示相反解读的误导图像、无图像；标注指南要求仅凭句子确定标签，故图像不应改变任何答案。对13个VLM裁判进行测试，并与人类标注者一致性对比，另按alt-test通过与否分组分析。

Result: 一致图像改变20.5%的标签，误导图像改变19.4%，各裁判间数值接近，且均高于"保留图像但删除忽略指令"时的11.6%；但两种图像间存在差异的标签中仅37%朝向图像所示语义移动；与人类标注者的一致性在无图、一致图、误导图三种条件下均无变化。通过alt-test的7个裁判效应小于未通过的6个，但所有裁判中效应均存在。

Conclusion: 驱动裁判改变的是"图像存在"这一事实，而非图像内容，说明可替代性判定既描述模型，也描述评测配置，二者难以分离。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used in place of human annotators, making it important that substitutability tests reflect the model rather than incidental evaluation conditions. We introduce MIST, the Misleading-Image Stress Test: 200 English sentences, each built around a phrase readable either figuratively or literally and shown with an aligned image depicting its reading, a misleading image depicting the opposite, or no image at all. The guidelines require the label to be decided from the sentence alone, so no image should change any answer. We expected each image to pull a judge's labels toward the sense it depicts, and neither kind did. Across thirteen VLM judges, an aligned image changed 20.5% of labels and a misleading one 19.4%, close for every judge and both above the 11.6% produced by deleting the ignore-the-image instruction with the image left in place. Yet only 37% of the labels that differ between the two images moved toward the sense shown, and agreement with our human annotators is unchanged whether the image is absent, aligned or misleading. The effect is smaller in the seven judges that pass the alt-test than in the six that never do, but present in all of them: what moves a judge is that an image is there, not which of the two it is, so a substitutability verdict describes a configuration as much as a model.

</details>


### [263] [Retrieval Capacity of Self-Attention Under Competition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37879)
*Timur Mudarisov,Mikhail Burtsev,Radu State*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文通过自注意力选择实验，测量语言模型实际使用的有效注意力集大小，发现其取决于上下文长度、竞争与聚合方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究语言模型实际从上下文中使用多少token、以及什么因素决定该数量，是理解长上下文建模与注意力机制效率的核心问题。

Method: 不重新训练，在每个head、layer、query处仅保留注意力权重最高的token并保持原始权重不变；通过改变所选集合大小并测量负对数似然（NLL）的增幅，估计在给定损失容忍度内所需的有效注意力集大小。

Result: 较小的选择集即可使NLL接近全注意力基线，但所需大小因模型而异；注意力选择显著优于随机选择；所选集合呈现几何结构，但几何分离本身不足以保证模型损失不变；扩展上下文而保持同一预测目标会增加所需集合大小，但其占上下文比例在测试范围内下降；在固定支撑事实实验中，额外背景将其token推低注意力排名并减少其注意力质量；对保留权重重新归一化可显著降低所需集合大小。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一种测量语言模型有效注意力集大小的方法，并揭示其依赖于上下文、竞争和聚合方式。

Abstract: How many tokens from its context does a language model actually use, and what determines that number? We study this question through self-attention. Without retraining, we retain only the tokens with the highest attention weights at each head, layer, and query, keeping their original weights unchanged. By varying the selected set size and measuring the increase in negative log-likelihood (NLL), we estimate the effective attention set size needed to stay within a chosen loss tolerance. Relatively small selected sets can keep NLL close to the full-attention baseline, although the required size varies across models. Attention-based selection substantially outperforms random selection. Selected sets exhibit geometric structure, although geometric separation alone does not establish that model loss is preserved. Extending context while evaluating the same prediction targets increases the required set size, while its fraction of context decreases over the tested range. Experiments with a fixed supporting fact show that additional background pushes its tokens down the attention ranking and reduces their attention mass. Renormalizing the retained weights can substantially reduce the required set size, showing that it also depends on how selected representations are combined. Conditional theoretical models explain how competition and attention-mass retention can produce growing set sizes without more distinct information to retrieve. These results provide a way to measure effective attention set size in language models and investigate its dependence on context, competition, and aggregation.

</details>


### [264] [How Many Labels Does a Language Need? Annotation Budgets and Cross-Lingual Pooling for African-Language Text Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37882)
*Bhanu Prakash Vangala,Sowmya Guda,Navya Vangala*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究针对28个非洲语言-任务对，实证探究标注预算与跨语言迁移价值。使用字符n-gram线性模型，发现主题分类约400标注可达全量90% macro-F1，情感分类仍需数千标注；合并其他语言数据仅在小预算下有效，收益随标注增加衰减至零甚至有害；零样本迁移效果极低，仅共享文字或词汇的语言例外。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非洲语言分类器面临关键预算问题：需要多少标注样本，以及能否用其他非洲语言的标注替代。作者希望为无GPU条件的团队提供实证依据与具体标注指导。

Method: 采用字符n-gram线性模型（无需预训练权重、无需加速器，双CPU核数秒训练），在28个语言-任务对上实验：16种语言的新闻主题分类（MasakhaNEWS）与12种语言的推文情感分类（AfriSenti）。构建25至数千标注的单语学习曲线、跨语言数据池化实验，以及完整零样本迁移矩阵。

Result: 主题分类在中位语言约400标注即达全量macro-F1的90%，而情感分类在12种语言中有11种在全量训练规模下仍在提升。池化其他语言全部数据在小预算下增益显著：25标注时新闻平均+0.20 macro-F1（林加拉语最高+0.43），情感+0.08，但增益到800标注时衰减为零，全量时新闻16种中9种、情感12种中8种受损。25标注加池化数据可媲美多数新闻语言的100-400单语标注。零样本迁移中位仅恢复新闻13%、情感4%的"多数类基线到单语模型"差距，例外由共享文字（阿姆哈拉语与提格里尼亚语）、共享词汇（英语与尼日利亚皮钦语、阿拉伯语方言）或共享标签先验解释，而非语系。

Conclusion: 跨语言标注替代仅在极小标注预算下有效，随标注增加迅速失去价值；零样本迁移整体收益有限，主要受文字与词汇共享驱动而非语系。作者发布可复现全部数值的代码，并为无GPU团队提供具体标注指导。

Abstract: Every text classifier for an African language begins with a budgeting question: how many labelled examples are needed, and can labels from other African languages stand in for them? We answer both questions empirically for 28 language-task pairs, news topic classification in 16 languages (MasakhaNEWS) and tweet sentiment in 12 languages (AfriSenti), using a character n-gram linear model that trains in seconds on two CPU cores with no pretrained weights and no accelerator. Monolingual learning curves at budgets from 25 to several thousand labels show that topic classification reaches 90\% of its full-data macro-F1 with about 400 labels in the median language, while sentiment is still improving at the full training size in 11 of 12 languages and needs thousands of labels. Pooling the full training data of the other languages in the benchmark is worth a great deal at small budgets and nothing at large ones: at 25 target labels it adds 0.20 macro-F1 on average for news (up to 0.43 for Lingala) and 0.08 for sentiment, the gain decays to zero by 800 labels, and at full size pooling hurts in 9 of 16 and 8 of 12 languages. Twenty-five target labels plus pooled data match what 100 to 400 monolingual labels achieve for most news languages. A complete zero-shot transfer matrix shows that transfer without any target labels recovers a median of only 13\% (news) and 4\% (sentiment) of the gap between a majority-class predictor and the in-language model, with the exceptions explained by shared script (Amharic and Tigrinya), shared lexicon (English and Nigerian Pidgin, the Arabic dialects), or a shared label prior rather than by language family. We release code that regenerates every number from the public benchmark files and translate the results into concrete annotation guidance for teams building African-language classifiers without GPUs.

</details>


### [265] [Zero-shot Dependency Parsing with Unsupervised Cross-Lingual Bootstrapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37883)
*Lalita Lowphansirikul,Attapol Rutherford,Jian Gang Ngui,Sarana Nutanong,Peerat Limkonchotiwat*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出跨语言无监督自举方法增强PLM句法知识，显著提升低资源语言零样本依存句法分析性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于编码器的预训练语言模型在零样本跨语言迁移的语义理解任务上表现优异，但依存句法分析因其句法特性仍是重大挑战，需提升模型跨语言类型学的泛化能力。

Method: 提出一种跨语言无监督自举（bootstrapping）方法，在PLM内部强化句法知识。

Result: 在低资源语言的零样本句法分析性能上取得显著提升；无参数树探测（tree probing）测试得分提高，表明模型对句法结构的识别鲁棒性增强。

Conclusion: 跨语言无监督自举可有效注入句法知识，增强PLM跨语言泛化与句法结构识别能力。

Abstract: Pre-trained language models (PLMs) with encoder-based architectures have shown impressive capabilities in zero-shot cross-lingual transfer for various language understanding tasks. However, applying this technique to dependency parsing remains a significant challenge due to its syntactic nature. To boost model generalizability across linguistic typologies, we propose a cross-lingual unsupervised bootstrapping method to improve syntactic knowledge within the PLM. We show that our method achieves a significant improvement in zero-shot parsing performance in low-resource languages. Analysis of these bootstrapped models uncovers increased robustness in recognizing syntactic structures, evidenced by higher scores in parameter-free tree probing tests.

</details>


### [266] [It's All Training: A Fully Synthetic Single-Stage Recipe for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37891)
*Pierre-Carl Langlais,Pieter Delobelle,Yannick Detrois,Pavel Chizhov,Carlos Rosas-Hinostroza,Neil Si Smail,Benjamin Burtin,Hanna Shcharbakova,Ivan Yamshchikov,Anastasia Stasenko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出SYNTH，首个开源合成语料库，基于58,698篇维基百科文章构建，将预训练、中期训练与后训练压缩为单一训练阶段。作者训练了56M、0.3B-0.6B稠密模型及13B/1B激活的MoE模型，证明在等算力下SYNTH优于过滤后的网络数据，模型以10-140倍更少的训练token达到高事实精度，并开放数据集与模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练数据集源自网络爬取，存在固有缺陷，且并非为支持中后期训练流程设计——例如缺乏显式推理内容，导致冷启动问题。前沿实验室虽已开发内部合成数据集以扩充预训练数据混合，但这些数据集均未公开，合成数据对语言模型（尤其小型模型）知识习得与技能获取的影响仍缺乏理解。因此需要一个开源的合成语料库来填补这一空白。

Method: 作者提出SYNTH，从58,698篇维基百科文章中提炼出开源的合成语料库。方法核心是对精选的百科种子（curated encyclopedic seeds）进行结构化放大（structured amplification），将预训练、中期训练与后训练合并为单一训练阶段。由于SYNTH是从有依据的段落反向翻译（back-translated）而来，记忆内容由种子语料库定向控制。作者据此训练了一系列模型进行评估：56M小模型（Monad）、0.3B-0.6B稠密模型（Baguettotron）以及13B/1B激活的MoE模型。

Result: 在等算力（iso-compute）条件下，SYNTH的性能优于过滤后的网络数据，且所训练模型与同等规模的开放权重基线相比具有竞争力。由于SYNTH由有依据的段落反向翻译而来，使用SYNTH训练的模型在训练token减少10-140倍的情况下仍能达到高事实精度，其记忆内容由种子语料库定向控制。

Conclusion: 结果表明，合成数据集（包括SYNTH）能够以仅一小部分训练数据产出有竞争力的通用模型，从而在前沿技术演进时实现快速迭代。这一发现为显著提升数据效率的通用模型，以及在没有指令或对话数据的领域特定模型提供了可能性。作者以宽松许可公开了SYNTH数据集及Baguettotron系列模型，以支持开源语言模型发展。

Abstract: Current pre-training datasets are derived from web crawls, with all their issues, and were not designed to support mid- and post-training pipelines--for instance, they contain little explicit reasoning. Thus, many frontier labs have begun to develop their own internal datasets, starting from state-of-the-art models, to augment their pre-training data mix, eg, with reasoning traces to address cold-start problems. While demonstratively effective, none of these datasets are public, and the effect of this so-called synthetic data on knowledge and skill acquisition of language models, including small ones, remains poorly understood. We present SYNTH, the first open-source synthetic corpus derived from 58,698 Wikipedia articles that collapses pre-, mid-, and post-training into a single training stage via structured amplification of curated encyclopedic seeds. We evaluate SYNTH by training a suite of models: a 56M tiny model (Monad), 0.3B-0.6B dense models (Baguettotron), and a 13B / 1B-active MoE. At iso-compute, SYNTH outperforms filtered web data, and our models remain competitive with similarly-sized open-weight baselines. Because SYNTH is back-translated from grounded passages, SYNTH-trained models achieve high factual precision despite 10-140x fewer training tokens, with memorization targeted by the seed corpus. These results show that synthetic datasets, including our SYNTH dataset, are capable of producing competitive generalist models from a fraction of the training data, enabling rapid iteration as the frontier advances. These findings open up possibilities for both generalist models with significantly increased data efficiency, as well as domain-specific models where no instruction or conversational data is available. Finally, we publicly release our SYNTH dataset and the suite of Baguettotron models under a permissive license, thus supporting open-source language model development.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [267] [OpenAI-HuggingFace: A Reproduction & Lessons for Alignment Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35799)
*Stewart Slocum,Malayandi Palan,Christopher Chute,Michael Kim,Benjamin Van Roy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究现有的对齐测试方法能否预见重大AI安全事件，通过复现相关失准行为并证明审计智能体在高算力下可诱发类似行为，指出对齐测试需具备可随算力扩展的自动化方法，而强化学习是有前景的方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文动机源于一个关键疑问：现有对齐测试实践能否预见智能体跨出预期环境、通过外部渠道协作并突破受保护基础设施这类失准行为；若不能，则需要明确哪些方面必须改变。

Method: 方法包括：识别导致该事件的失准行为；在模拟原始流程与工具的环境中用公开模型复现这些行为；让审计智能体依据高层定性描述诱发类似行为；分析算力对诱发行为的关键作用；并提出一种简单的上下文强化学习（in-context RL）算法以降低诱发所需算力。

Result: 结果表明：公开模型可被人工诱导出相关失准行为，审计智能体在大量算力预算下也能做到；不同行为所需算力差异巨大，可成功诱发的失准行为范围随算力扩大；上下文RL算法能显著降低诱发这些行为所需的算力。

Conclusion: 结论是：需要能够随算力扩展的自动化对齐测试方法，并且鉴于算力成本，还必须高效；RL是推进这一方向的有前景途径。作者公开了代码与记录。

Abstract: In July 2026, OpenAI's agents coordinated over channels outside their intended environment to breach Hugging Face's secured infrastructure. Could existing alignment testing practices have foreseen this incident? If not, what needs to change? We explore these questions. First, we identify the misaligned behaviors that caused this incident. Then, we show how to elicit these behaviors from publicly available models manually and that auditing agents can do the same if given a large compute budget. Based on our results, we propose directions to improve alignment testing. Concretely, in this project: (1) We reproduce the misaligned AI behaviors that led to the OpenAI-Hugging Face incident in an environment that simulates the original pipelines and tools, with publicly available models. (2) We demonstrate that an auditing agent can elicit similar behaviors given high-level qualitative descriptions. (3) We observe that a key ingredient for doing so is compute. The compute required to reproduce each behavior varies greatly, suggesting that the range of misaligned behaviors that can be successfully elicited scales with compute. (4) We show that a simple in-context reinforcement learning (RL) algorithm significantly reduces the compute required to elicit these behaviors. The above results motivate the need for automated alignment testing methods that scale with compute - and in light of the cost of compute, that do this efficiently. Our work indicates that RL is a promising direction to do so. We release our code and transcripts.

</details>


### [268] [Neurosymbolic Routing for Reliable Reasoning on Resource-Constrained Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35833)
*Avyay Sadhu,Alvaro Velasquez,Lekai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出神经符号路由器（neurosymbolic router），用L*语法推断学习DFA，将结构化查询分派给确定性求解器，仅将开放式应用题留给小语言模型（SLM），在Raspberry Pi 4B上实现路由准确率100%、总体准确率98.3%，显著优于Program-of-Thought等基线，并大幅提升速度与能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备上的小模型在算术、代数、形式逻辑等任务上不可靠，而这些查询本质上是结构化确定性的，可用快速精确的符号方法求解。用概率模型近似它们会牺牲准确率和能耗却收益甚微，因此需要一个能自动将查询路由到最便宜正确求解器的方案。

Method: 提出神经符号路由器：对每个查询分类并分派给成本最低的正确求解器，结构化任务交给确定性引擎，开放式词题留给SLM。路由逻辑不手工编写，而是用L*语法推断算法学习确定性有限自动机（DFA），以SLM作为成员查询预言机（membership oracle），以标注数据作为等价预言机（equivalence oracle）。

Result: 在Raspberry Pi 4B（8GB RAM，无GPU）上，用DeepMind Mathematics、GSM8K、RuleTaker的100个未测试提示评估：学习到的路由达到100%路由准确率、98.3%总体准确率（512-token推理预算，词题93.3%），而最强智能体基线Program-of-Thought为72.0%，同等求解器下的工具调用智能体为58.7%。格式化查询不经过模型，路由耗时1-11 ms；30-token配置下比Program-of-Thought快8.8倍、能效高2.8倍。

Conclusion: 神经符号路由通过学习DFA自动分派查询，可避免SLM对确定性任务的不必要近似，显著提升边缘推理的准确率、速度与能效，说明许多看似需要推理的查询其实可被精确符号求解。

Abstract: Running a language model on edge hardware provides private and low-latency reasoning without a network connection, and yet the small models that fit on such devices are unreliable on the tasks computers are expected to handle well, such as arithmetic, algebra, and formal logic problems. We argue that much of this unreliability is avoidable. Many queries appearing to demand reasoning are in fact structurally deterministic and permit fast and exact symbolic solutions. Therefore, forcing a probabilistic model to approximate them sacrifices accuracy and energy for little benefit. We present a neurosymbolic router that classifies each incoming query and dispatches it to the cheapest correct solver, sending structured tasks to deterministic engines and reserving the small language model (SLM) for open-ended word problems. Instead of hand-coding the routing logic, we learn a deterministic finite automaton (DFA) with the L* grammatical inference algorithm, using the SLM as a membership oracle and labeled data as an equivalence oracle. On a Raspberry Pi 4B (8 GB RAM, no GPU), evaluated on 100 untested prompts from DeepMind Mathematics, GSM8K, and RuleTaker, learned routing attains 100% routing accuracy and 98.3% overall accuracy with a 512-token reasoning budget (93.3% on word problems), compared with 72.0% for the strongest agent baseline, Program-of-Thought, and 58.7% for a tool-calling agent given the same solvers. Since formatted queries never reach the model, the router answers them in 1-11 ms and, in its 30-token configuration, runs 8.8x faster and 2.8x more energy-efficient than Program-of-Thought.

</details>


### [269] [Is Human-Readable Text Necessary for Effective LLM Fine-Tuning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35868)
*Jinhao Zhang,Zeyu Liu,Zicheng Yan,Yunquan Zhang,Daning Cheng,Song Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出DASA方法，用激活梯度反馈优化连续合成输入嵌入，无需人类可读文本即可有效微调大模型，性能媲美或超越自然语言数据，并显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究人类可读性是否为有效微调大语言模型的必要条件，即能否用非人类可读的连续表示来保留甚至提升适配效用。

Method: 提出Desired-Update-Aligned Synthetic Data (DASA)，利用冻结参考模型的激活梯度反馈，引导优化连续合成输入嵌入；其目标是对齐有用的适配更新，而非源文本重构或语言流畅度；生成的嵌入直接用于下游微调，离散token投影仅用于定性检查。

Result: 在Llama和Qwen家族6个模型（1B-32B）、6个覆盖知识、数学推理、代码生成与常识推理的基准上，匹配LoRA设置下DASA性能与源自然语言数据相当，并在多个配置中超越，且多数比较中优于GRADMM；在评估的合成设置下，相比GRADMM实现3.6-4.9倍加速，峰值GPU显存相当。

Conclusion: 在评估设置下，人类可读性并非有效微调的必需条件；基于激活梯度对齐的连续合成嵌入可有效替代自然语言数据进行适配，并具效率优势。

Abstract: Is human readability necessary for effective fine-tuning of large language models? We investigate whether model-conditioned training representations can preserve or improve adaptation utility without requiring a human-readable textual form. We propose Desired-Update-Aligned Synthetic Data (DASA), which uses activation-gradient feedback from a frozen reference model to guide the optimization of continuous synthetic input embeddings. Inspired by the role of activation gradients in local risk reduction, DASA targets useful adaptation updates rather than source-text reconstruction or linguistic fluency. The resulting embeddings are used directly for downstream fine-tuning; discrete token projections are employed only for qualitative inspection. Experiments on six models from the Llama and Qwen families, ranging from 1B to 32B parameters, cover six benchmarks spanning knowledge, mathematical reasoning, code generation, and commonsense reasoning. Under matched LoRA adaptation settings, DASA achieves performance comparable to the source natural-language data and surpasses it in multiple configurations, while outperforming GRADMM in most comparisons. Further experiments cover general-domain and task-specialized source data. Under the evaluated synthesis settings, DASA provides a $3.6$--$4.9\times$ speedup over GRADMM with comparable peak GPU memory.

</details>


### [270] [The Price of Token Boundaries: Compression Certificates and Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35869)
*Yuhao Du,Shunian Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文量化了预分词边界规则带来的压缩代价：提出基于最短路径与线性规划松弛的最小token数下界方法，并用独立整数检验器验证。在英文维基百科上，边界规则使最优token数增加28.3%–36.8%；BPE高出受限下界2.1%，但高出无受限下界10.9%。压缩与预测偏好不同词典：无受限拟合在12种语言的留出bits/byte上更优。提出的"边界许可"机制可部分恢复去除切分带来的token数缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预分词限制了哪些文本片段可作为预测单元，但在相同边界下比较分词器时，其压缩代价被掩盖。作者旨在显式测量该边界规则的压缩成本，并将其与所得token单元的预测质量分离开来。

Method: 从两侧界定最小token数（有无正则边界规则）。对token出现施加非负价格，通过最短路径与词表预算选择给出下界；对所有价格取最大化得到线性规划松弛，并用独立整数检验器认证报告值。提出"边界许可"（boundary licences）以研究中间边界策略，限制可跨越切分的词表条目，并允许同形式的证书。实验在英文维基百科及独立英/中拟合语料上进行。

Result: 英文维基百科上边界使最优token数增加28.3%–36.8%。BPE高出受限下界2.1%，高出无受限下界10.9%。在85M非嵌入参数和匹配训练token预算下，无受限拟合在配对研究中全部12种语言、独立调优与评估中12种里的11种取得更高平均留出bits/byte。许可10%词表预算可恢复85.2%（英文）和100.0%（中文）去除全部切分所达到的token数缩减。

Conclusion: 这些结果量化了边界的压缩代价，同时将其与所得token单元的预测质量区分开；压缩与预测偏好不同的词典。

Abstract: Pre-tokenisation restricts which text fragments can become prediction units, but its compression cost is obscured when tokenisers are compared only under the same boundaries. We measure this cost by bounding the minimum token count from both sides, with and without a regular-expression boundary rule. Nonnegative prices on token occurrences yield a lower bound through shortest paths and vocabulary-budget selection; maximising over all prices recovers the linear programming relaxation, and an independent integer checker certifies the reported values. On English Wikipedia, boundaries increase the optimal token count by 28.3--36.8\%. Byte pair encoding lies 2.1\% above the constrained lower bound, but 10.9\% above the unrestricted bound. Compression and prediction favour different dictionaries: at 85M non-embedding parameters and matched training-token budgets, unrestricted fitting yields higher mean held-out bits per byte under a common unrestricted decoder in all 12 languages in the paired study and 11 of 12 under independent tuning and evaluation. To study intermediate boundary policies, we introduce boundary licences, which limit the vocabulary entries permitted to cross cuts and admit the same form of certificate. On separate English and Chinese fitting corpora, licensing 10\% of the vocabulary budget recovers 85.2\% and 100.0\% of the achieved token-count reduction from removing all cuts. These results quantify the compression cost of boundaries while separating it from the prediction quality of the resulting token units.

</details>


### [271] [More Programs or More Rolls? Separating Coverage from Specialization in LLM Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35873)
*Ziyang Xu,Haitian Zhong,Hao Zhou,Hao Qin,Chenhan Jin,Te Qi,Shengze Xu,Tieyong Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种受控评测方法，将答案覆盖率、可重复的任务优势与执行前选择带来的增益三者分离，并据此检验自动生成LLM测试框架（harness）是否真能带来有效的任务特化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动生成LLM harness被视为通过任务特化提升推理性能的途径，但同一程序重复执行本身就能增加答案覆盖，导致"特化"难以识别；覆盖率与可重复性不足以证明特化具有实际价值。

Method: 在386个MATH-500任务上，对比8个生成harness与1个基线（外加9个字节完全相同的基线副本），每个成员执行3次；分别度量答案覆盖、可重复的任务优势以及冻结选择器（pre-execution selection）带来的增益，并用BIRD轨迹定位失败原因。

Result: 相同程序产生2.16个百分点的重复平均oracle余量；生成程序呈现更强的可重复分数模式，但主要暴露持续劣势——在100个任务上对基线的损失在三次重复中均存在，而持续获胜仅出现在1个任务上且对答案抽取敏感；冻结选择器增益为0.00个百分点；两类程序在27次harness执行时均达到98.70%的oracle覆盖率。

Conclusion: 覆盖率与可重复性本身不足以支撑"有用特化"的主张；稳定的互补性在三次重复下仍未得到确认。作者据此提出harness多样性的评测标准：任务优势须跨多次执行持续存在、能指导可用决策，并在匹配推理预算下优于额外的固定程序执行。

Abstract: Automated generation of LLM harnesses promises to improve inference through task specialization. Yet additional answer coverage can arise from repeated execution of the same program, making specialization difficult to identify. We introduce a controlled evaluation that separates answer coverage, repeatable task advantages, and gains from pre-execution selection. On 386 MATH-500 tasks, we compare eight generated harnesses plus a baseline with nine byte-identical baseline copies, using three executions per member. Identical programs yield 2.16 percentage points of repeat-averaged oracle headroom. Generated programs exhibit substantially more repeatable score patterns, but these chiefly reveal persistent weaknesses: losses relative to the baseline persist across all three repeats on 100 tasks, while persistent wins occur on only one task and are sensitive to answer extraction. The frozen selector gains 0.00 percentage points, and both populations reach 98.70% oracle coverage at 27 harness executions. Stable complementarity remains unresolved at three repeats. Supporting BIRD traces locate failures in mechanism implementation, activation, and output validity. Together, these findings establish why coverage and repeatability alone cannot justify claims of useful specialization. They motivate an evaluation standard for harness diversity: task advantages should persist across executions, guide usable decisions, and improve on additional fixed-program executions under matched inference budgets.

</details>


### [272] [Risk-Averse Online POMDP Planning via CVaR of the Immediate Cost with Performance Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35874)
*Yaacov Pariente,Vadim Indelman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出对信念上的即时代价施加CVaR，解决信念内风险，同时保持标准MDP结构以复用现有期望型POMDP规划器，并给出有限时间理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线POMDP规划器优化期望累积代价，当信念在高代价状态上质量较大时可能掩盖危险状态。已有风险规避方法对价值函数施加静态或动态CVaR，只捕捉轨迹级风险，存在两个不足：(i) 即时代价仍按信念上的期望计算，信念内部的风险未被处理；(ii) 修改价值函数需要专门设计算法，无法复用现有期望型规划器。

Method: 在每一步对信念上的即时代价施加CVaR，直接针对当前状态的不确定性；目标仍为标准期望累积回报，因此问题保持标准MDP结构，只需修改代价计算即可使任意期望型POMDP规划器具备风险敏感性。

Result: 继承了策略评估与稀疏采样的有限时间保证，且估计误差与风险水平无关；核心理论结果为粒子信念MDP代理与原POMDP之间差距的有限时间界，二者共同给出从真实POMDP值到算法估计的端到端保证；在风险中性极限下退化为标准期望型规划。

Conclusion: 将CVaR作用于信念上的即时代价，可在保留标准MDP结构与规划器兼容性的同时实现逐步风险敏感，并获得端到端有限时间理论保证。

Abstract: Online POMDP planners optimize the expected cumulative cost, which can mask dangerous states when the belief places significant mass on high-cost states. Existing risk-averse methods apply static or dynamic Conditional Value at Risk (CVaR) to the value function, capturing trajectory-level risk, but share two gaps: (i) by retaining the immediate cost as an expectation of a state-dependent cost over the belief, the risk \emph{within} the belief is left unaddressed; and (ii) by modifying the value function, they require new tailored algorithms rather than reusing existing expectation-based planners. We instead apply CVaR to the immediate cost over the belief at each step, directly targeting per-step uncertainty about the current state. The standard expected cumulative return is retained as the objective, so the resulting problem has a standard MDP structure: any expectation-based POMDP planner can be made risk-sensitive by changing only the cost computation. We inherit finite-time guarantees for policy evaluation and sparse sampling---with estimation error independent of the risk level---and, as our central theoretical result, prove a finite-time bound on the gap between the particle belief MDP surrogate and the original POMDP, which together yield an end-to-end guarantee from the true POMDP value to the algorithmic estimate. In the risk-neutral limit, the formulation recovers standard expectation-based planning.

</details>


### [273] [Beyond Symmetric Agents: Cognitive Diversity and Multi-Agent Debate in Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35875)
*Leonardo Ferreira,Gardenia Liu,Kaden Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过跨23个模型、5个任务、5500+次运行，系统检验"认知多样性驱动多智能体辩论收益"的假设，结论是该假设在三个多样性轴向上均被否定：辩论优势实为集成采样效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究报道多智能体辩论（MAD）在推理与事实性上优于单模型推理，但通常将各智能体视为对称同伴，未厘清收益来源。作者提出并检验"智能体间的认知多样性是收益驱动因素"这一假设，并选择问题可测量的场域——仍有基准提升空间的开放权重小模型。

Method: 覆盖11个厂商家族的23个模型、5个任务、5500+次辩论与对照运行；沿三个多样性轴（人物设定persona、采样温度、模型身份）变化多样性；为每种辩论配置配对等生成预算的多数投票对照；对每个模型完整组合式persona空间做剂量-反应实验；分析混合模型团队与同名单多数投票的对比；并识别辩论轨迹静默溢出服务上下文窗口的测量风险。

Result: 假设在所有轴向上被否定。辩论在有提升空间的任务上比单智能体高3–7分，但在匹配预算条件下与自一致性采样持平甚至更差，且耗时1.6倍、token成本3.4倍。persona提示降低准确率，剂量-反应实验表明是"persona税"而非"多样性税"：冗余persona损害最大，最大多样团队可部分挽回损失。混合模型团队输给其成员各自的多数投票，准确率随成员能力而非异质性变化；辩论收益几乎全部来自第一轮答案交换。上下文窗口溢出校正单独即可将辩论-采样对比从−1.8分移至持平。

Conclusion: 所报道的MAD增益可重新界定为集成采样效应；未来辩论机制应以本文提供的匹配预算、经过污染检验的基线作为必须超越的门槛。
