Abstract: Multi-agent debate (MAD) reportedly improves reasoning and factuality over single-model inference, but prior work treats agents as symmetric peers, leaving open what drives the gains. We test the hypothesis that cognitive diversity among agents is the driver, in the setting where the question is still measurable: small open-weight models with benchmark headroom. Across 23 models from eleven vendor families, five tasks, and 5,500+ debate and control runs, we vary diversity along three axes - personas, sampling temperature, and model identity - pairing every debate configuration with a generation-budget-matched majority-vote control. The hypothesis is rejected on every axis. Debate beats single-agent inference (3--7 points where tasks have headroom) but at matched budget conditions it ties or even loses to self-consistency sampling at 1.6$\times$ the wall-clock and 3.4$\times$ the token cost. Persona prompting reduces accuracy and a dose-response experiment over each model's full combinatorial persona space shows the cost is a persona tax, not a diversity tax: redundant personas hurt most, while maximally-diverse teams recover part of the loss. Furthermore, mixed-model teams lose to majority votes over their own rosters, with accuracy tracking member capability rather than heterogeneity, and nearly all of debate's benefit comes from the first exchange of answers. We further identify a pervasive measurement hazard in which debate transcripts silently overflow serving context windows, whose correction alone moves our debate-versus-sampling comparison from $-1.8$ points to parity. Our results recast reported MAD gains as an ensemble-sampling effect and provide the budget-matched, contamination-checked baseline bar that future debate mechanisms should be required to clear.

</details>


### [274] [Representational Simplicity and Circuit Size Dissociate in a Threshold-Dependent Way: A Controlled Test via Adversarial Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35890)
*Adam Elimadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过对抗训练对照实验，检验表征/归因简洁性是否预示更小、更可解的因果电路，发现二者关系是"情形依赖"的：鲁棒模型更具SAE可分解性、任务归因中启用更少SAE特征，但在高保真度（90%、95%）下反而需要更少边，而低保真度（<85%）下标准模型领先或持平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏自编码器可分解性与集中式特征归因日益被视为模型计算更易逆向工程的证据，但表征与归因上的"整洁性"是否真能预测更小或更易处理的因果电路，仍是未解问题。作者希望直接检验这一假设，并指出这是首个在电路层面控制性地检验"表征/归因简洁性是否转化为因果简洁性"的实证研究。

Method: 以对抗训练作为控制工具（它可稳定重塑内部表征，但其本身不构成对电路规模的检验），从同一GPT-2 Small预训练检查点出发，施加匹配的标准与对抗持续训练；要求两种条件均保持间接宾语识别（IOI）任务能力并通过独立鲁棒性验证后才比较机制。随后沿三条互补轴进行比较：(1) SAE可分解性；(2) SAE特征在任务归因中的参与度；(3) 从原始计算图中恢复的忠实电路规模（逆向工程复杂度，即在固定忠实度下恢复模型行为所需的因果结构）。

Result: 鲁棒模型更具SAE可分解性，且在任务归因中启用更少的SAE特征。电路规模呈"情形依赖"：在能力匹配的IOI任务上，当忠实度低于85%时标准模型领先或持平；但在高忠实度（90%、95%）下，鲁棒模型需要显著更少的边。该模式在主实验对上确立，表征趋势在七点扫描和第二语料上具有泛化性。

Conclusion: 表征与归因简洁性并不能单向地预测因果电路更小：电路规模依赖忠实度阈值。高忠实度下鲁棒模型电路更小，低忠实度下则无优势甚至更差，说明"更可分解/归因更集中"不等于"因果电路更简单"，逆向工程复杂度需在固定忠实度水平下评估。

Abstract: Sparse-autoencoder decomposability and concentrated feature attribution are increasingly treated as evidence that a model's computation is easier to reverse-engineer. Whether this representational and attributional cleanliness actually predicts a smaller or more tractable causal circuit remains an open question. We test this directly using adversarial training as a controlled instrument: it reliably reshapes internal representations, but this alone does not constitute a test of circuit size. We investigate this question through reverse-engineering complexity: the causal structure required to recover a model's behavior at a fixed level of faithfulness. To our knowledge, this is the first controlled empirical test of whether representational or attributional simplicity translates into causal simplicity at the circuit level. Starting from the same pretrained GPT-2 Small checkpoint, we apply matched standard and adversarial continual training, requiring both conditions to retain competence on indirect object identification and pass independent robustness verification before comparing mechanisms. We then compare the models along three complementary axes: sparse-autoencoder decomposability, SAE feature engagement in task attribution, and the size of faithful circuits recovered from the raw computational graph. The robust model is more SAE-decomposable and engages fewer SAE features in task attribution. Circuit size is regime-dependent: on competence-matched IOI, standard leads or ties below 85% faithfulness, but robust needs substantially fewer edges at high faithfulness (90%, 95%), a pattern established on the primary pair while representational trends generalize across a seven-point sweep and a second corpus.

</details>


### [275] [Self-discovering RL in the Era of Experience: Is Learning History an Asset or a Burden?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35897)
*Haomin Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对自我发现的强化学习规则 Disco103 进行首个因果机制审计，围绕"经验时代"五大支柱，通过固定、冻结、移植循环状态等干预手段，揭示历史状态在学习中的双重作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归自我改进（RSI）与通用智能研究存在宏观语言模型扩展与"经验时代"交互驱动原则的分歧，但任何自我改进架构最终依赖底层优化引擎。若通用智能需从具身交互中学习，RL更新规则本身必须具备累积适应能力。已有算法自发现成果 Disco103 在基准上超越 PPO 达到 SOTA，但其内部更新机制仍是不透明的黑箱，缺乏机制层面的检验。

Method: 对自我发现的 RL 规则开展首个因果机制审计，结构围绕"经验时代"五大支柱：扩展视界、具身奖励尺度、持续数据流、生命周期内变化、探索深度。方法上通过外科式操作——固定（pinning）、冻结（freezing）与移植（transplanting）循环状态，同时保持元参数不变，检验学习历史何时成为资产、何时成为负担。

Result: 三项发现：（1）循环历史主动扩展可用奖励尺度，在零固定条件下维持六十年窗口，而非三年。（2）将历史内容与其维持机制解耦后，发现不匹配历史的惩罚源于持续钳制；允许导入状态自然演化可减轻该负担。（3）在环境变化下，控制回放保留会反转相对于 DQN 的表观适应优势，说明外部数据更替可能混淆内部可塑性。结论经验证能力阈值并移植至第二种规则（OPEN）。

Conclusion: 该工作将宏观 RSI 愿景扎根于微观学习动态，为下一代自演化 RL 算法建立了基础性审计标准。

Abstract: The pursuit of recursive self-improvement (RSI) toward general intelligence is divided between macro-level language model scaling and the interaction-driven principles of "Era of Experience". Yet, any self-improving architecture ultimately rests upon its underlying optimization engine: if general intelligence requires learning from grounded interaction, the reinforcement learning (RL) update rule itself must be capable of cumulative adaptation. While algorithm self-discovery has produced Disco103 that surpassed PPO to achieve SOTA benchmark performance -- its internal update machinery remains an uninspected black box. We present the first causal mechanistic audit of a self-discovered RL rule, structured directly around the five pillars of the Era of Experience: extended horizon, grounded reward scales, continuing streams, within-lifetime change, and exploration depth. By surgically pinning, freezing, and transplanting recurrent states while holding meta-parameters fixed, we test when learning history acts as an asset or a burden. Three findings organize the audit: (1) Recurrent history actively expands usable reward scales, sustaining a six-decade window versus three under zero-pinning. (2) Decoupling historical content from its maintenance reveals that the penalty of mismatched history stems from perpetual clamping; allowing imported state to evolve naturally attenuates this burden. (3) Under environmental change, controlling replay retention reverses the apparent adaptation advantage over DQN, demonstrating that external data turnover can confound internal plasticity. Validated through capability thresholds and ported to a second rule (OPEN), this work grounds macro-RSI ambitions in micro-level learning dynamics, establishing a foundational audit standard for next-generation, self-evolving RL algorithms.

</details>


### [276] [Grab a Coffee: Future-Aware Guidance for Discrete Diffusion with Compiled Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35924)
*Hua,Xu,Dongxin Li,Gwen Yidou-Weng,Guy Van den Broeck,Wei Wang,Anji Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: COFFEE 是一个即插即用的离散扩散引导框架，通过分离序列依赖与目标函数，避免了对未解决位置组合的指数级枚举，从而在不重训练扩散模型的情况下实现序列级偏好引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型通过并行解析多个 token 来生成序列，比从左到右生成更灵活。但用序列级目标引导该过程很困难：某个未解决 token 的价值取决于它能与其他 token 组成的高奖励序列，枚举所有补全会使引导计算随未解决位置数呈指数增长。

Method: COFFEE 将序列依赖与目标分离。每个扩散步中，一个无目标载体（target-free carrier）吸收去噪器预测的边缘 token 分布，构建未解决 token 的联合模型；同时一个编译的有限状态模型（compiled finite-state model）记录这些 token 组合如何影响序列级偏好。通过配对二者状态，COFFEE 将全局偏好迁移到未解决位置，并采样干净重建，无需重训练扩散模型。该框架同时支持显式硬约束与学习到的软目标。

Result: 在多个符号、语言和生物学基准上评估 COFFEE，取得了强控制结果，并呈现随任务而变的质量与多样性权衡。

Conclusion: COFFEE 将目标从仅用于评估转为可用于推理，把联合条件化、补全加权引导和基于优化的约束引入预训练神经生成，展示了神经符号方法在扩散引导中的潜力。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models generate sequences by iteratively resolving multiple tokens in parallel, offering a flexible alternative to left-to-right generation. However, guiding this process with a sequence-level objective is difficult because the value of one unresolved token depends on the other tokens with which it can form a high-reward sequence. Enumerating all such completions makes the whole guidance computation grow exponentially with the number of unresolved positions. We introduce COFFEE, a plug-and-play framework that avoids this enumeration by separating sequence dependence from the objective. At each diffusion step, a target-free carrier absorbs the marginal token distributions predicted by the denoiser to construct a joint model over the unresolved tokens, while a compiled finite-state model records how their combinations affect the sequence-level preference. Pairing their states allows COFFEE to transfer global preferences to unresolved positions and sample a clean reconstruction without retraining the diffusion model. The same framework supports explicit hard constraints and learned soft objectives. We evaluate COFFEE across multiple symbolic, language, and biological benchmarks, where it achieves strong control results with task-dependent quality and diversity trade-offs. By making objectives available to inference rather than only evaluation, COFFEE brings joint conditioning, completion-weighted guidance, and optimization-based constraints into pretrained neural generation, showing the potential of neural-symbolic methods in diffusion guidance.

</details>


### [277] [Right Words, Wrong Moment: A Clinician-Grounded Analysis of Distress in 19,930 Conversations between Young People and ChatGPT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35953)
*Marx Wang,Ella Zhang,Cameron Tan,Andrea Mock,Songling Ngo,Zijing Wang,Robert Wolfe,Shirin Amouei,Rachel A. Hanebutt,Desmond C. Ong,Caroline Figueroa,Katie Davis,Anind K. Dey,Alexis Hiniker*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究通过1.99万条对话与158名年轻人问卷，结合10名临床医生对5个痛苦情境对话的评审，发现痛苦者与ChatGPT的情感投入和行为改变更高，ChatGPT倾向夸张回应与过度行动建议，并据此提出三阶段设计指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 年轻人日益在心理困扰时求助ChatGPT等通用对话智能体，但其在情绪危机情境中的回应质量与安全性尚缺乏实证与临床视角的评估。

Method: 混合方法研究：收集158名18-25岁年轻人的19,930条ChatGPT对话及问卷数据；筛选出5个反映用户痛苦的示例对话；邀请10名临床医生对这些对话进行评审。

Result: 处于痛苦状态的参与者报告了更高的情感投入和更强的行为改变；在急性痛苦时，ChatGPT迅速给出过度戏剧化的回应与过多行动导向建议；临床医生认可其可及性与部分措辞，但识别出七类过程性失误，如过早跳至解决方案。

Conclusion: 基于临床医生反馈，提出三阶段设计指南：1）询问安全性；2）降低强度以恢复情绪调节；3）在不表示认同的前提下探索其担忧。

Abstract: Young people increasingly turn to General-Purpose Conversational Agents (GPCAs), such as ChatGPT, in moments of distress. We examine young adults' (ages 18-25) experiences using ChatGPT. We first collected 19,930 ChatGPT conversations and survey data from 158 young adults. We then selected five example conversations reflecting user distress. Finally, we asked ten clinicians to review those five conversations. We found distressed participants reported greater emotional engagement with ChatGPT and greater behavioral change from using it than their peers. When they turned to ChatGPT in moments of acute distress, ChatGPT was quick to give overly dramatic responses and excessive action-oriented suggestions. Clinicians endorsed ChatGPT's availability and much of its wording, but identified seven process failures, such as prematurely jumping to solutions. We translated clinicians' feedback into design guidelines following three stages: 1) asking about safety, 2) de-escalating intensity to restore emotional regulation, and 3) exploring concerns without agreeing with them.

</details>


### [278] [SAGE: A Statistical Acceptance Gate for Self-Evolving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36043)
*Yihao Wang,Linhan Xia,Rui Liu,Zhaofeng Zhang,Hongyu Wu,Yang Yang,Jinglu He,Yu Guo,Kai Lei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出统计接受门控SAGE，用逐项配对比较与单侧配对检验替代“验证集总分提升即接受”的朴素规则，显著降低自进化智能体的性能回退率并提升最终得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大模型的智能体通过编辑持久化技能文档实现自进化，该循环包含优化器（提出编辑）与门控（接受或拒绝编辑）两步。已有工作聚焦优化器，而门控仍采用朴素规则：只要聚合验证分数提升就接受编辑。该规则存在两个缺陷：一是会引入永久性回退，因为编辑可能抬高均值却破坏技能原本已解决的条目；二是易受“优化器诅咒”影响，因为在有限且带噪声的验证集上观测到的最优分数存在向上偏差。

Method: 提出自进化智能体的统计接受门控（SAGE），包含两项设计：其一为逐项配对比较，在同一批验证条目上评估当前技能与编辑后技能，从而暴露被聚合分数掩盖的回退，并对回退施加非对称惩罚；其二为单侧配对检验，仅当编辑的收益相对损失在统计上可靠时才提交该编辑，否则弃权。SAGE 是标准门控的保守精化，在边界设定下可精确退化为基线。

Result: 在等预算协议下，覆盖5个基准与4个主干大模型：20种设置中，SAGE在19种中降低了回退率，剩余1种与基线持平；例如在DeepSeek-V4上，LiveMath由36.5%降至0%，OfficeQA由42.8%降至0%。同时SAGE在全部20种设置中取得最高最终分数，LiveMath由34.15提升至48.78。

Conclusion: 聚合验证分数提升不足以作为接受编辑的判据，逐项配对比较与统计显著性检验能有效过滤不可靠收益及以破坏已解条目为代价的收益。SAGE仅提交基线编辑的子集，作为保守精化在降低回退率与提升最终性能上均取得一致优势。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based agents increasingly self-evolve by editing a persistent skill document that encodes their workflow, tool-use rules, and decision logic. This loop has two steps, an optimizer that proposes a candidate edit and a gate that accepts or rejects it. Prior work has concentrated on the optimizer, while the gate still follows a naive rule that keeps any edit which improves an aggregate validation score. We show that this rule fails in two ways. First, it admits permanent regressions, since an edit can raise the average while breaking items the skill already solves. Second, it is vulnerable to the Optimizer's Curse, since the best observed score on a finite and noisy validation set is upward biased. To solve the above two limitations, we propose a statistical acceptance gate for self-evolving agents (SAGE). Compared with previous work, SAGE has two contributions. First, SAGE proposes a per-item paired comparison that evaluates the current skill and the edited skill on identical validation items, which exposes regressions that an aggregate score hides and penalizes them asymmetrically. Second, SAGE also employs a one-sided paired test that commits an edit only when its wins are statistically reliable against its losses, and it abstains otherwise. SAGE is a conservative refinement of the standard gate that recovers the baseline exactly at a boundary setting. It commits only a subset of the baseline's edits, filtering out those whose gains are unreliable or purchased by breaking already-solved items. Across five benchmarks and four backbone LLMs under an equal-budget protocol, SAGE lowers the regression rate in 19 of 20 settings and matches the baseline in the remaining one, for example from 36.5% to 0% on LiveMath and from 42.8% to 0% on OfficeQA with DeepSeek-V4. SAGE also attains the highest final score in all 20 settings, raising LiveMath from 34.15 to 48.78.

</details>


### [279] [PowerZooJax: A JAX-based Power System Benchmark for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36052)
*Zhanhua Pan,Xiao Liu,Zhilong Cao,Jianhong Wang,Dawei Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PowerZooJax是一个基于JAX的GPU加速强化学习基准套件，涵盖电力系统五个约束MDP任务，实现全流程GPU训练评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力系统运行是安全关键的序贯决策问题，是强化学习的理想试验床；但现有RL环境范围狭窄，且受限于CPU仿真流程，难以进行大规模评估。

Method: 将潮流计算、经济调度、市场出清和设备动态重写为JAX计算图，使整个训练与评估循环运行在GPU上，构建包含发电、输电、配电、分布式能源和数据中心微电网五个约束MDP任务的基准套件。

Result: 实验显示相较CPU仿真获得显著加速，并实现了对策略回报、安全违规及分布外压力条件的标准化评估。

Conclusion: PowerZooJax为电力系统强化学习提供了可扩展、GPU加速的开源基准，支持大规模标准化评估。代码已开源。

Abstract: Power system operation is a safety-critical sequential decision-making problem, making it a natural testbed for reinforcement learning (RL). However, existing RL environments for power systems are often narrow in scope and computationally limited by CPU-based simulation workflows, making large-scale evaluation difficult. We introduce PowerZooJax, a JAX-based benchmark suite for RL in power system operation. It provides five constrained Markov decision process tasks spanning generation, transmission, distribution, distributed energy resources, and data center microgrid. By rewriting power flow, economic dispatch, market clearing, and device dynamics as JAX computation graphs, PowerZooJax keeps the entire training and evaluation loop on the GPU. Experiments show substantial speedups over CPU-based simulations and demonstrate standardized evaluation of policy returns, safety violations, and out-of-distribution stress conditions. Our open-source benchmark is available at: https://github.com/powerzoojax/PowerZooJax.

</details>


### [280] [GeoWind2Plan: Mission-Time 3D Urban Wind Prediction for Energy-Efficient UAV Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36056)
*Shaoxiang Qin,Yucheng Zhao,Fuyuan Lyu,Di Zhou,Jiachen Yao,Xue Liu,Anima Anandkumar,Liangzhu Leon Wang,Xiongye Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出GeoWind2Plan框架，将建筑几何转为参考风坐标系，用几何条件神经算子预测任务相关3D风场并拼接成可查询风场，结合物理能量模型做路径与速度优化，3秒内完成城市风推断（CFD约8小时），在CFD评估下节能达4.5%–12.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市低空飞行中建筑将环境风重塑为空间变化的三维流场，使无人机能耗不仅取决于路径长度，还取决于局部风暴露；但任务规划时通常缺乏建筑分辨的风场信息。CFD虽可生成高保真城市流场，却绑定固定入流边界条件且耗时数小时至数天，而城市无人机任务通常仅持续数分钟至数十分钟，两者时间尺度不兼容。因此需要一种能在任务时刻快速获取可用三维城市风信息的方法。

Method: GeoWind2Plan为几何—风—规划一体化框架，输入仅为背景风矢量、三维建筑几何和起止点对。流程为：将建筑几何变换到参考风坐标系；用局部化的几何条件神经算子预测任务相关三维风斑块；将其拼接为可查询的局部风场；再用具有物理依据的无人机能量模型优化可行三维路径与速度剖面。方法不以CFD级完美重建为目标，而面向决策有用的风预测：用预测风规划轨迹，再用高保真CFD风评估。

Result: 在留出城市区域、风速与任务风向工况下，GeoWind2Plan约3秒完成走廊局部化风推断，CFD约需8小时。CFD评估显示，相比风无关规划，GeoWind2Plan规划轨迹在顺风、逆风、侧风任务中分别节能6.9%、12.7%、4.5%，分别达到CFD参考节能量的87.9%、85.7%、75.0%。

Conclusion: 快速的走廊局部化三维城市风预测可使风感知的无人机能量规划在任务时刻变得可行，说明面向决策的风预测精度已足以支撑实际节能规划。

Abstract: In urban low-altitude flight, buildings reshape ambient wind into spatially varying 3D flow, making unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) energy depend on local wind exposure as well as path length. However, building-resolved wind information is rarely available when a mission must be planned. Computational fluid dynamics (CFD) can produce high-fidelity urban flow fields, but each simulation is tied to a fixed inflow boundary condition and can take hours to days, which is incompatible with urban UAV missions that typically last minutes to tens of minutes. We present GeoWind2Plan, a geometry-to-wind-to-planning framework for mission-time 3D urban wind prediction and energy-efficient UAV planning. Given only a background wind vector, 3D building geometry, and a start-goal pair, GeoWind2Plan transforms the building geometry into a reference-wind frame, predicts mission-relevant 3D wind patches with a localized geometry-conditioned neural operator, stitches them into a queryable local wind field, and optimizes a feasible 3D path and speed profile using a physically grounded UAV energy model. Rather than pursuing CFD-perfect reconstruction, GeoWind2Plan targets decision-useful wind prediction: trajectories are planned with predicted wind and evaluated under high-fidelity CFD wind. Across held-out urban domains, wind speeds, and mission wind-angle regimes, GeoWind2Plan performs corridor-localized wind inference in about 3 seconds, compared with roughly 8 hours for CFD. Under CFD evaluation, trajectories planned with GeoWind2Plan reduce energy by 6.9%, 12.7%, and 4.5% in tailwind, headwind, and crosswind missions relative to wind-agnostic planning, recovering 87.9%, 85.7%, and 75.0% of CFD-reference savings. These results show that fast, corridor-localized 3D urban wind prediction can make wind-aware UAV energy planning practical at mission time.

</details>


### [281] [Mirror-Score: Calibrated, Inference-only Scoring Exposes the Limits of Sequence-compatibility Ranking in D-peptide Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36057)
*Jiada Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出Mirror-Score——面向异手性D-肽/L-蛋白复合物的校准式纯推理打分框架，并发布涵盖4个靶标家族、31个晶体复合物的公开基准。研究证明原始ProteinMPNN NLL不能作为可靠的亲和力排序指标，而Boltz-2镜像空间共折叠的界面pLDDT在病毒入侵家族上表现出结构级Spearman rho=0.90的方向一致性，但跨家族校准不迁移，故建议采用家族匹配校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: D-肽兼具抗蛋白酶降解与高靶标特异性，但其计算设计仍不成熟。Mirror-Peptidizer虽提出镜像筛选流程，然而其依赖的原始ProteinMPNN NLL排序未与实测亲和力做验证，9个MDM2设计中仅4个可检测结合，缺乏可靠的打分与基准。

Method: 构建Mirror-Score校准式、仅推理的打分框架，针对异手性D-肽/L-蛋白复合物；建立含4个靶标家族、31个晶体复合物（其中18个有文献验证亲和力）的公开基准；评估原始ProteinMPNN NLL的排序能力，并引入Boltz-2镜像空间共折叠置信度（界面pLDDT）作为替代打分；采用结构级留一法Spearman相关分析，并考察跨家族校准迁移性。

Result: 原始ProteinMPNN NLL并非有效的亲和力排序器：汇总Spearman相关仅0.19，且在MDM2/CHIP（+0.62）与gp41（-0.70）间相关性方向相反。在完整病毒入侵家族（7个结构、3条肽）上，界面pLDDT达到结构级留一法Spearman rho=0.90（p=0.006）并正确排列全部三条肽的亲和力，而NLL失败（rho=0.18）。因仅含三个独立化学型，该结果仅表明方向一致性而非统计验证的预测器。跨家族校准在当前样本量下不迁移。

Conclusion: 原始ProteinMPNN NLL不宜用作D-肽亲和力排序；Boltz-2镜像空间界面pLDDT在家族内展现出方向一致的排序潜力，但受限于化学型数量尚不能确认为统计验证的预测器，家族匹配校准是当前可行的部署模式。作者进一步给出针对铜绿假单胞菌抗药靶标LasR与LecB的前瞻性设计协议（含镜像结构、配体衍生热点图、扩散模型可用输入及Mirror-Score排序），代码、基准数据、结构与分析脚本均已开源。

Abstract: D-peptides combine protease resistance with high target specificity, but computational design of D-peptide binders remains immature. Mirror-Peptidizer introduced an in silico mirror-image screening pipeline using target reflection, backbone generation, and ProteinMPNN sequence design, but its raw ProteinMPNN negative log-likelihood (NLL) ranking was not validated against measured affinities, and only 4 of 9 tested MDM2 designs bound detectably. We introduce Mirror-Score, a calibrated, inference-only scoring framework for heterochiral D-peptide/L-protein complexes, and a public benchmark of 31 crystal complexes across four target families, including 18 with literature-verified affinities. Raw ProteinMPNN NLL is not a valid affinity ranker: its pooled Spearman correlation with affinity is 0.19, and correlations reverse between MDM2/CHIP (+0.62) and gp41 (-0.70). We therefore evaluate Boltz-2 mirror-space cofolding confidence. For the complete viral-entry family (7 structures representing 3 peptides), interface predicted local distance difference test (pLDDT) achieves structure-level leave-one-out Spearman rho = 0.90 (p = 0.006) and correctly orders all three peptides by affinity, whereas NLL fails (structure-level rho = 0.18). Because only three independent chemotypes are represented, this result indicates directional consistency rather than a statistically validated predictor. Cross-family calibration does not transfer at current sample sizes, supporting family-matched calibration as the practical deployment mode. We also specify a prospective design protocol for the antimicrobial-resistance targets LasR and LecB from Pseudomonas aeruginosa, including mirrored structures, ligand-derived hotspot maps, diffusion-model-ready inputs, and Mirror-Score ranking. Code, benchmark data, structures, and analysis scripts are openly available at https://github.com/Jiadalee/Mirror-Score.

</details>


### [282] [SMat-Attention: Structured Long-Context Sequence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36062)
*Emile Anand,Abdullah Ateyeh,Archer Wang,Marin Soljačić*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出结构化矩阵注意力（SMat-Attention），用VC维$d$作为可调结构旋钮，统一softmax注意力与线性注意力两种范式，并给出兼具硬件效率的分块前后向算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文序列建模存在根本性权衡：softmax注意力具备灵活的词元级交互但计算量为二次；线性注意力训练线性、解码常数时间，但将历史压缩为固定大小状态，牺牲了交互灵活性。作者希望用一个可调的结构性度量把两种极端范式连接起来。

Method: 引入带结构化长程路由的因果掩码族，其行支撑的VC维为$d$：$d=1$退化为标准因果掩码，$d$增大则允许更丰富的子集路由。给出分块（chunkwise）前向与反向算法以保证硬件效率。将该框架扩展到Mamba-2和Gated DeltaNet，使用带top-$k$查询读取的可学习路由。

Result: 长度为$T$的序列，硬路由构造在工作量上为$O(T^{2-3/d}+T)$，尽管掩码是稠密的；在固定视野流式场景中，越过远端前缀后每词元解码为常数时间，需缓存$O(T^{1-1/d})$个状态。子集路由与规则辅助多键检索实验验证了路由表达能力；Mamba-2与Gated DeltaNet扩展保持次二次预填充，在多个设置下提升召回精度，小规模语言建模性能相当。

Conclusion: SMat-Attention将VC维变成一个显式旋钮，统一调控访问模式复杂度、预填充开销与解码内存，在softmax注意力与线性注意力之间提供了连续可调的结构化谱系，并在检索与语言建模任务上展示了可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Long-context sequence models face a fundamental tradeoff: softmax attention uses flexible token-level interactions at quadratic cost, whereas linear attention obtains linear-time training and constant-time decoding by compressing history into a fixed-size state. In this work, we ask whether we can connect these regimes through a tunable notion of structure. To this end, we introduce Structured Matrix Attention (SMat-Attention) via a family of causal masks with structured long-range routing whose row supports have VC-dimension $d$. In our construction, $d=1$ recovers the standard causal mask, and increasing $d$ permits richer subset-routing patterns. We give chunkwise forward and backward algorithms to enable hardware-efficiency. For sequences of length $T$, the hard-routing construction takes $O(T^{2-3/d}+T)$ work, despite the mask being dense, for our prescribed family. In fixed-horizon streaming, decoding after the distant prefix takes constant time per token using $O(T^{1-1/d})$ cached states. SMat-Attention therefore makes VC-dimension an explicit knob governing access-pattern complexity, prefill cost, and decoding memory. Empirically, subset-routing and rule-assisted multi-key retrieval experiments illustrate the masks' routing expressiveness. Extensions to Mamba-2 and Gated DeltaNet using learned routing with top-$k$ query reads retain subquadratic prefill, improve recall accuracy over the backbones in several settings, and achieve comparable small-scale language-modeling performance.

</details>


### [283] [LongCat-DeepResearch Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36071)
*Meituan LongCat Team,He Zhu,Yue Xu,Wanli Wu,Haolin Ren,Yuxin Bian,Jiarui Zhao,Rongzhi Zhang,Quanchi Weng,Jinghao Cui,Yu Fan,Yuhan Liu,Yunhu Ye,Jiyuan Ren,Fengcheng Yuan,Zhao Yang,Jiacheng Zhang,Yuchuan Dai,Ruixuan Xiao,Haozhe Sun,Xiangyuan Liu,Cheng Sun,Yao Du,Yiming Hao,Hongbo Guo,Shuo He,Lei Wang,Xunliang Cai,Yan Chen,Fan Yang,Lingchuan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 LongCat-DeepResearch 深度研究系统，将增强的 LongCat 模型与多智能体工作流结合，生成有证据支撑的综合报告，并在多个基准上取得较优成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度研究系统在生成长篇报告时，往往需要反复重写全文，效率低且难以保证证据落地。作者希望构建一种能分离全局规划与细节调研、并在章节级别进行协调修订的工作流。

Method: 系统将全局规划与细节调研分离，并在章节级别协调修订。多个规划智能体先探索外部来源，提炼出可执行的研究计划 ResearchSpec；随后研究智能体并行调研并撰写各自章节，在独立上下文中持续收集证据。章节汇总后，由全局审阅指导针对性局部修订，减少对全文反复重写的依赖。该工作流还支持为 LongCat 通用模型的中期训练与后期训练构建研究任务与轨迹。

Result: 在 DeepResearchBench 上得分 55.25，DeepResearchBench II 上 51.35，ResearchRubrics 上 79.83；在内部基准上得分 76.04，在四个对比系统中排名第二。开发集分析显示，组合多种规划视角有益，而进一步细化规划效果不一；额外编辑提升了两个基准上的平均自动可读性偏好，但两个基准上的趋势不同。

Conclusion: LongCat-DeepResearch 通过多智能体规划—调研—审阅修订的分解式工作流，实现了有证据支撑的长报告生成，并在多项基准上表现良好；规划视角组合与局部编辑对性能与可读性有积极作用，但规划细化与编辑的效果存在基准差异，仍需进一步分析。

Abstract: We present LongCat-DeepResearch, a deep research system that combines an enhanced LongCat model with a multi-agent workflow for producing comprehensive, evidence-grounded reports. The workflow separates global planning from detailed investigation and coordinates revision at the section level. Multiple planning agents first explore external sources and refine an actionable research plan, termed ResearchSpec. Research agents then investigate and draft their assigned sections in parallel, gathering additional evidence in separate contexts as their analyses develop. Once the sections are assembled, global review guides targeted local revisions, reducing reliance on repeated full-report rewriting. This workflow also supports the construction of research tasks and trajectories for the mid-training and post-training of LongCat's general-purpose models. LongCat-DeepResearch achieves 55.25 on DeepResearchBench, 51.35 on DeepResearchBench II, and 79.83 on ResearchRubrics. On an in-house benchmark, it scores 76.04, ranking second among four compared systems. Development-set analyses show benefits from combining planning perspectives, while further planning refinement has mixed effects. Additional editing improves average automatic readability preference across two benchmarks, with different trends on each.

</details>


### [284] [A Polyphonic Conception of AI Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36079)
*Matthieu Queloz,Pierre Beckmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文认为，大语言模型的理解是"复调的"（polyphonic）：输出由多个并行、可靠性不一的机制联盟涌现产生，任一联盟都不是不可或缺。作者据此提出一种适用于复调AI的理解概念——以可靠且正确地被调用并控制输出的"健全电路"为核心，使理解归因成为关于内部组织的可操作主张，从而指导对AI的信任。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医生、法官或工程师在决定是否信任AI输出时，无法回避"模型是否理解"这一问题。纯数学或统计描述难以区分可信与不可信输出，实质上以另一种名义重新引入了AI理解问题。但该问题本身框架有误，因为它承袭了"单音范式"（monophonic paradigm）——即认为认知系统对某事物的理解必须定位于单一机制，并支撑该理解所赋予的全部能力。

Method: 作者基于广泛的机制性证据（mechanistic evidence）进行论证，对LLM的输出生成方式进行机制层面的分析，提出并刻画"复调性"（polyphony）这一特征，并据此发展出一个新的理解概念。

Result: LLM普遍呈现复调性：输出由多个并行机制的联盟涌现产生，这些机制可靠性参差不齐，彼此间可能互补、重复或相互淹没；多个联盟均可胜任同一任务，且没有任何一个是不可或缺的。复调性不仅使理解归因变得复杂，还使单音式的推理模式变得危险。作者提出的理解概念以"健全电路"（sound circuitry）为核心，强调该电路被可靠且正确地调用并控制输出。

Conclusion: 在新的复调理解框架下，理解归因成为关于内部组织的可处理主张，能够承担起指导人们对AI建立信任的功能。

Abstract: When a doctor, a judge, or an engineer must decide whether to trust an AI model's output, they cannot avoid asking what the model understands. Purely mathematical or statistical descriptions struggle to distinguish trustworthy from untrustworthy outputs without reintroducing the question of AI understanding in all but name. Yet the question is ill-framed as it stands, because the inherited concept operates within a monophonic paradigm: the idea that a cognitive system's understanding of something must be localised to a single mechanism underpinning all the capacities conferred by such understanding. Drawing on a wide range of mechanistic evidence, we show that LLMs are pervasively polyphonic: outputs emerge from coalitions of parallel mechanisms of uneven reliability, which variously complement, duplicate, or drown out one another, with several coalitions sufficing for a task without any one being indispensable. Polyphony not only complicates attributions of understanding, but renders monophonic inference patterns hazardous. In response, we develop a conception of understanding fit for polyphonic AI. It centres on sound circuitry that is reliably and correctly recruited and in control of outputs. Attributions of understanding thereby become tractable claims about internal organisation, and can do the work of guiding trust in AI.

</details>


### [285] [GeoOutageBench: Benchmarking Ambiguity-aware, Ontology-grounded Geospatiotemporal KGQA for Multimodal Power Outage and Resilience Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36082)
*Ethan D. Frakes,Amy Kvien,Rishabh Kundu,Redad Mehdi,Van D. Tran,Vibha S. Mandayam,Kristopher O. Davis,Erika I. Barcelos,Roger H. French,Yinghui Wu,Mengjie Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出GeoOutageBench，一个面向多模态停电与韧性分析的LLM地学时空KGQA基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KGQA基准主要针对Web知识，缺乏面向真实基础设施韧性分析的地学时空、多模态知识图谱评测基准。

Method: 构建整合视觉、文本与结构化数据的时空KG（涵盖停电记录、遥感、天气观测、风暴与电力事件、地理实体及领域本体），设计分层难度与能力的查询分类体系，并提供用户可配置的三类评测任务。

Result: 提供覆盖多模态KG与查询类别的三层评测：(1)歧义地学时空问题的NL到SPARQL理解，(2)查询驱动下的本体效用评估，(3)多模态KGQA检索的答案准确率。

Conclusion: GeoOutageBench为评估支持真实基础设施韧性分析的LLM-KG系统提供了设计原则与基础，基准、代码、数据与文档已开源。

Abstract: We introduce GeoOutageBench, a benchmark for assessing LLM-based geospatiotemporal KGQA for multimodal outage and resilience analysis. Unlike existing KGQA benchmarks for Web knowledge, GeoOutageBench considers a spatiotemporal KG that integrates visual, textual, and structured data from outage records, remote sensing, weather observations, storm and power events, geographic entities, and domain ontologies. It provides a competency query taxonomy at different difficulty levels from spatiotemporal containment and proximity, spatiotemporal co-occurrence analysis, multimodal evidence, to hypothetical evaluation. Over multimodal KG and query classes, GeoOutageBench provides user-configurable evaluation of three important, highly coherent yet less studied tasks: (1) LLMs' understanding for ambiguous geospatiotemporal questions in terms of NL to SPARQL interpretation, (2) query-driven assessment of ontology utility, and (3) answer accuracy of multimodal KGQA retrieval. GeoOutageBench provides a design principle and foundation for assessing LLM-KG systems that support real-world infrastructure resilience analysis. Our benchmark, source code, data, results, and other documentation are available at https://github.com/UCF-SAGE/GeoOutageBench.

</details>


### [286] [An Exact Generate - Transform Decomposition of Small-LLM Team Scaling Across Orchestration Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36104)
*Blaz Bertalanic,Carolina Fortuna*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 团队扩展收益高度依赖任务与架构：算术应用题从3到30次调用可提升约17个百分点，而ARC、GPQA、MMLU最多提升4点；Proposer-Critic在算术上增益最大且总量最优，但其他任务上垫底，无架构通吃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 替换单个LLM为协作团队可提升准确率，但团队规模扩展是否有效、哪种架构值得扩展尚不明确，且任务平均指标会掩盖任务间差异。

Method: 在五种指令微调7-9B模型、五个短答案基准及一个可执行代码基准上，扫描八种多智能体编排架构，调用预算最高30次；并提出精确的generate-transform分解，将任意工作流拆为提议覆盖与下游变换，使准确率变化严格分解为广泛的覆盖红利与外延变换变化。

Result: 从3到30次调用，两个算术应用题基准(GSM8K、GSMHard)准确率提升最高17点，而ARC、GPQA、MMLU在所有架构上至多提升4点；Proposer-Critic在算术上增益最陡、总预算下最强（聚类区间不含零），但在其他任务中最弱；等调用预算下token成本仍相差2.1倍。

Conclusion: 分解可诊断任务：算术存在覆盖余量且批评者引导的变换能转化之；选择题基准或覆盖饱和或无法转化；开放式代码中生成式恢复几乎消失，准确率随覆盖变化。团队扩展是任务与架构特定的赌注，而非统一杠杆。

Abstract: Replacing one LLM agent with a collaborating team can raise accuracy, but whether scaling the team helps, and which architecture to scale, is unclear. Sweeping eight agent orchestration architectures across five instruction-tuned 7-9B models, five short-answer benchmarks, and an executable-code benchmark up to 30 calls, we find that the returns to team scaling are sharply task-dependent: from three to thirty calls accuracy rises by up to 17 points on the two arithmetic word-problem benchmarks (GSM8K, GSMHard) but by at most four on ARC, GPQA, and MMLU, for every architecture, a split the usual task-averaged number conceals. Proposer-Critic captures the arithmetic gains, scaling steepest and, in aggregate, surpassing every other architecture at the largest budget (item-clustered intervals exclude zero), though it ranks among the weakest elsewhere, and no architecture wins across tasks.
  We explain these trajectories with an exact generate-transform decomposition. Partitioning any workflow into proposal coverage and a downstream transform, any accuracy change splits exactly into an extensive coverage dividend and an intensive transformation change. The decomposition diagnoses each task: arithmetic offers coverage headroom that a critic-guided transform converts, whereas the multiple-choice benchmarks either saturate in coverage or fail to convert it, and on open-ended code generative recovery nearly vanishes so accuracy tracks coverage. At equal call budgets token cost still varies 2.1x. Extra calls therefore create candidate opportunity that only some architectures, on some tasks, convert. Team scaling is a task- and architecture-specific bet, not a uniform lever.

</details>


### [287] [The Layer Mystery of VLA: An Information-Theoretical Analysis of VLA Latent Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36118)
*Yuxiang Liu,Lizhi Yang,Fengze Xie,Aaron Ames,Yisong Yue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究VLA策略中视觉-语言主干层与动作头的接口选择问题，发现多层级融合收益有限，而InfoNCE可作为高效、可靠的层质量代理指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略通过潜接口将预训练视觉-语言主干连接到动作头，但接口应暴露哪些主干层尚不明确。层选择对策略性能影响重大，而穷举策略扫描成本高昂。

Method: 在三个预训练模型和LIBERO、CALVIN两个操作基准上，对冻结主干进行单层选择与多层融合研究（每种配置三个训练种子），涵盖三种融合机制和三种层子集策略；并从理论上推导动作条件InfoNCE与动作预测的信息瓶颈目标之间的重加权等价关系，据此以InfoNCE作为层质量代理。

Result: 54个融合配置中有47个劣于最佳单层策略，统计分析确认融合优势非常有限；最佳层在不同主干与基准间差异显著。InfoNCE在四种代理中与策略成功率正相关性最稳定。选最高InfoNCE层所需GPU算力比穷举扫描少9–33倍，将平均选择遗憾从最深层的17.89个百分点降至3.71个百分点，接近回顾式固定层启发式的3.28–3.50个百分点。

Conclusion: 多层融合并未带来明显收益，层选择本身才是关键；基于InfoNCE的层选择是一种高效且接近最优的代理方案，无需闭环评估即可获得较低选择遗憾。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies connect a pretrained vision-language backbone to an action head through a latent interface, but which backbone layers this interface should expose remains unclear. We study single-layer selection and multi-layer fusion for frozen backbones across three pretrained models and two manipulation benchmarks, LIBERO and CALVIN, with three policy-training seeds per configuration. Across three fusion mechanisms and three layer-subset strategies, 47 of 54 configurations underperform the best observed single-layer policy. Our stastical analysis further confirms that fusion's advantage is very limited. However, the best layer varies substantially across backbones and benchmarks, making layer selection consequential and exhaustive policy sweeps expensive. We further derive a reweighting equivalence between the proposed information-bottleneck objectives for action-conditioned InfoNCE and action prediction, motivating InfoNCE as a proxy for layer quality. Empirically, InfoNCE provides the most consistent positive association with policy success among four evaluated proxies. Selecting the layer with the highest InfoNCE score requires 9-33 times less GPU compute than exhaustive policy sweeps and reduces mean selection regret from 17.89 percentage points for deepest-layer selection to 3.71 points across six settings. Its mean regret is close to the 3.28-3.50 points achieved by fixed-layer heuristics optimized retrospectively using all six oracle sweeps, without requiring closed-loop evaluations during selection.

</details>


### [288] [AdaST: Adaptive Coupling for Spatial-Temporal Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36119)
*Zhenyu Lei,Chenghao Liu,Yushun Dong,Qi R. Wang,Jundong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AdaST：基于分解-重组范式的自适应时空预测框架，通过异构感知专家解耦不同耦合模式并动态重组，显著超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法隐含假设时空强耦合，但真实时空数据存在从时间主导、空间主导到强耦合的多种异质耦合机制。该假设错配导致模型产生伪依赖，当某一相关性主导时性能退化。

Method: 提出AdaST框架，采用分解-重组范式：用异构感知专家将输入分解为刻画不同耦合模式的组件，每个组件由角色对齐模块处理，再由相关性驱动的自适应重组器整合以完成最终预测。针对三个挑战——未知耦合结构、异构耦合动态、次优空间建模。

Result: 大量实验表明AdaST显著优于当前最先进基线，验证了自适应方法的必要性。

Conclusion: 时空数据的耦合结构具有异质性，需根据数据内在耦合结构动态调制空间与时间建模；自适应分解-重组范式可有效缓解伪依赖问题并提升预测性能。

Abstract: Spatial-temporal (ST) forecasting underpins many real-world systems such as traffic, climate, and energy networks. While existing methods implicitly assume strong spatiotemporal coupling, we observe that real-world ST data exhibits distinct coupling regimes, ranging from temporal-dominated and spatial-dominated to strongly coupled patterns. This mismatch causes current models to suffer from spurious dependencies and degraded performance when one correlation dominates. To overcome this limitation, we aim to dynamically modulate spatial and temporal modeling based on the data's inherent coupling structure. However, three key challenges exist: unknown coupling structure, heterogeneous coupling dynamics, and suboptimal spatial modeling. We propose AdaST, an adaptive ST forecasting framework that tackles these challenges through a decompose-recompose paradigm. AdaST factorizes inputs into components capturing different coupling patterns using heterogeneity-aware experts. Each component is processed by role-aligned modules, and a correlation-informed adaptive recomposer integrates them for final prediction. Extensive experiments confirm that AdaST significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, validating the necessity of an adaptive approach.

</details>


### [289] [Memory Is a Derivation: The Distributed-Evidence Paradox in Long-Term Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36130)
*Hongjun Liu,Chen Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出记忆推导问题：持久记忆是否真由写入时可见的交互历史支持，并用 DerivAudit 框架从证据范围、组合有效性、准入可靠性三方面审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时运行的 LLM 智能体将过去交互压缩为持久记忆，并复用为后续任务的前提，因此需要判断记忆是否真正由交互历史推导而来。压缩可能引入历史未确立的关系或事件状态，引用缺失会使有效记忆看似无据，而单独成立的事实也可能被组合成历史并未确立的更强陈述。

Method: 作者将问题刻画为三个耦合要求：证据范围、组合有效性、准入可靠性；提出 DerivAudit 审计框架，分离三类问题：支持证据是否超出写入方提供的引用、组合记忆是否引入不受支持的语义、写入时准入决策如何影响后续记忆使用。并在两个自然记忆语料上进行审计实验。

Result: 使用更广的写入前历史进行审计，可为近 60% 仅凭引用看似不受支持的记忆恢复支持；但仍有 17–21% 在扩展后仍不受支持。更广证据本身并不能使准入可靠：不受支持的记忆在多种验证模型下仍常被接纳，且证据扩展在两个骨干模型上反而加剧了该问题。

Conclusion: 记忆有效性不能仅凭引用或更广证据检索来判断，写入时的准入可靠性是独立且关键的环节；DerivAudit 将证据范围、组合有效性与准入决策解耦审计，揭示了扩大证据与提升准入可靠性之间的落差。

Abstract: Long-running LLM agents compress past interactions into persistent memories that may be reused as premises for later tasks. This creates a distinct derivation problem: whether the memory actually follows from what the interaction history supports. Relevant evidence may be scattered across earlier interactions, while compression can introduce relations or event status that the history never established. A valid memory may therefore appear unsupported because its citations omit relevant evidence, while individually supported facts may be composed into a stronger statement the history never established. We characterize this problem through three coupled requirements: (1) Evidence scope; (2) Compositional validity; (3) Admission reliability. We therefore ask whether the interaction history available at write time supports what enters persistent memory. We introduce DerivAudit, a framework for auditing whether a memory is actually supported by the history available when it was written. The audit separates three questions: whether supporting evidence lies beyond writer-provided citations, whether the composed memory introduces unsupported meaning, and how write-time admission decisions affect later memory use. Across two natural memory corpora, audits using broader pre-write history recover support for nearly 60% of memories that appear unsupported from citations alone, while 17-21% remain unsupported after expansion. Yet broader evidence does not by itself make admission reliable: unsupported memories are still frequently admitted across verification models, and evidence expansion alone worsens it on two backbones.

</details>


### [290] [More Features Are Not More Evidence: Limits of Training-Free Human Activity Recognition with Jev](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36154)
*Orhan Konak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究通用概率决策模型Jev能否无需训练直接解读传感器信号以替代或补充HAR监督模型。在WISDM、UCI341、PAMAP2上，Jev的宏F1仅为0.038–0.118，远低于监督模型的0.686–0.907，说明免训练传感器决策不仅取决于信号信息量，还取决于模型能否利用承载该信息的表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用模型有望在无需训练任务专用分类器的前提下实现基于传感器的决策，从而降低HAR对标注数据的依赖。但目前尚不清楚这类模型能否直接解读物理传感器信号的确定性描述，达到替代或补充已训练HAR模型的程度。

Method: 使用固定通用概率决策模型Jev，在来自WISDM、UCI341和PAMAP2的1,800个类别均衡加速度计窗口上进行评估，不给任何标注样本、检索上下文或HAR专用参数更新。比较三种确定性传感器表示，并将5,400次Jev决策与一个生成式基线及三个监督HAR模型对照；同时进行数值特征增减、语义渲染增强及事后融合分析。

Result: Jev最强表示在三个数据集上的宏F1分别为0.038、0.118、0.089，而监督模型为0.686–0.907。增加数值特征并未提升反而在三个数据集上均降低识别性能；加入确定性语义渲染可部分恢复性能，但实验未能将语义因素与随之而来的序列化及冗余变化分离。Jev查询快速且成本低，但概率校准不可靠。事后融合分析仅在WISDM上有小幅提升，未能在UCI341或PAMAP2上复现。

Conclusion: 免训练传感器决策既取决于信号中可获得的信息，也取决于模型能否通过信息所暴露的表示加以利用。因此，传感器到模型的接口应作为模型评估的一部分，而非中性的预处理步骤。

Abstract: General-purpose models promise sensor-based decisions without training a task-specific classifier, which could reduce the dependence of Human Activity Recognition (HAR) on labeled data. Yet it remains unclear whether such models can directly interpret deterministic descriptions of physical sensor signals well enough to replace or complement trained HAR models. We study this question using Jev, a fixed general-purpose probabilistic decision model, on 1,800 class-balanced accelerometer windows from WISDM, UCI341, and PAMAP2. Jev receives no labeled examples, retrieval context, or HAR-specific parameter updates. We evaluate three deterministic sensor representations and compare 5,400 Jev decisions with a generative baseline and three supervised HAR models. Jev remains far below supervised recognition, with its strongest representation reaching macro-F1 of 0.038, 0.118, and 0.089 across the three datasets, compared with 0.686 to 0.907 for the supervised models. More numerical features do not improve Jev. Instead, they reduce recognition on all three datasets, while augmenting the same numerical evidence with a deterministic semantic rendering partially recovers performance, although the experiment does not isolate semantics from the accompanying serialization and redundancy changes. Jev is fast and inexpensive to query, but its probabilities are not reliably calibrated for recognition. A post-hoc fusion analysis finds a small improvement on WISDM that does not replicate on UCI341 or PAMAP2. These results show that training-free sensor decisions depend not only on the information available in the signal, but also on whether the model can use the representation through which that information is exposed. The sensor-to-model interface should therefore be treated as part of the model evaluation rather than as a neutral preprocessing step.

</details>


### [291] [Principled Thoughts for Latent Recursive LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36159)
*Fahd Seddik,Fatemeh Fard*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出REST（表示监督思维）训练目标，将有效思维表示的四个属性（因果性、最小性、可分离性、稳定性）转化为可微损失，与交叉熵联合训练，从而提升潜空间推理（单智能体与多智能体）的准确率与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型可在连续潜空间中推理（依赖自身隐状态或智能体间传递状态），但训练仅以最终解码答案的交叉熵（CE）为监督信号，不对思维过程施加约束。理论与实证分析表明，仅用CE训练会导致四类失败，使正确答案概率降低，例如不同问题的思维坍缩、保留无关信息等。

Method: 提出REST（REpresentation-Supervised Thoughts）训练目标：把有效思维表示的四个属性——因果性、最小性、可分离性、稳定性——转化为可微损失，叠加在CE损失之上。该目标在潜空间单智能体与多智能体系统中实例化，推理阶段不做架构改动、不增加参数。

Result: 在涵盖数学、科学、医学与代码生成等7个基准上，在相同训练数据、算力与潜空间预算下，相较于仅用CE训练，REST在不同智能体设置与模型规模上提升准确率最高达7.5个百分点，最终答案收敛率提升30%。此外，REST的思维编码了更多达成正确答案所需信息，解码时可更好地还原智能体的预期输出，使潜空间通信更易解释。

Conclusion: 仅监督最终答案的交叉熵不足以约束潜空间思维；通过将思维表示的四项属性显式正则化为附加损失，REST在无需推理期额外架构或参数的情况下，同时提升了潜空间推理的准确率、收敛性与可解释性。

Abstract: Large language models can reason in continuous space instead of decoded text, by recurring on their own hidden states or by passing those states between agents, while training supervises only the Cross-Entropy (CE) of the final decoded answer and does not constrain the thought. Theoretical and empirical analyses establish and confirm four failures of CE-only training that lead to a lower probability of the correct answer such as collapsing thoughts across distinct questions and retaining irrelevant information. We introduce REST (REpresentation-Supervised Thoughts), a training objective that turns four properties of a valid thought representation (causality, minimality, separability, and stability) into differentiable losses added to CE. We instantiate it in latent single-agent and multi-agent systems, without architectural changes or added parameters at inference. Across 7 benchmarks spanning mathematics, science, medicine, and code generation, with the same training data, compute, and latent budget, REST increases accuracy over CE-only training across agent settings and model sizes by up to 7.5 percentage points and convergence on a final answer by 30\%. Furthermore, REST thoughts encode more of what is required to achieve the correct answer, and decoding them better recovers the intended output of the agent, which makes latent communication easier to interpret. Project Website: https://fard-lab.github.io/REST

</details>


### [292] [FigAct: Turning Scientific Figures into Active Canvases for Explanation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36190)
*Shishi Xiao,Zichao Wang,Alexa Siu,David H. Laidlaw,Jennifer Healey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 FigAct 框架，将静态科学图表转化为问题条件下的视觉演示，通过直接操作图中已有元素（如同人类讲解者）生成旁白、定位证据并施加视觉动作引导注意力，结合分层搜索与三项任务奖励训练 FigAct-8B，并构建基准以评估多模态大模型生成有依据视觉解释的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型（MLLM）解释科学图表时，通常将视觉内容"翻译"回文本，读者需自行把文字解释映射回图中，理解负担重、缺乏对视觉证据的定位。作者受人类讲解视觉信息方式的启发，希望让模型直接在图上去"表演"，从而让解释更清晰、更易跟随。

Method: 提出 FigAct 框架：将静态科学图表作为演示画布，生成一系列简短旁白，将每条旁白定位（grounding）到对应视觉证据，并施加视觉动作以引导观看者注意力。采用分层搜索策略进行高效元素定位，token 使用量降低约 40 倍。使用三项任务特定奖励（定位准确性、搜索效率、渲染质量）训练 FigAct-8B。同时基于真实科学论文图表构建经人工验证的基准，用于评估 MLLM 生成有依据视觉解释的能力。

Result: 分层搜索策略使 token 使用量降低约 40 倍；实验结果表明 FigAct 方法有效，将科学图表视为演示画布可使解释更清晰、更易跟随。

Conclusion: 将科学图表当作可演示的画布、以问题为条件直接操图元并配以旁白与视觉动作，是提升多模态模型科学图表解释可理解性的有效范式；配套的训练奖励设计与人工验证基准为此类任务提供了方法与评估支撑。

Abstract: Scientific figures are designed to communicate information visually, yet MLLMs typically explain them by translating their visual content back into text. This requires readers to manually map the resulting explanations back to the figure. Inspired by how people present visual information, we introduce FigAct, a framework that transforms static scientific figures into question-conditioned visual presentations by acting directly on their existing graphical elements. Like a human presenter, FigAct generates a sequence of short narrations, grounds each narration in the corresponding visual evidence, and applies visual actions to guide the viewer's attention. We develop a hierarchical search strategy for efficient element localization, reducing token usage by approximately 40$\times$. We further train FigAct-8B using three task-specific rewards for grounding accuracy, search efficiency, and rendering quality. We further build a human-verified benchmark from figures in real-world scientific papers to evaluate the ability of MLLMs to generate grounded visual explanations. Our results demonstrate the effectiveness of FigAct and show that treating scientific figures as presentation canvases makes explanations clearer and easier to follow.

</details>


### [293] [An Empirical Study and Assessment of EU AI Act Compliance Checkers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36228)
*Zhen Tao,Alize Kahraman,Shidong Pan,Zhenchang Xing,Chiara Ullstein,Jens Grossklags,Chunyang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次对12款主流欧盟《人工智能法案》自动化合规检查工具（AIACC）进行实证评估，发现其质量参差、仅能作为早期导向工具，并提出更可靠合规支持工具的设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧盟《人工智能法案》对AI系统的开发、部署与集成提出广泛合规要求，涉及安全、隐私、数据治理、透明度、准确性与鲁棒性等。然而中小企业和个人开发者普遍缺乏法律专业知识，难以将法定义务转化为工程与治理实践。这一鸿沟催生了多种自动化合规检查工具，但其实际有效性与质量尚缺乏系统性检验。

Method: 本文开展首个针对AIACC的实证研究与评估。选取12款主流AIACC，从多个维度刻画其特征；评估其法律覆盖范围与条款对齐程度；并对工具生成的合规报告从结构、确定性（determinacy）和可行动性（actionability）三方面进行分析。

Result: AIACC质量差异显著，当前仅能充当早期导向工具。具体表现为：交互模式与易用性不一致；倾向过度简化或遗漏关键义务；未能提供确定且可操作的指导。因此依赖当前一代AIACC可能产生“虚假合规感”。

Conclusion: 本文提供了当前AIACC生态的关键基线，并进一步提出实现更可靠合规支持工具的设计原则。

Abstract: The EU AI Act introduces extensive compliance requirements for organizations that develop, deploy, or integrate AI systems. Many of these requirements are directly relevant to security and privacy, while also addressing closely related issues such as data governance, transparency, accuracy, and robustness. However, stakeholders such as small-to-medium businesses and individual developers often lack the legal expertise required to interpret these obligations and translate them into engineering and governance practices. This disconnect creates challenges for implementing the EU AI Act and may lead to missing safeguards or misdirected development and deployment efforts. To address this, various automated EU AI Act compliance checkers (AIACCs) have emerged, claiming to streamline compliance assessments and provide practical guidance. In this paper, we present the first empirical study and assessment of AIACCs. We characterize 12 mainstream AIACCs across multiple dimensions, evaluate their legal coverage and alignment, and analyze checker-generated compliance reports for structure, determinacy, and actionability. We find that the quality of AIACCs varies significantly and that they currently can only serve as early-stage orientation tools. Specifically, we observe inconsistent interaction modes and user-friendliness, a tendency to overly simplify or omit key obligations, and a failure to provide determinate, actionable guidance. As a result, reliance on the current generation of AIACCs may foster a false sense of compliance. With our study, we provide a critical baseline of the current AIACC landscape. We further offer design principles for the implementation of more reliable compliance-support tools.

</details>


### [294] [MERID: Multimodal Exploration via Recursive Self-Improvement Agents for Major Depression Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36235)
*Lei Liu,Zhaokang Liang,Qingcheng Zeng,Chenda Duan,Lu Mi,Zhen Tan,Tianyu Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出MERID框架，通过经验驱动的递归自我改进智能体自动演化多模态抑郁症预测流水线，在抑郁基准上优于现有多模态与智能体基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抑郁症检测依赖访谈录音、传感器测量等多模态异构数据，通常需要定制化预测流水线。现有手工设计架构与智能体辅助开发方法虽取得进展，但难以依据实验反馈自主修改流水线，并将已验证的改进稳定继承到后续设计中。

Method: MERID通过经验驱动的递归自我改进（RSI）构建抑郁流水线，包含三个核心机制：Grounded State Construction（GSC）将多模态记录与受试者级抑郁标签对齐以锚定经验；Coupled Pipeline Exploration（CPE）联合调整表征、融合方式与预测器，生成用于分类和严重度估计的后继流水线；Evidence-Guided Evolution（EGE）依据反馈引导修改，并在小样本抑郁队列的不确定性下验证增益后再进行继承。

Result: 在抑郁症基准上的大量实验表明，MERID在多项任务上取得了优于多模态基线和智能体基线的结果；进一步分析显示声学与语言线索对抑郁检测具有重要价值。

Conclusion: MERID验证了以递归自我改进智能体自动演化多模态抑郁分析流水线的可行性与有效性，并强调了声学与语言模态的贡献，代码已开源。

Abstract: Major depressive disorder (MDD) severely impacts daily activities and quality of life. Detecting MDD involves multimodal data, such as interview recordings and sensor measurements. This is particularly challenging, as these heterogeneous modalities often demand distinct, customized prediction pipelines. Existing efforts to address this challenge have explored both manually engineered multimodal architectures and agent-assisted pipeline development. Despite their progress, it remains challenging to autonomously revise pipelines based on experimental feedback and carry verified improvements forward into subsequent designs. To this end, we propose Multimodal Exploration via Recursive Self-Improvement Agents for Major Depression Analysis (MERID). The framework develops depression pipelines through experience-based recursive self-improvement (RSI). Grounded State Construction (GSC) grounds experience by aligning multimodal records with subject-level depression targets. Coupled Pipeline Exploration (CPE) jointly modifies representations, fusion, and predictors to build successor pipelines for classification and severity estimation. Evidence-Guided Evolution (EGE) guides revisions through feedback and verifies gains under uncertainty in small depression cohorts before inheritance. Extensive experiments on depression benchmarks show that MERID achieves the best results on multiple tasks compared with multimodal and agent-based baselines. Further analysis highlights the value of acoustic and linguistic cues for depression detection. Our code is available at https://github.com/DiscoAILab/MERID

</details>


### [295] [ChronoSRL: Temporal Geometry for Self-Supervised Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36238)
*Nico Bohlinger,Jan Peters*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ChronoSRL，在critic的表示空间中引入显式的时间几何，使状态-动作与目标嵌入的距离直接对应到达目标所需时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对比式与生存式强化学习的critic未在表示空间中以时间为单位度量距离，而空间上接近的目标在时间上可能很远。障碍、地形与智能体自身能力决定了到达目标所需时间，因此需要显式的时间几何来刻画目标可达性。

Method: ChronoSRL训练状态-动作与目标嵌入之间的距离以匹配目标到达时间；对未到达目标及其他轨迹的目标，将其推离至少一个折扣视界。策略不直接沿时间距离行动，而是基于生存式强化学习，从时间嵌入预测目标到达时间的完整分布以及停留在目标附近的时间，使策略倾向于更快、更可靠到达并保持在目标附近。

Result: 在七个标准运动与导航基准上，ChronoSRL比对比式、动作分块对比式与生存式强化学习基线学习更快、性能更高，且网络规模小得多。四足机器人仿真到现实的任务（速度跟踪、目标位置到达、箱子攀爬）中，ChronoSRL是唯一能保持指令速度与目标位置、并爬上最高箱子的自监督强化学习方法。

Conclusion: 显式时间几何的critic表示与生存式强化学习结合，可有效提升自监督强化学习的学习效率与最终性能，并能自然融入机器人领域常见的塑形项。

Abstract: A goal that is close in space can be far away in time. Obstacles, terrain, and the agent's own capabilities determine how long it takes to get there. Yet, critics in contrastive and survival reinforcement learning do not measure the distances in their representation space in units of time. We therefore introduce ChronoSRL, which gives the critic's embeddings an explicit temporal geometry. The distance between state-action and goal embeddings is trained to match the time that the agent takes to reach the goal (goal-reaching time), while goals that were not reached, and goals from other trajectories, are pushed at least one discount horizon away. Furthermore, reaching a goal quickly once does not mean that reaching it is reliable in general, so the policy should not follow the temporal distance directly. Instead, we build on survival reinforcement learning and predict from our temporal embeddings not only the full distribution of goal-reaching times but also the time spent near the goal. Thereby, the policy is trained to favor actions that reach the goal sooner and more reliably and that keep the agent near it. ChronoSRL learns faster and reaches higher performance than contrastive, action-chunked contrastive, and survival reinforcement learning baselines on seven standard locomotion and navigation benchmarks, even with much smaller networks. To test the limits of self-supervised reinforcement learning, we introduce velocity tracking, goal-position reaching, and box climbing tasks with a quadruped robot in a realistic sim-to-real locomotion setup, and show how the shaping terms that are typical for robotics can be naturally incorporated into our framework. ChronoSRL is the only one of the tested self-supervised reinforcement learning methods that learns to stay at the commanded velocities and goal positions, and climbs the highest boxes.

</details>


### [296] [CoRe: Co-Evolving Reward Models for Mitigating Latent Reward Hacking in Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36245)
*Zhaolong Su,Yujin Han,Feng Wang,Jameson Dong,Hins Hu,Difan Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文发现潜在奖励模型(LRM)对齐视频扩散模型时存在"潜在奖励黑客"问题，根源是分布逃逸；提出协同演化奖励框架CoRe，持续在生成器当前样本上重拟合奖励模型并锚定真实视频偏好，在Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B上稳定提升生成质量并避免质量崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在奖励模型可在潜空间直接对中间状态打分，从而高效对齐视频扩散模型。但作者发现：针对固定潜在奖励优化会迅速引发"潜在奖励黑客"——预测奖励保持高位，而感知质量与运动质量却持续恶化。因此需要解决该失效模式以实现可靠的潜在空间对齐。

Method: 通过分析识别出核心成因是"分布逃逸"：数百次更新后生成器已移出奖励模型的训练支撑域，其打分不再反映视频质量。基于此提出CoRe协同演化奖励框架，将潜在空间对齐视为生成器与奖励模型之间的动态交互：不再针对静态代理优化，而是持续在生成器当前样本上重拟合奖励模型，同时将其锚定到真实视频偏好上，使生成器无法通过偏离数据分布来获取奖励。

Result: 在Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B上的实验表明，CoRe相比预训练模型和已有对齐方法均能持续提升生成质量，同时避免了固定奖励优化带来的质量崩溃。

Conclusion: 固定潜在奖励优化因分布逃逸导致奖励黑客与质量崩溃；将奖励模型与生成器协同演化、并以真实视频偏好作为锚点，可有效抑制分布逃逸，实现稳定且高效的视频扩散模型潜在空间对齐。

Abstract: Latent reward models (LRMs) enable efficient alignment of video diffusion models by scoring intermediate states directly in latent space. However, we find that optimizing against a fixed latent reward rapidly leads to latent reward hacking: the predicted reward stays high while perceptual and motion quality deteriorate. Our analysis identifies distributional escape as the central cause: within a few hundred updates, the generator moves beyond the reward model's training support, where its scores no longer reflect video quality. Based on this insight, we introduce CoRe, a co-evolving reward framework that treats latent-space alignment as a dynamic interaction between the generator and the reward model. Rather than optimizing against a stationary proxy, CoRe continually refits the reward model on the generator's current samples while anchoring it to real-video preferences, so the generator cannot gain reward by drifting away from the data. On Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B, experiments show that CoRe consistently improves generation quality over both the pretrained model and prior alignment methods, while avoiding the quality collapse of fixed-reward optimization.

</details>


### [297] [Towards Mitigating Deceptive Safety Alignment in Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36254)
*Xiangyu Zhou,Saleh Zare Zade,Rafi Ibn Sultan,Alexander Kotov,Dongxiao Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究大推理模型(LRM)中"欺骗性安全对齐"现象，提出DSAR指标量化推理链与最终答案的安全不一致性，分析其成因（隐表示差异），并提出强化学习方法SARA加以缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LRM通常用强化学习训练以提升思维链(CoT)推理，但RL奖励一般只基于最终答案，对中间推理几乎无直接监督。这可能导致"欺骗性安全对齐"——推理轨迹与最终答案传递的安全信号不一致。作者希望系统性地研究这一现象，并加以解决。

Method: 1) 提出DSAR(Deceptive Safety Alignment Rate)指标，联合评估推理轨迹与最终答案以量化二者的安全不一致性；2) 在多个LRM与基准上进行评估，并考察标准提示与prefilling攻击下的表现；3) 通过隐表示分析，比较模型在最终答案阶段与中间推理阶段的安全判别能力差异；4) 提出SARA(Safety-Aware Reasoning Alignment)，一种基于RL的方法，同时对安全感知推理与安全最终答案给予奖励，鼓励早期识别有害意图并强制推理-答案一致性。

Result: 在多个LRM和基准上，欺骗性安全对齐在标准提示下普遍存在，并在prefilling攻击下显著加剧。隐表示分析显示模型在最终答案阶段的安全判别能力明显强于中间推理阶段。SARA在标准与对抗设置下均显著缓解欺骗性安全对齐，同时保持有用性与效用。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 仅基于最终答案的RL奖励会忽视中间推理监督，导致普遍且可被攻击放大的欺骗性安全对齐。通过SARA对推理过程与最终答案同时进行安全对齐，可有效缓解该问题且不损害模型有用性，为LRM安全对齐提供了新方向。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) are commonly trained with reinforcement learning (RL) to improve their generation of chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning before producing final answers. However, RL rewards are typically assigned based on final answers, providing little or no direct supervision over intermediate reasoning. This can lead to deceptive safety alignment, where the reasoning trace and final answer convey inconsistent safety signals. To systematically investigate this phenomenon, we introduce DSAR (Deceptive Safety Alignment Rate), a metric that jointly assesses reasoning traces and final answers to quantify their safety inconsistency. Across multiple LRMs and benchmarks, we find that deceptive safety alignment is pervasive under standard prompting conditions and is substantially amplified under prefilling attacks. We further provide a hidden representation analysis showing that models exhibit stronger safety discrimination at the final-answer stage than during intermediate reasoning. To close this gap, we propose SARA (Safety-Aware Reasoning Alignment), an RL-based method that rewards both safety-aware reasoning and safe final answers, encouraging early harmful intent recognition and enforcing reasoning-answer consistency. Experiments show that SARA significantly mitigates deceptive safety alignment under both standard and adversarial settings while preserving helpfulness and utility. Code is available at https://github.com/xzhou98/SARA.

</details>


### [298] [OTROPE: Optimal Transport-based Robust Off-policy Evaluation for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36264)
*Liner Xiang,Wenbo Zhang,Hengrui Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OTROPE，一种基于最优传输的无似然离线策略评估方法，用于在标签稀缺、行为-目标分布偏移及黑盒无法获取似然的情况下评估新目标LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠评估LLM对开发与部署至关重要，但在线评估成本高、风险大且难以安全执行。离线策略评估中，行为模型的人类标注数据有限，且存在行为-目标分布偏移，黑盒LLM的响应似然常不可得，现有评估器在此情形下表现不佳。

Method: 提出OTROPE（Optimal Transport-based Robust Off-Policy Evaluation）。在语义空间中通过最优传输进行分布校正，将已标注的行为策略样本与未标注的目标策略样本对齐；将校正后的人类标注残差与代理预测器结合，形成双重稳健式评估，无需行为策略建模或密度比估计。

Result: 理论刻画了基线评估器在LLM分布偏移下失效的原因，并证明当重加权行为分布或代理预测器之一收敛时OTROPE具有一致性与收敛速率。在合成与真实LLM评估任务上，OTROPE持续优于基线，并能使较弱LLM评估器的集成接近甚至超越更强评估器。

Conclusion: OTROPE为LLM离线评估提供了一种无似然、双重稳健的分布校正方案，缓解标签稀缺与分布偏移问题，具有良好的理论保证与实证表现。

Abstract: Reliable evaluation of large language models (LLMs) is essential for their development and deployment, yet is often costly, risky, and difficult to perform safely online. We study off-policy evaluation for LLMs, where limited human-labeled data from a behavior model are used to evaluate a newer target LLM. This setting is challenging because labels are scarce, behavior--target distribution shift is common, and response likelihoods are often unavailable for black-box LLMs. We propose the Optimal Transport-based Robust Off-Policy Evaluation (OTROPE), a likelihood-free evaluation that performs distributional correction in a semantic space via optimal transport to align labeled behavior-policy samples with unlabeled target-policy samples. OTROPE combines corrected human-labeled residuals with proxy predictors, yielding a doubly robust-style evaluation without behavior-policy modeling or density-ratio estimation. We theoretically characterize why baseline evaluators fail under LLM distribution shift, and establish consistency and convergence rates for OTROPE when either the reweighted behavior distribution or the proxy predictor converges. Experiments on synthetic and real LLM evaluation tasks show that OTROPE consistently outperforms baselines while enabling ensembles of weaker LLM evaluators to approach and sometimes surpass stronger evaluators. Code is available at https://github.com/LinerXiang/OTROPE.

</details>


### [299] [From Surfaces to Volumes: Registered Geometry for Protein Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36277)
*Siyuan Chen,Cai Zhou,Jinrui Zhang,Zhaokang Liang,Taku Komura,Wojciech Matusik,Stephen Bates,Tommi Jaakkola,Wengong Jin,Peter Yichen Chen,Minghao Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Protein-TetSphere，一种残基级配准体积表示，通过四面体化和共享Laplacian基实现跨残基一致的体积坐标，在结合口袋分类、界面预测和binder设计上均取得提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蛋白质几何模型多用采样点、法向量、曲率等局部表面特征表示分子表面，能刻画暴露形状，但未显式建模表面下的体积组织，也未提供残基级体积结构的一致坐标系。

Method: 将每条蛋白链四面体化，得到与各残基关联的局部体积区域；将其配准到共享的固定拓扑四面体参考，并在统一Laplacian基中表示，从而建立跨残基一致的体积坐标，并将局部三维变形与表面及化学信息整合为多模态蛋白质表示。

Result: 在配体结合口袋分类上，平衡准确率从0.795提升至0.826；在Pinder-Pair/Site上AUROC从0.914/0.852提升至0.932/0.866；binder设计成功率在BoltzGen Challenge Set上从14.95%提升至19.90%，在ProtDBench backbone层面从27.62%提升至32.19%。

Conclusion: 配准的体积几何为蛋白质识别、相互作用与设计提供了超越分子表面的互补空间信息。

Abstract: Existing protein geometry models typically represent molecular surfaces using local geometric features such as sampled points, normals, and curvature. While effective for capturing exposed molecular shape, these representations do not explicitly model the volumetric organization beneath the surface or provide a consistent coordinate system for residue-wise volumetric structure. We introduce Protein-TetSphere, a registered residue-wise volumetric representation for proteins. Each protein chain is tetrahedralized to obtain local volumetric regions associated with individual residues, which are then registered to a shared fixed-topology tetrahedral reference and represented in a common Laplacian basis. This registration establishes consistent volumetric coordinates across residues, enabling local three-dimensional deformation to be integrated with surface and chemical information in a multimodal protein representation. We evaluate Protein-TetSphere on ligand-binding pocket classification, protein--protein interface prediction, and de novo protein binder design. Across the three tasks, Protein-TetSphere improves ligand-binding pocket balanced accuracy from $0.795$ to $0.826$, Pinder-Pair/Site AUROC from $0.914/0.852$ to $0.932/0.866$, and binder-design success from $14.95\%$ to $19.90\%$ on the BoltzGen Challenge Set and from $27.62\%$ to $32.19\%$ at the ProtDBench backbone level. These results show that registered volumetric geometry provides complementary spatial information beyond molecular surfaces across protein recognition, interaction, and design.

</details>


### [300] [Illusory Truth or Mere Exposure? Model-Dependent Repetition Effects in LLM-Based Social Media Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36278)
*Azza Bouleimen,Nicolò Pagan,Anikó Hannák*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文在社交媒体模拟情境下检验四种LLM（Gemma-3-4b-it、Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct、Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct、GPT-5-nano）是否复现人类的"错觉真理效应"（ITE），并区分真正的ITE与单纯曝光效应。结果显示四种模型呈现四类截然不同的模式，且温度无显著影响，提示不能假定LLM间ITE的均一复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式智能体模型（GABMs）日益用于模拟社交媒体动态（如虚假信息传播）。此类社会模拟要成为人类行为的有效代理，其LLM智能体需复现已知的人类认知偏差，其中"错觉真理效应"（ITE）——即对某主张的重复接触会提升其被感知的真实性——尤为关键。然而，LLM在该情境中是否及如何表现ITE尚不明确，直接影响社会模拟的有效性。

Method: 提出两阶段、情境内（within-context）实验设计，将重复操纵嵌入真实的新闻信息流交互中。跨4个LLM、100条陈述、10种信息流变体、3次重复，共收集336,000条真实性、重要性、情感与兴趣评分。核心比较为：在模拟阶段反复出现的陈述与在同一实验情境窗口内完全未见的陈述之间的评分差异，并据此区分真正的ITE（真实性特异性的重复增益）与单纯曝光效应。采用OLS回归，进而使用线性混合效应模型（LMM）控制模型与评分类型差异。

Result: 呈现四种性质迥异的模式：Gemma-3表现出真正的ITE；Qwen2.5仅表现为单纯曝光效应；GPT-5-nano对真实性无重复效应，且对重复内容呈轻度怀疑；Llama-3.1出现小幅真实性增益，同时评价性维度下降。温度对这些结果无影响；方差分解显示LLM评分行为具有高度情境敏感性。

Conclusion: 研究警示不应假定ITE在LLM间能均一复现，这对基于GABM的社会模拟构成重要限制；同时提示Gemma-3-4b-it在虚假信息相关模拟中可能提供最具行为现实性的近似。

Abstract: Generative agent-based models (GABMs) are increasingly used to simulate social media dynamics, including misinformation spread. For such social simulations to be valid proxies of human behavior, LLM agents should replicate established human cognitive biases, among them the Illusory Truth Effect (ITE), where repeated exposure to a claim increases its perceived truth value. We investigate whether and how the ITE manifests across four LLMs (Gemma-3-4b-it, Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, and GPT-5-nano) in a social media simulation context. We propose a two-phase within-context experimental design that embeds the repetition manipulation inside a realistic news feed interaction. Using this design, we collect 336,000 truth, importance, sentiment, and interest ratings across 100 statements, 10 feed variants, and 3 replications. The key comparison is between ratings assigned to repeated statements, seen throughout a simulation phase, and completely unseen ones, rated within the same experimental context window. We distinguish genuine ITE (truth-specific repetition boost) from mere exposure effects. We run an OLS regression followed by a Linear Mixed-Effect Model to account for differences across models and ratings. Our results reveal four qualitatively distinct patterns: Gemma-3 exhibits a genuine ITE; Qwen2.5 shows a mere exposure effect; GPT-5-nano displays no repetition effect on truth and mild skepticism toward repeated content; Llama-3.1 shows a small truth boost alongside decreases in evaluative dimensions. Crucially, temperature has no effect on these findings, and a variance decomposition highlights the high context-sensitivity of LLM rating behavior. Our findings caution against assuming uniform ITE replication across LLMs in social simulations, while suggesting that Gemma-3-4b-it may offer the most behaviorally realistic approximation for misinformation-related simulations.

</details>


### [301] [CheatBench: Measuring Reward Gaming in AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36308)
*Long Phan,Stephen K. Yang,Jason J. Lim,Mantas Mazeika,Wenyu Zhang,Zheyuan Liu,Richard Ren,Jingxiang Meng,Yaoteng Tan,Weiliang Zhao,Addison Wu,Matei Anghel,Dan Hendrycks*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 CheatBench——一个衡量 AI 智能体"作弊行为"的基准，覆盖数学研究、知识工作、编程、视觉任务等领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习虽让 AI 智能体解决越来越难的任务，但高奖励并不总代表完成用户真正期望的工作。近期事件与受控评估显示，以最大化奖励为目标的智能体曾访问未授权信息、试图规避监控，甚至突破沙箱防护攻击外部系统。随着能力增强，此类行为风险日益严重，因此需要量化这一"作弊"问题。

Method: 构建 CheatBench 基准，包含数学研究、知识工作、编程、视觉任务等多个领域。其环境将高难度任务与作弊机会相结合，使研究者能够考察智能体在诚实工作困难时如何追求目标。

Result: CheatBench 支持跨模型、跨任务类别的比较，为衡量和减少智能体作弊行为提供测试平台；基准已在 https://cheatbench.ai 公开发布。

Conclusion: 随着智能体承担更关键的责任，CheatBench 可作为测量与降低作弊行为的测试平台，推动对齐与安全研究。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has helped AI agents solve increasingly difficult tasks, but high rewards do not always reflect the work users intended. In recent incidents and controlled evaluations across the AI industry, agents trained to maximize reward have accessed unauthorized information, attempted to evade monitoring systems, and even breached sandbox protections to attack external systems. As agents become more capable, this behavior could pose increasingly serious risks. To measure this problem, we introduce CheatBench, a benchmark of cheating in AI agents across mathematical research, knowledge work, coding, visual tasks, and other domains. Its environments combine challenging assignments with opportunities to cheat, allowing researchers to study how agents pursue a goal when honest work is difficult. CheatBench supports comparisons across models and task categories, providing a testbed for measuring and reducing cheating as agents take on more consequential responsibilities. We publicly release CheatBench at https://cheatbench.ai

</details>


### [302] [StateTape: Action-Conditioned Evidence Lifecycle Modeling for Long-Horizon Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36319)
*Ziyang Yu,Liang Zhao,Bowen Zhu,Hasibul Haque*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 StateTape 框架，将编码智能体的上下文维护从"基于文本历史"改为"基于代码图随仓库变更的重写"，通过符号级代码图与写入磁带追踪失效记录，在六个智能体与三个编辑密集型基准上提升解决率且开销极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长周期编码任务中，每条观察都会追加进上下文，导致上下文不断膨胀。现有基于历史的维护方法（掩码、摘要、剪枝）仅依据历史文本判断，无法感知代码之间的连接关系；而编码智能体在单任务中会多次编辑代码，每次写入都可能改变其他代码的含义，因而这类维护会保留已被写入证伪的记录、丢弃仍然有效的记录、并遗漏智能体下一步所需的代码。

Method: StateTape 将仓库建模为符号级代码图，利用其依赖关系与语言规则揭示一次写入可能影响的符号；在此图上用"磁带"(tape) 标记每次写入所改动的符号，把"陈旧性"从对文本的推断转变为对智能体写入行为的观察。进一步提出逐写入流程：由磁带提名该次写入可能证伪的记录，再由一个小型 manager 模型裁定写入日志无法确定的部分。同时给出理论分析，并构建 TraceBench 基准，用于标注智能体当前持有的内容与真正所需内容之间的差距。

Result: StateTape 能有效清除被证伪的记录并检索到所需内容，在涵盖六个编码智能体与三个编辑密集型基准的所有实验中均取得更高的 resolve rate，且计算开销很小。

Conclusion: 上下文维护应随仓库变更而非随上下文增长而进行；以符号级代码图为基础的磁带机制可将失效判定转化为可观察的写入事件，兼顾正确性与效率，为长周期编码智能体的上下文管理提供了可扩展方案。

Abstract: Despite the recent success of coding agents built on large language models, it remains challenging to run them over long horizons, since every observation is appended to the context and the context grows with each one. History-based maintenance is a common remedy, which masks or summarizes old observations, or prunes what a model reads as useless, and bounds the context at little cost. However, it decides from the text of the history alone and sees nothing of how the code is connected. Since a coding agent edits code many times over a single task, and each write can change what code elsewhere means, such maintenance may keep records a write has falsified, drop ones that still hold, and miss code the agent needs next. To overcome these challenges, this paper proposes StateTape, a novel and scalable framework that rewrites a coding agent's context as the repository changes rather than as the context grows. The key idea of StateTape is to model the repository as a symbol-level code graph, whose dependencies and language rules expose which symbols a write can affect. Upon this graph, a tape marks the symbols each write changed, which turns staleness from an inference about text into an observation of the agent's writes. We propose a per-write procedure in which the tape nominates the records a write could have falsified while a small manager model settles what the write log cannot, and further provide a theoretical analysis and TraceBench, a benchmark that labels what an agent is holding against what is actually needed. Empirically, we demonstrate that StateTape can effectively clear falsified records and retrieve what is needed, and thus achieve a higher resolve rate in all experiments spanned by six coding agents and three edit-heavy benchmarks with little computational overhead.

</details>


### [303] [Towards an AI Software Factory for Data Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36323)
*Anna Pavlenko,Bogdan Crivat,Brandon Haynes,Carlo Curino,Fotis Psallidas,Jaro Slawinski,Johannes Freischuetz,Laura Pereira Sanchez,Markus Weimer,Mathieu Demarne,Matthias Jasny,Mauktik Gandhi,Max Bovykin,Mirco Milletari,Purbasha Ghosh,Qiushi Bai,Raghu Ramakrishnan,Rahul Pandita,Sergiy Matusevich,Shivaram Venkataraman,Subru Krishnan,Md. Tareq Mahmood,Tiemo Bang,Venkatesh Emani,Xuan Zhao,Yiwen Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出构建加速软件开发生命周期（SDLC）全阶段的"AI SW Factory"，通过元数据自改进机制，在Microsoft大规模部署中实现较智能体编码高3倍的工程效率和最高22倍的token效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助编码工具虽显著加速编码环节，但对端到端SDLC整体影响有限，呈现Amdahl定律效应——仅优化单一阶段无法带来整体效率的大幅提升。因此需要构建能加速SDLC所有阶段（Targeting、Coding、Reviewing、Ops）的AI SW Factory。

Method: 聚焦数据系统与"演化编码任务"（具有可度量目标、可爬山优化的任务类），构建AI SW Factory。该工厂产生元数据"尾气"（metadata exhaust），通过微调模型权重和更新"世界模型"（World Model，一种丰富的数据基底）来实现自我改进。

Result: 在Microsoft进行规模化部署（数十个代码仓库），实现比智能体编码高3倍的工程效率，以及最高22倍的token效率，并报告了若干开放挑战。

Conclusion: 加速SDLC全阶段需超越单一编码环节的优化；通过元数据驱动的自我改进与演化编码任务的规模化实践，可显著提升整体开发效率，但仍面临若干待解决的开放问题。

Abstract: AI-assisted coding tools deliver significant acceleration of coding, but only limited impact across the end-to-end software development lifecycle (SDLC)--an Amdahl's law effect!
  In this paper, we discuss our progress towards building an AI SW Factory that accelerates all the stages of SDLC-Targeting, Coding, Reviewing, and Ops. The AI SW Factory produces a metadata exhaust that enables self-improvement by fine-tuning model weights and updating our World Model (a rich data substrate).
  We focus on Data Systems and the important class of Evolutionary Coding Tasks (i.e., those with a measurable objective to hill-climb) and report on 1) scaled deployments at Microsoft (tens of repositories) leading to 3x engineering efficiency above agentic coding and up to 22x token efficiency, and 2) several open challenges.

</details>


### [304] [PILLAR: Private Inverted-Index Lexical Lookup for Augmented Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36326)
*Truong Son Nguyen,Daniel Blackley,Ni Trieu,Evgenios M. Kornaropoulos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PILLAR 是一种基于私有信息检索（PIR）的隐私保护 RAG 系统，通过两阶段（稀疏+稠密）混合检索，在服务器完全不知查询内容与访问模式的前提下，实现低延迟且高质量的检索，并提供 PILLAR-Bin 与 PILLAR-Tree 两种协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RAG 将用户查询直接交给语料托管方，导致查询词项与访问模式泄露给服务器。已有的隐私保护 RAG（PPRAG）方案仅依赖稠密检索，需要将近似最近邻搜索转化为多轮依赖查询的 PIR 操作，从而在延迟与检索质量上付出较大代价。

Method: PILLAR 采用两阶段私有混合检索：稀疏阶段针对预先计算的 BM25 分数索引发起少量固定轮数的 PIR 查询，在服务器不知具体词项的情况下筛出与查询共享词项的候选文档；稠密阶段仅取回这些候选文档的嵌入并在本地重排，避免私有稠密检索所需的大量昂贵 PIR 查询。系统以两种私有词法检索实现为实例：PILLAR-Bin 将倒排列表分桶进哈希表，为单轮设计，延迟低于现有最先进私有检索方案；PILLAR-Tree 将块最大值剪枝转为不经意树遍历并结合布谷鸟哈希表。

Result: PILLAR-Bin 延迟低于现有最先进私有检索方案；PILLAR-Tree 在延迟低于现有最先进方案的同时取得最高的检索质量。

Conclusion: 通过将私有稀疏检索与本地稠密重排结合，PILLAR 在不泄露查询词项及访问模式的前提下，同时改善了隐私保护 RAG 的延迟与检索质量，并提供面向不同延迟—质量权衡的两种协议。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) hands the user's query to whoever hosts the corpus. We propose PILLAR, a Privacy-Preserving RAG (PPRAG) system based on Private Information Retrieval (PIR) in which a client utilizes the k documents most similar to their query from a server-held and publicly known corpus to respond to their query, while the server learns nothing about the query, either its terms or its access pattern. Prior PPRAG constructions rely on dense retrieval alone, translating approximate nearest-neighbor search into many query-dependent rounds of PIR, and pay for it in both latency and retrieval quality. PILLAR instead performs private hybrid retrieval in two stages. A sparse stage issues a small, fixed number of PIR queries against a carefully designed index of precomputed BM25 scores, filtering the corpus down to candidates that share terms with the query without the server ever seeing which terms these are. A dense stage then fetches only those candidates' document embeddings and re-ranks them locally, avoiding the many costly PIR queries that private dense retrieval typically requires. We instantiate PILLAR with two protocols that trade latency against retrieval quality, each built on a different private rendering of lexical search. PILLAR-Bin bins posting lists into a hash table and is a single-round design that achieves lower latency than state-of-the-art private retrieval schemes. PILLAR-Tree turns block-max pruning into an oblivious tree traversal combined with cuckoo hash tables and achieves the highest retrieval quality at lower latency than state-of-the-art schemes.

</details>


### [305] [ThuRunel: Dynamic Decoupling for Structured Advisory Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36340)
*Yuyan Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出高风险咨询场景的"两阶段结构"（前期共情引导、后期专家判断），将核心设计挑战形式化为"动态解耦"，并给出咨询智能体ThuRunel（有限状态信念管理+思维链教师合成+学习式生成适配器），在11个基线上取得引导完整性与专家简报质量的提升，并已上线双语Web应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗美容、法律咨询、教育规划等高风险咨询领域存在两阶段结构：早期需要共情式信息引导与情感支持，后期需要权威的专家判断。全自动智能体缺乏权威性，初级人类顾问难以规模化，二者均无法同时满足该结构，因此需要研究AI咨询智能体如何在规模化条件下完成两阶段的衔接。

Method: 将核心设计挑战形式化为"动态解耦"（dynamic decoupling），即决定问什么、何时停止、哪些自主解决、哪些转交专家；据此构建ThuRunel智能体，包含三个组件：有限状态信念管理框架、思维链教师合成协议、学习式生成适配器。从客户端一侧同样执行解耦决策，并基于可溯源的知识库（每条回答均标注引用来源）。

Result: 在11个基线的对比实验中，ThuRunel在引导完整性（elicitation completeness）与专家简报质量（specialist brief quality）两项指标上均取得一致的改进。系统已作为双语Web应用公开部署。

Conclusion: 面向高风险咨询的两阶段结构，"动态解耦"是一种有效的问题形式化；结合信念管理、教师合成与生成适配器的ThuRunel能够同时提升前期引导与后期简报质量，并具备可部署性与答案可溯源性。

Abstract: High-stakes advisory domains such as medical aesthetics, legal consultation, and educational planning exhibit a two-phase structure. The early phase requires empathetic elicitation and emotional support, and the late phase requires authoritative specialist judgment. Neither fully automated agents nor human junior consultants adequately address this structure at scale. We formalize the core design challenge as dynamic decoupling, asking how an AI advisory agent should decide what to ask, when to stop, what to resolve autonomously, and what to forward to the specialist. We present ThuRunel, an advisory agent combining a finite-state belief management framework, a chain-of-thought teacher synthesis protocol, and learned generation adapters. Against eleven baselines, ThuRunel achieves consistent improvements in elicitation completeness and specialist brief quality. ThuRunel is publicly deployed as a bilingual web application in which the same decoupling decisions operate from the client's side, grounded in a curated knowledge base that cites its sources in every answer.

</details>


### [306] [HyperZip: Efficient Data Compression through Personalized Diffusion LLMs with Hypernetworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36357)
*Thai Nguyen,Khang Tran,NhatHai Phan*

Main category: cs.AI
