TL;DR: HyperZip：基于扩散式LLM与多token预测的高效无损压缩框架，用超网络生成数据特定更新，兼顾低压缩率与高吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM无损压缩受限于自回归解码的高计算成本与低吞吐，需在保持压缩率的同时提升速度。

Method: 采用扩散式LLM（dLLM）结合多token预测（MTP）加速压缩；识别到解码吞吐提升会恶化压缩率的权衡；引入超网络，从上下文表示生成数据特定的参数更新，使dLLM适配目标数据而无需昂贵微调。

Result: 大量实验表明，与当前最优基线相比，HyperZip在压缩率与速度之间取得了更优的权衡。

Conclusion: HyperZip通过扩散解码与超网络自适应，实现了高效可扩展的LLM无损压缩，缓解了压缩率与吞吐间的矛盾。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong potential for lossless data compression, but existing approaches are constrained by the high computational cost and low throughput of autoregressive decoding. We propose HyperZip, an efficient and scalable LLM-based compression framework that leverages diffusion-based LLMs (dLLMs) with Multi-Token Prediction (MTP) to accelerate LLM-based data compression processes. We identify a trade-off in diffusion-based compression, where increasing decoding throughput degrades the compression rate. To mitigate this trade-off, HyperZip employs a hypernetwork to generate data-specific updates from a context representation, adapting the dLLM to the target data without costly fine-tuning, resulting in a low compression rate and high throughput. Extensive experiments show that HyperZip achieves a superior trade-off between compression rate and speed compared with state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [307] [Better Nearest Neighbor Graph Indices via (Efficient) LLM-Guided Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36359)
*Fangzhou Wu,Haike Xu,Sandeep Silwal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出LLM引导的图剪枝框架LGP，通过LLM推理直接精炼ANN图索引，缓解图构建的几何结构与检索评估的语义相关性之间的"几何-语义"错配，在DiskANN与HNSW上均优于原始贪心搜索和LLM重排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于图的近似最近邻搜索（ANNS）广泛用于大规模语义检索，但其索引主要依据嵌入向量间的几何关系构建，而非显式优化语义相关性；然而下游检索却以结果与查询的语义相关性来评估。这种索引构建方式与检索评估标准之间的"几何-语义"错配是根本性结构问题。现有LLM重排序方法仅在查询时部分缓解该错配，未解决图结构本身的缺陷。

Method: 提出LLM-Guided Graph Pruning（LGP），一个通用框架，直接利用LLM推理来精炼现有ANN图索引。具体而言，LGP识别节点在结构上"低价值"的邻居，并用LLM选择的替代邻居替换它们：这些替代邻居既提供有用的语义信息，又保留原图所需的几何结构特性，包括稀疏性与高效可导航性。

Result: 在代表性语义检索基准上的实验表明，LGP在多种主流基于图的ANN索引（如DiskANN和HNSW）上，端到端检索性能持续优于原始贪心图搜索以及基于LLM的重排序方法。

Conclusion: LGP通过将LLM语义推理引入图索引构建阶段，从结构层面直接缓解几何-语义错配，是一种可推广、可叠加于现有ANN索引之上的改进方案。

Abstract: Graph-based approximate nearest neighbor search (ANNS) is widely used for large-scale semantic search. Its indices are constructed primarily based on geometric relationships among embeddings of an input dataset (e.g., documents or images), rather than explicitly optimizing for semantic relevance. However, when using these indices for downstream query retrieval, performance is evaluated based on the semantic relevance of the retrieved results to the query. This creates a fundamental "geometry-semantic" mismatch between how the indices are constructed and how their retrieval results are evaluated. While existing LLM-based reranking methods can partially mitigate this mismatch at query time, they leave this underlying structural problem in the graph unresolved. We therefore propose LLM-Guided Graph Pruning (LGP), a general framework that addresses this mismatch directly by leveraging LLM reasoning to refine an existing ANN graph index itself. LGP identifies structurally "low-value" neighbors of nodes and replaces them with LLM-selected alternatives that provide useful semantic information while retaining desired geometric structures of the original graph, including sparsity and efficient navigability. Experiments on representative semantic retrieval benchmarks show that LGP consistently improves end-to-end retrieval performance over both vanilla greedy graph search and LLM-based reranking across widely used graph-based ANN indices such as DiskANN and HNSW.

</details>


### [308] [Engineering Simplicity: Simple Mechanism Interfaces Steer LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36365)
*Kehang Zhu,Anand Shah,David Parkes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究以拍卖与匹配等多智能体环境为基准，考察交互格式与文本支架对LLM决策质量的影响，并检验决策改善是否伴随解释质量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常以语言解释衡量LLM智能体的策略理解，但缺少既有明确最优策略基准、又可操纵问题呈现方式的测试环境。作者借人类简化性理论，探索界面设计与推理支架能否让智能体做出更优决策，并厘清"行为改善"与"解释改善"之间的关系。

Method: 在拍卖与匹配两类规则明确、存在已知最优策略的多智能体环境中，操纵问题呈现方式：对比要求完整出价/完整排序的界面与使安全选择更易识别的序贯界面；随后固定交互格式，变化推理支架与规则描述。跨四个模型家族测量出价偏离、排序误差及简短计划中的语言策略指标。

Result: 递增式拍卖界面显著降低出价偏离；匹配任务表明序贯回答需要与完整排序不同的误差核算方式。呈现收益或然性与解释"说真话为何安全"能改善选择，而提示规划匹配轮次或形成对对手的信念则整体上损害表现。拍卖中行为收益并未伴随语言策略指标的相应提升；其他提示能改变这些指标却不改善出价。

Conclusion: 人类简化性理论可为人工智能体决策环境设计提供指导；支架评估应同时基于实际选择与解释，因为二者改善并不必然同步。

Abstract: Can interaction formats and textual scaffolds help large language model (LLM) agents make better decisions, and do better decisions come with better explanations? We study these questions in auctions and matching, multi-agent environments with explicit rules and known optimal strategies. These settings let us vary how a decision problem is presented while retaining a benchmark for evaluating behavior. Drawing on human-motivated theories of simplicity, we compare interfaces that elicit a complete bid or ranking with sequential interfaces that make safe choices easier to identify. We then hold the interaction format fixed and vary reasoning scaffolds and rule descriptions. Across four model families, the ascending auction interface substantially reduces bid deviations. The matching comparison also shows why sequential responses require different error accounting from complete rankings. Laying out payoff contingencies and explaining why truth-telling is safe also improve choices, whereas prompts to plan through matching rounds or form beliefs about opponents worsen play overall. In auctions, these behavioral gains are not accompanied by corresponding improvements in measured verbal indicators of strategic understanding in the agents' short stated plans. Other prompts change those indicators without improving bids. Our findings suggest that human-motivated theories of simplicity can inform the design of decision environments for artificial agents. They also show why scaffolds should be evaluated through realized choices as well as explanations: improvements in one need not appear in the other.

</details>


### [309] [Support-Set Target Leakage in Relational Foundation Models during In-Context Learning: Impact, Detection, and Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36384)
*Roshan Reddy Upendra,Alexandre Dorais,Joe Meyer,Andrew Pouret,Anastasios Lambrianos Stappas,Dinesh Katupputhur Ramprasath,Tom Palczewski,Minghua Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将关系型上下文学习中的一种新失效模式形式化为"支持集目标泄漏"：目标派生（泄漏）列仅存在于有标签的支持集中而查询集干净。作者构造14类合成泄漏列（共20列），在RelBench上评估冻结关系编码器+ICL头，并用Integrated Gradients定位并移除可疑列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关系型ICL依赖支持集的标签与关联表进行预测，当支持集包含查询阶段不可得的目标派生特征时会产生失效。该问题不同于数据集构建泄漏、时间切分泄漏或表示学习泄漏，此前缺乏独立的形式化与系统评估。

Method: 形式化"支持集目标泄漏"设定；构造14种合成leaker类型、对应20列，覆盖不同噪声水平、覆盖率、模态、语义透明度与关系距离；在留出的RelBench数据库上评估冻结关系编码器加ICL头；使用Integrated Gradients对可疑列排序并移除。

Result: 支持集泄漏的影响随任务与关系距离而异：目标表leaker造成最明显性能退化，而一跳、两跳leaker未被模型一致使用；IG在多个数据集上对目标表leaker排序靠前，并在泄漏影响最大的设定中部分恢复性能。

Conclusion: 支持集目标泄漏是关系型ICL中独立且值得关注的失效模式；目标表泄漏危害最大，IG可作为有效的诊断与缓解手段，但一跳/两跳泄漏与缓解的完整性仍有待进一步研究。

Abstract: Relational in-context learning (ICL) conditions predictions on the labeled support examples and their linked tables, creating a failure mode when the support set contains target-derived features that are unavailable for the query. We formulate this problem as support-set target leakage, distinct from leakage during dataset construction, temporal splitting, or representation learning. Here, the target-derived (leaker) columns are present only in the labeled support set during relational in-context inference, while queries remain clean. We construct 14 synthetic leaker types, corresponding to 20 columns, spanning proxies with different noise levels, coverage, modalities, semantic transparency, and relational distances. We evaluate a frozen relational encoder with an ICL head on held-out RelBench databases and use Integrated Gradients (IG) to rank and remove suspicious columns. Our results show that the effect of support-set leakage varies across tasks and relational distances. Target-table leakers cause the clearest degradation, while one- and two-hop leakers are not consistently used by the model. IG ranks target-table leakers highly across datasets and partially recovers performance in settings where leakage has the largest effect.

</details>


### [310] [Persona Dosing: Calibrated Activation Steering for Graded Trait Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36388)
*Zehao Jin,Junran Wang,Ruixuan Deng,Jiahao Chen,Jingyuan Zhang,Yuxuan Zhang,Xinjie Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 PersonaDose，通过共享的、描述条件化的 FLAS 控制器实现“人格剂量”控制，即用特质描述与请求的平均强度来操控语言模型，并借助流量时间标定实现强度对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活引导系数虽能设定干预强度，但用户期望的是某种人格表达程度，这是一种行为尺度，二者之间缺少可直接映射的桥梁；且训练响应并未与请求的目标强度配对，导致强度请求难以落地。

Method: PersonaDose 先在人格响应上特化一个共享的、由描述条件化的 FLAS 控制器，再用测得的特质表达对标定其 flow time；训练数据不含请求目标强度，因此标定用于建立从请求强度到控制参数的行为映射。

Result: 在 Llama-3.1-8B、Qwen3-8B、Gemma-3-4B 上，PersonaDose 在 Persona Vectors 的 75 连贯性下限处，核心特质表达较对比式激活加法分别高出 33.2、18.3、17.8 分；标定后设定的参数在留出问题上保持表达优势，但并非每个特质都能维持该连贯性下限。七个已训练特质上，标定请求在每模型 28 个目标中可标定覆盖 14-22 个，平均瞄准误差为 4.7-6.2 分。

Conclusion: 结果区分了控制器所学到的行为范围与其范围内请求的准确度，说明“能表达多强”与“能否精准命中请求强度”是两个可分离的能力维度。

Abstract: An activation-steering coefficient sets intervention strength, but requesting a particular degree of persona expression requires a behavioral scale. We study persona dosing: controlling a language model through a trait description and a requested mean intensity. PersonaDose specializes a shared, description-conditioned FLAS controller on persona responses, then calibrates its flow time against measured trait expression. Training responses are not paired with requested target intensities. Across Llama-3.1-8B, Qwen3-8B, and Gemma-3-4B, PersonaDose raises core-trait expression at the Persona Vectors coherence floor of 75 by 33.2, 18.3, and 17.8 points over contrastive activation addition. Calibration-selected settings retain an expression advantage on held-out questions, although the coherence floor does not hold for every trait there. Across seven trained traits, calibrated requests yield mean targeting errors of 4.7-6.2 points over 14-22 calibration-reachable targets out of 28 per model. These results separate the behavioral range learned by a controller from the accuracy of requests within that range.

</details>


### [311] [ARCagent: An Adaptive Retrieval Calibration Agent for Clinical Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36392)
*Yuyan Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ARCagent，一个面向ME/CFS的自适应检索校准临床问答代理，通过冲突感知的知识库与检索校准管线，在LLM-as-Judge基准上达到95.3%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在临床指南不完整、存在争议或相互矛盾的疾病场景中，标准RAG系统无法提供知识完备性与动态冲突感知的综合能力，这对医疗安全至关重要。

Method: ARCagent包含三个组件：(1) 一个1,706个文本块、10个来源的知识库，附带覆盖所有现行ME/CFS诊断框架的结构化指南间冲突登记表；(2) 一个冲突感知的检索校准管线，利用查询特定的关注信号与冲突信号对检索证据进行重排序；(3) 一个由LLM-as-Judge评分的基准，避免关键词匹配导致的系统性低估。

Result: ARCagent取得95.3%的成绩，优于所有基线LLM；同时证明关键词匹配会带来平均10.1个百分点的系统性低估。

Conclusion: ARCagent通过冲突感知的知识库构建与检索校准，为指南矛盾场景下的临床问答提供了兼具知识完备性与冲突感知的安全解决方案。

Abstract: In diseases where clinical guidelines are incomplete, contested, or mutually contradictory, knowledge completeness and dynamic conflict-aware synthesis are two safety-critical properties that standard Retrieval-Augmented Generation systems do not provide. Therefore, we present \sysname, an adaptive retrieval calibration clinical question-answering agent for ME/CFS, a disease where diagnostic frameworks coexist and major guidelines actively contradict each other on treatment. ARCagent contributes three components. First, a 1,706-chunk, 10-source knowledge base with a structured inter-guideline conflict registry spanning all active ME/CFS diagnostic frameworks. Second, a conflict-aware retrieval calibration pipeline that re-ranks retrieved evidence using query-specific focus and conflict signals. Third, a benchmark scored by LLM-as-Judge, avoiding systematic underestimation averaging 10.1 percentage points caused by keyword matching. ARCagent achieves 95.3%, outperforming all base LLMs. Code is available at https://github.com/Yukyin/ARCagent.

</details>


### [312] [From Retrieval to Reasoning: Agentic Mechanism Prediction from Cell Painting Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36406)
*Jiayuan Chen,Botao Yu,Tianyu Liu,Thai-Hoang Pham,Meng Wu,Ping Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文将Cell Painting的MOA预测从"表示匹配"重构为"校准证据推理"，提出可靠性感知多智能体框架PhenoAIR，在JUMP基准上超越表示匹配与LLM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Cell Painting表型推断（尤其是MOA预测）普遍采用表示匹配范式，即依据形态特征空间中的近邻参考扰动直接给出预测。然而检索到的近邻常因批次效应、非特异性细胞毒性、表型收敛以及来源依赖性变异而成为噪声甚至误导性证据，导致推断不可靠。因此需要将邻居视为需要评估、比较乃至剔除的不确定观测，而非可直接采信的证据。

Method: 提出PhenoAIR，一个可靠性感知的多智能体框架：维护以候选机制为中心的证据记忆，并在控制器引导下对表型侧与机制侧证据进行迭代精化；通过离线参考集校准，依据来源可靠性、表型稳定性与机制层混淆度对证据进行加权。

Result: 在基于JUMP Cell Painting图谱与注释构建的基准上评估，覆盖受控、真实化以及发现导向的开放世界MOA预测三种设置，PhenoAIR在所有设置下均优于表示匹配方法与基于LLM的基线。

Conclusion: 将Cell Painting的MOA预测建模为校准证据推理问题、并引入可靠性加权的多智能体证据精化机制，可有效抑制噪声邻居带来的误导，提升机制预测的稳健性与泛化能力，为表型组学推断提供了新的方法论范式。

Abstract: Cell Painting is a high-content morphological profiling assay widely used for phenotype-based biological inference, with mechanism of action (MOA) prediction as a central application. Existing approaches largely formulate Cell Painting-based inference as representation matching, assigning predictions from nearby reference perturbations in morphological feature space. However, retrieved neighbors are often noisy and partially misleading evidence due to batch effects, non-specific cytotoxicity, phenotypic convergence, and source-dependent variability. We reformulate Cell Painting-based MOA prediction as a calibrated evidence reasoning problem, where retrieved neighbors are treated as uncertain observations that must be evaluated, compared, and sometimes rejected before supporting a mechanistic conclusion. We propose PhenoAIR, a reliability-aware multi-agent framework that maintains a candidate-centric evidence memory and performs controller-guided refinement over phenotype- and mechanism-side evidence. PhenoAIR uses offline reference-set calibration to weight evidence by source reliability, phenotype stability, and mechanism-level confusion. We evaluate PhenoAIR on a benchmark constructed from JUMP Cell Painting profiles and annotations, covering controlled, realistic, and discovery-oriented open-world MOA prediction settings. PhenoAIR outperforms representation-matching and LLM-based baselines across all settings.

</details>


### [313] [Support-Set Target Leakage in Relational Foundation Models during In-Context Learning: Model Dependence and Evaluation Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36417)
*Roshan Reddy Upendra,Alexandre Dorais,Joe Meyer,Andrew Pouret,Anastasios Lambrianos Stappas,Dinesh Katupputhur Ramprasath,Viswanath Ganapathy,Tom Palczewski,Minghua Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究关系型上下文学习（Relational ICL）中的支持集目标泄漏问题，构建20个受控的目标派生特征，在13个RelBench任务和5种关系型ICL配置下评估0-hop、1-hop、2-hop及Full泄漏的影响，发现0-hop与Full泄漏造成的评估偏差最大，泄漏效应高度依赖任务与模型，且现有泄漏检测方法难以稳定恢复干净评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关系型ICL使用带标签的支持样本及其关联的关系上下文来预测新查询的标签。当支持上下文中存在由目标派生（target-derived）的特征、而查询端却无法获得这些特征时，便会产生一种失效模式。作者将这一情形形式化为"支持集目标泄漏"（support-set target leakage），旨在揭示支持/查询信息边界对可靠评估的重要性。

Method: 作者构造了20个受控的目标派生特征，在信号保真度、表征形式、语义透明度、覆盖率以及0-hop、1-hop、2-hop关系位置等维度上变化；在13个RelBench任务与5种关系型ICL配置（变化ICL head、消息传递深度、预训练队列或关系编码器架构）上进行评估。实验对比匹配的0-hop、1-hop、2-hop泄漏设置及包含全部20个泄漏列的Full泄漏条件。在泄漏检测方面，比较了基于Integrated Gradients（IG）的筛选方法与互信息（MI）、留一列（LOCO）方法，并开展四任务的rel-salt案例研究。

Result: 在测试配置内，目标表（0-hop）与Full泄漏相对干净评估产生的总体偏差最大，而更高跳数的效应通常更弱，这与时间可达性、采样和聚合保真度带来的有效暴露差异一致。泄漏效应强烈依赖任务与模型，即使在总体变化较小时也可能反转不同模型变体之间的相对结论。在泄漏检测上，排序质量在影响最大的0-hop与Full条件下最强，但基于检测器的移除并不能稳定恢复干净评估。

Conclusion: 支持/查询信息边界是可靠关系型ICL评估的重要组成部分。研究提示，在评估关系型ICL时需显式考虑支持集中目标派生特征带来的泄漏风险，且仅依赖现有检测方法难以保证评估的干净性。

Abstract: Relational in-context learning (ICL) uses labeled support examples and their linked relational context to predict labels for new queries. This creates a failure mode when target-derived features are present in the support context but unavailable for the query. We study this setting as support-set target leakage. We construct 20 controlled target-derived features that vary in signal fidelity, representation, semantic transparency, coverage, and zero-, one-, and two-hop relational placement, and evaluate them across 13 RelBench tasks and five relational ICL configurations that vary the ICL head, message-passing depth, pretraining cohort, or relational encoder architecture. We evaluate matched 0-hop, 1-hop, and 2-hop leakage settings, together with a Full leakage condition containing all 20 leaker columns. Within the tested configurations, target-table (0-hop) and Full leakage produce the largest aggregate deviations from clean evaluation, while higher-hop effects are often weaker, consistent with differences in effective exposure associated with temporal reachability, sampling, and aggregation fidelity. Leakage effects are strongly task- and model-dependent and can reverse relative conclusions between model variants even when aggregate changes are small. For leaker detection, we compare an Integrated Gradients (IG)-based screening method with mutual information (MI) and leave-one-column-out (LOCO) on a common Baseline subset. Ranking quality is strongest in the high-impact 0-hop and Full leakage conditions, but detector-based removal does not consistently restore the clean evaluation. A four-task rel-salt case study further shows the same evaluation concern with native-schema leakage candidates from the original relational schema. These results identify the support/query information boundary as an important component of reliable relational ICL evaluation.

</details>


### [314] [The Safety Operator: Modulating the Expression of Safety Instructions via Spectral Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36434)
*Benoit Dherin,Michael Munn,Xavier Gonzalvo,Adrian Goldwaser,Blaz Bratanic,Ananth Balashankar,Andrey Vlasov,Pinzhi Huang,Cecile Loge,Nicole Mitchell,Andre Fernandes,Trilok Acharya,Wendy Kan,Ziyue Wang,Hanna Mazzawi,Felipe Tiengo Ferreira,Mor Geva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将上下文token吸收为权重中的乘性算子，其主特征值充当安全指令影响力的连续调节旋钮，据此提出带抑制权重的对比安全损失，可获得帕累托改进的安全指令。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究上下文token被吸收进Transformer权重后所形成的乘性算子，尤其是其在安全指令场景中的作用机制，并寻找可视化或可调控指令影响强度的手段。

Method: 将安全指令相关的上下文token视为权重中的乘性算子，分析其主特征值对生成的影响；设计对比安全损失（Contrastive Safety Loss），引入抑制权重，在有害查询上强化安全指令、在无害查询上抑制安全指令，并调节该权重以观察攻击成功率与过度拒答率之间的关系。

Result: 抑制权重的变化在攻击成功率与过度拒答率之间勾勒出可映射的关系，支持算子主特征值作为指令影响力连续调节旋钮的假设；且该关系在安全损失参数化方式上保持相对独立，在合适的抑制权重下可得到帕累托改进的安全指令。

Conclusion: 乘性算子的主特征值可充当安全指令影响力的连续控制变量，通过调节对比安全损失中的抑制权重，能够在攻击成功与过度拒答之间实现帕累托改进，为安全指令的设计与优化提供新的机制性视角。

Abstract: Context tokens in a transformer-based language model can be absorbed into the model's weights as a multiplicative operator. We study this operator in the setting of safety instructions and show that influencing its dominant eigenvalue modulates how strongly the instruction shapes generation. We derive a Contrastive Safety Loss with a suppression weight that controls the tradeoff between emphasizing the safety instruction on harmful queries while suppressing it on harmless queries. Varying the suppression weight maps a relationship between the attack success and the over-refusal rates, supporting the hypothesis that the operator's eigenvalue acts as a continuous dial for the instruction's influence. Moreover, this relationship holds relatively independently of how the Safety Loss is parameterized, yielding Pareto-improved safety instructions for appropriate values of suppression weight.

</details>


### [315] [Bits Under ZK-LLM: Evaluating Zero-Knowledge-Friendly Quantization for Verifiable Private LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36437)
*Taeung Yoon,Yupeng Zhang,Xiaojing Liao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究面向LLM的ZK友好量化，形式化其定义，并在9个模型（含Qwen2.5-14B、Qwen3-30B-A3B）上评估权重、激活与非线性查找表精度设计空间，揭示激活精度比权重更敏感、非线性近似易成效用退化主因，并指出低比特启发式不能直接转化为证明效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零知识证明为LLM治理与审计提供隐私、可验证方案，但ZK场景下量化直接决定算术结构、约束复杂度与证明成本；当前对ZK友好量化的理解有限，阻碍ZK-LLM实用化。

Method: 形式化ZK友好量化的定义，捕捉ZK证明生成所需性质；在权重、激活与非线性查找表精度的广泛设计空间上，评估九个语言模型，包括Qwen2.5-14B与MoE模型Qwen3-30B-A3B。

Result: 激活精度比权重精度敏感得多；非线性查找近似可成为效用退化主因；RMSNorm逆平方根查找在多个大模型中反复成为瓶颈，仅在瓶颈处选择性提高精度可恢复近基线效用；降低比特宽度或查找表规模不必然带来成比例的端到端证明开销节省。

Conclusion: 常规低比特量化启发式无法直接迁移到ZK证明效率，需要面向算子感知的精度选择策略。

Abstract: Zero-knowledge proofs are emerging as a promising approach for enabling private, verifiable LLM governance and auditing, where regulators, users, and auditors need to verify claims about training-data usage or LLM inference-time behavior, while model providers must protect proprietary model parameters. However, despite the growing interest in ZK-LLMs, the understanding of ZK-friendly quantization remains limited. This gap matters because in the ZK setting, quantization directly shapes the arithmetic structure, constraint complexity, and proving cost of ZK inference. ZK protocols operate over finite fields and incur costs that depend heavily on the number and type of arithmetic operations, nonlinearities, and lookup constraints. Understanding ZK-friendly quantization is therefore essential for making ZK-LLMs practical. In this work, we present the first systematic study of ZK-friendly quantization for LLMs. We first formalize the definition of ZK-friendly quantization, capturing the properties required for ZK proof generation. We then evaluate nine language models, including Qwen2.5-14B and the mixture-of-experts model Qwen3-30B-A3B, across a broad design space of weight, activation, and nonlinear lookup table precision. Our results show that activation precision is substantially more sensitive than weight precision, while nonlinear lookup approximations can become the dominant source of utility degradation. Also, we identify RMSNorm inverse-square-root lookups as a recurring bottleneck in several large models and recover near-baseline utility by selectively increasing precision only at the bottleneck. Finally, we show that reducing bit-width or lookup-table size does not necessarily yield proportional end-to-end proving savings, showing that conventional low-bit quantization heuristics do not directly translate to ZK proving efficiency and motivating operator-aware precision selection.

</details>


### [316] [Rethinking Reasoning Paths as Phase-Structured Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36461)
*Zhenghao He,Guangzhi Xiong,Sanchit Sinha,Bohan Liu,Wenqian Ye,Aidong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将多步推理路径视为固定问题内的分阶段轨迹，提出PAIR方法，按相对进度对齐阶段并在同问题同阶段内对比成功/失败轨迹，从而分离路径质量信号；标准跨问题正确性探针在问题内评估中大幅失效，PAIR提升了轨迹排序、Best-of-N选择，并通过阶段引导提供因果证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常把最终答案正确性标签赋给每个中间状态，并跨异质问题训练探针，这存在两个问题：(1) 正确性预测可能利用问题层面的差异而非路径质量；(2) 按绝对步索引对齐的状态在不同问题中可能对应不同的功能阶段，导致推理动态被混淆。

Method: 提出PAIR（Phase-Aligned Intra-question Reasoning）。对每个问题采样多条轨迹；基于归一化轨迹进度将变长路径映射到共享的相对阶段；仅在同一问题、同一阶段内比较成功与失败轨迹，得到阶段特定的路径质量方向。

Result: 标准的跨问题正确性探针在问题内评估下预测能力大幅下降，说明其部分依赖问题层面信息；PAIR在跨模型和基准上改善了问题内轨迹排序与Best-of-N轨迹选择；阶段级引导能改变生成结果，提供其捕获轨迹相关信息的因果证据。

Conclusion: 推理路径应被视为固定问题内具有阶段结构的轨迹；通过阶段对齐与问题内对比可更好分离路径质量信号，PAIR在轨迹评估与选择上有效，且所学方向具有因果作用。

Abstract: Large language models often improve problem-solving performance by generating multi-step reasoning paths, yet how to analyze the hidden states along these paths remains unclear. Existing approaches typically assign each intermediate state the final-answer correctness label and train probes across heterogeneous questions. We argue that this protocol obscures reasoning dynamics in two ways: (1) correctness prediction can exploit question-level variation rather than path quality, and (2) states aligned by absolute step indices may correspond to different functional phases of reasoning. In this work, we propose to view reasoning paths as phase-structured trajectories within fixed questions. We instantiate this view as PAIR, short for Phase-Aligned Intra-question Reasoning. PAIR samples multiple trajectories for each question, maps variable-length paths into shared relative phases based on normalized trajectory progress, and compares successful and unsuccessful trajectories only within the same question and phase. This yields phase-specific path-quality directions that better isolate path-quality signals from question-level variation. Empirically, we find that standard across-question correctness probes lose much of their predictive power under within-question evaluation, suggesting that these probes partly rely on question-level information. PAIR improves within-question trajectory ranking and Best-of-N trajectory selection across models and benchmarks. Phase-wise steering further shows that the learned directions can change generation outcomes, providing causal evidence that they capture trajectory-relevant information.

</details>


### [317] [Going Beyond State-Reaching: Learning Abstractions for Intrinsically Motivated Option Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36473)
*Akhil Bagaria,Anita De Mello Koch,George Konidaris*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种为每个子目标识别少量相关特征维度的选项发现算法，使选项具有广泛泛化能力并加速稀疏奖励环境中的探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模环境中，基于选项的时间抽象可改善探索，但现有选项发现算法在寻找子目标时同时针对状态的各个方面（state-reaching），导致选项仅适用于状态空间的狭窄区域，最终选项数量爆炸，压垮智能体并阻碍奖励最大化任务。

Method: 新算法不再以完整状态为子目标，而是为每个子目标识别一个小而相关的特征子集，从而生成能广泛泛化、加速探索的抽象且可迁移的选项。

Result: 在三个稀疏奖励的基于图像的环境中（包括 Atari 游戏 Montezuma's Revenge）实现了快速探索，并学到抽象、可迁移的选项。

Conclusion: 通过只关注与子目标相关的少量特征，可避免选项数量爆炸，提升选项泛化性与探索效率，为稀疏奖励图像域提供有效的分层探索方案。

Abstract: Temporal abstraction via options can improve exploration in large environments. However, existing option discovery algorithms find subgoals that target all aspects of the state simultaneously. This state-reaching approach produces options that only apply in narrow regions of the state-space, eventually causing an explosion in the number of options that overwhelms the agent, and impedes progress on its primary task of reward maximization. We introduce an algorithm that instead identifies a small, relevant subset of features for each subgoal, yielding options that generalize broadly and accelerate exploration. Our approach learns abstract, transferrable options and achieves rapid exploration in three sparse-reward, image-based domains, including the Atari game MontezumasRevenge.

</details>


### [318] [Learning to Harvest Without Collapse in a Regenerative Commons: A Lagrangian Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36478)
*Jose Tupayachi,Xueping Li,Soham Das*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文将再生性公地（commons）建模为带消耗预算约束的马尔可夫博弈/多智能体MDP，提出非平稳拉格朗日框架，通过无约束博弈解构造策略序列，给出可行性证书与近似最优性保证，并在渔业实验中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公地悲剧是多智能体安全问题：追求奖励的智能体可能耗尽共享资源，而用户间合作本身并不规定须保留多少资源。作者希望把"资源保护"变为显式约束，需一个可量化的机制来强制保留水平，并给出理论保证。

Method: 将再生性公地形式化为约束马尔可夫博弈（或约束多智能体MDP），由设计者指定消耗预算。构建非平稳拉格朗日框架，从无约束博弈/合作控制问题的解中构造策略序列；在折扣回报与终端成本的reset episode设定下，推广时间平均构造，提出平均-epoch解概念；并处理无偏采样成本。

Result: 证明了三项理论结果：(1) 与奖励无关的可行性证书；(2) 合作可行性及相对于可行策略混合的近似最优性；(3) 对无偏采样成本的推广。对自利智能体，给出约束Nash证书，量化由跨epoch重新分配预算的偏离所引入的价格-分散项。基于求解器精度与乘子更新的假设，得到使用无约束问题解的约束策略序列保证。在Gordon-Schaefer渔业中用约束IPPO和MAPPO实验，考察消耗预算对存量保留、捕捞奖励与价格适应的影响。

Conclusion: 通过显式消耗预算约束与非平稳拉格朗日框架，可将无约束求解器复用至多智能体资源保护问题，并提供可行性、近似最优性及Nash意义下的理论保证，实验显示消耗预算能有效调节渔业中的资源保留与收益权衡。

Abstract: The tragedy of the commons poses a multi-agent safety problem: reward-seeking agents can deplete a shared resource, and cooperation among its users does not itself specify how much must be preserved. We make preservation an explicit requirement by formulating a regenerative commons as a constrained Markov game or a constrained multi-agent MDP with a designer-specified depletion budget. We develop a nonstationary Lagrangian framework that constructs a policy sequence from solutions of unconstrained games or cooperative control problems. Extending earlier time-average constructions, we introduce average-epoch solution concepts for reset episodes with discounted rewards and terminal costs. We prove a reward-independent feasibility certificate, cooperative feasibility and approximate optimality against feasible policy mixtures, and an extension to unbiased sampled costs. For self-interested agents, a constrained Nash certificate quantifies the price-dispersion term introduced by deviations that redistribute budget across epochs. Under the stated assumptions on solver accuracy and multiplier updates, these results give constrained policy-sequence guarantees using solutions of unconstrained problems. Experiments with constrained IPPO and MAPPO in a Gordon-Schaefer fishery examine how depletion budgets shape stock retention, harvest rewards, and price adaptation.

</details>


### [319] [Human-AI Collaboration: From Paradoxes to Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36481)
*Michael Weiss*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文考察人机协作的两个关键设计维度——自主性（autonomy）与主动性（initiative），采用悖论视角识别其内在张力，并提出从张力浮现到悖论映射的过程，最终归纳出四种人机协作模式：指令（Instruction）、委派（Delegation）、辅助（Assistance）与共创（Co-creation）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有证据表明，人类与AI系统通过协作比各自单独工作表现更好，因此如何设计有效的人机协作成为重要问题。现有研究对人机协作的设计维度及其生成的具体协作模式缺乏系统性刻画，本文旨在弥补这一空白。

Method: 概念性/理论分析研究。从识别底层问题与解决方案入手，进而考察问题内部张力；采用悖论视角（paradox perspective）分析这些张力，提出一套浮现张力并映射底层悖论的过程，并说明如何由悖论映射推导出协作模式的描述。

Result: 基于自主性与主动性两个设计维度，识别并记录了四种人机协作模式：Instruction（指令）、Delegation（委派）、Assistance（辅助）与Co-creation（共创）。

Conclusion: 人机协作模式可通过自主性与主动性的组合及其内在悖论加以系统刻画；悖论映射为理解和设计不同协作模式提供了分析框架，有助于指导人机协作的实践与后续研究。

Abstract: Evidence shows that humans and AI systems perform better together, by collaborating, than alone. This paper examines two key design dimensions of human-AI collaboration (autonomy and initiative) and explores the collaboration patterns that they generate. Documenting these patterns starts with identifying the underlying problems and solutions, followed by examining the internal tensions within the problems. The paper uses a paradox perspective to analyze those tensions. It describes a process for surfacing the tensions and mapping the underlying paradoxes. It also illustrates how the pattern descriptions can be derived from mapping these paradoxes. Finally, the paper documents four human-AI collaboration patterns: Instruction, Delegation, Assistance, and Co-creation.

</details>


### [320] [AVIO: Learning to Add and Remove Sounding Objects in Audiovisual Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36503)
*Weihan Xu,Kan Jen Cheng,Koichi Saito,Jingyu Shi,Tingle Li,Yisi Liu,Liming Wang,Masato Ishii,Takashi Shibuya,Gopala Anumanchipalli,Paul Pu Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 AVIOBench 数据集与 AVIO 模型，用于局部非语音音视频编辑（目标对象的声音与视觉同步增删）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音视频中增删发声对象需要视觉与听觉的协同修改，同时保持周围场景不变；但现有配对的局部非语音音视频编辑监督数据有限，且视觉与音频编辑须针对同一对象并需将其声音从重叠声源中分离出来。

Method: 构建 AVIOBench 数据集（37.9 小时配对音视频样本，覆盖 1,878 个目标对象名称），通过共享身份与视觉掩码关联目标对象的视觉存在与听觉贡献；自动化流程利用视觉定位与跨模态一致性筛选目标声音移除候选，并联合精修音视频对以提升感知质量与跨模态一致性。基于该数据集提出 AVIO，通过源条件特征调制适配预训练文本到音视频生成模型，联合学习对象添加与移除；训练中采用参考帧课程逐步减少参考条件，使单一模型支持仅指令编辑并可选用视觉引导。

Result: 定量与定性评估表明，该方法能有效完成音视频对象移除与添加，可选参考引导可为添加操作提供外观与位置控制。

Conclusion: AVIOBench 与 AVIO 为局部非语音音视频编辑提供了数据与模型基础，实现了同步的音视频对象增删，并支持可选参考引导以实现外观和位置控制。

Abstract: Adding or removing a sounding object requires coordinated changes to visual content and sound while preserving the surrounding scene. Yet paired supervision for localized non-speech audiovisual editing remains limited, as visual and acoustic edits must target the same object and isolate its sound from overlapping sources. To address this gap, we introduce \textit{AVIOBench}, a dataset comprising 37.9 hours of paired audiovisual examples spanning 1{,}878 target-object names. AVIOBench links the visual presence and acoustic contribution of each target object through a shared identity and visual mask. Our automated pipeline uses visual grounding and cross-modal consistency to select target-sound removal candidates, then jointly refines the audiovisual pairs to improve perceptual quality and cross-modal consistency. Building on this dataset, we propose \textit{AVIO}, which adapts a pretrained text-to audiovisual generation model through source-conditioned feature modulation to jointly learn object addition and removal. A reference-frame curriculum gradually reduces reference conditioning during training, enabling one model to perform instruction-only editing with optional visual guidance. Quantitative and qualitative evaluations demonstrate effective audiovisual object removal and addition, with optional reference guidance providing appearance and placement control for addition.

</details>


### [321] [BRIDGE: Bilevel Retrieval-Credit-Aware Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36505)
*Quan Xiao,Mingda Liu,Gaowen Liu,Katsuki Fujisawa,Tianyi Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将检索增强的智能体强化学习建模为双层优化问题，并设计内存高效的一阶双层方法BRIDGE，联合训练检索器与LLM策略，在多个开放域QA和医学QA基准上取得最优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体强化学习（ARL）仅优化LLM生成的token，将检索到的证据视为环境观测，导致"信息信用缺口"：由缺失或误导性证据引起的失败被错误归因于LLM策略而非检索器。这促使联合训练LLM与检索器。论文进一步发现检索与策略学习存在顺序敏感性——先适配检索器再优化策略，比相反顺序带来更大奖励增益。

Method: 为在允许两者协同适配的同时保持上述层级关系，本文将检索增强的智能体RL形式化为双层优化问题，并基于对RL目标与检索目标损失景观的分析，提出内存高效的一阶双层方法BRIDGE。

Result: 在七个开放域QA基准上，BRIDGE在3B与7B骨干模型上均取得最高平均准确率，多跳平均比最强基线分别提升9.6和3.4个EM点；在医学QA基准上也取得最佳平均答案准确率与推理质量。

Conclusion: 检索与LLM策略学习具有顺序敏感性，双层优化框架可有效保持该层级并实现协同适配；BRIDGE以内存高效的一阶方式求解该问题，验证了联合训练检索器与LLM在知识密集型任务上的有效性。

Abstract: Agentic reinforcement learning (ARL) with verifiable rewards improves the ability of large language models (LLMs) to tackle knowledge-intensive tasks by learning to interleave search and reasoning. However, most existing ARL methods optimize only LLM-generated tokens and treat retrieved evidence as environment observations. This creates an information-credit gap: failures caused by missing or misleading evidence are attributed to the LLM policy rather than to the retriever, which motivates training the LLM and the retriever jointly. In this paper, we show that retrieval and LLM policy learning are order-sensitive: adapting the retriever before optimizing the policy yields a larger reward gain than the reverse order. To preserve this hierarchy while allowing both components to co-adapt, we formulate retrieval-augmented agentic RL as a bilevel optimization problem. To solve it efficiently, we introduce BRIDGE, a memory-efficient first-order bilevel method motivated by a loss-landscape analysis of the RL and retrieval objectives. Across seven open-domain QA benchmarks, BRIDGE achieves the highest average accuracy with both 3B and 7B backbones, improving the multi-hop average over the strongest baseline by 9.6 and 3.4 EM points, respectively. It also achieves the best averaged answer accuracy and reasoning quality across medical QA benchmarks.

</details>


### [322] [MAADBench: The Refreshable Paradigm for Anomaly Detection in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36556)
*Lei Ma,Dennis Hofmann,Haowen Xu,Joshua DeOliveira,Peter VanNostrand,Lei Cao,Elke Rundensteiner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 MAADBench，首个可再生（refreshable）的多智能体系统（MAS）异常检测（AD）基准，通过可采样耦合生成任务、可刷新轨迹生成与自动化步骤级标注，缓解任务泄漏、模式过期与标注成本问题，并基于 5 个 LLM 后端构建 5,200 条步骤级标注轨迹，评测 25 种 AD 方法揭示现有方法局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 驱动的多智能体系统失败率高达 41%-87%，但尚无基准支持对其开展系统性异常检测。构建此类基准的核心难点在于"保鲜"：任务可能泄漏进训练数据而被 LLM 记忆，轨迹与异常模式随骨干模型演化而失效，且每次刷新都需可靠标签。

Method: MAADBench 由三部分构成：(1) 在约 10^37 规模的任务空间上进行采样并耦合的生成式任务，以降低任务泄漏风险；(2) 在可配置 LLM 骨干下可刷新的轨迹生成机制；(3) 自动提供零成本、确定性的步骤级（step-level）标签，用于细粒度异常检测评估。

Result: 作者在 5 个前沿 LLM 骨干上运行 MAADBench，发布包含 5,200 条步骤级标注轨迹的 MAADBench-Full 数据集；在该数据集上评测 25 种异常检测方法，结果显示现有方法存在显著不足：高度依赖监督信号、难以识别 MAS 特有的细微异常、跨 LLM 骨干鲁棒性差。

Conclusion: 这些缺陷指向 MAS 专属异常检测的研究议程，而 MAADBench 为方法开发与评估提供了系统化、可刷新的测试平台。数据集已开源：https://huggingface.co/datasets/hww123/MAADBench-full。

Abstract: Recent studies report that LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) fail at rates of 41%-87%, yet to our knowledge, no benchmark to date supports systematic anomaly detection (AD) for them. Building MAS AD benchmarks is hard because they must remain fresh as LLM systems evolve: tasks may leak into training data and thus be memorized by LLMs, traces and anomaly patterns expire as backbones evolve, and labels must be provided reliably for each refresh. To address these challenges, we present MAADBench (MA: multi-agent; AD: anomaly detection), the first refreshable MAS AD benchmark designed for diverse, evolving LLM backbones underlying the agents. MAADBench combines (1) sampled-and-coupled generative tasks over an approximately 10^37-task space to mitigate task leakage, (2) refreshable trace generation under configurable LLM backbones, and (3) automated provision of cost-free, deterministic step-level labels for fine-grained AD evaluation. Beyond offering the paradigm itself, we run MAADBench with five state-of-the-art LLM backbones and release the MAADBench-Full dataset with 5,200 step-labeled traces. Benchmarking 25 AD methods on the MAADBench dataset reveals substantial limitations in current approaches: they rely heavily on supervision, struggle with subtle MAS-specific anomalies, and lack robustness across LLM backbones. These gaps point to a rich research agenda for MAS-specific anomaly detection, with MAADBench providing a systematic and refreshable testbed for method development and evaluation. We open-source MAADBench-Full at https://huggingface.co/datasets/hww123/MAADBench-full.

</details>


### [323] [Visual sensitivity is not claim retractability: persistence-aware credit assignment for multimodal reinforcement learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36572)
*Zhongan Bi,Kepeng Lin,Xuanang Gao,Yuhan Sun,Lianrun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文发现RLVR训练下LVLM会出现"敏感度-持续性解耦"(SPD)现象：部分算法提升视觉敏感度的同时，反而使无视觉依据的声明更难被撤回，并提出无需标签与额外推理成本的"持续性感知信用门控"(PACG)来修正这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉强化学习(RLVR)及感知感知方法促使策略依赖图像证据，但"依赖图像"不等于"视觉声明被图像支持"。研究发现Qwen2.5-VL-7B在四个多模态推理基准上，训练前正确回答中有27.81%至少包含一个图像不支持的直接视觉声明；而结果级RL奖励以整条回答为单位，使这些无依据声明继承了正确答案的正向信用。

Method: 提出固定rollout反事实诊断：在干预图像下对同一回答重新打分，将"证据函数敏感度(EFS，模型预测变化强度)"与"声明持续性(是否坚持同一声明而非撤回)"分离。基于诊断结果提出持续性感知信用门控(PACG)，对异常持续存在的视觉声明衰减正向信用，其余信用不变，无需支持/不支持标签，且不增加推理开销。

Result: 诊断揭示敏感度-持续性解耦(SPD)：DAPO与VPPO下EFS升高、整体声明更可撤回，但无依据声明反而显著更持久；GRPO提升EFS却无此恶化。在Qwen2.5-VL-7B上，PACG将九基准三seed平均分由58.1%提升至59.9%(DAPO)、由59.8%提升至60.9%(VPPO)，并使无依据声明更易撤回；增益可扩展至更大模型、更新骨干网络，HallusionBench准确率也一致提升。

Conclusion: 视觉敏感度与声明可撤回性是多模态信用分配的两个互补维度；仅提升对图像的敏感性并不足以保证视觉声明有据可依，需在信用分配中显式考虑声明持续性。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has been extended to Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs), and perception-aware methods further encourage policies to rely on visual evidence. Yet relying on the image does not guarantee that visual claims are supported by it. Before RL training, 27.81% of the correctly answered responses of Qwen2.5-VL-7B on four multimodal reasoning benchmarks contain at least one direct visual claim that the image does not support. Since outcome-level RL rewards each response as a whole, these claims inherit the positive credit of the correct answer. We introduce a fixed-rollout counterfactual diagnostic that re-scores the same response under an intervened image to separate Evidence-Function Sensitivity (EFS), how strongly the model's predictions change, from claim persistence, whether the model keeps supporting the same claim rather than retracting it. The diagnostic reveals Sensitivity-Persistence Decoupling (SPD): under DAPO and VPPO, EFS increases and claims become more retractable overall, yet unsupported claims become significantly more persistent, whereas GRPO raises EFS without this deterioration. We therefore propose Persistence-Aware Credit Gating (PACG), which attenuates positive credit for unusually persistent visual claims and leaves all other credit unchanged. It requires no supported/unsupported labels and adds no inference cost. On Qwen2.5-VL-7B, PACG raises the nine-benchmark average over three seeds from 58.1% to 59.9% with DAPO and from 59.8% to 60.9% with VPPO, while making unsupported claims more retractable. The gains extend to a larger model, a newer backbone, and the accuracy of HallusionBench also improves consistently. These results suggest that visual sensitivity and claim retractability are complementary dimensions of multimodal credit assignment.

</details>


### [324] [Divide and Inject: Can Agents Reconstruct an Indirect Prompt Injection from Fragments?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36576)
*Michael Lee,Zhipeng Wei,Yue Dong,N. Benjamin Erichson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 AdaLCPI——自适应长上下文提示注入攻击方法，通过将攻击目标拆分为不完整片段分散嵌入长上下文，并借助 OpenEvolve 迭代优化片段与重构线索，宏观平均攻击成功率（ASR）达 61.4%，显著超越 Trojan Hippo 风格（32.8%）和 AgentVigil（30.0%）等强自适应基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理系统（agentic systems）广泛用于调度工具并在长上下文中推理，而驱动代理的大模型能力提升同时带来间接提示注入的新攻击面。攻击者可能无需在检索内容中放置完整恶意指令：只要代理能从分散于长上下文中的不完整片段重构出攻击目标即可。现有安全评测与防御方法可能无法覆盖这一威胁。

Method: 提出自适应长上下文提示注入（AdaLCPI）：1) 将攻击目标拆解为不完整的片段；2) 把这些片段嵌入代理工具检索到的外部内容中；3) 使用重构线索（reconstruction cue）诱导代理将片段组合还原；4) 利用 OpenEvolve 进行迭代优化，结合分级评分（graded scoring）与来自目标代理的自然语言执行反馈，不断精炼片段与线索。该方法将"长上下文碎片化"与"自适应搜索"结合。

Result: AdaLCPI 的攻击成功率高于强自适应基线：宏观平均 ASR 为 61.4%，而 Trojan Hippo 风格为 32.8%，AgentVigil 为 30.0%。

Conclusion: 安全评测应检验代理在"有害目标需从分散的不完整片段中被重构"这一场景下是否依然保持鲁棒性，即需将长上下文碎片化重构威胁纳入代理安全评估。

Abstract: Agentic systems are now being widely used to orchestrate tools and reason over long contexts. However, the improving capabilities of the large language models powering these agents also create new attack surfaces for indirect prompt injection. In particular, an attacker may not need to place a complete malicious instruction in retrieved content if the agent can reconstruct the objective from incomplete fragments distributed across a long context. In this work, we introduce adaptive long-context prompt injection (AdaLCPI), which combines long-context fragmentation with adaptive search. AdaLCPI splits an attack objective into incomplete fragments, embeds them in external content retrieved through the agent's tools, and uses a reconstruction cue to prompt the agent to combine them. It then iteratively refines the fragments and cue with OpenEvolve using graded scoring and natural-language execution feedback from the target agent. Empirically, AdaLCPI achieves higher attack success than strong adaptive baselines, reaching 61.4\% macro-average ASR compared with 32.8\% for Trojan Hippo-style and 30.0\% for AgentVigil. Safety evaluations should therefore test whether agents remain robust when harmful objectives must be reconstructed from incomplete fragments.

</details>


### [325] [SafeCoEvo: Co-Evolving Safety Harnesses and Guards for LLM Agents at Test-Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36580)
*Yu Cheng,Yongkang Hu,Shuaijie Ma,Zhihang Lin,Weicheng Meng,Jingyang Qiao,Jiuan Zhou,Yushuo Zhang,Yihang Chen,Weilin Luo,Kun Shao,Dong Li,Zhizhong Zhang,Yuan Xie,Zhaoxia Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 SafeCoEvo 测试时 Harness-Guard 协同演化框架，使 LLM agent 外部安全系统能从累积运行时经验中持续适应，兼顾即时与长期安全能力，安全与任务效用同步提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实部署中 LLM agent 不断遇到新任务与安全风险，执行反馈仅在任务完成后才可得；而现有自演化方法依赖对固定且可反复访问的任务分布进行多轮优化，与测试时适应场景根本不同——后者只能利用过去任务积累的经验来改进对未来未见任务的安全决策。

Method: SafeCoEvo 在不同时间尺度上协同改进两种互补安全能力：S-Harness 快速将近期运行时经验外化为可更新的显式安全知识，及时影响后续任务；GuardVPO 在更长的时间尺度上将累积的运行时安全经验内化为参数化的风险判断能力。

Result: 以短期快速适应与长期能力固化相结合，相比最强基线，不安全结果率降低 10.05%，任务成功率提升 12.15%，实现安全与任务效用的同时增益。

Conclusion: SafeCoEvo 为 LLM agent 的测试时安全适应提供了可行路径，通过显式知识外化与参数化内化的协同演化，突破固定任务分布多轮优化的限制，在真实部署条件下持续提升安全决策能力。

Abstract: LLM agents deployed in real-world environments continually encounter new tasks and safety risks, while execution feedback typically becomes available only after each task is completed. However, existing self-evolving approaches commonly rely on multiple rounds of optimization over fixed and repeatedly accessible task distributions, fundamentally differing from test-time adaptation in real-world deployment, where only experience accumulated from past tasks can be used to improve safety decisions on future unseen tasks. To address this limitation, we propose SafeCoEvo, a test-time Harness-Guard co-evolution framework for LLM agent safety that enables the external safety system to continually adapt from accumulated runtime experience. SafeCoEvo jointly improves two complementary safety capabilities at different timescales: S-Harness rapidly externalizes recent runtime experience into updatable explicit safety knowledge that can promptly influence subsequent tasks, while GuardVPO internalizes accumulated runtime safety experience over a longer timescale into parametric risk-judgment capabilities. By combining short-term rapid adaptation with long-term capability consolidation, SafeCoEvo continually improves the agent's safety capabilities, reducing the unsafe outcome rate by 10.05% while improving the task success rate by 12.15% over the strongest baseline, thereby achieving simultaneous gains in safety and task utility.

</details>


### [326] [MemEvo: Automatic Discovery of Streaming Video Memory Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36581)
*Guohong Liu,Jialei Ye,Shanhui Zhao,Yunxin Liu,Yuanchun Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将流式视频理解中的记忆机制设计形式化为对可执行记忆程序的搜索问题，提出领域特定语言与LLM驱动的自动研究框架MemEvo，自动发现免训练、有界内存的记忆机制，在流式视频理解基准上兼顾性能与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 查询无关的流式视频理解要求视觉语言模型在未知未来查询的前提下，将无限增长的视觉流压缩进有界内存。性能关键取决于记忆机制（保留什么、如何表示与整合、查询到来时检索什么），但手工设计单一记忆架构困难，亟需自动化搜索方法。

Method: 提出轻量领域特定语言，通过表示、准入、保留、整合、预算与检索等结构化原语表达记忆机制，并施加因果性和有界内存约束；在此基础上提出MemEvo，利用预训练LLM作为语义感知的提议模型，依据累积实验反馈迭代生成与改进候选记忆程序，运行时由确定性评测流水线验证与评估候选，视觉语言模型在发现过程中保持冻结。

Result: 在StreamingBench与OVO-Bench上取得强劲的流式视频理解性能，同时显著提升上下文与推理效率，并最终获得免训练、有界内存的记忆机制。

Conclusion: 将记忆设计视为对可执行记忆程序的搜索，结合DSL与LLM驱动的自动研究，可在无需训练视觉语言模型的情况下自动发现高效有界的流式视频记忆机制，为流式视频理解提供性能与效率兼得的方案。

Abstract: Query-agnostic streaming video understanding requires vision-language models to continuously compress an indefinitely growing visual stream into a bounded memory before future queries are known. The performance depends critically on the memory mechanism--what observations to preserve, how to represent and consolidate them, and what information to retrieve when a query eventually arrives. Rather than designing a single memory architecture by hand, we formulate memory design as a search problem over executable memory programs. We introduce a lightweight domain-specific language that expresses memory mechanisms through structured primitives for representation, admission, retention, consolidation, budgeting, and retrieval, while enforcing causal and bounded-memory constraints. Although structured, the derived program space remains large and contains heterogeneous, conditionally dependent design choices whose effects can only be assessed via downstream execution. We therefore propose MemEvo, an LLM-driven auto-research framework that uses pretrained LLM as a semantics-aware proposal model to iteratively generate and refine candidate memory programs based on accumulated experimental feedback. At runtime, a deterministic evaluation pipeline validates and evaluates each candidate, while the underlying vision-language model remains frozen throughout discovery. We finally produce a training-free, bounded-memory mechanism. Extensive experiments on StreamingBench and OVO-Bench demonstrate strong streaming video understanding performance together with substantial context and inference efficiency.

</details>


### [327] [Transformers Stop Thinking Too Early, and a Tiny LoRA Fixes It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36585)
*Zehao Jin,Ruixuan Deng,Junran Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 预训练Transformer仅用极少深度追踪上下文引用（基础模型仅可靠跟随1.4-3.6行），通过在早期层注入任务训练的rank-8 LoRA（冻结全部原权重），可将引用追踪能力扩展至数十行，机制为中层"接力"传递链式身份。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者关注一个能力瓶颈：预训练Transformer在上下文中追踪引用（follow references）时只用到很少的层深度，深层容量被闲置；额外堆叠预训练循环也收效甚微。因此希望探究这种"过早停止思考"是否为默认行为，以及能否用极小的参数改动释放被浪费的深度计算。

Method: 在全部模型权重冻结的前提下，于单个早期层插入任务训练的rank-8 LoRA。以长链引用任务为测试床：Qwen3-8B在24行链上的精确准确率、更长训练LoRA在50行链上的表现、Ouro-1.4B在四循环与八循环下的表现。并通过可解释性分析定位机制（链身份在中层接力传递、冻结输出头逐步向上游读取、移除父行注意力是否中断接力），以及用冻结模型测量来预测最后有效干预层。

Result: Qwen3-8B从15.5%提升到99%精确准确率（24行链），训练更久的LoRA达50行；Ouro-1.4B四循环达60行，八循环至少160行。机制上，LoRA启动一个"接力"：程序行通过一段中层传递其链身份，冻结输出头逐步读取更上游的链，移除父行注意力会终止接力。冻结模型测量能在四分之三的留出模型中容差内定位最后有效干预层。任务专用LoRA在MuSiQue上也有提升。

Conclusion: 默认答案低估了通过极小编辑即可访问的计算能力；预训练模型的引用追踪能力受限于深度利用不足而非容量本身，一个早期层的rank-8 LoRA即可显著扩展该能力。代码与交互演示已公开。

Abstract: Pretrained transformers use little of their depth to follow references in context. Thirteen base models reliably follow only 1.4-3.6 lines, and extra pretrained loops add little. A task-trained rank-8 LoRA at one early layer extends this computation with all model weights frozen. Qwen3-8B improves from 15.5% to 99% exact accuracy on 24-line chains; a longer-trained LoRA reaches 50 lines. Ouro-1.4B reaches 60 lines after four loops and at least 160 after eight. The LoRA starts a relay: program lines pass on their chain identity through a short range of middle layers. Frozen heads read progressively further up the chain, and removing parent-line attention stops the relay. A frozen-model measurement locates the last useful intervention layer within tolerance in three of four held-out models. Task-specific LoRAs also improve MuSiQue. Default answers therefore understate the computation accessible through a tiny edit. Code and an interactive demo are available at https://lunamos.github.io/stop-thinking-too-early/

</details>


### [328] [SAKI: Maximal-Coupling-Routed Teacher Supervision for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36601)
*Miteto Wei,Xiaohan Wang,Zehao Chen,Jiajun Chai,Sichao Liu,Li Wang,Haoyuan Xu,Zhaoyu Hu,Wei Lin,Guojun Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SAKI通过KL约束的教师引导展开与最大耦合，利用接受/纠正事件进行token级监督路由，提升on-policy蒸馏效果，并实现4.22x吞吐加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-policy蒸馏虽能缓解训练-测试状态失配，但弱学生在教师不对齐的前缀上获得代表性不足的监督信号。

Method: SAKI（KL约束插值的监督分配）：结合KL约束的教师引导展开与最大耦合；接受位置保留采样token的反向KL监督，纠正位置对教师最高概率token进行直接监督；理论证明纠正概率恰为TV(p_t, q_t)，信任域半径同时控制展开偏差并上界干预/专门监督频率；此外实现引擎内投机验证器，保持精确q轨迹分布与耦合语义。

Result: 引擎常驻投机验证器在匹配工作负载下将展开吞吐提升4.22倍；在七个数学推理基准上，SAKI在1.7B与0.6B学生的Mean@8和Pass@8上均优于匹配的教师引导基线；放置控制实验与固定前缀分析支持纠正触发路由作为冲突自适应监督信号。

Conclusion: SAKI通过耦合事件路由监督信号，有效改善on-policy蒸馏中的监督质量问题，并兼顾效率与理论保证。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) reduces train-test state mismatch by training a student on its own generated trajectories, but weak students may visit teacher-misaligned prefixes where supervision is less representative. We introduce SAKI (Supervision Allocation with KL-constrained Interpolation), which combines a KL-constrained teacher-guided rollout with maximal coupling and reuses realized accept/correction events to route token-level supervision. Accepted positions retain sampled-token reverse-KL supervision, while correction positions receive direct supervision on the teacher's highest-probability token. Under maximal coupling, the correction probability is exactly TV(p_t, q_t), so the same trust-region radius controls rollout deviation and upper-bounds intervention and specialized-supervision frequency. We further implement an engine-resident speculative verifier that preserves the exact-q trajectory distribution and coupling semantics while improving matched-workload rollout throughput by 4.22x. Across seven mathematical reasoning benchmarks, SAKI improves the matched teacher-guided baseline in Mean@8 and Pass@8 for both 1.7B and 0.6B students. Placement controls and fixed-prefix analysis further support correction-triggered routing as a conflict-adaptive supervision signal.

</details>


### [329] [Multi-Channel Mitigation of Source-Trust Shortcuts in Fact-Checking RL Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36611)
*Jianchang Su,Yiwei Yang,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TrustSwap反事实测试，发现检索增强事实核查模型存在"依赖来源标签而非证据内容"的捷径，并据此提出trust-swap augmentation（TSA）方法以缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事实核查模型接收来源可靠性标签（如HIGH/LOW trust），理想情况下标签应调节模型置信度与是否继续搜索证据的决策，而最终判定应基于证据内容本身。然而现有模型可能将标签作为判定捷径，破坏证据驱动的决策逻辑。

Method: 提出TrustSwap反事实测试：在保持所有证据文本不变的前提下，对来源标签进行交换、降低或移除，并分别测量三个输出通道（判定、置信度、搜索决策）。基于此提出TSA：对每条主张同时使用原始证据与标签互换证据，在相同金标准判定下训练GRPO。

Result: 在两种规模、三个数据集、两种提示下，置信度与搜索在50个对比中的49个按预期响应标签；但仅改变标签即可使Qwen3模型4-23%、现有RL事实核查器最高50%的置信判定发生翻转。标准GRPO在8B下六个设置中均放大该捷径。TSA在4B下将判定翻转率相对降低7-35%（六个设置中的四个），保持准确率及预期的置信度与搜索响应，优于基于奖励的替代方案，并能迁移到未见过的标签移除扰动；附加一致性奖励仅在被训练的互换上有效。8B下TSA效果不可检测。

Conclusion: 来源标签捷径在事实核查模型中普遍存在且被RL训练放大，TSA可有效缓解该问题并保持主要能力，但其效果存在规模依赖，8B下不可检测，规模效应是核心待解问题。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented fact-checkers often receive a reliability label, such as HIGH or LOW trust, for each evidence source. These labels should adjust the model's confidence and its decision to search for more evidence, while the verdict should follow the evidence content. We introduce TrustSwap, a counterfactual test that swaps, lowers, or removes source labels while keeping every evidence text fixed, and measures its three output channels (the verdict, the confidence, and the search decision) separately. Across untrained and RL-trained models at two scales, three datasets, and two prompts, confidence and search respond to the labels as intended in 49 of 50 comparisons, yet a label change alone alters 4-23% of confident verdicts for Qwen3 models and up to 50% for an existing RL-trained fact-checker. Standard GRPO fine-tuning amplifies this shortcut at 8B in all six settings. To reduce it, we propose trust-swap augmentation (TSA), which trains GRPO on each claim with both its original and its label-swapped evidence under the same gold verdict. At 4B, TSA lowers the verdict flip rate by 7-35% (relative) in four of six settings, keeps accuracy and the intended confidence and search responses, outperforms reward-based alternatives in the main setting, and carries over to an unseen label-removal perturbation. An added consistency reward helps on the trained-on swap but not on unseen perturbations. At 8B, TSA's effect is not detectable, which makes scale the main open question.

</details>


### [330] [Neural Structural Reasoner: A Brain-inspired Architecture for Reasoning over Structured Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36620)
*Zixing Jia,Yuhang Pan,Ni Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出脑启发网络NSR，将关系结构保留在耦合神经元群体的连接与动力学中，在知识图谱链接预测上取得有竞争力的精度、较低训练时间，并具备原生可解释性与隐结构发现能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构推理是人类认知的核心能力，但现有方法常把关系拓扑压缩为扁平嵌入，既无法发现隐藏结构，也缺乏可解释性。作者希望直接在网络的连接与动力学中保留关系结构，以同时获得性能、效率与可解释性。

Method: NSR受三种生物机制启发：（1）多层架构编码层级知识；（2）实体与概念的稳定表征；（3）路径积分实现输入驱动的状态推断。查询阶段，NSR在候选关系结构上并行计算，并用置信度加权评分完成链接预测。

Result: 在标准知识图谱基准上，NSR获得有竞争力的精度（并非在每个数据集上都领先），且报告的训练时间低于若干神经基线。推理以人类可读的神经元激活序列实现，可通过追踪中间推理步骤获得原生可解释性；模型还能提取潜在的关系层级与组合规则。

Conclusion: 该脑启发架构是进行结构推理的有效、高效且高度可解释的基底，验证了将关系结构直接嵌入神经元群体连接与动力学的可行性。

Abstract: Structural reasoning, the ability to recognize and make inferences over the relational structure between objects and concepts, is a hallmark of human cognition, yet prevailing methods often collapse relational topology into flat embeddings, cannot discover hidden structure and lack interpretability. We introduce Neural Structural Reasoner (NSR), a brain-inspired network that preserves relational structure directly in the connectivity and dynamics of coupled neuronal populations. NSR draws inspiration from three biological mechanisms: multi-layered architecture for encoding hierarchical knowledge, stable representations of entity and concepts, and path integration for input-driven state inference. At query time, NSR parallelizes computation over candidate relational structures and leverages confidence-weighted scores to perform link prediction. Across standard knowledge-graph benchmarks, NSR achieves competitive accuracy without leading on every dataset, and has lower reported training times than several neural baselines. Because reasoning is implemented through sequences of human-readable neuron activations, NSR affords native interpretability by tracking intermediate inference steps. The model further extracts latent relational hierarchies and compositional rules, demonstrating the brain-inspired architecture as an effective, efficient, and highly interpretable substrate for structural reasoning.

</details>


### [331] [DualTrack: Synchronized speech-gesture generation via symmetric coupling of pretrained priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36624)
*Yuanzhuo Hu,Zehan Liu,Xiaoyi Qin,Ming Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DualTrack：通过共享12.5 Hz时间线耦合预训练语音与动作先验，用因果适配器与当前状态融合实现双向交互的联合语音-手势合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合语音-手势合成需在配对数据有限条件下协调两个模态。现有方法常缺乏双向交互、语言覆盖有限，或过度简化身体与手指表示。

Method: 将预训练的语音先验与动作先验耦合在共享的12.5 Hz时间线上；因果适配器交换上一包（previous-packet）信息，当前状态融合在两条流各自完成十六码本包之前对其进行协调。

Result: 在四种语言、43段BEAT2录音上评估，说话人从联合训练与验证中留出。在共享的英语/西班牙语输入、无语音或动作前缀条件下，DualTrack相较所评估的GELINA基线取得更低词错误率与全动作Fréchet Gesture Distance，并具有更高节拍一致性与语音自然度。

Conclusion: DualTrack证明了在有限配对数据下，基于共享时间线与双向交互的语音-手势联合合成可同时提升语音识别质量、动作保真度与跨模态一致性，并具备多语言泛化能力。

Abstract: Joint speech-gesture synthesis must coordinate two modalities despite limited paired data. Existing approaches often lack bidirectional interaction, have limited language coverage, or simplify body and finger representations. We present DualTrack, which couples pretrained speech and motion priors on a shared 12.5 Hz timeline. Causal adapters exchange previous-packet information, while current-state fusion coordinates the streams before they separately complete sixteen-codebook packets. We evaluate 43 BEAT2 recordings in four languages, with speakers held out from joint training and validation. On the shared English/Spanish inputs, without speech or motion prefixes, DualTrack achieves lower word error rate and full-motion Fréchet Gesture Distance, higher beat consistency and speech naturalness than the evaluated GELINA baseline.

</details>


### [332] [Semantic Projection for Continual Self-Evolution of Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36626)
*Ziyu Liu,Jun Chen,Lixu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出语义范围投影演化（SSPE），将正交梯度下降（OGD）的投影思想从参数空间迁移到行为空间，用于缓解语言模型智能体在持续技能修订中的灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型智能体依赖持久化的自然语言技能以适应冻结参数之外的场景，但当共享技能面对非平稳、异构的任务流被反复修订时，新任务的改进可能覆盖旧任务所需的流程，产生干扰。持续学习中的OGD通过将新任务梯度投影到保持先前预测的子空间来缓解此类干扰，但自然语言技能修订既无梯度，也无规范向量空间可供投影。

Method: 提出Semantic-Scope Projected Evolution（SSPE），将梯度投影的功能原理从参数空间转移到行为空间。SSPE将无约束的技能修订视为候选更新，识别可能发生干扰的已获得能力，并利用观测到的收益与退化来构造兼容的修订，而非简单拒绝更新。该方法使单一共享技能能够在潜在和循环出现的任务上下文中演化，同时不向演化模型暴露语义领域身份。

Result: 在受控合成任务流和异构真实智能体基准上，SSPE相较强技能演化基线提升了最终跨域能力并减轻了遗忘。演化后的技能在迁移到不同执行器模型后仍保持最强平均性能。

Conclusion: 结果表明语义投影是语言智能体实现稳定且自适应自演化的一条有前景的原则。

Abstract: Language-model agents increasingly rely on persistent natural-language skills to adapt beyond their frozen model parameters. When a shared skill is repeatedly revised from a non-stationary, heterogeneous task stream, however, improvements for new tasks can overwrite procedures needed for earlier ones. In continual learning, Orthogonal Gradient Descent (OGD) addresses analogous interference by projecting a new-task gradient onto a subspace that locally preserves prior predictions. Natural-language skill revisions, however, have neither gradients nor a canonical vector space in which such a projection can be performed. We introduce \emph{Semantic-Scope Projected Evolution} (SSPE), which transfers the functional principle of gradient projection from parameter space to behavior space. SSPE treats an unconstrained skill revision as a proposed update, identifies acquired capabilities with which it may interfere, and uses the observed gains and regressions to construct a compatible revision rather than merely rejecting the update. This enables one shared skill to evolve across latent and recurring task contexts without exposing semantic domain identities to the evolution model. Across controlled synthetic streams and heterogeneous real-agent benchmarks, SSPE improves final cross-domain competence and mitigates forgetting relative to strong skill-evolution baselines. The evolved skill also retains the strongest average performance after transfer to a different executor model. These results establish semantic projection as a promising principle for stable and adaptive self evolution of language agents.

</details>


### [333] [Distilling Agentic Systems: A Roadmap across Models, Artifacts, and Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36630)
*Ziluowen Luo,Senzhang Wang,Chaozhuo Li,Jun Yin,Hao Yan,Ming Cheng,Chenxu Wang,Songyang Liu,Litian Zhang,Qiwei Ye,Zheng Liu,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"Agent Distillation"（智能体蒸馏）概念，将其定义为任务求解知识从教师智能体到学生智能体的持久迁移，并按知识保留位置（模型内部、工件、执行外壳、跨基底）组织该领域，构建了将保留、因果贡献与部署效用相关联的评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代智能体的能力已超越模型参数本身，日益依赖记忆、工具与执行逻辑。这一转变暴露了传统知识蒸馏的局限——传统蒸馏仅追问学生模型如何模仿教师模型，而无法刻画智能体层面知识的跨组件迁移。

Method: 1）提出Agent Distillation的定义，即任务求解知识从教师智能体向学生智能体的持久迁移；2）按知识保留位置对领域进行分类组织：模型内部、作为工件、通过执行外壳、跨基底；3）该视角将迁移证据与其结果相分离，厘清知识如何在智能体各组件间流动；4）构建评估框架，将知识保留与因果贡献及部署效用相关联。

Result: 提出了Agent Distillation的系统化定义与分类视角，并给出一个把保留（retention）、因果贡献（causal contribution）与部署效用（deployed utility）关联起来的评估框架。

Conclusion: 上述贡献为日益复杂的智能体系统实现可靠、可维护且安全的开发奠定了基础。

Abstract: Modern agents increasingly rely on memories, tools, and execution logic, so their competence extends beyond model parameters. This shift exposes a limitation of conventional knowledge distillation, which asks how a student model imitates a teacher model. We define Agent Distillation as the persistent transfer of task-solving knowledge from a teacher agent to a student agent. Our study organizes the field by where transferred knowledge is retained: within the model, as artifacts, through the execution harness, or across substrates. This perspective separates transfer evidence from its outcome and clarifies how knowledge moves between agent components. We develop an evaluation framework that relates retention to causal contribution and deployed utility. Together, these contributions establish a foundation for the reliable, maintainable, and safe development of increasingly complex agentic systems.

</details>


### [334] [RankBuffer: Efficient Ranking-Based Rewards for Open-Ended Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36652)
*Zixuan Yang,Yiqun Chen,Qi Liu,Wei Yang,Erhan Zhang,Liyi Chen,Qimeng Wang,Yan Gao,Jiaxin Mao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 RankBuffer：以可复用的有序质量量表缓冲区，用独立粗判 + 同区间局部精排构建相对奖励，在四个开放式基准上超越所有逐点基线，并与最强排序奖励基线性能相当同时显著降低评判成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式生成缺乏标准答案，逐点奖励难以校准，不适合基于群体的强化学习；直接对同一查询的多次 rollout 排序可提供更合适的相对奖励信号，但现有排序式奖励方法评判成本过高。

Method: 维护一个按查询组织的、有序的已评判响应缓冲区，作为可复用的质量量表。每个 rollout 先经由独立粗粒度判断插入某个锚点区间，仅落入同一区间的 rollout 进行局部精排；由此得到的完整序被转换为有界秩奖励。通过边界扩展、局部细化与非活跃锚点剪枝，使缓冲区随策略演化而自适应更新。

Result: 在四个开放式基准上一致优于所有逐点基线；与最强排序式奖励基线性能几乎持平，同时大幅降低评判成本。消融实验表明局部精排与锚点响应内容均至关重要；缓冲区分析显示由 rollout 衍生的锚点会逐步扩展并细化所覆盖的质量量表。

Conclusion: 响应复用是构建高效相对奖励的一种有效途径。

Abstract: Open-ended generation lacks canonical answers, making pointwise rewards difficult to calibrate for group-based reinforcement learning. Directly ranking same-query rollouts provides a more suitable relative reward signal, but existing ranking-based reward methods can incur substantial judging cost. We introduce RankBuffer, which maintains an ordered, query-specific buffer of previously judged responses as a reusable quality scale. Each rollout is first inserted into an anchor interval through an independent coarse judgment, after which only rollouts assigned to the same interval undergo local fine ranking. The resulting complete order is converted into bounded rank rewards, while boundary expansion, local refinement, and inactive-anchor pruning adapt the buffer as the policy evolves. Across four open-ended benchmarks, RankBuffer consistently outperforms all pointwise baselines. It also achieves nearly on-par performance with the strongest ranking-based reward baseline while substantially reducing judging cost. Ablations demonstrate the importance of both local fine ranking and anchor response content, while buffer analyses show that rollout-derived anchors progressively extend and refine the covered quality scale. These results establish response reuse as an effective approach to efficient relative reward construction.

</details>


### [335] [FineSID: Scalable and Efficient Semantic Identifier Learning for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36670)
*Song-Li Wu,Weinan Gan,Zhaocheng Du,Xianquan Wang,Jingyi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 FineSID，一种突破 Top-1 硬分配的量化框架，通过在全部码本上实现细粒度软梯度传播，缓解 SID 冲突并提升码本利用率与推荐精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐需要兼顾可扩展性与可学习性的语义标识符（SID）。现有 SID 学习方法依赖向量量化中的 Top-1 硬分配，其稀疏梯度传播导致梯度集中于少数高频码字，多数码字欠训练，从而引发严重的 SID 冲突。基于聚类初始化或事后强制去冲突等启发式策略虽能人为提升码本覆盖率，但破坏端到端语义对齐，且未触及优化瓶颈。

Method: FineSID 是一种统一量化框架，摒弃 Top-1 分配，改为以软、可微分的方式将学习信号分配给码本中的全部码字，而非仅更新被选中的单一码字，从而在不依赖复杂初始化先验的前提下实现细粒度梯度传播，促进全局均衡的码本优化并严格保持语义一致性。

Result: 在多个公开基准上的大量实验表明，FineSID 对初始化配置具有鲁棒性，并持续提升码本利用率与推荐准确率。

Conclusion: 该工作为语义标识符学习提供了有原则、与初始化无关的解决方案，有效缓解 SID 冲突、稳定大规模高维码本的训练，推动了生成式推荐的实用化。

Abstract: A critical prerequisite of generative recommendation is designing semantic identifiers (SIDs) that are both scalable to large item sets and efficiently learnable. Existing SID learning methods fundamentally rely on Top-1 hard assignment during vector quantization. While heuristic strategies -- such as clustering-based initialization or forced post-hoc collision resolution -- can artificially inflate codebook coverage, they often disrupt end-to-end semantic alignment and fail to address the underlying optimization bottleneck: sparse gradient propagation. In standard Top-1 assignment, gradients concentrate on a narrow subset of frequently selected codewords, leaving the majority inherently under-trained and causing severe SID collisions. To overcome this limitation natively without relying on complex initialization priors, we propose FineSID, a unified quantization framework that moves beyond Top-1 assignment by enabling fine-grained gradient propagation across the entire codebook. Instead of updating only a single selected codeword, FineSID distributes learning signals to all codewords in a soft, differentiable manner. This design promotes globally balanced codebook optimization while strictly preserving semantic consistency, effectively alleviating SID collisions and stabilizing training in large, high-dimensional codebooks. Extensive experiments on multiple public benchmarks demonstrate that FineSID is robust to initialization configurations and consistently improves both codebook utilization and recommendation accuracy. Our work provides a principled, initialization-agnostic solution for semantic identifier learning, advancing the practicality of generative recommendation.

</details>


### [336] [FairDiff: Mitigating the Self-Reinforcing Matthew Effect in Diffusion Recommender Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36671)
*Song-Li Wu,Xianquan Wang,Zhaocheng Du,Weinan Gan,Jingyi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示扩散推荐模型（DRMs）通过其生成动态独特地放大了流行度偏差，形成自我强化的"马太效应"，并提出即插即用的公平性扩散框架FairDiff加以缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统中的"马太效应"与过滤气泡是公认的结果层面偏差，但作者发现DRMs不只是继承数据不平衡，而是通过其生成动态进一步复合、自我强化地放大流行度偏差。因此需要剖析其背后机制并提出针对性解决方案。

Method: 作者识别出两个复合机制：一是优化损失普遍被高频物品主导；二是DRMs在生成时存在独特的结构性先验错配——长尾数据的前向终端分布显著偏离标准高斯先验，导致反向采样轨迹向高密度流行物品坍缩。针对此，提出FairDiff框架，包含两个组件：（1）流行度条件引导（PCG），作为推理阶段的分布重加权机制，在不改变训练目标的前提下重塑造基于分数的梯度场，惩罚高流行度区域并引导轨迹走向长尾语义；（2）语义校准（SC）模块，通过一步最优传输弥合先验错配，对齐前向与反向分布。

Result: 综合评估表明，FairDiff在达到最先进性能的同时，有效缓解了自我强化的马太效应，体现了其作为DRMs通用框架的价值。

Conclusion: DRMs通过生成动态独特地复合并自我强化流行度偏差，源于优化损失主导与先验错配两大机制；FairDiff作为即插即用的公平性扩散框架，通过推理时重加权与语义校准可有效打破这一自强化循环，兼具性能与公平性。

Abstract: While the "Matthew Effect" and filter bubbles are widely recognized outcome-level biases in recommender systems, we reveal that Diffusion Recommender Models (DRMs) uniquely compound this issue through their generative dynamics. Rather than merely inheriting data imbalances, DRMs trigger a self-reinforcing amplification of popularity bias. We identify that this phenomenon is driven by two compounding mechanisms. First, while optimization loss is universally dominated by high-frequency items across recommenders, DRMs suffer from a unique structural prior mismatch during generation. Because the forward terminal distribution of long-tailed data deviates significantly from the standard Gaussian prior, reverse sampling trajectories inherently collapse toward high-density popular items, fundamentally suppressing niche item generation. To dismantle this self-reinforcing loop, we propose FairDiff, a plug-and-play fairness-aware diffusion framework. To overcome the popularity-dominated loss, we introduce Popularity Condition Guidance (PCG). Rather than altering the training objective, PCG acts as an inference-time distributional reweighting mechanism, mathematically reshaping the score-based gradient field to penalize high-popularity regions and guide trajectories toward niche semantics. Furthermore, we design a Semantic Calibration (SC) Module to bridge the prior mismatch, aligning the forward and reverse distributions via one-step optimal transport. Comprehensive evaluations demonstrate that FairDiff achieves state-of-the-art performance while effectively mitigating the self-reinforcing Matthew Effect, highlighting its value as a general framework for DRMs.

</details>


### [337] [MLToolBench: Learning Tool-Augmented Agents for Machine Learning Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36679)
*Xin Yu,Lizhu Zhang,Jiamu Bai,Yanhong Wu,Zellux Wang,Serena Li,Weiwei Li,Lingzhou Xue,Xiangjun Fan,Bo Peng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ToolMLBench（可执行诊断工具套件）与SPICE（基于特权上下文似然差的轮级奖励）训练方法，让MLE智能体学会在何时调用诊断工具并据其证据行动，在域内与域外任务上均大幅提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ML实验工程中实验成本高昂（时间与算力），合成环境虽降低成本但引入数据与实验设置差异，需任务特定诊断；仅提供诊断工具并不保证智能体会适时调用或依发现行动。且诊断调用的价值取决于后续决策，最终结果对"该强化哪些调用"指导有限。

Method: 构建ToolMLBench：数据检查、代码验证与实验诊断的可执行工具套件，并配套SFT与RL训练流水线。针对诊断信用分配问题提出SPICE：度量特权上下文如何改变采样工具动作的似然，以该似然差作为轮级奖励，与最终结果奖励联合使用。训练用80个合成任务，评估用25个域内与10个域外任务。

Result: 仅提供工具接口与描述在未适配模型上收益不稳定。在相同诊断接口下，训练流水线将域内成功率：Qwen3-8B由24.8%升至52.4%，Qwen3.5-35B-A3B由35.6%升至69.2%；后者域外亦由31%升至48%。

Conclusion: 学习式诊断工具使用可被有效训练：工具接口本身不足，需配合SFT+RL与SPICE轮级信用分配；性能提升可泛化至未见过的来源与目标任务。

Abstract: Machine learning engineering (MLE) agents have made substantial progress, but learning through ML experimentation remains costly in time and computation. Synthetic environments reduce these costs while introducing variations in data and experimental settings that require task-specific diagnosis. Access to diagnostic tools alone does not ensure that agents learn when to use them or how to act on their findings. We introduce ToolMLBench, a suite of executable tools for data inspection, code verification, and experiment diagnosis, together with an SFT and RL pipeline for learning their use. Diagnostic calls acquire evidence whose value depends on subsequent decisions, so final outcomes provide limited guidance on which calls to reinforce. We address this challenge with SPICE, which measures how privileged context changes the likelihood of a sampled tool action and uses this difference as a turn-level reward alongside the final outcome. We train on 80 synthetic tasks and evaluate on 25 in-domain and 10 out-of-domain tasks. Providing tool interfaces and descriptions alone yields inconsistent gains across unadapted models. With the same diagnostic interface, our training pipeline raises in-domain success from 24.8% to 52.4% for Qwen3-8B and from 35.6% to 69.2% for Qwen3.5-35B-A3B. The latter also improves from 31% to 48% out-of-domain, supporting learned diagnostic tool use on held-out sources and targets.

</details>


### [338] [JudgeProfile: Understanding and Steering Subjectivity in LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36705)
*Qi Cao,Kangning Liu,Xuan Kan,Shunwen Tan,Yang Pei,Dake Chen,Yatai Ji,Zixuan Ye,Yuanpeng Tu,Daniel Li,Junbiao Tang,Pengtao Xie,Zihao He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出JudgeProfile框架，将LLM评判者的主观性分解为"感知"（对属性的比较判断）与"优先级"（各属性对最终选择的影响权重），并构建SubjectiveSet数据集验证：感知上存在隐藏共识，而优先级因评判者而异，且可通过重新加权属性将决策对齐到目标标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM评判者在成对比较中常表现出主观性，当两个回答都无明显错误时会偏向不同选项。作者希望系统性地剖析这种主观性来源，而非笼统归因于评判者偏差，从而为理解与调控LLM评判提供可操作的分解视角。

Method: 提出JudgeProfile框架，将LLM评估拆解为感知（跨清晰度、正确性、细节等属性比较两个回答）与优先级（各属性对最终选择的贡献权重）。构建SubjectiveSet数据集，包含来自17个公开数据源的50,013个回答对，由21个LLM评判者在87个属性上评估。基于该分离，先用评判者自身整体选择估计其属性权重以刻画优先级，再从参考标签学习新权重以适配目标评估标准。

Result: 感知层面存在隐藏共识，评判者即使在整体选择上分歧，也常在属性判断上达成一致。优先级权重在不同评判者间存在差异，即使从相同属性判断中估计也是如此。通过对感知属性重新加权，平均留出集与参考标签的一致率从66.48%提升至71.97%，优于微调和评分标准提示（rubric prompting）。

Conclusion: 理解并引导LLM评判者的主观性，不仅需要关注其"感知到什么"，还需关注其"如何排序与权衡"这些感知。将主观性分解为感知与优先级两个维度，为分析和调控LLM评估提供了有效且可推广的路径。

Abstract: LLM judges are inherently subjective, often favoring different responses in pairwise comparison when neither option is objectively wrong. To study this subjectivity, we introduce JudgeProfile, a framework that dissects LLM evaluation into perception (how a judge compares two responses across specific attributes like clarity, correctness, and detail) and prioritization (how much each attribute influences the final choice). We curate SubjectiveSet, a dataset of 50,013 response pairs from 17 public data sources, evaluated by 21 LLM judges across 87 attributes. We find a hidden consensus in perception: judges frequently agree on attribute judgments even when their overall choices diverge. Building on this separation, we first characterize each judge's prioritization using attribute weights estimated from its own overall choices. These weights differ across judges even when estimated from the same attribute judgments. We then learn new weights from reference labels to adapt their decisions to a target evaluation standard. Reweighting perceived attributes improves average held-out agreement with reference labels from 66.48% to 71.97%, outperforming fine-tuning and rubric prompting. Our findings show that understanding and steering the subjectivity of LLM judges requires attention not only to what they perceive, but also to how they prioritize it.

</details>


### [339] [Can AI Scientists Change Their Minds? Prior-Evidence Conflict in Synthetic Universes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36726)
*Kargi Chauhan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出"合成宇宙"基准，将经典著名世界与其"扭曲孪生"世界配对，分别以预测验证与机制恢复两条独立标准评估科学智能体所报告定律，发现二者可双向解离，故预测充分性与机制恢复是两类需分别检验的科学主张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是：科学智能体能否区分"从证据中推断出的定律"与"仅凭识别而认出的定律"。即预测表现好是否等同于真正把握了生成机制，二者是否可被混为一谈。

Method: 构建"合成宇宙"受控基准：将经典著名世界与由相近非经典机制支配的匹配"扭曲孪生"世界配对。对每个报告定律做双重评估——一是将其在执行层面用于留出续写与迁移设定，二是独立核查其是否复原生成机制。研究为预先设定的60单元实验，另设著名对照、可识别性扫描校正与"证据阶梯"等组件。

Result: 当前检查点中22个试验完成评分，另1次运行因基础设施故障中止。20个孪生试验里，8个通过预测验证，5个复原生成机制；解离呈双向：6个可解析输出预测成功却未抓住机制，3个复原机制却在预测 rollout 上失败。Drag呈前者模式（预测5/5通过，机制恢复1/5）；Gravity呈后者模式（预测1/5通过，机制恢复4/5）。

Conclusion: 由于匹配著名对照、校正后的可识别性扫描与证据阶梯尚不完整，作者不主张确证性的因果先验冲突效应。已完成运行仅支持一个更窄的验证性结论：预测充分性与机制恢复是彼此不同的科学主张，必须用不同的检验来判定。

Abstract: Can a scientific agent distinguish a law it inferred from evidence from one it merely recognizes? We introduce Synthetic Universes, a controlled benchmark that pairs canonical famous worlds with matched twisted twins governed by nearby noncanonical mechanisms. We evaluate each reported law twice: by executing it on held-out continuations and transfer settings, and by independently checking whether it recovers the generating mechanism. In the current checkpoint of a pre-specified 60-cell study, 22 trials were graded and one additional run ended in infrastructure failure. Among 20 twin trials, 8 pass predictive verification while 5 recover the generator. The dissociation is bidirectional: six parsable outputs predict successfully while missing the mechanism, whereas three recover the mechanism but fail predictive rollout. Drag exhibits the first pattern (5/5 predictive pass, 1/5 mechanism recovery); Gravity exhibits the second (1/5 predictive pass, 4/5 mechanism recovery). Because matched famous controls, the corrected identifiability sweep, and the Evidence Ladder remain incomplete, we do not claim a confirmatory causal prior-conflict effect. Instead, the completed runs establish a narrower verification result: predictive adequacy and mechanism recovery are distinct scientific claims and require distinct tests.

</details>


### [340] [Can Agents Design Libraries for Agents?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36730)
*Gabriel Orlanski,Alex L. Zhang,Avi Trost,Vincent Sunn Chen,Frederic Sala,Aws Albarghouthi,Ludwig Schmidt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 LibraryDesignBench，一个两阶段基准，用于评估智能体为其他智能体设计库的能力：设计者智能体依据规格实现完整库，再由三个不同模型家族的用户智能体编写程序，从正确性与简洁性两方面评测该库。基准涵盖 4 种语言、15 个库设计任务、242 个专家验证问题。结果显示 15 个任务中有 11 个能复现人类生产库的抽象；下游智能体对智能体库与人类库同样采用但均使用不足，失败主因是智能体库僵硬难用而非能力缺失；加入更规定性的智能体优先指导与子智能体测试可提升下游得分并生成更简洁的程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体越来越多地基于其他智能体编写的代码构建，但它们倾向于重新实现而非复用，导致后续智能体所需维护的代码库不断膨胀。现有工作缺乏衡量"智能体为其他智能体设计库"这一能力的基准。

Method: 提出 LibraryDesignBench，两阶段基准：第一阶段，设计者智能体依据仅规定所需能力与潜在用例、不规定设计的规格实现完整库；第二阶段，由来自三个不同模型家族的用户智能体编写程序，通过程序的正确性与简洁性评测该库。基准覆盖 4 种语言、15 个库设计任务、242 个专家验证编程问题。此外还实验了两种干预：给设计者更规定性的智能体优先指导，以及让设计者用子智能体测试其库。

Result: 15 个任务中有 11 个，设计者智能体复现了人类编写的生产库的抽象。下游智能体对智能体库和人类库的采用程度相近，但都存在使用不足、重新实现库已提供能力的问题。失败分析表明，下游智能体多写代码主要因为智能体编写的库僵硬或难用，而非能力缺失。加入更规定性的智能体优先指导并用子智能体测试库后，下游得分提升且程序更简洁。

Conclusion: LibraryDesignBench 既为评估面向智能体用户的库设计实践提供了测试平台，也提供了可改善下游复用的初始设计基线。

Abstract: Agents increasingly build on code written by other agents, and they reimplement rather than reuse, growing the codebases later agents must work in. To measure how well agents design libraries for other agents, we introduce LibraryDesignBench, a two-phase benchmark in which an agent implements a full-featured library from a specification that defines required capabilities and potential use cases without prescribing the design. We evaluate the library through the correctness and simplicity of programs written by three user agents from different model families. The benchmark spans 242 expert-validated programming problems across 15 library-design tasks in four languages. On eleven of the fifteen tasks, agent designers reproduce the abstractions of the human-written production library. Downstream agents adopt agent- and human-written libraries alike but underuse them, reimplementing capabilities the library already provides. Our failure analysis finds that downstream agents write extra code mainly because agent-written libraries are rigid or hard to use, not because capabilities are missing. We also experiment with giving designers more prescriptive, agent-first guidance and having them test their library with subagents; this improves downstream scores and yields simpler programs. LibraryDesignBench provides both a testbed for evaluating library-design practices for agent users and an initial design baseline that improves downstream reuse.

</details>


### [341] [BiFE: Search-Efficient Discovery of CPU-Only Branching Policies via LLM-based Bi-Fidelity Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36735)
*Ce Zhang,Bin Zhang,Zhiwei Xu,Hao Chen,Xinyue Lu,Shanwei Fan,Yingxuan Teng,Guoliang Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 BiFE（双保真进化框架），利用 LLM 生成轻量级分支规则，并结合低保真模仿评分预筛选与高保真实例评估，高效发现优于 SCIP 及部分 GPU 神经策略的 CPU 分支策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合整数线性规划的分支定界中，分支变量选择对效率至关重要。现有神经分支策略多依赖 GPU 推理，而 CPU 高效的符号表达式表达能力不足，难以刻画复杂逻辑。LLM 生成代码为设计兼具多样算法逻辑的轻量分支规则提供了灵活搜索空间，但如何在 LLM 进化框架中高效发现有效规则存在核心难题：真实实例上的完整 B&amp;B 评估代价过高，而离线模仿学习又面临分布偏移。

Method: 提出双保真进化框架 BiFE：以低保真模仿评分作为快速预筛选器，仅对精英候选者选择性执行高保真真实实例评估，从而在搜索效率与性能可靠性之间取得平衡。

Result: 实验验证了 BiFE 的搜索效率及其发现规则的竞争力：在 CPU 上优于 SCIP 求解器和其他基线，甚至超越部分基于 GPU 的神经策略。

Conclusion: BiFE 通过双保真进化机制有效缓解了 B&amp;B 评估昂贵与模仿学习分布偏移的矛盾，能够自动发现 CPU 高效且性能优越的分支规则。

Abstract: In branch-and-bound (B&B) for mixed-integer linear programming (MILP), branching variable selection critically impacts efficiency. Existing neural branching policies often require GPU inference, while CPU-efficient symbolic expressions lack the representational capacity for complex logic. Large Language Model (LLM)-generated code provides a flexible search space for designing lightweight branching rules with diverse algorithmic logic.
