To discover effective rules within LLM-based evolutionary frameworks, a core challenge arises: full B&B evaluation on real instances is prohibitively expensive, whereas offline imitation learning suffers from distribution shift. To address this, we introduce a Bi-Fidelity Evolutionary framework (BiFE). It employs low-fidelity imitation scores as a rapid pre-screener and selectively applies high-fidelity on-instance evaluation only to elite candidates, effectively balancing search efficiency with performance reliability. Experiments validate both the search efficiency of BiFE and the competitiveness of its discovered rules, which outperform the SCIP solver and other baselines on CPUs, and even surpass certain GPU-based neural policies.

</details>


### [342] [Distinguish or Homogenize: Last-Chance Policy Identification and Risk-Budgeted Recovery under Irreversible Resource Depletion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36741)
*Yibo Guo,Xiaodan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出"区分-同质化"原则与"最后机会策略辨识"(LCPI)框架,将故障模型到可接受策略的映射视为智能体自身动作的函数,在智能体到达的状态处评估正确性,并给出确定性图上的 Exact-LIM 递推与噪声情形下的 RBCP 规划方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不可逆资源消耗下,智能体可将资源用于区分潜在故障模型,或用于改变系统状态使剩余模型共享一个可接受的延续,后者可使进一步诊断变得不必要。既有辨识、规划与诊断框架未显式刻画这一路径:它们把故障模型到可接受策略的映射当作既定量,而非智能体动作的函数。

Method: 形式化最后机会策略辨识(LCPI),正确性在智能体到达的状态而非初始状态处评估;用"最后可辨识边际"(LIM)刻画区分与同质化之间的可行性边界。对确定性诊断图给出 Exact-LIM 递推;对噪声有限时域恢复提出风险预算兼容性规划(RBCP),在硬性最坏情况失败约束下搜索兼容性感知前沿。并以成本匹配但保持模型不兼容的 sham 对照进行验证。

Result: 在抽象微服务拓扑的故障恢复与 MiniGrid 的潜在损伤导航任务上,RBCP 在满足失败预算的同时提升了风险可行恢复率;sham 对照完全消除该增益,表明收益来自改变"哪些策略对哪些模型可接受",而非额外搜索或额外预算。

Conclusion: 该工作表明,将辨识问题重新表述为可由动作塑造的可接受性映射,可在资源不可逆消耗场景下获得现有框架难以显式利用的收益;增益的因果归因由对照实验确认。

Abstract: Under irreversible resource depletion, an agent can spend resources to distinguish among latent fault models, or to change the system state so that the remaining models admit a common acceptable continuation--at which point further diagnosis becomes unnecessary. This distinguish-or-homogenize principle identifies a path that existing frameworks for identification, planning, and diagnosis do not make explicit: prior formulations treat the mapping from fault models to acceptable policies as a given, whereas LCPI makes it a function of the agent's own actions. We formalize this principle through Last-Chance Policy Identification (LCPI), where correctness is evaluated at the state the agent reaches rather than at the initial state. The Last Identifiable Margin (LIM) marks the feasibility boundary between distinguishing and homogenizing. For deterministic diagnostic graphs we provide the Exact-LIM recursion; for noisy finite-horizon recovery we propose Risk-Budgeted Compatibility Planning (RBCP), which searches a compatibility-aware frontier under a hard worst-case failure constraint. Across incident recovery on abstract microservice topologies and latent-damage navigation in MiniGrid, RBCP improves risk-feasible recovery while satisfying the failure budget. A sham control--cost-matched actions that preserve model incompatibility--eliminates the gain entirely, confirming that the benefit comes from changing which policies are acceptable for which models, not from extra search or additional budget.

</details>


### [343] [SIPO: Unifying Reinforcement Learning with On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36742)
*Zhenrui Yue,Huimin Zeng,Yueqi Wang,Yaokun Liu,Fengran Mo,Jinghan Zhang,Mung Yao Jia,Gyuseok Lee,Yang Zhang,Na Wei,Dong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SIPO方法，通过对比自教师提供token级密集信号，解决RLVR稀疏奖励与OPSD过度自信问题，在多推理与代码基准上优于RLVR和OPSD基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR是提升LLM任务表现的标准范式，但其结果奖励稀疏，缺乏对中间步骤的token级信用分配。OPSD借助带特权上下文的自教师提供密集信号，然而自教师常过度自信，对长推理轨迹施加过度惩罚，实践中效果不佳。

Method: SIPO（self-instructing policy optimization）引入对比自教师。每次迭代对每个prompt采样多条rollout，用环境奖励打分，并将参考答案与组内所犯错误配对，为每条rollout构造两个教师上下文。模型在两上下文下重新评估自身回答，以两教师对数概率之差作为token级反馈，使共享偏差相互抵消。目标函数为每条rollout产生token级优势：奖励决定更新主方向，自教师负责在token间重新分配信用。

Result: 在多个推理与代码生成基准上的大量实验表明，SIPO优于RLVR和OPSD基线，且无需外部教师或额外生成。

Conclusion: SIPO在保留任务奖励直接优化的同时提供密集token级反馈，弥合了强化学习与在线自蒸馏；即便组内全部rollout失败、组相对优势消失时仍能提供学习信号。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become a standard paradigm for improving large language models (LLMs) on various tasks, yet its sparse outcome rewards lack token-level credit assignment for intermediate steps. To address this, on-policy self-distillation (OPSD) leverages a self-teacher with privileged context to provide additional dense learning signals. However, because the self-teacher is often overconfident and imposes excessive penalties on long reasoning trajectories, OPSD frequently struggles in practice. To mitigate this, we propose self-instructing policy optimization (SIPO) with a contrastive self-teacher to provide dense credit. At each iteration, SIPO samples multiple rollouts per prompt from the current policy, scores them with environment rewards, and constructs two teacher contexts for each rollout by pairing the reference answer with mistakes made within the group. The model then re-evaluates its own responses under both contexts, using the difference between the two teacher log-probabilities as token-level feedback, so that biases shared by both contexts are expected to largely cancel. The resulting objective yields a token-level advantage for every rollout: the reward still sets the main direction of each update while the self-teacher redistributes credit across tokens. Even in groups where every rollout fails and group-relative advantages vanish, SIPO still provides a learning signal. By preserving direct optimization of the task reward while providing dense, token-level feedback, this approach bridges reinforcement learning and on-policy self-distillation. Extensive experiments across multiple reasoning and code-generation benchmarks demonstrate that SIPO outperforms both RLVR and OPSD baselines without an external teacher or additional generation.

</details>


### [344] [EASE: Behavior-Adaptive Skill Curation for Self-Evolving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36746)
*Zhen Xiong,Qiaoyu Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出行为自适应技能策管（behavior-adaptive skill curation）与EASE框架：用行为画像条件化单一策管器，跨多个冻结执行器用强化学习联合训练，实现跨执行器的技能增删改，并在多任务多模型上超越基线且技能更少、检索与编辑效用更高、推理token更省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习式技能策管器在优化时未显式建模下游执行器行为。作者发现这会导致系统性的“跨执行器退化”：用某执行器训练出的策管器与自己的训练执行器配对时表现最好，说明有效的技能策管依赖执行器。因此需要让策管器能自适应不同执行器行为。

Method: 形式化行为自适应技能策管问题并提出EASE框架：维护近期执行模式的在线行为画像（online behavioral profile），并以其连同当前轨迹与检索到的技能为条件，让策管器在演化技能库中执行添加、修改、删除操作。训练上，在多个冻结执行器间联合训练共享策管器，使用强化学习，并采用检索感知与行为感知的时序归因（retrieval-aware and behavior-aware temporal attribution），把优化聚焦到对下游有可观测影响的策管动作上。

Result: 在ALFWorld、ScienceWorld、WebShop上，执行器覆盖Qwen3-8B/32B、GPT-OSS-120B以及未见过的Kimi K2.6、DeepSeek V4 Flash、Gemini 3.5 Flash，EASE在无需逐执行器微调的情况下优于强技能/记忆基线。同时技能数量减少34.5–41.0%，技能检索提升36.3–38.7%，可测编辑效用提升51.8–60.0%，部署时推理token减少9.1–14.5%。

Conclusion: 结果表明行为自适应技能策管是构建自演化智能体的一条有效原则：通过显式建模并适配执行器行为，可提升跨执行器泛化与效率，同时保持技能库精简与检索/编辑质量。

Abstract: Agent skills provide a lightweight mechanism for self-evolving agents to accumulate reusable procedural knowledge without updating model parameters. However, existing learned skill curators typically optimize curation without explicitly modeling downstream executor behavior. We show that this can cause systematic cross-executor degradation: curators trained with different executors perform best when paired with their own training executor, indicating that effective skill curation is executor-dependent. We formulate behavior-adaptive skill curation and introduce EASE, a framework that learns a single curator that adapts its decisions to different executor behaviors. EASE maintains an online behavioral profile of recent execution patterns and conditions the curator on this profile, the current trajectory, and retrieved skills to add, modify, or remove skills from an evolving repository. We train the shared curator jointly across multiple frozen executors with reinforcement learning, using retrieval-aware and behavior-aware temporal attribution to focus optimization on curation actions with observable downstream influence. Across ALFWorld, ScienceWorld, and WebShop, with executors ranging from Qwen3-8B/32B and GPT-OSS-120B to unseen Kimi K2.6, DeepSeek V4 Flash, and Gemini 3.5 Flash, EASE outperforms strong skill- and memory-based baselines without per-executor finetuning. EASE also maintains 34.5--41.0% fewer skills, improves skill retrieval by 36.3--38.7% and measured edit utility by 51.8--60.0%, and reduces deployment-time inference tokens by 9.1--14.5%. These results establish behavior-adaptive skill curation as an effective principle for building self-evolving agents.

</details>


### [345] [Generalizable Lifelong Model Editing via Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36748)
*Dahyun Jung,Suhyune Son,Heuiseok Lim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出GLIME框架，将知识编辑与生成行为的偏好优化结合，并引入回放式编辑与梯度约束，在终身编辑场景下提升知识泛化能力，同时保持编辑效果与通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实场景需要持续更新知识的终身编辑框架，而非一次性修改。现有编辑方法在终身设置下容易过拟合目标提示，导致编辑知识的泛化性和模型通用能力显著下降。

Method: GLIME将知识编辑与针对生成行为的偏好优化相结合，并进一步引入基于回放的编辑机制与梯度约束，以保留先前编辑的知识。

Result: 实验结果表明，GLIME在终身编辑设置下显著提升了知识泛化能力，同时维持了编辑性能和通用能力。

Conclusion: GLIME通过偏好优化与回放、梯度约束的协同，有效缓解终身编辑中的过拟合问题，实现泛化性与通用能力的平衡。

Abstract: Knowledge editing enables rapid updates of specific factual knowledge in large language models (LLMs) without full retraining. However, more realistic scenarios call for a lifelong framework that handles continual updates rather than one-off modifications. In such settings, existing editing methods often overfit to target prompts, significantly degrading both the generalization of the edited knowledge and the model's general capabilities. To address this issue, we propose GLIME (Generalizable Lifelong Model Editing), which combines knowledge editing with preference optimization over generation behavior. GLIME further incorporates replay-based editing and a gradient constraint to preserve previously edited knowledge. Experimental results show that GLIME significantly improves knowledge generalization in lifelong editing settings while maintaining both editing performance and general capabilities.

</details>


### [346] [Emergent Specialization in Populations of Self-Supervised Collaborative Vision Experts Without a Shared Gate or Cross-Agent Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36770)
*Aram Davtyan,Pablo Acuaviva,Sebastian Stapf,Paolo Favaro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究无共享门控、无梯度通信条件下神经网络群体能否自发形成有用分工，提出DISCO机制。实验表明专业化可涌现且有用，语义路由群体超越单一通才，局部路由器98%情况下选中专家，效果跨多种设置稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合专家模型依赖联合训练的中心门控将输入分配给专家，专业化受中心控制。作者想探究：若每个网络拥有独立权重、独立训练，且只能通过前向传播向其他智能体求助、智能体之间无梯度传递，能否在没有中心控制的情况下涌现出有用的分工与专业化。

Method: 在预测性视觉预训练的小规模代理任务上验证：初始相同的智能体在六个视觉域的无标注混合数据上微调，使用冻结DINOv3特征的掩码预测。以"最优智能体是否对应输入潜在域"衡量专业化，以"责任是否分散"衡量利用率。逐步移除中心控制，最终提出DISCO：每个智能体局部选择助手，通过无梯度通道读取其内部状态，仅依据帮助带来的提升来奖励自身路由器。

Result: 专业化涌现且有效：随机路由群体表现不及单一通才，语义路由群体则超越它，说明增益源于专业化而非群体规模。无中心路由器时专业化仍持续，无梯度通信使非专家能利用涌现的专家能力。在DISCO中，被专家帮助的随机智能体可匹敌独立通才，专家则超越之；局部路由器在98%输入上选中涌现专家。这些效果在群体规模、模型容量、数据不平衡与微调随机种子上均稳健。

Conclusion: 研究表明，去中心化条件下分工与专业化可自发涌现并具有实用价值，为去中心化预测性预训练所需动力学提供了可测量的证据。专业化本身而非群体规模是性能增益的关键驱动因素。

Abstract: Can a population of neural networks develop a useful division of labor without a shared gate or gradients between agents? We study a setting where each network has its own weights, trains independently on the same heterogeneous data, and can ask another agent for help through a forward pass. Unlike mixtures of experts, where a jointly trained gate assigns inputs to experts, specialization here must emerge without central control. We test this in a small scale proxy for predictive visual pretraining. Initially identical agents are finetuned on an unlabeled mixture of six visual domains using masked prediction of frozen DINOv3 features. We measure specialization by asking whether the best agent for an input aligns with its latent domain, and utilization by asking whether responsibility is distributed across agents. We progressively remove central control, ending with DISCO (DIStributed COllaboration) where each agent locally selects a helper, reads its internal state through a gradient free channel, and rewards its router only for the improvement that help provides. Specialization emerges and is useful. Randomly routed populations underperform a single generalist, while semantically routed populations outperform it, showing that specialization rather than population size drives the gain. Specialization persists without a central router, and gradient free communication lets nonexperts exploit emergent expertise. In DISCO, a random agent helped by the expert matches the solo generalist, while experts surpass it, including on data outside the specialization mixture. Local routers select the emergent expert for 98% of inputs. These effects persist across population size, model capacity, data imbalance, and finetuning seeds, providing measurable evidence for the dynamics needed by decentralized predictive pretraining.

</details>


### [347] [Code4Scene: Benchmarking Coding Agents for Constructing and Editing 3D Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36777)
*Xiaokang Ye,Siddhant Hitesh Mantri,Zimeng Chen,Edward Zhang,Zhaoxu Zheng,Yuanheng Li,Yizhao Chen,Tianyang Huang,Lianhui Qin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Code4Scene：一个包含190个Unreal Engine案例的基准，用于评测编码智能体的3D场景构建与编辑能力，揭示"看似合理的3D生成"与"可靠的空间推理与状态控制"之间存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿编码智能体已能编写并执行代码来生成3D环境，但其是否真正理解3D结构、能否精确控制场景状态仍不明确。生成的3D场景是持久化的可执行产物，逼真的渲染可能掩盖错误的空间关系、物体穿插或非预期的修改，因此需要一套直接面向引擎原生场景的评测基准。

Method: 提出Code4Scene基准，由人工搭建场景转化出的190个Unreal Engine案例构成，在统一执行接口下评测两类互补任务：Construction（从开放式语言规格进行场景级空间推理，存在多种合法实现）与Editing（依据参考图像精确恢复目标场景并保持其余部分不变）。评测不针对代码或渲染视图，而是检查生成引擎原生场景的任务完成度、产物完整性与静态物理有效性，编辑任务另与保留的真值对比。在95个公开案例上评测14种编码智能体配置。

Result: Construction与Editing表现强相关但不可相互替代（Spearman ρ=0.78）；Claude Fable 5.1在Construction上领先，Gemini 3.8 Flash在Editing上领先，GPT-6 Astra综合略优。Spatial Composition对所有智能体均为最弱子类；编辑精度不足，最佳Repair F1仅0.527，且35.8%完全恢复目标的编辑仍会在场景其他位置引入非预期改动。

Conclusion: Code4Scene通过直接评测引擎原生场景，暴露了当前编码智能体在3D空间推理与状态控制上的实质缺陷，表明"可生成逼真3D"并不等同于"可靠理解与控制3D结构"，为该方向的能力提升提供了可量化的基准。

Abstract: Frontier coding agents can now write and execute code that authors 3D environments, but whether they reliably understand 3D structure and precisely control scene state remains unclear. The generated 3D scene is a persistent, executable artifact: a convincing render can hide incorrect spatial relations, intersecting objects, or unintended modifications. We introduce Code4Scene, a benchmark of 190 Unreal Engine cases built from human-assembled scenes that evaluates coding agents on two complementary settings under a shared execution interface. Construction tests scene-level spatial reasoning from open-ended language specifications, where many realizations are valid; editing tests precise control of scene state, where the agent must recover the target scene from reference images while preserving everything else. Rather than scoring code or rendered views, Code4Scene evaluates the generated engine-native scene for task fulfillment, artifact integrity, and static physical validity, with edits additionally compared against withheld ground truth. Across 14 coding-agent configurations on the 95-case public set, construction and editing performance are strongly correlated but not interchangeable (Spearman $ρ= 0.78$): Claude Fable 5.1 leads construction, Gemini 3.8 Flash leads editing, and GPT-6 Astra narrowly leads overall. Spatial Composition is the weakest construction category for every agent, while editing remains imprecise: the best Repair F1 is only 0.527, and 35.8% of edits that fully recover the target still introduce unintended changes elsewhere in the scene. These results expose a gap between plausible 3D generation and reliable spatial reasoning and state control.

</details>


### [348] [Aperture: Merge-Consistent Rotary States for Compressed Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36781)
*Yuhao Du,Shunian Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究token压缩中位置信息的最小保存形式，提出"孔径"（Aperture）表示：在模型旋转频率处存储token加权支撑的傅里叶矩，并证明其在所需旋转交互下具有最小实维数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 位置编码（如旋转位置嵌入RoPE）通常为合并后的token只分配一个坐标，但token压缩会融合多个位置的内容。作者追问：在后续合并中，哪些位置信息必须被保留下来，才能维持所需的旋转交互。

Method: 提出Aperture表示，在模型各旋转频率上存储token加权支撑的傅里叶矩；证明这些矩在所选定期望旋转交互的连续状态中具有最小实维数。表示的质量使更新可加，注意力归一化则作为独立的读出选择。对均匀区间，该表示分解为中心旋转乘以sinc增益，并刻画中心能确定区间宽度的条件，构造中心无法确定宽度的配对反例。数值实验验证加权支撑实现。

Result: 数值检查验证了加权支撑实现。在训练好的时间读取器中，压缩迁移效果随增益校准与特征放置而变化。在预设的native视频问答对比中，存储支撑方法达到65.63%准确率，而所部署的合并规则为67.12%。

Conclusion: 结果区分了压缩下精确的位置保持与下游收益：傅里叶矩在理论上以最小维数完整保留位置信息，但这一精确保持并不自动转化为下游任务性能提升。

Abstract: Token compression combines content from several positions, yet rotary position embeddings usually assign the merged token one coordinate. We ask what positional information must survive later merges. Aperture stores Fourier moments of the token's weighted support at the model's rotary frequencies. We prove that these moments have minimal real dimension among continuous states sufficient for the selected expected rotary interactions. Represented mass makes updates additive; attention normalisation remains a separate readout choice. Uniform intervals give a centre rotation times a sinc gain. We characterise when centres determine interval widths and construct matched examples where they do not. Numerical checks verify the weighted-support implementation. In trained temporal readers, compression transfer varies with gain calibration and feature placement. In a prespecified native video question-answering comparison, stored support reaches $65.63\%$ accuracy versus $67.12\%$ for the deployed merging rule. These results separate exact positional preservation under compression from downstream benefit.

</details>


### [349] [AI as a Compiler: Compiling Triton kernels without the Triton compiler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36800)
*François Costa,Charly Castes,Thomas Bourgeat,Azalia Mirhoseini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"AI lowering"：用LLM智能体将Triton内核直接翻译为NVIDIA PTX，绕过传统优化与降层流水线。在Ada、Hopper、Blackwell及近期ML论文内核上，性能达自动调优Triton的0.83x–3.34x，最大收益来自Triton降层不做的变换。作者还扩展PTX验证器Volta以支持Blackwell tcgen05 Tensor Core接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编译器后端随编程模型、工作负载与加速器演进，构建和维护成本高昂。作者希望探究LLM能否替代传统优化与降层流水线，从而降低为新芯片（通用或定制）搭建软件栈的时间和工程投入。

Method: 研究从Triton到NVIDIA PTX的AI lowering：构建可评估候选PTX的环境与智能体式测试框架（agentic harness），由LLM将Triton内核直接翻译为PTX；并基于已有PTX验证器Volta做大幅扩展，支持现代GPU架构，特别是引入对Blackwell tcgen05 Tensor Core接口的支持，需建模受管的tensor memory、基于描述符的操作数布局，以及通过commit、wait、memory barrier和proxy fence协调的异步执行。

Result: 在Ada、Hopper、Blackwell GPU上的12个常见内核，以及来自近期ML论文的10个内核上，AI lowering达到自动调优Triton性能的0.83x–3.34x。最大收益来自Triton降层流水线不执行的变换：将打包二值权重直接解码为Tensor Core操作数（BitDelta上3.34x）、在tensor memory中为每个线程分配完整softmax行（FlashAttention上1.37x）、以及复用重叠卷积窗口（最高2.23x）。这些结果依赖于具备全面验证支持的健壮评估框架。

Conclusion: 结果指向一种新兴未来：AI编译器可替代手写的中间表示与检查器，从而减少为新通用及定制芯片开发软件所需的时间与工程成本。作者同时讨论了形式化上述架构特性所面临的挑战与当前局限。

Abstract: Compiler backends are expensive to build and maintain as programming models, workloads, and accelerators evolve. We investigate whether large language models can replace the conventional optimizing and lowering pipeline, a process that we call AI lowering. We study AI lowering from Triton to NVIDIA PTX: an LLM agent translates Triton kernels directly into PTX. We build an environment that evaluates candidate PTX, and an agentic harness in which an LLM translates Triton kernels into PTX. Across twelve common kernels on Ada, Hopper, and Blackwell GPUs and ten kernels from recent ML papers, AI lowering achieves 0.83x-3.34x the performance of autotuned Triton. The largest gains come from transformations that Triton's lowering pipeline does not perform, such as decoding packed binary weights directly into Tensor Core operands (3.34x on BitDelta), assigning each thread a complete softmax row in tensor memory (1.37x on FlashAttention), and reusing overlapping convolution windows (up to 2.23x). These results rely on a robust evaluation harness with comprehensive verification support. We build on Volta, an existing PTX verifier, and substantially extend it to support modern GPU architectures by introducing support for Blackwell's tcgen05 Tensor Core interface. This requires modeling three architectural features: managed tensor memory, descriptor-based operand layouts, and asynchronous execution coordinated through commits, waits, memory barriers, and proxy fences. We discuss the challenges involved in formalizing them, as well as the current limitations. Our results suggest an emerging future in which AI compilers replace custom-written intermediate representations and checkers, reducing the time and engineering effort required to bring up software for new general-purpose and custom chips.

</details>


### [350] [UpliftMem: Learning Set-Level Uplift for Agent Memory Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36805)
*Mengkun Liang,Haoran Qiang,Guannan Liu,Junjie Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 UpliftMem，用"相对无记忆执行器的集合级执行增益"作为学习信号来训练记忆检索打分器，并通过 EVSI 准则定向探测替代记忆集合，以更高效地利用有限的执行反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体复用外部记忆来指导新任务，但有效检索需要学习"哪些记忆集合能改善执行"。这类学习依赖昂贵的执行结果反馈：普通检索只能观测到已执行集合，评估备选集合则需要额外 rollout，导致反馈覆盖受限、训练代价高。

Method: 提出 UpliftMem：以同一执行器在"有记忆"相对"无记忆"时的集合级执行增益（uplift）作为训练信号，用冻结执行器训练共享打分器。理论分析检索偏好如何限制反馈覆盖，据此对替代记忆集合做定向探测；探测选择采用期望样本信息价值（EVSI）准则，在相关高斯模型下给出闭式解，按对局部检索决策的期望改进来分配有限训练 rollout。测试时无需探测即可选择记忆集合。

Result: 在 ALFWorld、WebShop、BigCodeBench 上，UpliftMem 在主评测集的成功率均优于所评估的基线。固定记忆库与匹配探测预算的受控实验进一步表明，其记忆使用决策更优、执行反馈利用更有效。

Conclusion: 以执行增益为监督信号、结合 EVSI 定向探测，可在有限执行反馈下有效学习 LLM 智能体的记忆检索，并带来稳定性能提升。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents reuse external memory to guide new tasks, but effective retrieval requires learning which memory sets improve execution. Such learning relies on costly outcome feedback: ordinary retrieval observes only executed sets, while evaluating alternatives requires additional rollouts. We introduce \textsc{UpliftMem}, which learns memory retrieval from set-level execution uplift relative to the same executor without memory. A theoretical analysis of how retrieval preferences restrict feedback coverage motivates targeted probing of alternative memory sets. Probe selection follows an expected value of sample information (EVSI) criterion, derived in closed form under a correlated Gaussian model, to allocate limited training rollouts according to their expected improvement in local retrieval decisions. The shared scorer is trained with a frozen executor and selects memory sets without test-time probes. Across ALFWorld, WebShop, and BigCodeBench, \textsc{UpliftMem} achieves the best success rates among evaluated baselines on the main evaluation sets. Controlled fixed-store and matched probe budget evaluations further demonstrate improved memory-use decisions and more effective use of execution feedback.

</details>


### [351] [CAD-Native Transformer Operators for AI-Aided Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36806)
*Daniel Leibovici,Nikola Borislavov Kovachki,Dawon Ahn,Ruben Ohana,Ira J. S. Shokar,Abouzar Ghasemi,Semih Akkurt,Rishikesh Ranade,Neil Ashton,Jan Kautz,Jean Kossaifi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CANTO：直接以连续CAD几何（NURBS）为输入的Transformer神经算子，无需网格化即可预测物理场，在四个气动基准上取得最优精度并支持基于梯度的逆设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值仿真需对连续参数化CAD几何进行网格划分，该过程计算昂贵且脆弱，常需人工干预，并将连续表示替换为离散近似。多数神经代理模型虽加速了仿真，却依赖网格、点云、体素等采样近似，继承了这一表示鸿沟。因此需要一种直接从连续CAD几何映射到物理场的算子学习方法。

Method: 提出CANTO，一种从几何流形到物理场函数空间的算子学习理论框架，以参数化面片序列表示几何。实例化时直接对NURBS面片的控制点、节点向量和权重进行token化，在任意查询位置预测连续曲面与体场，全程无需对输入几何进行网格划分。

Result: 在AhmedML、WindsorML、DrivAerML、HiLiftAeroML四个汽车与航空气动工业基准上评估。CANTO在多数表面与体预测任务上达到最优精度，在HiLiftAeroML上表面压力相对L2误差相较AB-UPT降低19.8%。对CAD参数的可微性支持基于梯度的逆设计：在AhmedML上，在满足相同体积与升力约束下，找到的构型阻力比数据集最优设计低4.4%~20.4%，并用原数据集相同的CFD设置验证了改进。

Conclusion: CANTO弥合了连续CAD表示与神经代理模型之间的表示鸿沟，实现了从连续几何到物理场的端到端映射，兼具高精度与可微逆设计能力，为无需网格化的工程仿真与优化提供了新范式。

Abstract: Modern engineering systems, from automobiles to aircraft, are designed by using precise, continuous parametric computer-aided design (CAD) models. Evaluating design changes through numerical simulation requires meshing the continuous geometry, a computationally expensive and often brittle process that can require manual intervention and replaces the continuous representation with a discrete approximation. Most neural surrogates accelerate the simulation, but inherit this representation gap by relying on meshes, point clouds, voxels, or other sampled approximations of geometry. We introduce CANTO, a transformer neural operator that maps directly from continuous CAD geometry to physical fields, without meshing the input geometry. We develop a theoretical framework for learning operators from geometric manifolds to function spaces of physical fields, representing geometry through sequences of parametric patches. CANTO instantiates this framework by directly tokenizing non-uniform rational B-spline (NURBS) patches from their control points, knot vectors, and weights, and predicts continuous surface and volume fields at arbitrary query locations. We evaluate CANTO on four automotive and aircraft aerodynamics industry benchmarks: AhmedML, WindsorML, DrivAerML, and HiLiftAeroML. CANTO achieves state-of-the-art accuracy on most evaluated surface and volume prediction tasks, including a 19.8% reduction in surface-pressure relative $L_2$ error compared with AB-UPT on HiLiftAeroML. Differentiability with respect to CAD parameters further enables gradient-based inverse design of designs. On AhmedML, CANTO identifies designs with 4.4 to 20.4% lower drag than the best dataset designs satisfying the same volume and lift constraints, with the improvements verified using the same CFD setup used to generate the original dataset.

</details>


### [352] [Geometry-Conditioned Fixed-Scaffold Encoders for Time-Warp Robust Sequence Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36809)
*Cassandra Yang,Yufan Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 GeoPatch——一种固定支架的 patch 编码器,使 token 支撑集不随几何变化而变化,将几何信息仅作为连续条件变量,从而在非规范时钟下提升基于嵌入的检索稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于嵌入的检索适合长序列集合,因为每个条目只需编码一次并通过最近邻排序检索。难点在于被索引对象常处于非规范时钟下:心搏周期随心率伸缩、语音随语速变化、传感器轨迹以不同速度到达相似状态。此类情形中一个关键不稳定源是:若 patch 边界由信号几何决定,则同一时间形变既会导致分词(tokenization)改变,而表示本应容忍该形变。

Method: GeoPatch 采用固定支架(fixed-scaffold)的 patch 编码方案,令 token 支撑集与几何无关;仅将斜率、曲率、加速度、仿射残差与置信度等描述子用作连续条件变量。该设计把边界变化转化为特征调制:几何可通过受控路径改变嵌入,但无法改变局部 token 的数量、顺序与支撑集。

Result: 通过机制层面的稳定性分析,区分了边界漂移、仿射时序变化、置信度加权几何扰动与检索间隔(retrieval-margin)效应。同一组局部 token 同时支持全局嵌入检索与后期交互(late-interaction)打分,使打分规则可与评测协议对齐。在 ECG、语音与多变量时间序列检索任务上,GeoPatch 在时序变化下提升了早期排名检索效果,同时揭示出局部表面匹配与严格非重叠检索之间的明显权衡。

Conclusion: GeoPatch 通过解耦 token 边界与几何,为时序形变下的嵌入检索提供了更稳定的分词与可解释的稳定性机理,并在多类任务上验证了有效性;但其代价是局部表面匹配与严格非重叠检索之间的结构性权衡。

Abstract: Embedding-based retrieval is attractive for long sequence collections because each item can be encoded once and searched by nearest-neighbor ranking. The difficulty is that the objects being indexed are often observed under a noncanonical clock: cardiac cycles stretch with rate, speech changes with tempo, and sensor traces reach comparable states at different speeds. This paper studies a specific source of instability in patch-based encoders for this regime. If patch boundaries are chosen from signal geometry, then the tokenization can change under the same temporal deformation that the representation is expected to tolerate. We propose GeoPatch, a fixed-scaffold patch encoder that keeps token support independent of geometry and uses slope, curvature, acceleration, affine-residual, and confidence descriptors only as continuous conditioning variables. The design turns boundary variation into feature modulation: geometry can change the embedding through a controlled pathway, but it cannot change the number, order, or support of local tokens. We formalize this distinction through a mechanism-level stability analysis that separates boundary drift, affine timing variation, confidence-weighted geometry perturbation, and retrieval-margin effects. The same local tokens support global embedding retrieval and late-interaction scoring, so the scoring rule can be matched to the evaluation protocol. Across ECG, speech, and multivariate time-series retrieval tasks, GeoPatch improves early-rank retrieval under timing variation while exposing a clear trade-off between local surface matching and strict non-overlap retrieval.

</details>


### [353] [Calibrate the Decisions That Change the Future: On-Policy Post-Training Quantization for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36828)
*Wenxiao Fan,Jingling Fu,Lichen Ma,Yu He,Luohang Liu,Jinbao Xue,Ke Zhang,Junshi Huang,Kan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 OnPTQ，一种面向多模态大语言模型训练后量化（PTQ）的在线策略校准框架。它通过在当前量化策略访问的轨迹上校准，识别量化侵蚀的决策边界，结合反事实短推演构建"决策—后果风险"，从而在低比特设置下提升下游性能并减少正确性翻转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型 PTQ 的校准通常重建固定序列并使用局部目标，忽略了自回归反馈：量化导致的 token 变化会改变前缀并影响后续状态。同时，仅依赖 on-policy 覆盖也不充分，因为许多决策不匹配几乎不影响未来生成，需要区分关键状态。

Method: OnPTQ 在当前量化策略访问的轨迹上进行校准：在共享前缀上识别量化侵蚀的边界，通过短反事实推演评估竞争 token，将当前差异与分支后果结合为"决策—后果风险"（Decision–Consequence risk）。该风险用于优先处理关键状态，并采用上下文锚定与轨迹刷新来保留多模态行为、保持校准与更新后策略一致。此外推导了决策—后果界，将行为偏差与当前策略差异及动作条件未来值跨度关联。

Result: 在视觉—语言和全模态 Qwen 模型的多种低比特设置下，OnPTQ 提升了下游性能，并且相对于对应的 Dense/FP16 参考产生更少的正确性翻转，同时不改变部署推理图。

Conclusion: OnPTQ 通过引入自回归反馈感知的在线策略校准与决策—后果风险度量，有效解决了多模态大模型 PTQ 中被忽视的反馈问题，在多种低比特场景中实现性能提升与部署友好的量化。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) lowers deployment cost for multimodal large language models, but calibration typically reconstructs fixed sequences with local objectives. This overlooks autoregressive feedback: a quantization-induced token change redirects the prefix and changes future states. Yet on-policy coverage alone is insufficient because many decision mismatches barely affect future generation. We propose OnPTQ, an on-policy framework that calibrates on trajectories visited by the current quantized policy. On shared prefixes, OnPTQ identifies quantization-eroded boundaries, evaluates competing tokens through short counterfactual rollouts, and combines current discrepancy with branch consequence into a Decision--Consequence risk. The risk prioritizes critical states, while context anchoring and trajectory refresh preserve multimodal behavior and keep calibration aligned with the updated policy. We further derive a Decision--Consequence bound linking behavioral deviation to current policy discrepancy and action-conditioned future-value span. Across vision--language and omni-modal Qwen models under multiple low-bit settings, OnPTQ improves downstream performance and yields fewer correctness flips against the corresponding Dense/FP16 references, without changing the deployed inference graph.

</details>


### [354] [The Default Trap: Rethinking Plan Evaluation in Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36829)
*Xueqi Li,Jingjie Ning,Yibo Kong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出“默认陷阱”（default trap）概念：执行体对计划优先级的响应可能很强，但仅凭“计划 vs 无计划默认”对比会误判其对替代优先级的响应性；作者用成对计划对比与会计恒等式分离这些行为差异，并在大量决策窗口与完整任务中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当移除一个与默认对齐的整体计划时，信息选择概率变化很小，但同一执行体对计划优先级变化的响应却很强。若仅依据“计划 vs 默认”的对比来解释，就会把这种小变化误读为对替代优先级响应弱，即“默认陷阱”。因此需要区分并联合报告优先级响应性、呈现方式导致的默认效应以及任务成功与成本。

Method: 研究设计以成对计划为核心：两个计划优先不同信息目标，并与共享的“无计划”参考进行对比；通过会计恒等式关联这些不同的行为对比。实验覆盖 Retail、Airline 和 AgentDojo 任务，包含 3,200 个决策窗口（160 个选定任务）、2,160 个额外窗口（反转账户列表顺序）、3,240 个窗口的组件研究（单一优先级句子与附加文本）以及 1,080 个完整任务回合。

Result: 在 3,200 个决策窗口中，切换优先级强烈改变两个模型的选择，而“计划 vs 默认”的两种对比并不相同。在 2,160 个额外窗口中，反转账户列表顺序使默认目标选择变化 63.3–98.3 个百分点；优先级切换效应在两种顺序下仍为 96.7–100.0 个百分点。3,240 窗口组件研究显示单一优先级句子下控制较强，附加文本效应因组别和方向而异。1,080 个完整任务回合中，相对无计划的观测成功差异为 -19.4 至 +8.3 个百分点；Retail 和 Airline 的成功区间均包含零，AgentDojo 结果仅描述四个固定应用世界。

Conclusion: 应联合报告三项指标：对优先级的响应性、依赖于呈现方式的默认效应、以及任务成功与成本。仅用“计划 vs 无计划”的对比会落入默认陷阱，导致对执行体优先级响应性的错误解释。

Abstract: An executor can respond strongly to a change in a supplied plan's priority while showing a small change in the same information-selection probability when a default-aligned whole plan is removed. We call the risk of interpreting the latter as weak responsiveness to alternative priorities the default trap. We compare paired plans that prioritize different information targets with a shared no-plan reference. An accounting identity relates these distinct behavioral contrasts. Across 3,200 decision windows on 160 selected Retail, Airline, and AgentDojo tasks, switching priorities strongly redirects two models' choices, while the two plan-versus-default contrasts differ. In 2,160 additional windows, reversing account-list order shifts default target selection by 63.3-98.3 percentage points; priority-switching effects remain 96.7-100.0 points in either order. A separate 3,240-window component study finds strong control under single priority sentences, with effects of additional text varying by group and direction. Finally, 1,080 full-task episodes yield observed success differences of -19.4 to +8.3 points relative to no plan. All Retail and Airline success intervals include zero; AgentDojo results describe four fixed application worlds. These findings support joint reporting of priority responsiveness, presentation-dependent defaults, and task success and cost.

</details>


### [355] [Where Does Staleness Accumulate? Pool Aware Effective Staleness Control for Asynchronous RL in LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36830)
*Chenliang Li,Neiwen Ling,Zijun Wei,Alfredo Garcia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 PACE 方法，将异步 RL 的策略滞后分解为生成滞后与等待滞后，通过池感知的自适应拒绝预算控制有效滞后，在相同墙钟预算下大幅提升验证精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全异步强化学习通过重叠轨迹生成与策略优化提升资源利用率，但轨迹排队期间策略持续更新会引入策略滞后（policy lag）。已有做法未能区分滞后在轨迹生命周期中的不同累积阶段，且可能因长轨迹跨越多个策略版本而被不合理惩罚，因此需要在不牺牲异步墙钟收益的前提下有效控制滞后。

Method: 将轨迹陈旧度分解为生成滞后（Generation Staleness，轨迹生成完成前累积）与等待滞后（Waiting Staleness，轨迹进入轨迹池后累积）。基于该分解提出 PACE（Pool-Aware Control of Effective Staleness）：将超出容量的池占用转化为自适应拒绝预算，并使用有效滞后分数对已完成轨迹排序，该分数融合等待滞后与前缀感知的生成滞后，从而避免仅因轨迹跨越多个策略版本而惩罚长或被中断的轨迹。

Result: 单轮数学推理中，相同墙钟预算下 PACE 相较未过滤异步 RL 的六基准平均验证准确率提升 18.7%，并以少 47.1% 的 GPU 时间达到同步 RL 的性能。在多轮工具集成推理中，PACE 验证性能优于同步与未过滤异步 RL。混合专家模型及另一种 RL 算法的实验进一步支持其跨模型架构与训练算法的适用性。

Conclusion: 通过显式分解并区分生成滞后与等待滞后，并以池占用驱动的自适应拒绝机制控制有效滞后，PACE 能在保持异步执行墙钟优势的同时缓解策略滞后，提升训练效果并具备跨架构、跨算法的通用性。

Abstract: Fully asynchronous reinforcement learning (RL) improves resource utilization in large language model post-training by overlapping rollout generation with policy optimization, but it also introduces policy lag as trajectories are generated and queued while the trainer continues to update. We study how this lag accumulates over a trajectory's lifetime and how it can be controlled without sacrificing the wall-clock benefits of asynchronous execution. We decompose trajectory staleness into Generation Staleness, accumulated before rollout completion, and Waiting Staleness, accumulated after a completed trajectory enters the pool. Motivated by this decomposition, we introduce PACE (Pool-Aware Control of Effective Staleness). PACE converts excess pool occupancy into an adaptive rejection budget and ranks completed trajectories using an effective-staleness score that combines Waiting Staleness with prefix-aware Generation Staleness. This avoids penalizing long or interrupted rollouts solely because they span multiple policy versions. In single-turn mathematical reasoning, PACE improves the six-benchmark average validation accuracy by 18.7\% over unfiltered asynchronous RL at the same wall-clock budget and matches synchronous RL performance with 47.1\% less GPU time. PACE also improves validation performance in multi-turn tool-integrated reasoning, outperforming both synchronous and unfiltered asynchronous RL. Further experiments with the mixture-of-experts model and an alternative RL algorithm support its applicability across model architectures and training algorithms.

</details>


### [356] [ARC-KV: Amortizing Anchor Search for Reconstruction-Based KV Cache Compaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36835)
*Zheyu Shen,Guanhua Wang,Dezhan Tu,Mengchi Zhang,Yanjia Li,Adnan Aziz,Chunqiang Tang,Ang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ARC-KV，一种基于重建的KV缓存压缩方法，通过可复用的锚点选择策略摊销迭代搜索成本，在保持下游性能的同时将压缩时间降低约25.73倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM推理中KV缓存随序列长度线性增长，尤其对可复用的长上下文前缀负担严重，其缓存需服务大量下游查询。基于重建的方法（如Attention Matching）虽能以紧凑KV缓存获得良好性能，但OMP式Attention Matching的迭代锚点搜索主导了压缩成本，亟需降低该开销。

Method: 遵循选择性摊销原则：跨上下文学习可复用的锚点选择策略，同时保留上下文特定的重建。具体地，先训练一个值感知索引器，通过单次打分遍历选择真实键锚点；再进行凸包约束的键合并，并针对完整缓存拟合注意力质量偏置与紧凑值。推理时每上下文仅用冻结索引器构建一次紧凑缓存并复用于后续查询。

Result: 在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上，于QuALITY、RULER、LongBench的多数设置中优于已报道的压缩方法。QuALITY 10% KV保留率下，准确率从0.6409提升至0.6474（相对Attention Matching），压缩时间从959.8 s降至37.3 s，降低25.73倍。

Conclusion: ARC-KV通过将锚点搜索从每上下文的迭代优化转为可学习的单次打分索引，在压缩效率与下游性能间取得更优平衡，验证了选择性摊销原则的有效性。

Abstract: Long-context large language model inference is bottlenecked by KV caches that grow linearly with sequence length. This burden is especially severe for long, reusable context prefixes, whose cache must serve many downstream queries. Reconstruction-based methods such as Attention Matching achieve strong downstream task performance with compact KV caches. However, iterative anchor search dominates the compaction cost of OMP-based Attention Matching. This motivates our selective amortization principle of learning a reusable anchor-selection policy across contexts while retaining context-specific reconstruction. In this work, we propose ARC-KV, a novel reconstruction-based KV cache compaction method that follows this principle. To this end, we first train a value-aware indexer to select real-key anchors in a single scoring pass. ARC-KV then applies convex-hull-constrained key merging and fits an attention-mass bias and compact values against the full cache. At inference time, ARC-KV builds the compact cache once per context using the frozen indexer and reuses it for all subsequent queries. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ARC-KV outperforms reported compaction methods in most settings across QuALITY, RULER, and LongBench on Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct. In particular, at 10% KV retention on QuALITY, ARC-KV improves accuracy from 0.6409 to 0.6474 over Attention Matching while reducing compaction time by a factor of 25.73, from 959.8 s to 37.3 s.

</details>


### [357] [Automated Screw Planning for Reduced Pelvic Fractures Based on Statistical Shape Models and Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36847)
*Yang Gao,Sutuke Yibulayimu,Yanzhen Liu,Zian Zhao,Yudi Sang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出全自动经皮骶髂螺钉术前规划流程，在200例骨盆骨折病例中实现安全通道安全裕度提升2%、规划时间缩短90%以上、临床接受率达95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经皮骶髂螺钉固定是骨盆不稳定骨折的重要微创治疗方式，但骶髂区解剖复杂、螺钉通道狭窄，置钉精度与安全性直接影响手术效果；传统术前规划依赖人工测量和主观临床经验，耗时且一致性差，亟需自动、可靠的术前规划方法。

Method: 基于患者个体化三维解剖模型，构建全自动术前规划流程：自动识别安全螺钉通道，并生成个体化置入轨迹，以支持临床术前规划。

Result: 在200例临床骨盆骨折病例上评估：相比传统人工测量，四类螺钉的安全置入通道安全裕度提升2%，平均规划时间减少90%以上，临床接受率为95%。

Conclusion: 该全自动规划流程可安全、高效地生成经皮骶髂螺钉置入轨迹，显著降低规划耗时并保持高临床接受度，具备临床术前规划应用价值。

Abstract: Percutaneous iliosacral screw fixation is an important minimally invasive treatment for unstable pelvic fractures. Because the sacroiliac region has complex anatomy and narrow screw corridors, the accuracy and safety of screw placement directly affect surgical outcomes. Accurate and reliable preoperative screw planning is therefore essential to improve surgical success and reduce intraoperative risks. Conventional preoperative planning typically requires surgeons to determine screw trajectories through manual measurements, a labor-intensive process that depends on subjective clinical experience. To address these challenges, we propose a fully automated pipeline for preoperative iliosacral screw planning in patients with pelvic fractures. Using patient-specific three-dimensional anatomy, the pipeline automatically identifies safe screw corridors and generates individualized insertion trajectories to support clinical preoperative planning. We evaluated the proposed pipeline on 200 clinical cases of pelvic fractures. Compared with conventional manual measurements, the safety margin of the safe insertion corridors increased by 2% across the four screw types, the mean planning time decreased by more than 90%, and the clinical acceptance rate reached 95%.

</details>


### [358] [When Upstream Messages Override Correct Answers: A Controlled Study of Multi-Agent LLM Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36855)
*Yaxin Gong,Gangyi Zhang,Chongming Gao,Leyang Shen,Chenxiao Fan,Jiakai Wang,Dong Wang,Yang Liu,Wenjie Wang,Xiangnan He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文通过控制实验区分多智能体LLM系统中通信的收益与错误消息造成的损害，发现消息既能帮助下游智能体纠正错误，也会导致其放弃原本正确的答案，并将该现象命名为“答案替换”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统依赖智能体间的消息传递，但上游消息可能有用，也可能因错误答案导致下游智能体覆盖其基于自身证据得出的正确答案。已有工作未能清晰区分通信带来的收益与错误消息造成的损害。

Method: 在五个基准和五个接收方智能体上开展控制实验，保持下游任务与证据不变，对比三种条件下的答案：无消息、上游智能体的原始消息、以及结论相反的消息。

Result: 三项关键发现：一是当下游智能体本会答错时，消息通常有帮助；二是消息也可能有害，当下游本可答对时，错误的上游消息最多在32%的情况下改变其答案；三是在审计的有害案例中，94%的下游智能体直接复制上游的具体错误答案，即“答案替换”。

Conclusion: 移除不可靠消息可恢复部分损失的准确率，说明通信应基于上游可靠性以及下游智能体已有证据进行选择性使用。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems rely on message passing among specialized agents to accomplish complex tasks. However, an upstream agent may provide useful information or an incorrect answer that causes a downstream agent to override a correct answer supported by its own evidence. Prior work has not clearly separated the benefits of communication from the damage caused by incorrect messages. We study this problem with controlled experiments across five benchmarks and five receivers, keeping the downstream task and evidence fixed while comparing answers under three conditions: no message, the upstream agent's original message, or a message with the opposite conclusion. Our experiments reveal three key findings. First, messages often help when the downstream agent would otherwise answer incorrectly. Second, messages can also hurt: when the downstream agent would answer correctly without a message, an incorrect upstream message changes the answer in up to 32% of cases. Third, in 94% of audited harmful cases, the downstream agent copies the upstream's specific wrong answer--a pattern we term answer substitution. Removing unreliable messages recovers part of the lost accuracy, suggesting that communication should be selective based on upstream reliability and the evidence already available to the downstream agent.

</details>


### [359] [IronLLM: Forging Compact Edge-Native Language Models for Real-Time Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36860)
*Changdi Yang,Fengquan Jiao,Haochih Lin,Haoran Yang,Jing Xiao,Liangyu Huo,Suxin Lu,Tiance Chen,Wei Liu,Yinggan Xu,Yunxiang Lu,Zai Zheng,Zhirui Xie,Zhongyang Che,Ziyan Tang,Zuoxiang Zhao,Jian Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出IronLLM-0.6B（654M参数端侧模型），采用混合注意力与X-MTP多token预测，经6.2T token预训练和多域在线策略蒸馏后训练，性能对标更大模型；并给出量化友好的IronLLM-0.6B-Light轻量变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端侧部署对低延迟、低资源有强需求，需要在受限算力下兼顾推理效率与模型能力，而现有较大模型难以满足该场景的性能-效率平衡。

Method: 1) 混合注意力架构；2) X-MTP：轻量共享KV的多token预测，消除逐层KV缓存重放，配合轻量验证头实现免回滚草稿，获得1.48x解码加速；3) 约6.2T token的质量导向数据管线预训练；4) 多域在线策略蒸馏（Multi-Domain On-Policy Distillation）整合领域专家教师能力；5) 采用Instruct-Only设计降低延迟；6) IronLLM-0.6B-Light用Dynamic Tanh替换RMSNorm并简化高开销组件以提升推理与量化效率。

Result: IronLLM-0.6B相对Qwen3.5-0.8B、MiniCPM5-1B等更大模型表现具竞争力，且在许多任务上输出更简洁；X-MTP带来1.48x解码加速；Light变体改善了推理和量化效率。

Conclusion: IronLLM系列在资源受限部署场景下提供了有效的性能-效率权衡。

Abstract: We present IronLLM-0.6B, a 654M-parameter language model designed for efficient on-device inference. IronLLM-0.6B combines a hybrid attention architecture with X-MTP, a lightweight shared-KV multi-token prediction design that eliminates per-depth KV-cache replay and employs a lightweight verification head for rollback-free drafting, achieving a 1.48x decoding speedup. The model is pretrained on approximately 6.2 trillion tokens using a quality-oriented data pipeline and is further post-trained with Multi-Domain On-Policy Distillation to integrate capabilities from domain-specialized teachers. To better meet the low-latency requirements of on-device scenarios, IronLLM-0.6B adopts an Instruct-Only design. Evaluations show that IronLLM-0.6B achieves competitive performance relative to larger models such as Qwen3.5-0.8B and MiniCPM5-1B, while producing more concise responses on many tasks. We further present IronLLM-0.6B-Light, which replaces RMSNorm with Dynamic Tanh and simplifies several computationally expensive components to improve inference and quantization efficiency. Together, the IronLLM models provide an effective performance-efficiency trade-off for resource-constrained deployment.

</details>


### [360] [State Trace Rationale As Auxiliary Task in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36867)
*Muhammad U. Nasir,Alex Vogt,Steven D. James,Julian Togelius*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出STRAT辅助任务，让深度强化学习智能体预测自身状态的简短文本轨迹描述，在60个稀疏奖励任务中显著提升性能并增强可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准强化学习在稀疏奖励环境下往往难以求解复杂任务，且智能体内部状态表征缺乏可解释性。受人类空间导航中地标、路线、勘测知识的启发，作者希望借助文本形式的自状态描述来辅助智能体学习。

Method: STRAT作为辅助任务，训练RL智能体预测其自身状态的简短文本轨迹。该文本由环境规则在线生成，无需人工标注。描述融合了地标、路线与勘测知识，跟踪智能体的位置、物品、目标及即时进展。实现上仅需在标准策略网络中添加一个辅助预测头。

Result: 在60个稀疏奖励的XLand-MiniGrid任务上，STRAT能够求解标准RL完全失败的复杂环境，同时使状态表征更加紧凑并防止秩崩溃。此外，预测出的文本轨迹为每一步的智能体信念提供了可读的解释，且无额外成本。

Conclusion: STRAT通过预测自状态文本轨迹这一辅助任务，同时提升了稀疏奖励下的RL性能、表征质量与可解释性，是一种有效且低成本的方法。

Abstract: We propose STRAT, an auxiliary task that trains deep reinforcement learning (RL) agents to predict a short textual trace of their own state. Inspired by human spatial navigation, the description combines landmark, route, and survey knowledge, tracking the agent's position, inventory, goals, and immediate progress. Environment rules generate this text online without human labelling. Our method adds a single auxiliary head to a standard policy. Across 60 sparse-reward XLand-MiniGrid tasks, STRAT solves complex environments where standard RL fails outright, while compacting state representations and preventing rank collapse. Beyond performance gains, the predicted trace provides a readable account of agent beliefs at every step for no extra cost.

</details>


### [361] [WEFT: Scaling Tool-Use Post-Training for General-Purpose Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36887)
*Bo Mao,Hang He,Linting Wang,Lizhi Lin,Maosen Zhou,Guanming Liu,Jinxiu Liu,Tianyu Huai,Chaoyun Zhang,Bingxuan Li,Kepeng Lei,Guanting Dong,Zhou Shao,Rui Zheng,Hang Yan,Jie Zhou,Chengcheng Wan,Tao Gui,Liang He,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出WEFT框架，通过整体系统进化进行工具使用后训练，在多个基准上超越仅扩展环境的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具使用后训练主要聚焦于可执行环境的合成，但环境只是更广泛的智能体交互系统（环境、任务、智能体框架、评估器）的一个组成部分。单独扩展环境无法保证模型性能的相应提升，因为可靠的学习信号依赖系统各组件间的连贯交互。

Method: WEFT将可扩展的智能体交互系统构建、执行驱动的自进化与稳定的后训练三者耦合。系统构建跨环境广度、任务复杂度和交互多样性进行扩展；自进化利用执行轨迹与状态证据定位失败原因并修正责任组件，通过新的rollout评估变更；稳定后训练方面，前缀保持采样保留已验证进展，原子轮信用分配定位学习信号，MegaMCP在共享工具服务的并发rollout间维持隔离、可恢复的状态。

Result: 在多种模型和基准上的实验证明了WEFT工具使用后训练的有效性。WEFT-8B和WEFT-14B在BFCL V4、τ²-Bench和Claw-Eval上均优于所有同规模环境扩展基线，其中WEFT-14B相较Agent-World-14B分别提升6.41、2.23和12.27个百分点。WEFT-35B-A3B进一步在Toolathlon-Verified和AutomationBench等更具挑战的长时程工作流基准上延续了收益。

Conclusion: 整体系统进化优于孤立的环境扩展，将环境、任务、智能体框架与评估器协同优化可带来工具使用后训练性能的一致提升。

Abstract: Recent efforts to scale tool-use post-training have largely centered on the synthesis of executable environments, which constitute only one component of a broader agentic interaction system comprising the environment, task, agent harness, and evaluator. Scaling environments in isolation, however, does not guarantee commensurate gains in model performance, because reliable learning signals depend on coherent interactions among all components of the agentic interaction system. To address this problem, we introduce WEFT (Whole-system Evolution For Tool-use Post-training), which couples scalable agentic interaction system construction, execution-driven self-evolution, and stable post-training. WEFT scales agentic interaction system construction across environment breadth, task complexity, and interaction diversity. Execution-driven self-evolution iteratively uses execution traces and state evidence to attribute failures and revise the responsible components, with fresh rollouts evaluating the changes and providing evidence for subsequent evolution rounds. For stable post-training at scale, WEFT addresses both optimization and execution reliability: prefix-preserving sampling retains verified progress and atomic-turn credit assignment localizes learning signals, while MegaMCP maintains isolated, recoverable state across concurrent rollouts over shared tool services. Extensive experiments across various models and benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of WEFT for tool-use post-training. WEFT-8B and WEFT-14B outperform all evaluated matched-size environment-scaling baselines on BFCL V4, $τ^2$-Bench, and Claw-Eval. In particular, WEFT-14B improves over Agent-World-14B by 6.41, 2.23, and 12.27 percentage points. WEFT-35B-A3B further extends these gains to more challenging long-horizon workflow benchmarks, including Toolathlon-Verified and AutomationBench.

</details>


### [362] [Beyond Sub-Gaussian Detector Scores: Robust Weighted Profile-Loss Change Point Detection for Human-LLM Text Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36888)
*Wan Tian,Zhongyi Li,Yawen Li,Rui Zhang,Yijie Peng,Fuzhen Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RWCP方法，通过可靠性加权-稳健损失框架在混合人机文本中定位作者身份转换点，理论保障下显著优于现有加权变点检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合人机（human-LLM）文档需要从检测器分数中定位作者身份的转换边界，但检测器可靠性在不同文本单元间存在差异。现有加权均值对比方法易受极端分数影响，而直接用稳健中心替换均值又会掩盖边界误置对总体目标函数的影响。

Method: RWCP（Robust Weighted Profile-Loss Change Point Detection）结合三个要素：截断的可靠性权重（capped reliability weights）、Huber剖面增益（Huber profile gains）以及可靠性坐标下的最窄超阈值搜索（narrowest-over-threshold search）。关键分析将真实切分与错位切分之间的总体差距表示为合并代价（merge cost），从而避免对混合片段的非线性中心求闭式解。在明确的曲率、间距和依赖条件下证明核心RWCP能恢复变点数量并定位边界；其二次损失极限退化为加权平方CUSUM。另提出RWCP-R，一种独立评估的解码器，在非相邻段落间共享源中心。

Result: 在五个回顾性缓存分数基准系列上，核心RWCP相较加权变点检测将family-macro WindowDiff降低17.6%，RWCP-R进一步降低。边界恢复在孤立变点情形改善最明显，但两种固定配置在协作型及密集交替文本中均漏检变点。

Conclusion: RWCP为检测器可靠性异质的作者转换点检测提供了兼具稳健性与理论保障的加权损失框架，其二次损失极限连接至经典加权CUSUM；边界定位在孤立变点上表现最佳，而在协作或密集交替文本上的漏检表明变点间距与依赖性假设是未来改进方向。

Abstract: Mixed human-LLM documents require locating authorship transitions from detector scores whose reliability varies across text units. Existing weighted mean contrasts are vulnerable to extreme scores, while directly replacing means with robust centers obscures how a misplaced boundary changes the population objective. We propose Robust Weighted Profile-Loss Change Point Detection (RWCP), which combines capped reliability weights, Huber profile gains, and narrowest-over-threshold search in reliability coordinates. Our key analysis expresses the population gap between a true and a displaced split as a merge cost, avoiding a closed-form solution for the nonlinear center of a mixed segment. Under explicit curvature, spacing, and dependence conditions, core RWCP recovers the number of changes and localizes their boundaries; its quadratic-loss limit recovers squared weighted CUSUM. We also study RWCP-R, a separately evaluated decoder that shares source centers across nonadjacent passages. Across five retrospective cached-score benchmark families, core RWCP reduces family-macro WindowDiff by 17.6\% relative to weighted change-point detection, and RWCP-R lowers it further. Boundary recovery improves most clearly for isolated changes, while both fixed configurations miss changes in collaborative and densely alternating text.

</details>


### [363] [Harness Evolution as Learning: Approximation, Generalization, and Optimization Limits of Self-Improving Personal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36892)
*Zeyu Gan,Zixuan Gong,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文将个人智能体的能力提升归结为"harness engineering"（管理上下文、记忆、工具与执行的周边层设计与演化），围绕harness架构、规模与自演化算法三个问题，结合偏好导向的实证基准与理论学习论分析，揭示个人化的能力边界并为harness设计提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM能力增强，如何将其能力转化为实际可用行为成为关键问题。个人智能体需在底层模型固定的前提下服务单个用户并持续适应其偏好，此时适应依赖harness engineering。然而，决定harness演化有效性的因素研究不足，作者希望弥合这一认知空白。

Method: 采用实证与理论互补的分析：(1) 提出一个偏好导向（preference-oriented）的基准，系统刻画个人智能体在harness架构、harness规模、自演化算法三个维度上的能力与局限；(2) 将harness演化形式化为一个学习问题，用近似误差、泛化误差与优化误差加以解释，并分析可达策略集、有限交互证据下的容量以及有偏更新动态。

Result: 实证上系统识别了三维度下个人智能体的能力边界与局限；理论上通过可达策略、有限交互信息下的容量、更新动态偏差等分析，为观察到的现象提供了理论解释。三个维度共同构成对harness演化机制的统一刻画。

Conclusion: 研究给出关于经harness演化实现个人化的能力极限的统一视角，为未来harness设计提供依据与方向。

Abstract: As the capabilities of large language models (LLMs) continue to advance, increasing attention is turning to how to translate their abilities into useful behavior. Personal agents bring this question into everyday settings, where models are expected to serve individual users and continually adapt to their preferences. With the underlying model held fixed, such adaptation relies on harness engineering: designing and evolving the surrounding layer that manages context, memory, tools, and execution. Despite rapid progress, the factors governing effective harness evolution remain insufficiently understood. To narrow this gap, we investigate three central questions concerning harness architecture, harness scale, and self-evolution algorithms through complementary empirical and theoretical analyses. Empirically, we introduce a preference-oriented benchmark and systematically characterize the capabilities and limitations of personal agents associated with these three dimensions. Theoretically, we formulate harness evolution as a learning problem and explain these phenomena through approximation, generalization, and optimization errors. Analyses of reachable policies, capacity under finite interaction evidence, and biased update dynamics provide theoretical accounts of the observed phenomena. Together, these results offer a unified perspective on the limits of personalization through harness evolution and inform future harness design.

</details>


### [364] [HorizonFlow: Variable-Length Planning for Offline Goal-Conditioned RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36896)
*JunHyeok Oh,Zian Jang,Byung-Jun Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出HorizonFlow，一种层次化规划器，将规划长度视为生成输出而非预设输入，通过插入式生成与流匹配联合生成连续规划内容与长度，在多个基准上取得最优平均性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹修复方法在生成规划内容前需预先指定规划时域，而合适的时域依赖具体路径本身。时域过短会强制产生不可行转移，过长则引入冗余运动。

Method: HorizonFlow为层次化规划器：子目标路径规划器通过一系列潜在子目标引导动作前缀控制器；两个组件均结合基于插入的生成与流匹配，联合生成连续的规划内容与长度，并利用部分生成的规划指导token插入；同时复用长度信息进行候选选择并引导生成更短规划，无需单独学习价值模型。

Result: 在Maze2D、Multi2D以及OGBench的导航与视觉操作基准上，HorizonFlow在所比较方法中取得最高平均性能。

Conclusion: 将规划长度作为生成输出可避免预设时域的次优性，基于插入与流匹配的联合生成框架能有效处理可变长度规划，且长度信息可用于候选挑选与偏好短规划，无需额外价值模型。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative planning have made trajectory inpainting a promising approach to offline goal-conditioned reinforcement learning. However, these methods typically specify the planning horizon before generating plan content, even though the appropriate horizon depends on the route itself. A horizon that is too short can force infeasible transitions, whereas one that is too long can introduce redundant motion. We introduce HorizonFlow, a hierarchical planner that treats plan length as an output of generation rather than a prescribed input. Its subgoal route planner guides its action-prefix controller through a sequence of latent subgoals. Both components combine insertion-based generation with flow matching to jointly generate continuous plan content and length, using the partially generated plan to guide token insertion. HorizonFlow reuses the resulting length information to select candidates and steer generation toward shorter plans without a separate learned value model. Across Maze2D, Multi2D, and OGBench navigation and visual manipulation benchmarks, HorizonFlow achieves the highest average performance among the compared methods.

</details>


### [365] [STAR-GRPO: Canonical Anchoring and Reliability-First Advantages against Representation-Dependent Reward Hacking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36900)
*Wan Tian,Zhongyi Li,Xiang Xu,Minhao Zou,Yijie Peng,Fuzhen Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出STAR-GRPO，一种基于同一rollout配对评估的可靠性优先优势估计器，通过将质量信号与其学习影响力分离，缓解组相对策略优化中的奖励黑客问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励黑客发生在策略优化利用脆弱的奖励接口或过于宽松的代理目标，从而提高训练分数却未提升真实响应质量。该问题在组相对策略优化中被放大：不受支持的奖励会移动组基线并改变其他rollout的更新，且事后或纯相对的加权方式无法表示组级不确定性。

Method: 提出STAR-GRPO。其基于同一rollout的配对评估，分离质量信号与其学习影响力：评分分歧决定rollout可靠性；相对可靠性在组归一化前进入自调优的鲁棒位置-尺度拟合；绝对组可靠性对所得有界优势进行衰减。理论分析建立了坐标与二阶矩界，通过加权位置方程刻画精确中心化，并给出针对离群奖励的可靠性依赖衰减保证。

Result: 在两种互补的奖励黑客场景中评估：在token接口利用中，STAR阻止部署接口分数的失控优化，同时提升规范质量信号；在医学推理的评分标准代理过度优化中，STAR改善独立语义评估、缩小代理与评判者差距并减少过度声称，同时优化同一任务代理。

Conclusion: 可靠性优先的归一化为限制不受支持奖励对组基线和策略更新的影响提供了有原则的方法，同时保留任务奖励作为优化目标。

Abstract: Reward hacking occurs when policy optimization exploits a brittle reward interface or an overly permissive proxy objective, improving the training score without improving the underlying response quality. This phenomenon is amplified in group-relative policy optimization: an unsupported reward can shift the group baseline and alter the updates of other rollouts, while post-hoc or purely relative weighting cannot represent group-wide uncertainty. We propose \emph{Self-Tuned Anchored Reliability Group-Relative Policy Optimization} (STAR-GRPO), a reliability-first advantage estimator based on paired assessments of the same rollout. STAR separates the quality signal from its learning influence: score disagreement determines rollout reliability, relative reliability enters a self-tuned robust location--scale fit before group normalization, and absolute group reliability attenuates the resulting bounded advantage. The analysis establishes coordinate and second-moment bounds, characterizes exact centering through the weighted location equation, and gives reliability-dependent attenuation guarantees for outlying rewards. We evaluate STAR-GRPO in two complementary reward-hacking regimes. In token-interface exploitation, STAR prevents runaway optimization of the deployed-interface score while improving the canonical quality signal. In rubric-proxy overoptimization for medical reasoning, STAR improves independent semantic evaluation, narrows the proxy--judge discrepancy, and reduces overclaim while optimizing the same task proxy. Together, these results show that reliability-first normalization offers a principled way to limit unsupported reward influence on both group baselines and policy updates, while retaining the task reward as the optimization target.

</details>


### [366] [PrecogUI: Proactive GUI Agents via Pre-cognitive Simulation and Experience Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36923)
*Bin Kang,Jiarui Ouyang,Li Jiang,Bin Chen,Zhuotao Tian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 PrecogUI，一种预认知（pre-cognitive）GUI 智能体架构，通过经验池、仿真执行器与预认知控制器实现从反应式执行到主动决策的转变，并构建 InterfereBench 基准验证其抗干扰能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反应式 GUI 智能体在长时程、动态场景中表现不佳：意外扰动会分散注意力并引发级联失败。因此需要从被动响应转向主动预测与提前规避异常。

Method: 1）Proactive Experience Pool（PEP）：以"state-action-result"元组形式在双记忆库中缓存反复出现的异常与成功模式；2）Proactive Simulation Executor（PSE）：学习根据候选动作预测下一符号化 UI 布局，实现早期异常规避并按预测可靠性对候选动作排序；3）Pre-cognitive Execution Controller（PEC）：融合上述先验与预测，优先处理可预见异常，并通过闭环纠错机制保障执行鲁棒性。

Result: 在 InterfereBench 上超越现有最先进方法，同时在公开基准上保持有竞争力的性能。为支撑评估，作者开发了自动数据生成引擎 AutoTraj，并据此构建了含强扰动的长时程任务基准 InterfereBench。

Conclusion: 预认知（proactive）范式可有效提升 GUI 智能体在长时程动态干扰场景下的鲁棒性；代码将公开。

Abstract: Existing reactive Graphical User Interface (GUI) agents often fail in long-horizon, dynamic scenarios, where unexpected disturbances trigger attention-diverting and cascading failures. To address this, we propose PrecogUI, a pre-cognitive architecture that shifts the paradigm from reactive execution to proactive decision-making. Specifically, we design a Proactive Experience Pool (PEP), which caches recurring anomaly and success patterns as "state-action-result" tuples in a dual-memory repository. Furthermore, we introduce a Proactive Simulation Executor (PSE) that learns to forecast the next symbolic UI layout given a candidate action, enabling early anomaly avoidance and ranking candidate actions by predicted reliability. Finally, a Pre-cognitive Execution Controller (PEC) fuses these priors and predictions, prioritizes handling of foreseen anomalies, and ensures execution robustness through a closed-loop error correction mechanism. For robust evaluation, we develop AutoTraj, an automatic data-generation engine, to construct InterfereBench, a benchmark for long-horizon tasks with strong disturbances. Experiments demonstrate that PrecogUI surpasses state-of-the-art methods on InterfereBench while maintaining competitive performance on public benchmarks. The code will be publicly available.

</details>


### [367] [Neuro-Symbolic Computer Use: Learning Reusable Policies for Reliable and Efficient Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36927)
*Hyewon Suh,Thanh Minh Nguyen,Chih-Lun Lee,Darrow Hartman,Lizhao Liu,Xin Eric Wang,Ang Li,Jiachen Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出神经符号计算机使用方法：将重复工作流固化为可执行代码策略（固定顺序、变量、循环、分支），仅将依赖观测的决策（grounding、状态检查）交给神经模型，通过神经符号策略迭代从单条轨迹学习策略，在OSWorld-Verified和ScienceBoard上取得最高Pass^3，并大幅降低开销与延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多计算机任务具有重复性：同一工作流需以新输入、不同初始状态反复执行，而现有计算机使用代理每次都逐步重新规划，导致成本高、可靠性差。

Method: 神经符号策略迭代：从一条代理轨迹出发，执行策略，用任务完成与步骤级评判器诊断失败，由编码模型结合代理在失败点的续行来修订代码，且不使用基准评测器；通过生成的参数与初始状态变体进行迭代以提升可复用性；部署时用动作前验证器守护每个改变状态的步骤。

Result: 在OSWorld-Verified与ScienceBoard的全部四种设置中，所学策略取得最高Pass^3，比基础代理高3.6–15.8分，单次运行成本降低15–217倍，延迟降低3.4–5.1倍；在OSWorld-Verified上，仅基于变体构建的策略可迁移到留出的原始任务，Pass^3超过AutoRPA 8.6–17.5分。

Conclusion: 将重复工作流以可执行代码策略形式学习并复用，配合神经模型处理观测相关决策，可在显著降低成本与延迟的同时提升可靠性与跨任务迁移能力。

Abstract: Many computer tasks recur: the same workflow runs many times, with new inputs and from different starting states. Current computer-use agents re-plan every step of every run, which makes them costly and unreliable on such tasks. We introduce neuro-symbolic computer use, in which a recurring workflow is executed by a learned policy rather than re-derived by an agent on each run. The policy fixes the decisions that are stable across runs (ordering, variables, loops, and branches) in executable code, and delegates observation-dependent decisions, such as grounding and state checks, to neural models. We learn these policies with neuro-symbolic policy iteration: starting from one agent trajectory, it executes the policy, diagnoses failures with task-completion and step-level judges, and revises the code with a coding model informed by an agent's continuation from the point of failure, without access to the benchmark evaluator. Iterating on generated parameter and initial-state variants makes the policy reusable, and a pre-action verifier guards each state-mutating step at deployment. On OSWorld-Verified and ScienceBoard, the learned policies achieve the highest Pass^3 of all methods in all four settings, 3.6-15.8 points above the base agent, while cutting per-run cost by 15-217$\times$ and latency by 3.4-5.1$\times$. On OSWorld-Verified, policies built only on variants transfer to the held-out original tasks, exceeding AutoRPA by 8.6-17.5 points in Pass^3.

</details>


### [368] [Learn from the Gap: Differential-Aware Advantage Pruning with Adaptive Rollout Sampling for GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36932)
*Jiahua Yang,Zhiwei Yang,Xianpeng Zhang,Dongyu Chen,Xing Chen,Tianhuang Su,Haonan Lu,Quanlong Guan,Kai Tang,Chuangchuang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FastRL，一个即插即用的强化学习框架，通过优势感知剪枝与自适应采样，在提升训练效率的同时增强策略学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO及其变体虽带来性能提升，但每问题多次rollout采样与逐token重复概率评估导致计算开销巨大；同时低信息或高度同质的轨迹会降低下游学习信号效率，阻碍优化并限制最终性能。

Method: 1）优势感知剪枝策略：选择性保留高优势轨迹，同时最大化轨迹间梯度多样性；2）自适应rollout采样机制：依据历史剪枝分布动态调整不同训练阶段的采样规模，平衡探索充分性与计算效率。

Result: FastRL可无缝集成到GRPO、DAPO和GSPO变体中，在Geometry3K与GeoQA8K-R1V上实现平均2.07倍训练加速，并在视觉推理基准上取得约1.64%的平均准确率提升。

Conclusion: FastRL作为一种即插即用框架，同时改善了训练效率与策略学习有效性，具备良好的通用性与可迁移性。

Abstract: Recently, Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) and its variants have been developed for policy optimization and demonstrated notable performance gains. However, these methods usually incur substantial computational overhead due to per-question multi-rollout sampling and repeated per-token probability evaluation across rollouts. Furthermore, low-information or highly homogeneous trajectories can degrade downstream learning signal efficiency, hindering model optimization and limiting final performance. To address these issues, we propose FastRL, a novel plug-and-play reinforcement learning framework that simultaneously improves training efficiency and the effectiveness of policy learning. Specifically, 1) We introduce an advantage-aware pruning strategy to selectively preserve high-advantage trajectories while maximizing inter-trajectory gradient diversity. 2) Then, we design an adaptive rollout sampling mechanism to dynamically adjust the sampling scale across different training stages based on historical pruning distributions, balancing exploration adequacy and computational efficiency. Experiments demonstrate that FastRL can be seamlessly integrated into GRPO, DAPO, and GSPO variants, achieving an average 2.07$\times$ training speedup on Geometry3K and GeoQA8K-R1V, along with an approximately 1.64\% improvement in average accuracy on visual reasoning benchmarks. Source codes will be available at https://github.com/Nicozwy/FastRL.

</details>


### [369] [VLALight: A Vision-Language-Action Model for Traffic Signal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36934)
*Pan Zhang,Siqi Lai,Kemu Dong,Hao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VLALight是首个面向端到端交通信号控制的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，直接将多视角路侧视频映射为协调信号动作，在七个真实数据集上超越交通、RL及LLM/VLM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TSC方法依赖人工设计的交通状态或独立感知模块，导致物理观测与控制决策之间存在割裂；路侧摄像头虽已广泛部署并提供丰富视觉观测，却未被端到端利用。

Method: 1) 基于多视角路侧视频直接映射到协调信号动作，通过多目标时空交通推理与跨路口拓扑感知协同感知；2) 两阶段监督冷启动训练，先学视觉交通理解再学信号决策；3) 协同智能体强化学习，联合优化局部控制与全网效率；4) 自适应快慢推理模式，配合平衡的模式感知rollout与相对优势优化，权衡决策质量与推理成本。

Result: 在三个城市路网的七个真实交通流数据集上，VLALight一致优于基于交通模型、RL及LLM/VLM的基线；消融实验验证了协同感知、网络级优化与自适应推理的有效性。

Conclusion: 结果展示了VLA模型在真实物理交通控制中的潜力。

Abstract: Traffic signal control (TSC) is essential for improving urban mobility and reducing congestion. Although roadside cameras are widely deployed at signalized intersections and provide rich visual observations of evolving traffic, existing TSC methods typically rely on manually engineered traffic states or separate perception modules, creating a gap between physical observations and control decisions. We present VLALight, the first vision-language-action (VLA) model for end-to-end traffic signal control from multi-view roadside videos. VLALight directly maps visual observations to coordinated signal actions through multi-target spatiotemporal traffic reasoning and topology-aware cooperative perception across intersections. To establish this capability, we develop a two-stage supervised cold-start training strategy for visual traffic understanding and signal decision-making, followed by cooperative agentic reinforcement learning that jointly optimizes local control and network-wide traffic efficiency. Furthermore, VLALight introduces adaptive fast and slow reasoning modes, enabling the policy to allocate deeper reasoning only when additional deliberation provides sufficient control benefits. Through balanced mode-aware rollouts and relative advantage optimization, VLALight learns to trade off decision quality and inference cost. Extensive experiments on seven real-world traffic-flow datasets across three urban networks demonstrate that VLALight consistently outperforms transportation-based, RL-based, and LLM/VLM-based baselines. Ablation studies validate the effectiveness of cooperative perception, network-level optimization, and adaptive reasoning. These results demonstrate the potential of VLA models for real-world physical traffic control. Our project is available at https://github.com/usail-hkust/VLALight.git.

</details>


### [370] [CoEM: Empowering Long-Context Reasoning with Commit-on-Evidence Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36935)
*Jingguang Li,Yebo Wu,Zuyi Guo,Kailang Ma,Xianjie Dai,Han Zheng,Benwang Chen,Li Li,Can Rong,Heye Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Commit-on-Evidence Memory（CoEM），通过延迟压缩与学习式记忆管理策略，在固定内存预算下提升大模型长上下文推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理对复杂任务至关重要，但 LLM 性能随上下文增长而下降。现有分块处理并维护有界文本记忆的方法存在过早压缩问题，会丢弃后续推理所需的关键细节。

Method: CoEM 在固定上下文-记忆预算下，将可能有用的源文本片段以原文形式保留在待定集合中，待后续上下文明确其相关性后再处理。新上下文到来时，学习到的策略重新审视每个待定片段，决定将其提升为已提交记忆、继续保留还是丢弃；同时由冻结的验证器确保新事实仅在得到保留片段与当前上下文支持时才被接受。策略通过强化学习训练，结合细粒度步级证据奖励与最终答案奖励。

Result: 大量实验表明 CoEM 可持续提升长上下文推理表现。在 6,400 篇文档的长上下文输入下，CoEM 在 Qwen3.5-9B 上比最强记忆基线高出 10.4–11.4 F1。

Conclusion: 延迟证据压缩并结合验证器与细粒度强化学习奖励，可有效缓解有界记忆中的信息损失，是提升长上下文推理的可行方向。

Abstract: Long-context reasoning is essential for complex and long-horizon tasks, yet the performance of large language models (LLMs) degrades as context length increases. Recent approaches address this by processing input chunk by chunk while maintaining a bounded textual memory in model context. However, premature information compression can discard critical details essential for subsequent reasoning. In this paper, we introduce Commit-on-Evidence Memory (CoEM), which learns when to convert source evidence into compact memory facts. Specifically, under a fixed context-memory budget, CoEM preserves potentially useful source excerpts verbatim in a pending set, allowing subsequent context to clarify their relevance before irreversible compression. As new context arrives, a learned policy revisits each pending excerpt and decides whether to promote it to the committed memory, retain it for further consideration, or discard it. A frozen verifier ensures proposed facts are accepted only if supported by retained excerpts and current context. To further guide effective memory management, we train this policy using reinforcement learning by combining fine-grained, step-level evidence rewards with final answer rewards. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CoEM consistently improves long-context reasoning. When evaluated on 6,400 documents long-context input, CoEM outperforms the strongest memory baseline by 10.4-11.4 F1 points on Qwen3.5-9B. Code repository: https://github.com/benmagnifico/CoEM.

</details>


### [371] [SCA: Spatial Credit Assignment for Reinforcement Learning of GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36939)
*Shengtian Yang,Ziyu Xiong,Kaibing Yang,Guangfeng Cai,Yewen Li,Peng Jiang,Gai Kun,Qingpeng Cai,Lei Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出空间信用分配（SCA）方法，利用点击坐标细化GUI智能体的组相对信用分配，无需改变部署策略即可改进策略更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有组相对强化学习通过比较同一GUI状态多个响应的奖励来改进动作预测，但存在两个缺陷：二元评估将空间上不同的失败点击视为相同；当所有采样点击均失败时缺乏相对信号。

Method: SCA利用采样点击的屏幕坐标细化组相对信用：在同时含成功与失败的组中，用其他响应预测被留出响应的奖励，以预测残差调整信用；当所有点击均失败时，按与标注目标的距离对它们排序。这些空间参考仅用于构造训练更新，部署策略保持不变。并通过受控合成实验对比其误差与方向对齐度与精确回报梯度。

Result: 在GUI grounding与离线动作预测基准上，SCA在专业领域提升grounding效果，在多数动作预测指标上取得强化微调模型中的最佳结果，且在所报告的GUI套件上增益一致。

Conclusion: SCA通过引入空间信息解决了二元奖励评估与全失败无相对信号的问题，在不改变部署策略的前提下有效改进策略更新。

Abstract: GUI agents automate tasks on digital devices by grounding language instructions in visual interfaces. Existing group-relative reinforcement learning improves GUI action prediction by comparing the rewards of multiple responses sampled from the same GUI state. However, binary evaluation treats spatially different failed clicks as identical and provides no relative signal when all sampled clicks fail. To address these limitations, we propose Spatial Credit Assignment (SCA), which uses the screen coordinates of sampled clicks to refine group-relative credit. Specifically, SCA predicts each held-out response's reward from the other responses in groups containing both successes and failures, then uses the prediction residual to adjust credit. When all sampled clicks fail, SCA instead orders them by distance to the annotated target. These spatial references are used only to construct the training update; the deployed policy remains unchanged. We evaluate whether this correction improves the policy update itself by comparing its error and directional alignment with the exact return gradient in a controlled synthetic study. Across GUI grounding and offline action-prediction benchmarks, SCA improves grounding across professional domains and achieves the strongest results among reinforcement-fine-tuned models on most action-prediction metrics, with consistent gains across the reported GUI suites.

</details>


### [372] [Dual-Channel Robust Group-Relative Policy Optimization via Advantage and Sequence-Weight Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36944)
*Zhongyi Li,Wan Tian,Xiang Xu,Yutian Xiao,Yikun Ban,Yijie Peng,Fuzhen Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RoVR-GSPO，一种双通道鲁棒优化器，分别针对奖励通道和比率通道的异常敏感性问题进行改进，在多项任务上优于GSPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组相对策略优化依赖奖励推导的优势值和序列级似然权重，二者对局部异常值敏感：极端奖励在组归一化后会破坏干净响应之间的对比度，而token级对数比扰动会改变序列权重和裁剪决策。

Method: RoVR-GSPO采用双通道设计：奖励通道结合鲁棒参考估计与有界残差信用；比率通道使用可微的SoftRoVR聚合来构建鲁棒序列权重。作者对两个通道分别进行了稳定性与效率分析。

Result: 在数学推理、长上下文摘要和工具调用标注任务上，相比GSPO取得持续提升；受控扰动实验表明对奖励污染和token比率异常具有更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过分通道处理两类失败模式，RoVR-GSPO在保持优化效率的同时提升了训练的鲁棒性与泛化表现。

Abstract: Group-relative policy optimization relies on reward-derived advantages and sequence-level likelihood weights, both of which can be sensitive to localized outliers. Extreme rewards can collapse the contrast among clean responses after group normalization, while token-level log-ratio perturbations can alter sequence weights and clipping decisions. We introduce RoVR-GSPO, a dual-channel robust optimizer that addresses these failure modes separately. Its reward channel combines robust reference estimation with bounded residual credit, while its ratio channel uses differentiable SoftRoVR aggregation to construct robust sequence weights. We provide stability and efficiency analyses for both channels. Experiments on mathematical reasoning, long-context summarization, and tool-call annotation show consistent improvements over GSPO, while controlled perturbation studies demonstrate stronger robustness to reward contamination and token-ratio anomalies.

</details>


### [373] [REALHOP: Rethinking Multi-Hop Reasoning Evaluation via Behavioral Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36984)
*Jiawen Tao,Xiaokun Yuan,Yaoming Li,Chenxu Liu,Mengzhou Wu,Tong Yang,Maxm Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出行为必要性率（BNR）衡量多跳推理对每一步证据的依赖，发现现有基准BNR仅16.6%–48.9%，据此构建REALHOP诊断—构造—验证框架，将BNR提升至94.4%并显著扩大模型区分度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多跳基准以预定义推理链构造问题，并把答案正确视为使用了预期组合推理。但答案正确并不能说明成功依赖各预期步骤的证据，模型可能依靠记忆关联、更短路径或部分证据。

Method: 定义行为必要性率（BNR）：在初始正确的实例上，移除目标证据后答案无法恢复的频率。据此提出REALHOP诊断—构造—验证框架：重绑定实体、分解选定关系、加入完整竞争路径、将证据置于可追踪位置；冻结前进行结构与语义检查，随后开展行为干预。

Result: 五个现有基准的面板平均BNR为16.6%–48.9%；790道配对的MuSiQue问题上BNR从27.4%升至94.4%且保持高Full准确率；REALHOP-FRAMES与REALHOP-LONGBENCH上BNR同样较高；216道长上下文问题上，16个模型的选择题差距从13.9分扩大至59.2分，并在重复开放式评估中保持。

Conclusion: 概念连贯的推理链与正确的最终答案本身并不能确立多跳推理；验证成功是否依赖每一个预期跳步，与测量答案准确率同等重要，应作为多跳评估的基本组成。

Abstract: Complex questions often require multi-hop reasoning that connects facts distributed across sources or distant regions of a long context through intermediate steps. Benchmarks commonly evaluate this ability with questions built around predefined reasoning chains, treating a correct answer as evidence that the intended composition was used. Yet answer correctness alone leaves open whether success depends on the evidence associated with each intended step: models may instead rely on memorized associations, shorter paths, or partial evidence. We examine this dependence using the Behavioral Necessity Rate (BNR), which measures how often targeted evidence removal prevents answer recovery on initially correct instances. Across five existing benchmarks, panel-mean BNR ranges from 16.6% to 48.9%, exposing a substantial gap between annotated structure and observed dependence. Guided by this diagnosis, we introduce REALHOP, a diagnose-construct-verify framework that rebinds entities, factorizes selected relations, adds complete competing paths, and places evidence at traceable locations. Structural and semantic checks precede freezing; behavioral interventions follow. On 790 paired MuSiQue questions, REALHOP raises panel-mean BNR from 27.4% to 94.4% while retaining high Full accuracy. It also yields high BNR on REALHOP-FRAMES and REALHOP-LONGBENCH. On 216 long-context questions, the matched multiple-choice spread across 16 models grows from 13.9 to 59.2 points and persists under repeated open-ended evaluation. Together, these results show that a conceptually coherent chain and a correct final answer do not by themselves establish multi-hop reasoning. Verifying that success depends on every intended hop is therefore as fundamental to multi-hop evaluation as measuring answer accuracy itself.

</details>


### [374] [CF-LoRA: Decoupled Factor Aggregation and Adaptation-Aware Client Clustering for Federated LoRA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36986)
*Mengjun Yi,Langxing Yang,Suhan Guo,Furao Shen,Jian Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CF-LoRA：解耦LoRA因子聚合+自适应聚类，缓解异构联邦微调的结构聚合与统计协作失配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦LoRA微调可在不共享私有数据的前提下实现参数高效适配，但在客户端数据异构时存在两个根本性失配：(1)结构聚合失配——对LoRA因子A、B独立平均会破坏低秩乘积结构；(2)统计协作失配——强制所有异构客户端共享单一全局适配器，导致负迁移。现有方法未同时解决这两类问题。

Method: CF-LoRA包含三步：(1)因子解耦学习：全局共享A因子，各客户端保留个性化B_i因子；(2)基于学得的B_i因子余弦相似度进行自适应聚类，识别适配模式相近的客户端；(3)簇内聚合：冻结A因子，仅在簇内对B因子聚合。通过解耦LoRA因子聚合保持低秩结构、缓解结构聚合失配；通过适配感知聚类促进相似客户端协作，减少统计异构带来的负迁移。

Result: 在4项语言任务（RoBERTa）和4个视觉数据集（ViT）上，CF-LoRA在两种模态下均取得最高平均准确率，且每轮优化仅通信一个LoRA因子。

Conclusion: CF-LoRA通过解耦因子聚合与适配感知聚类的联合设计，同时缓解结构聚合失配与统计协作失配，在语言与视觉任务上实现精度与通信效率的双重优势。

Abstract: Federated LoRA fine-tuning enables parameter-efficient adaptation of pre-trained models without sharing private data, but suffers from two fundamental mismatches under heterogeneous client data: a structural aggregation mismatch caused by independently averaging LoRA factors, and a statistical collaboration mismatch caused by enforcing a single global adapter across divergent clients. To address these issues, we propose CF-LoRA, a clustered federated LoRA fine-tuning framework that combines decoupled factor aggregation with adaptation-aware client clustering. CF-LoRA first learns a globally shared $A$ factor while retaining personalized $B_i$ factors, then identifies clients with similar adaptation patterns based on the cosine similarity of their learned $B_i$ factors, and finally performs intra-cluster $B$-factor aggregation with a frozen $A$ factor. By decoupling LoRA factor aggregation, CF-LoRA preserves the low-rank structure and mitigates the structural aggregation mismatch, while adaptation-aware clustering promotes collaboration among clients with similar adaptation patterns and reduces negative transfer caused by statistical heterogeneity. Experiments on four language tasks and four vision datasets with RoBERTa and ViT show that CF-LoRA achieves the highest average accuracy in both modalities while communicating only one LoRA factor per optimization round.

</details>


### [375] [ImbalancE: Inference-Time Latent Search Against Degree Imbalance in Link Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36996)
*Alberto Bernardi,Luca Costabello,Christophe Gueret*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 知识图谱嵌入模型在学得表示质量上存在显著区域差异，本文发现该差异与测试三元组中头尾实体间的度不平衡相关，并提出推理阶段的隐空间搜索优化方法，显著提升最不平衡三元组的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作仅将知识图谱嵌入表示质量差异松散地归因于关系类型或度偏差，但作者认为该问题更普遍，且与测试三元组中实体的度不平衡相关，尤其是目标实体度远小于锚实体度的三元组预测极差。这类三元组在推荐系统等场景中代表重要长尾/边角案例，因此亟需解决。

Method: 提出一种推理阶段的隐空间搜索优化方法（inference-time latent search optimization）。该方法构建于预训练模型之上，在评估时探索嵌入空间，混合已知信息与带外信息（out-of-band information），以缓解度不平衡偏差。

Result: 在常见基准数据集的不平衡三元组上，该方法优于传统方法，验证了其在最不平衡三元组预测上的有效性。

Conclusion: 度不平衡是知识图谱嵌入预测质量差异的普遍且关键因素，所提推理阶段优化方法能有效缓解该偏差，为知识图谱嵌入模型在关键长尾案例中的成功应用开辟了道路。
